# Whole-app audit and mock-up plan (12 Nov 2026)

Author: Fable (lead designer). Status: plan only, no mock-ups built.

**What this file is.** A list of every screen, menu, sheet, bar and dialog in the real app (`src/`), marked as already designed or not, followed by a plan to design everything that is left in 6 batches. The full feature lists with file:line are in Appendices A to I at the end of this file (nine scans of the code, about 4,400 lines), so nothing can get lost.

**How it was made.** Nine Sonnet scanners read the code and the approved mock-ups. Line numbers marked "~" in the appendices are approximate. The mock-ups were read as source code, not run in a browser.

Rules this plan follows: `rules/quality.md`, `docs/CONTINUITY.md`, `docs/design/quality-standard.md`, `docs/design/owner-ui-patterns.md`, `docs/plans/mock-feedback-2026-11-11.md`, the approved conditions in `docs/PENDING.md`, and the toolbar v7 design study.

---

## 1. Headline findings

1. **Beginner / Professional does not exist in the code yet.** No file in `src/` checks an experience level. Every feature below is visible to everyone today. So the Beginner/Professional split in this plan is new design, and "nothing lost" means: every feature listed must end up in Professional at least, each with its own unlock switch.
2. **The biggest gap is the page itself.** The "/" menu, the block handle menu, the page "More options" menu (about 22 items), the page breadcrumb, the page tab row, tables and databases have no mock-up at all.
3. **The six notebook tab bodies are one grey placeholder box in the Notebooks mock** (Summary, Cards, Revise, Database, Mind map, Slides).
4. **Three screens were in no inventory:** the Calendar screen (Day/Week/Month), the Study hub ("All cards" with Notes, Summary, Review) and the Databases hub.
5. **There is no shared sheet part, no alert dialog, no toast queue.** A second toast replaces the first, so a quick second action loses the first Undo. Each sheet is hand-built. These must become shared parts before the batches use them (quality rule 1).
6. **Things that do not exist anywhere in the code:** a keyboard-shortcut list, a command palette, notifications or reminders, a "what's new" screen, a feedback form, a custom right-click menu in the page (only a 1.5 s hold menu with the Text tool), bulk row actions in databases.
7. **Right-click and long-press are uneven.** Sidebar rows open their menu by long-press or "⋯" only. Trash buttons show on hover only, so touch users cannot see them.

"Partly" items are treated as not covered for planning: each one is assigned to a batch.

Status: **MOCK** = approved mock-up covers it. **V8** = toolbar v8 covers it (not touched here). **PART** = partly. **NONE** = no design yet. "App." = which appendix holds the full feature list.

### 3.8 Hand-over to the toolbar v8 job (not designed here)

The scan found these inside the toolbar's area but missing from the v8 feature map. They should be added to v8, not to a new batch: P12 Layout pop-up button, P14 box extras (8 handles, column dividers by drag, group pill, smart guides), P15 ink overlays (ruler handles, tape peel, eraser ring, hidden-layer chip), P16 Theme ▾ and the four Copy & paste items, hold-and-slide on a tool button (`appendix H, B9a`), Backspace hold-repeat, the table and video context strips. Full list: Appendix H, part B.

| Tag picker                                                                                          | in mocks                               | 1, 2, 4       | reuse                                              |

| **Command list** (searchable list with groups, keys shown; used by "/", "[[", "#", command palette) | NEW                                    | 1, 6          | one part, four uses                                |
| **Sheet** (docked right or bottom, fixed height, ✕)                                                 | NEW (none in code)                     | all           | owner note N4: fixed place and height              |
| **Tool frame** (full-screen tool with steps, ✓/✕ footer)                                            | NEW (close to `pdfTools/Shell.tsx:30`) | 3, 5          | crop, scan, OCR, organise, import                  |
| **Wizard steps** (clickable step strip)                                                             | NEW                                    | 2, 5          | CSV import, sign-in, restore                       |
| **Dialog set** (confirm, name, choice, alert)                                                       | partly (`ui.tsx`)                      | all           | add alert; red only for danger                     |
| **Toast queue** (stack of up to 3, kinds: done, undo, problem)                                      | NEW                                    | all           | fixes the lost-Undo problem                        |
| **Banner** (one line, icon, one action, ✕)                                                          | NEW                                    | 1, 5, 6       | template, journal, read-only, lock, backup, update |
| **Status pill stack** (bottom corner: sync, update, timer, now playing, recording)                  | NEW                                    | 3, 5, 6       | so pills never overlap                             |
| **Empty state** (picture, one line, one main action, optional second)                               | partly (`ui.tsx:142`)                  | all           | 34 inline ones move onto it                        |
| **Object bar** (selected table, picture, map node, database row)                                    | from v8 selection bar                  | 2, 3          | reuse v8's bar                                     |
| **Before / after view** (two columns with changes marked)                                           | partly (Claude Inbox)                  | 5             | History and conflicts                              |
| **Time range bar**                                                                                  | in Home mock                           | 2             | Calendar screen and database calendar              |

---

## 5. The six batches

Order: writing first, then what goes in a page, then media and reading, then study, then safety, then the system layer. Each batch = one tappable mock-up, opening in the default new-user state (Beginner), with a Beginner / Professional toggle, phone and iPad sizes, light and dark.

For every batch the spec lines ("When I do X → Y") become the acceptance tests. "Won't do" lists what is deliberately left out.

### Batch 1: Writing on the page (menus and page furniture)

**Covers:** W1, W2, W3, W5, W8, W9, W10, W11, W12, W13, W14, W15, W16, W17, A13, Y3 (+ first version of the shared Context menu, Command list, Sheet, Dialog set, Toast queue and Banner).

**What exists today (short; full lists in Appendices B, E, G, I):**

- "/" menu: BlockNote list with our items (headings, lists, table, card, card list, callout, database, mind map, maths, embed, file, "Insert template…", "Save page as template" and more). No guard inside table cells. `slashMenu.tsx:44`.
- Block handle: "+" adds a block, ⠿ drags; its menu has Delete, colours, card actions, picture items. Hover only, so touch users cannot reach it. `CardMenu.tsx:189`.
- Page ⋯ menu: about 22 items, among them History, Full-screen reader, Paper style and size, Import, Export, Save as template, Prompts, Lock, Search all features, Copy & paste ▸, Device mode ▸, Hide tool bar. `PageView.tsx:294-420`.
- Paste chooser with several "paste as" choices and a "Pasted as" chip; copy formats split over 3 menus. `PasteChooser.tsx:31`, `copyFormats.ts`.
- New-page start card (blank, templates, paper style row), template picker dialog, Templates sidebar group, starter templates. `NewPageStart.tsx:31`, `TemplatePicker.tsx:224`.
- Breadcrumb with "Pages at this level", "Hidden parts of the path" and "Full path" menus. `PagePath.tsx:178-320`.

**Planned premium improvements**

- One "/" menu drawn as a Command list: groups (Basic, Lists, Study, Media, Data, Page), a small picture per item, search as you type, the last 3 used on top.
- Block handle that works with touch and Pencil: tap the handle = menu, hold = drag, with the drop-line.
- One Context menu for right-click (PC), long-press (touch) and ⋯, with the same rows in the same order everywhere.
- The page ⋯ menu regrouped by task (Page, View, Share, Protect, Tools) into a Sheet with section tabs; most-used 5 on top.
- Paste: one small chip after pasting ("Pasted as text ▾") instead of a question before; the chooser only when the choice really matters.
- Template picker with real previews, "Start new pages as" (Settings S6), and Pick on top / Manage below (owner pattern 10).
- "Linked from" as a tidy collapsible section with a count.

**Customisation ("+", presets, favourites)**

- ★ on any "/" item pins it to a "Favourites" group at the top of the menu.
- "+" at the end of the "/" menu: "Arrange this menu" (hide, reorder, reset) using the shared Arrange sheet.
- Page ⋯ menu: "+" to pin up to 5 actions as a top row.
- Templates: favourites row, "Save this page as a template", own categories.
- Block presets: save a styled callout or table as "My blocks" and insert it from "/".

**Recommended split**

|                   | Beginner                                                                       | Professional (each item has its own unlock switch)                                |
| ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| "/" menu          | about 12 items in 3 groups, search                                             | all items, 6 groups, favourites, Arrange, My blocks                               |
| Block handle menu | Delete, Duplicate, Turn into, Move up / down                                   | + Colour, Copy link to block, Copy as…, Make card, Add to Summary, Comment for AI |
| Context menu      | Cut, Copy, Paste, Delete                                                       | + Paste as…, Copy as…, Turn into, Move to page                                    |
| Page ⋯            | 8 items (Rename, Move, Duplicate, Export, History, Lock, Delete, Reading mode) | all 22, pinned row, Arrange                                                       |
| Paste             | automatic, with the chip                                                       | + "Always ask", Clean up with AI                                                  |
| Templates         | pick from pictures                                                             | + manage, categories, save as template, starter page setting                      |
| Breadcrumb        | notebook › page                                                                | + sibling menu, full path, copy address                                           |
| Page tabs         | Notes, Summary, Review                                                         | + add / reorder / hide tabs (Settings S8)                                         |

**Spec lines**

- When I type "/" on an empty line → the menu opens under the cursor with Favourites and Recent first; typing filters; Enter inserts; Esc closes and leaves the "/".
- When I type "/" inside a table cell → only items that fit in a cell are shown.
- When I tap a block's handle → its menu opens beside it; when I hold it 0.3 s → the block lifts and a drop-line shows where it will land.
- When I right-click, or long-press 0.5 s with a finger, on a block, page row or notebook row → the same Context menu opens at that point.
- When I paste → the content appears at once and a chip offers "Pasted as … ▾" for 8 s; choosing another format re-pastes; Undo removes the paste in one step.
- When I open the page ⋯ → a sheet opens docked right at full height (full screen on phone) with my pinned row on top.
- When I create a new page → the start card offers Blank, my default template and "More templates…"; typing anything dismisses it.
- When I delete a block or page from any menu → a toast with Undo shows for 6 s and does not replace an earlier Undo toast.
- Won't do: comments between people, slash commands that call paid AI without the consent step, new block types.

**New shared parts needed:** Context menu, Command list, Sheet, Dialog set, Toast queue, Banner.

**Owner questions (my pick first)**

1. The "/" menu shows about 30 things today. In Beginner, should it show **a short list of about 12 with "Show all" at the bottom** (my pick), or everything in groups?
2. On the iPad there is no right-click. Should a **long press open the same menu as right-click on a PC** (my pick), or should the menu open only from the ⋯ / handle buttons?
3. After you paste, should the app **paste straight away and show a small "Pasted as… ▾" chip to change it** (my pick), or keep asking first each time?
4. The page ⋯ menu has 22 items. OK to turn it into **the same side sheet as the notebook "More", with tabs and your 5 pinned actions on top** (my pick), or keep it a small menu with "More…" at the end?
5. Should you be able to **star items in the "/" menu and save your own blocks (for example your styled callout)** in Professional (my pick: yes), or keep the menu fixed?
6. New page: show **the start card every time** (today), or **only until you choose "Always start with…"** (my pick)?

### Batch 2: Things inside a page (tables, databases, calendar, mind map, maths, embeds)

**Covers:** O1 to O16, A16, A17.

**What exists today (Appendices B, C, D):** table block with row/column grips and resize; 6 database views (Table, Board, Gallery, List, Calendar with Week/Month/Year, Dashboard); view switcher, Add view, View options, Group by; column header menu, hidden columns, cell editor per column type; Column editor with formula editor and "Columns and functions" helper; Chart dialog and chart renderers; CSV import/export; row pages with a properties panel; Databases hub with starter databases; Calendar screen (Day/Week/Month) with a Day card and swipe; mind map block with its own toolbar and keys (no zoom, collapse or colours today); maths block with a modal editor; embed, file, callout, code (no language picker), toggle blocks.

Gaps in today's app worth fixing in the design (features half there in the code): no row menu, no bulk select, no column reorder (the service has `moveColumn`), no row search, filter "match any" and "is not" have no UI, Board/Gallery/List have almost no filter or sort.

**Planned premium improvements**

- One database header for all 6 views: view tabs, then Filter, Sort, Group, Search, "⋯", all as ▾ cards in the v7 frame.
- Table block and database Table share one look, one Object bar and one cell editor set.
- Column editor as a Sheet with a live preview of the column; formulas with plain-word examples.
- Chart builder with a live chart on top, choices below (pattern 7).
- Row "peek" on the side instead of always leaving the page.
- Calendar screen uses the Home Time range bar; adds Year (it exists only inside databases today).
- Mind map: zoom, fold a branch, branch colours, Context menu on a node, export as picture placed where the toast says.
- Maths: live preview while typing, a symbol strip, recent formulas.

**Customisation**

- "+" on the view tabs (add a view), saved view presets ("My views"), a default view per database.
- "+" on the table header (add a column), drag to reorder columns, column presets (Status, Date, Money).
- Database templates gallery (starter databases become previews; save your own).
- Favourite databases on the hub; pin a database to Home (ties in with the Home dashboard).
- Mind map style presets; chart style presets.

**Recommended split**

|                 | Beginner                            | Professional                                                      |
| --------------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Table in a page | add / delete row and column, resize | + header row/column, cell colour, merge, sort, turn into database |
| Database views  | Table, Board, Calendar              | + Gallery, List, Dashboard, saved views                           |
| Filter / sort   | one filter, one sort                | + groups, "match any", multi-sort, search                         |
| Columns         | Text, Number, Date, Choice, Tick    | + all other types, formula, rollup, column editor                 |
| Rows            | open, delete                        | + peek, bulk select, duplicate, row menu                          |
| Charts          | 3 chart types from a preset         | + full chart builder                                              |
| Calendar screen | Month and Day                       | + Week, Year, which databases show                                |
| Mind map        | add, rename, delete, drag           | + colours, fold, layouts, export, from headings                   |
| CSV             | import with defaults                | + column matching, modes, export options                          |

**Spec lines**

- When I tap "+" after the last view tab → a picker shows the view types as tiles; picking one adds the tab and opens it.
- When I tap a column header → a ▾ card opens with Rename, Type, Sort, Filter by this, Hide, Delete; changes show at once.
- When I hold a row or column grip → it lifts and a drop-line shows; ▲▼ buttons do the same without dragging.
- When I select several rows → an Object bar appears with Delete, Duplicate, Set value; Undo restores all in one step.
- When I open a row in Professional → it slides in as a side peek; "Open as page" is one tap away.
- When I change a chart option → the chart above redraws within 150 ms.
- When I tap a mind map node → it is selected with a small Object bar; Tab adds a child, Enter a sibling, Delete or Backspace removes it (and the hint says both).
- When a database is empty → an empty state offers "Add a row" and "Import CSV".
- Won't do: sharing a database with other people, live links to outside spreadsheets, timeline (Gantt) view.

**New shared parts needed:** Wizard steps (CSV), Object bar reuse from v8, Time range bar reuse.

**Owner questions**

1. A simple table in a page and a database are two different things today. Should a table get a **"Turn into database" button so it can grow** (my pick), or stay separate?
2. Opening a row: **slide in from the side so you keep your place** (my pick, Professional), or always open as a full page (today)?
3. Beginner database: show only **Table, Board and Calendar** with the other three under "More views" (my pick), or all six?
4. Calendar: one **Calendar screen that can show any database's dates plus journal days, with Day / Week / Month / Year** (my pick), or keep the separate calendars as they are?
5. Mind map: add **zoom, folding branches and colours** now (my pick), or only restyle what exists?
6. Missing basics in databases (select several rows, reorder columns, search rows): **design them now as part of this batch** (my pick), or only restyle and add later?

### Batch 3: Pictures, PDF tools, audio, paper furniture and Reading mode for notes

**Covers:** P6, P7, P8, P10, P11, P13, P17, P18, P19.

**What exists today (Appendices C, H):** picture placement preview with ✓/✕; "Edit picture" sheet (crop and adjustments); camera tool; PDF Combine files, Scan to PDF with a corner editor and looks, Make searchable (OCR, English only on device), Export with my marks / clean copy; Audio panel with recordings, recording chip, "Audio & video" page tab; zoom pill, pinch zoom, page scroll bar with ▲▼, sheets with "Continues on next page", empty-page hint; Reading mode for notes with reader bar, "Aa" sheet (Text, Layout, Themes with up to 8 saved looks, More: line focus, blanks, dim, keep awake, daily goal, streak), font import, bookmarks, find (This page / This notebook / Everywhere), selection bar (Highlight, Look up, Read from here), read aloud and "Now playing" pill, progress chip and seek bar.

**Planned premium improvements**

- One Tool frame for crop, scan, OCR and combine: big preview, steps on top, ✓/✕ footer.
- Edit picture: crop ratios as tiles, rotate, straighten, a before/after hold.
- Scan: a look per page (today one look for all pages), reorder with drop-line and ▲▼ (today HTML drag only, which fails on touch).
- Audio: speed, rename, export, jump to the note written at that moment.
- Reading mode for notes uses the same reader parts as the approved Reader mock (one reading experience for books and notes): same Aa sheet, same lock, same Listen.
- Zoom pill and scroll bar become quiet, minimise like every bar, and never cover text.

**Customisation**

- Picture style presets (border, shadow, corner) and "Use for new pictures" as a switch.
- Scan presets ("Whiteboard", "Book page", "Receipt").
- Saved reading looks (exists, 8) shown as tiles with "+"; favourite fonts.
- "+" on the Audio panel to add outside audio or video links to the page's Resources.

**Recommended split**

|                 | Beginner                 | Professional                                                          |
| --------------- | ------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Edit picture    | crop, rotate             | + ratios, straighten, adjustments, presets                            |
| Placement       | ✓ / ✕, three sizes       | + wrap, exact size, lock                                              |
| PDF tools       | Scan, Combine            | + OCR, per-page looks, export choices                                 |
| Audio           | record, play, delete     | + speed, rename, export, time-linked notes                            |
| Reading (notes) | text size, theme, Listen | + layout, saved looks, line focus, bookmarks, daily goal, font import |
| Paper furniture | zoom pill                | + page scroll bar, sheet thumbnails                                   |

**Spec lines**

- When I add a picture → a preview shows where it will land with ✓ and ✕; ✓ places it, ✕ removes it, nothing else reacts until I choose.
- When I open Edit picture → the Tool frame opens full screen; ✕ asks "Discard changes?" only if I changed something.
- When I drag a scan corner → a magnifier shows the corner; the page straightens when I let go.
- When I run OCR offline → it works for English; other languages show a clear "needs internet" line before I start.
- When I tap Read from here → Listen starts and the "Now playing" pill joins the bottom pill stack without covering other pills.
- When I pinch the page → zoom follows my fingers and springs back within limits; the zoom pill shows the % for 2 s.
- Won't do: video editing, drawing on pictures outside the ink layer, cloud OCR without the cost consent.

**New shared parts needed:** Tool frame, Status pill stack.

**Owner questions**

1. Reading mode for your own notes and the book Reader are two designs today. Make them **one reading experience with the same controls** (my pick), or keep them different?
2. Scan: allow **a different look for each page** (my pick), or one look for the whole scan (today)?
3. Audio: add **speed, rename and export** now (my pick), or only restyle?
4. Picture tools in Beginner: just **Crop and Rotate** with the rest in Professional (my pick), or show everything?

### Batch 4: Study surfaces (the notebook and page tabs)

**Covers:** S1 to S8, S10, A14, A15, A18, A19.

**What exists today (Appendices B, C, E, I):** card block (Q with nested answer) and blanks (cloze): only these two card types in the editor; Card list block; notebook Cards tab with sort; Study hub with Notes, Summary and Review tabs plus daily highlights; Revise tab with marks, labels and "My summary"; Summary views for page, notebook and app, "Add to Summary"; page Review tab with counts and Practise; Slides tab (read-only viewer made from headings); Memory helpers (Major system, Acronym builder); Prompt library with 23 seeded prompts, editor, run views, a free "paste into any AI chat" path, skills and skill preview; AI page buttons with first-use consent, result preview and card approval; Capture screen with bookmarklet and YouTube; journal via the Today button.

Note: Revise "My summary" and Summary "In your own words" edit the same field in two places (a duplicate to merge).

**Planned premium improvements**

- The six placeholder tabs in the Notebooks mock get real designs, all with the same header (title, count, Filter, Sort, "⋯").
- Card block in the page looks like the Review card editor (the 8 types approved in the Review mock), so a card is the same thing everywhere.
- One Summary design for page, notebook and whole app, with the approved "summary layers" idea parked until Study modes are decided.
- Study hub merged into the Review space home, so there is one place for "what should I study".
- Slides: pick a theme, present full screen, swipe.
- Prompt library as Pick on top / Manage below, matching AI mode v4 workflows so there is one library, not two.

**Customisation**

- "+" on the page tab row to add any tab (Settings S8), tab presets per notebook type ("Lecture", "Book", "Project").
- Favourite prompts; own prompt folders; pin a prompt to the page AI buttons.
- Revise labels as presets (exists as a setting) shown with colours.
- Cards tab saved filters ("Hard ones", "New this week").

**Recommended split**

|                 | Beginner                     | Professional                                       |
| --------------- | ---------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| Cards in a page | Question / answer, blanks    | + the other card types, card list block, bulk edit |
| Cards tab       | list, search, Practise       | + sort, filters, saved filters, pause, Anki        |
| Revise          | marks and Practise           | + labels editor, counts by label                   |
| Summary         | page summary, Add to Summary | + notebook and app summaries, export               |
| Slides          | present                      | + themes, speaker notes                            |
| Memory helpers  | hidden                       | on (unlockable)                                    |
| Prompts         | 6 starter prompts, Run       | + all prompts, editor, skills, free paste-in path  |
| Capture         | paste a link                 | + bookmarklet, YouTube, own-AI notes               |

**Spec lines**

- When I open a notebook's Cards tab → I see its cards with status; tapping one opens the same editor as in Review.
- When I select text and tap "Make card" → a card appears under the text with the selection as the answer, and a toast offers Undo.
- When I tap "Add to Summary" → the text is added to the page Summary and the Summary tab count glows once.
- When I open the page Review tab → I see due, new and learned counts and one "Practise" button.
- When I run a prompt with AI off → I get the free path: copy the prompt, paste the answer back.
- When AI would spend money for the first time → "Before you use AI" appears once, with the cost limit shown.
- Won't do: new study modes (parked in PENDING), collaboration, AI features that skip the consent and limit steps.

**New shared parts needed:** none new (reuses Review editor, Tag picker, Command list, Sheet).

**Owner questions**

1. "All cards" (Study hub) and the Review home do nearly the same job. **Merge them into the Review space** (my pick), or keep both?
2. Revise "My summary" and the Summary tab's "In your own words" are the same text in two places. Keep it **only in the Summary tab** (my pick), or show it in both?
3. The Prompt library and the AI mode "workflows" overlap. Make them **one library, opened from both places** (my pick), or two separate lists?
4. Slides and Memory helpers are rarely used. **Professional only, off until unlocked** (my pick), or visible to everyone?
5. Should the card you make inside a page use **the same editor and the same 8 types as Review** (my pick), or stay the simple Q/A block?

### Batch 5: Safety, account and your data

**Covers:** D1 to D14, D16, S9, A23.

**What exists today (Appendix F):** page History dialog (300 copies, one every 2 minutes; the doc and the empty text wrongly say 30 and 5); Trash list with Restore and Delete forever (hover only, no auto-empty); Export dialog with formats and Print; Save & restore screen (backup file, password, restore, Replace my notes, Notion, NotebookLM, Anki imports); backup reminder bar (snooze 90 days in code); sign-in by Google, email code or password with a guided sheet; passphrase setup, printed recovery code, unlock form; Account and Sync settings, What syncs, per-setting sync badge, sync dot; "Changes on this device and in the cloud" dialog and "Changed on two devices" bars; read-only bar and "You were signed out" screen; Privacy lock with PIN or password; Connect Claude consent page; Layer gallery.

Problems to design away: five different minimum password lengths (4 to 6 digit PIN, 6, 8, 12, 15); Save & restore ignores the read-only gate; left-over texts from before accounts were required.

**Planned premium improvements**

- History as a timeline with a Before / after view, "Restore this version" and "Copy from this version"; named versions.
- Trash as one screen for pages, notebooks, cards and books, with visible buttons, "Deleted 3 days ago" and a clear keep-for rule.
- Share / Export as one Sheet: What (page, notebook, selection) → Format tiles with previews → Options → Export.
- Save & restore as three plain cards: Back up, Restore, Bring in from another app (wizard steps).
- Sign-in as a calm 3-step flow with one password rule shown before typing.
- One sync status place: tap the sync dot → a small card: "All saved", last sync, what is waiting, "Sync now", and problems in plain words.
- Conflicts: one Before / after view with "Keep mine", "Keep theirs", "Keep both"; the other copy always goes to History.
- Offline: a quiet "Offline, saved on this device" state on the sync dot, no pop-ups.

**Customisation**

- Export presets ("PDF for printing", "Markdown for Obsidian") with ★ favourites.
- Backup schedule and place presets; "what to include" switches.
- Auto-empty Trash after 30 / 60 / 90 days / never.
- Named versions as favourites in History.

**Recommended split**

|                | Beginner                              | Professional                                            |
| -------------- | ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| History        | list by day, preview, Restore         | + Before / after, named versions, copy part             |
| Trash          | restore, delete forever               | + filter by type, auto-empty rule, bulk                 |
| Export         | PDF, Word, Print                      | + Markdown, HTML, bundle, options, presets              |
| Save & restore | Back up now, Restore                  | + password, schedule, Replace, imports                  |
| Sync           | sync dot card                         | + What syncs per group, per-setting badge, reset device |
| Conflicts      | "Keep both" by default, with a notice | + choose per change                                     |
| Lock           | PIN on/off, lock a notebook           | + password, lock-after time, per-page lock              |

**Spec lines**

- When I open History → versions are grouped by day, newest first; tapping one previews it without changing my page.
- When I tap Restore → the page becomes that version, the previous state is saved as a new version, and a toast offers Undo.
- When I delete anything → it goes to Trash and a toast offers Undo; in Trash, Restore and Delete forever are always visible (no hover).
- When I tap Delete forever → a danger dialog names the item; nothing else in the app uses a red confirm button.
- When I export → I see a preview of the first page before the file is made.
- When I go offline → the sync dot turns grey with "Offline, saved on this device"; when I come back it syncs without asking.
- When two devices changed the same text → both versions are kept, a banner says so, and a Before / after view lets me choose.
- When I am signed out on a device → notes are readable, editing shows one banner with "Sign in", and Save & restore follows the same rule.
- Won't do: sharing with other people, public links, collaboration, paid storage without asking.

**New shared parts needed:** Before / after view, Wizard steps, Tool frame (imports).

**Owner questions**

1. Trash: empty itself **after 30 days, with a setting to change it** (my pick), or never (today)?
2. When two devices clash: **keep both versions automatically and tell you with a small notice** (my pick), or always stop and ask?
3. History in Beginner: a **simple list by day with Restore** (my pick), or the side-by-side Before / after view for everyone?
4. Export: **one Share / Export sheet for pages, notebooks, books and cards** (my pick), or separate ones per place?
5. Passwords: **one rule everywhere (at least 8 characters; PIN 6 digits)** (my pick), or keep the different rules?
6. The sync dot: tap it to open **one small status card** (my pick), or keep status only inside Settings?

### Batch 6: System layer (toasts, empty and error states, shortcuts, updates, first run, phone)

**Covers:** Y1, Y2, Y4 to Y11, A12, A20, A21, A22, D17, D18, and the phone layout check for batches 1 to 5.

**What exists today (Appendices F, G):** one toast kind with one optional button, timing by length, no queue; confirm, name and choice dialogs; 44 empty states (10 on the shared part) and 15 error states; error screen "This page could not be shown"; 70 keyboard shortcuts with no list; "Features" chip in Search as the nearest thing to a command palette; first-use tips (12 s); update pill, risk bar, "Update required" dialog, storage upgrade screen; alert banners (two of their buttons open a Settings category that does not exist); Welcome wizard (3 steps); "Which device is this?" dialog; Pencil offer card; settings notice bar; Pencil lab.

**Planned premium improvements**

- Toasts: three kinds (done, undo, problem), a stack of up to 3, each Undo stays alive; position above every bar and the keyboard.
- One empty-state family with small drawings and one clear action; one error family in plain words with "Try again" and "Copy details".
- A Shortcuts sheet (press "?" or open from Help) grouped by task, searchable, printable.
- A Command palette (Ctrl+K) built on the Command list: every action, page and setting.
- Updates as one quiet pill in the Status pill stack; the "Update required" screen says why and how long.
- First run joins the approved Settings first-launch flow (one welcome, not two).
- A phone pass: every surface from batches 1 to 5 shown at 390 px with the bottom tab bar.
- Reminders: the web app cannot notify today. Design in-app reminders (Home and a bell with a list) now; real notifications wait for the native apps.

**Customisation**

- Shortcuts: change a key, presets ("Default", "Like Notion", "Like OneNote"), reset.
- Command palette: favourites and recent on top.
- Toasts: position (bottom centre / bottom left), "fewer toasts" switch.
- Quiet corner: "+" to pin the actions you use.

**Recommended split**

|                 | Beginner                            | Professional                          |
| --------------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| Toasts          | default                             | + position, fewer toasts              |
| Shortcuts       | the sheet (read only)               | + change keys, presets                |
| Command palette | off; Search "Features" does the job | on (Ctrl+K), favourites               |
| Errors          | plain message, Try again            | + Copy details, health check          |
| Updates         | automatic, quiet pill               | + check now, update channel           |
| Device mode     | chosen once at first run            | + switch any time, per-device layouts |

**Spec lines**

- When I do two undoable things quickly → two toasts stack; each Undo undoes its own action.
- When a list is empty → I see one line saying what goes here and one button that adds the first item.
- When something fails → I see what happened in plain words, "Try again", and my work is not lost.
- When I press "?" (no text field active) → the Shortcuts sheet opens; typing filters it.
- When I press Ctrl+K in Professional → the Command palette opens; Enter runs the top result.
- When an update is ready → the pill says "Update ready"; it installs on the next quiet moment and never while I am writing.
- When I open any surface on a phone → it is full width, reachable with one thumb, with the tab bar still visible unless it is a Tool frame.
- Won't do: push notifications (need native apps), a feedback form that sends data without consent.

**New shared parts needed:** Toast queue (final), Empty state (final), Status pill stack (final), Command list reuse.

**Owner questions**

1. Do you want a **Command palette (Ctrl+K: type anything to do anything)** in Professional (my pick: yes), or is Search enough?
2. Shortcuts: let Professional users **change keys and pick presets** (my pick), or only show the list?
3. Reminders: design **in-app reminders now** (a bell with a list, and on Home) and real phone notifications later with the native apps (my pick), or wait entirely?
4. Toasts: when several things happen, **stack up to 3** (my pick), or show only the newest?
5. Add a small **"What's new" card after an update** (my pick: yes, one card, dismissible), or stay silent?

---

## 6. How each batch meets rules/quality.md

| Rule                  | How                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 1 One design system   | Section 4 lists the shared parts. Batch 1 defines the new ones on a "Parts" page inside its mock; later batches may only reuse them. A batch that needs a new part adds it to section 4 first.                                                                                                         |
| 2 Spec before code    | Each batch has "When I do X → Y" lines and a "Won't do" list. After owner approval they move, with the owner's comments, into `docs/features/<feature>.md` and become the acceptance tests.                                                                                                            |
| 3 Definition of Done  | Every mock shows Beginner and Professional, light and dark, iPad landscape and portrait, phone and PC. Every Professional row in the split tables gets an entry in Settings › Professional features. A coverage script (as in v6 to v8) probes every appendix feature in the mock, so nothing is lost. |
| 4 Tests stop the line | Each mock ships with a coverage probe and a function test; a missing feature fails the batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| 5 Three gates         | Builder (Sonnet) → QA sub-manager (Opus) against section 5 → Fable looks at the screenshots → owner.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| 6 Safe release        | In the build, each surface is a slice behind its own switch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 7 Learn from bugs     | Section 8 lists the bugs this audit found; each gets a test when fixed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

## 7. Continuity (docs/CONTINUITY.md): how each batch resumes

Same steps for all six batches:

1. **First step for the main manager:** copy this file and the nine raw scans out of the temp folder into the repo (`docs/plans/app-audit-2026-11-12.md`, raw scans in `docs/plans/audit-raw/`), commit and push. Until then this audit lives only in the scratchpad.
2. **One worktree and branch per batch:** `mock/b1-writing`, `mock/b2-objects`, `mock/b3-media-reading`, `mock/b4-study`, `mock/b5-safety`, `mock/b6-system`.
3. **Mock-up sources live in the repo:** `docs/mockups/b<N>-<name>/` (html, css, js, build script, coverage probe, `STATUS.md`). Nothing stays only in the scratchpad.
4. **Small commits:** one commit per surface ID (for example "b1: W3 block handle menu"), pushed after each.
5. **PENDING.md:** one line per batch, updated at each slice start and end: "Batch 2: O5 column editor in progress; done O1 to O4".
6. **STATUS.md in the batch folder:** a tick list of that batch's surface IDs from section 3, the last probe result, and open owner questions.
7. **Resume step (given to every Opus / Sonnet task):** "Read `docs/PENDING.md`, the batch `STATUS.md` and `git log -20` on your branch. Continue from the first unticked surface ID. Do not redo ticked ones. Re-run the coverage probe before you continue."
8. **Usage limit:** the manager schedules a restart at the reset time; the helper restarts with the resume step above.
9. **Approval:** when the owner approves a batch, its artifact link and conditions go into PENDING, the folder is merged to main, and the spec lines move into `docs/features/`.

Batches 1 to 5 do not share files, so two can run side by side once the shared parts from batch 1 are fixed. Batch 6 goes last because it needs the phone pass of the others.

## 8. Bugs and stale items found on the way (for the bug list, not part of the mocks)

- A second toast replaces the first and loses its Undo (`src/App.tsx:889`).
- Trash Restore / Delete forever show on hover only: invisible on touch (Appendix F, section 2).
- Alert-banner buttons "Open backups" and "Manage storage" open Settings category `data`, which does not exist (Appendix G, part F).
- Save & restore ignores the read-only sign-in gate (Appendix F).
- "As text box" paste never wires its Undo (`src/editor/pasteFlow.ts:402`).
- "/" menu opens inside table cells with no guard; code block promises highlighting but has no language picker (Appendix B, part c).
- Mind map: Backspace deletes a node but the hint names only Delete (Appendix C).
- PDF Organise reorders by HTML drag only (no touch fallback); the Files input offers `.heic` but the check rejects it (Appendix C).
- Docs out of date: History is 300 copies every 2 minutes (docs say 30 and 5); backup snooze is 90 days (docs say 24 h); `editor-and-blocks.md` says 14 block types, the schema has 21; `prompt-library.md` says 19 seeds, code has 23; `reading-mode.md` names a file that does not exist; about 30 `layout.assist.*` texts are orphaned.
- Left-overs from before accounts were required: `sync.notAllowed` text, "Use without an account" docs, About text pointing at an Update button that is gone.
- Stale placeholders: Home and Library placeholders in `src/app/spaces/SpacePlaceholder.tsx`; Search "Books" and "Highlights" chips still say "arrive with the Library".
- Five different minimum password lengths (Appendix F).
- Ribbon command "Card stats" opens the notebook Database tab (Appendix E).

# APPENDIX A (01-mock-coverage.md)

## 01 - Mock coverage audit (what each approved mock-up actually shows)

Method: read the mock HTML itself (function bodies, menu builders, dialog/sheet openers, toast strings, DATA tables), not only the inventories. Inventories (`*-inventory.md`) describe the real app; where an inventory item is not drawn in the mock it is NOT listed here. "Verified" = read in the mock source. Things I could not confirm are marked "not verified". Dev chrome (Beginner/Professional demo switch, Coverage badge/list, theme switch, "Reset demo") exists in most mocks and is not a product surface.

Paths: S = `C:\Users\pc\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\4d2a322303203713\e6354cc9-3f84-4a5e-9756-7304491d5da4\scratchpad`.

### 0. Shared shell (applies to every mock; from `shell-spec.md` and the `Shell` code pasted into each mock)

Surfaces every mock gets for free (so they are "shown" but only as shell):

- Rail (Home, Library, Notebooks, Review + Day/Night + gear). Phone <=640 px: bottom tab bar with the 4 spaces + "More". Switching to another space is NOT built: most mocks toast "X is a separate mock-up" / "Not part of this mock-up".
- Gear menu ("Settings and more"): Settings, Save & restore, Trash, Device mode, Help, theme Day/Night/Sepia. In every mock except Notebooks these entries only toast. In Notebooks, Trash opens the real Trash pane (see 1); Settings / Save & restore / Device mode / Help toast "opens here".
- Top bar (sidebar toggle, panel toggle, back/forward, breadcrumbs, "Saved", Search button, up to 3 screen actions).
- Sidebar (264 px) and left panel (264 px: Pages / Outline / Resources) docked, overlay or phone drawer, with scrim.
- "More" sheet (fixed 440 px right-edge sheet / phone full width; picker row on top, section tabs, hint, Cancel + Done; draft model: nothing applies until Done).
- Small dialogs (name / confirm), anchored pop menus, toast (pill, bottom centre, Undo variant), minimise chip + floating bars.
- Edit-tabs dialog (`Shell.tabs`): "Add a tab" menu, "Edit tabs" (rename, reorder, remove), "Create your own tab". Used by Library (3 tab rows), reader panel, Settings page-tabs, AI mode tabs. Not used by Notebooks/Search/Review/Home/v8.
- Shared AI bar (`aiPage`: Copy prompt, Paste the answer, Suggest tags, Summarise, Make cards, Send to Claude) appears in Annotations, Home v2, Library (via `Shell.ai`), v8. Its answers are demo ("Answer received (demo)", "Sent to Claude through the connector (demo)").
- Pro-feature gating helper (`PRO.on(id)`, `data-pro`): each Pro feature has its own switch (169 features in `pro-features.md`); only the Settings mock exposes the switch UI.

# APPENDIX B (02-editor.md)

## 02 - Page editor writing-surface inventory

Scope: the page editor (BlockNote 0.56 + custom blocks) in `c:\Users\pc\Downloads\learning-notes`. Read-only audit, 12 Nov 2026. All paths are relative to the repo root. `file:line` was checked against the code. Labels are the English strings from `src/i18n/en/*` (resolved by key). Where a label comes from BlockNote itself (not the repo) it is marked **[BN]** and the source is in `node_modules/@blocknote/...`.

Gating summary for the whole scope (checked by grep):

- **No "experience-level" (beginner/advanced) gating exists anywhere in this scope** (grep for experience/beginner/advancedMode/uiLevel in `src/editor`, `src/links`, `PageView`, `PagePath` = no hits).
- **Device mode** (`computer | touch | pencil`, `src/app/deviceMode.ts:8`) changes bar size, hover tips, shortcut hints, auto-hide via presets (`src/app/deviceMode.ts:41-65`). All three presets use bar placement `top`, so the docked bar shows in every mode (`PageEditor.tsx:397`); the BlockNote floating toolbar over selected text shows only when the bar is not docked **or** mode is `computer` (`PageEditor.tsx:935`).
- **Reading mode / read-only / reader** hides the bar and freezes text (`PageEditor.tsx:388-397`).
- **Touch vs mouse**: handles use `pointer:coarse` (`PictureHandles.tsx:504`), calm handles split mouse vs touch (`calmHandles.ts:38-51`), table grips split mouse (hover) vs touch (tap + 4 s) (`tableResize.ts:322-338`), hold menu reads pen/touch/mouse (`holdMenuWatch.ts:144-147`).
- **Phone width**: the `phone:` Tailwind variant is used in `DockedBar.tsx:294` (bar padding) and `LinkPicker.tsx:161,198` (full-width picker, hint hidden). `ChooserBox`/`CleanupDialog` become bottom sheets under `sm` (`PasteChooser.tsx:115`, `CleanupDialog.tsx:110`).

---

### A. Insert / trigger menus

#### "/" slash menu (kind: menu, BlockNote SuggestionMenu with our items)

- Opens from: typing `/` in the page text (`PageEditor.tsx:946-963`, `triggerCharacter="/"`). BlockNote's own slash controller is switched off (`PageEditor.tsx:912`, `slashMenu={false}`). Also opened by Insert tools via `setActiveEditor` actions (`PageEditor.tsx:842-851`). Filtering is BlockNote's `filterSuggestionItems(items, query)` (title + aliases) (`PageEditor.tsx:949-961`).
- Main file: `src/editor/slashMenu.tsx:44` (`buildSlashMenuItems`).
- Features (in menu order; BlockNote defaults first, from `getDefaultSlashMenuItems`, `node_modules/@blocknote/core/src/extensions/SuggestionMenu/getDefaultSlashMenuItems.ts:99-288`, labels **[BN]** `.../i18n/locales/en.ts`):
  - Heading 1 "Top-level heading" (badge Mod-Alt-1) `getDefaultSlashMenuItems.ts:130`
  - Heading 2 "Key section heading" (Mod-Alt-2) `:131`
  - Heading 3 "Subsection and group heading" (Mod-Alt-3) `:132`
  - Quote "Quote or excerpt" `:133` (the Callout is spliced in right after it, `slashMenu.tsx:73-83`)
  - Toggle List "List with hideable sub-items" (Mod-Shift-6) `:134`
  - Numbered List "List with ordered items" (Mod-Shift-7) `:135`
  - Bullet List "List with unordered items" (Mod-Shift-8) `:136`
  - Check List "List with checkboxes" (Mod-Shift-9) `:137`; extra search words todo / to-do / task / checkbox added by us (`slashMenu.tsx:16-18,65-70`)
  - Paragraph "The body of your document" (Mod-Alt-0) `:138`
  - Code Block "Code block with syntax highlighting" (Mod-Alt-c) `:140-151` (subtext promises highlighting; see section G, no language picker exists)
  - Divider "Visually divide blocks" `:153-161`
  - Table "Table with editable cells" (inserts **2 rows x 3 columns**, `:163-185`)
  - Image "Resizable image with caption": BlockNote's version opens its panel; ours is replaced by the app picture chooser `onChoosePicture` (`slashMenu.tsx:61-64`, `PageEditor.tsx:711-725`)
  - (BlockNote's own "File" item is removed, `slashMenu.tsx:59`; Video/Audio items do not appear because those block types are not in the schema)
  - Toggle Heading 1 / 2 / 3 `:267-269`; Heading 4 / 5 / 6 (Mod-Alt-4/5/6) `:270-272` (present only if the default heading spec exposes those levels/toggles, which the repo does not override, `schema.tsx:199-209`)
  - Emoji "Search for and insert an emoji" `:275-283` (opens the ":" grid menu, section A3)
  - Callout "Highlighted note with an emoji" (aliases callout note tip info highlight) `slashMenu.tsx:74-83`, label key `slash.callout.title`
  - Card "Question with a hidden answer, for review" (group "Recall", aliases card flashcard question recall anki, icon 🃏) `cardActions.tsx:102-140`; inside another card's answer asks "New card inside an answer" (Inside this answer / Next line) or follows setting `review.nestedCard` (`PageEditor.tsx:643-660`, `builtinSettings.ts:306-320`)
  - Card list "Live list of the cards on this page" (group Recall) `slashMenu.tsx:85-93`
  - Mind map "A tree of ideas you can rearrange" `slashMenu.tsx:95-106`
  - Formula "Maths with fractions, Greek letters and symbols" (icon ∑; aliases math maths formula equation latex fraction greek) `slashMenu.tsx:107-123`
  - Day record "What was done, carried and left open that day" `slashMenu.tsx:124-135`
  - Video or link "A YouTube video (plays after a tap) or a web link" (aliases youtube video embed link web bookmark) `slashMenu.tsx:136-144`
  - File "Attach a file or PDF to this page" (aliases file pdf attach attachment document upload) `slashMenu.tsx:145-153`
  - Camera "Take a photo and add it as a picture" `slashMenu.tsx:154-163` (only if `onTakePhoto` given; `PageEditor.tsx:955` always passes it)
  - Database "Show a database in this note" (group "Databases", aliases database board tracker) `slashMenu.tsx:164-171` -> `DatabasePicker`
  - Insert template… "Add a template's blocks here" (group "Templates") `slashMenu.tsx:172-179`
  - Save page as template "Reuse this page's layout for new pages" `slashMenu.tsx:180-189` (left out when `onSaveAsTemplate` undefined = template pages, `PageEditor.tsx:773-782`, `PageView.tsx:438`)
  - New sub-page "Create a page inside this one and link to it" (group "Pages") `slashMenu.tsx:190-197`, runs `PageEditor.tsx:578-589` (creates page, inserts a `pageLink` block, flushes, opens it)
  - Link to page "Link to a page, heading or card (or type [[)" (aliases link page heading [[) `slashMenu.tsx:198-205` -> link picker
- Gating: Camera, Save template, Table etc. as above; "/" is blocked in code blocks by BlockNote; **our controller omits BlockNote's `shouldOpen` guard** that stops "/" inside table cells (default controller: `BlockNoteDefaultUI.tsx:132-135`), so "/" may open in table cells here (verify in browser).
- Empty/error: BlockNote's "No items found" **[BN]** (`en.ts` suggestion_menu). Failures of new sub-page, template, database go through `runTask` (`PageEditor.tsx:579,618,734`), camera denied toast `camera.refused` "The camera is off for this site. On iPad: Settings › Safari › Camera, then choose Ask or Allow." (`PageEditor.tsx:666-674`).
- Keyboard/touch: arrow keys/Enter/Esc are BlockNote's; badges show Mod shortcuts.

#### "#" tag menu (kind: menu, suggestion menu)

- Opens from: typing `#` at line start or after whitespace, never in a code block (`tagActions.ts:40-48`; controller `PageEditor.tsx:964-976`, `TAG_TRIGGER` `tagActions.ts:31`).
- Main file: `src/editor/tagActions.ts:95` (`tagMenuItems`).
- Features: up to 8 matching tags shown as `#path` (group "Tags") (`tagActions.ts:34,116-122`); "Create #{name}" row when no tag has that name (`:123-135`); picking inserts a tag chip plus a space (`:51-53`); typed text wins over a stale list (`:59-60,110-114`); space in the query closes the menu (`:102-105`); "C#" never opens it, "#1" opens it but offers no Create (`:19-23`, `allowNumbers:false`).
- Gating: tags held in memory via live query (`PageEditor.tsx:575`).
- Empty/error: nothing typed -> list; unresolvable text is put back as plain `#text` (`:84-85`).

#### ":" emoji menu (kind: menu, BlockNote default, not customised)

- Opens from: typing `:` + 2 chars (`BlockNoteDefaultUI.tsx:138-145`), or the slash "Emoji" item. Not disabled in `PageEditor.tsx` (only slash/side/formatting/file defaults are turned off, `:912-915`). Labels **[BN]**.

#### Link picker "[[" (kind: popover/dialog)

- Opens from: typing `[[` (`linkActions.ts:13,23-46`, event `BRACKET_TYPED`, handled `PageEditor.tsx:599-608`), the slash item "Link to page", the Insert/"linkPage" tool (`PageEditor.tsx:847`).
- Main file: `src/links/LinkPicker.tsx:25`.
- Features: search box "Find a page" (`:172`); recent pages (up to 8) when empty, labelled "Recent" (`:53,197`); result rows "title · notebook" (`:263-268`); `›` button "Link to a heading or card on “{page}”" (`:270-280`) or `→` key opens the heading step (`:130-132`); heading step has "‹ Pages" back (`:184-190`), "Whole page" first row (`:213-224`), then headings (`#`) and cards (`◫`) (`:225-242`); hint "→ for a heading" hidden on phones (`:198-201`); picking inserts a noteLink and replaces the typed `[[` only if still there (`linkActions.ts:101-115`, `PageEditor.tsx:609-614`).
- Gating: `phone:` full width (`:161`); position under caret, width 320 (`:16,140-146`).
- Empty/error: "Type to find a page" (`:248`), `No page called “{query}”` (`:247`); close on outside pointerdown (`:68-74`).
- Keyboard: ↑ ↓ Enter, Esc, → (open headings, only at end of text), ← (back, only when text empty) (`:116-137`); IME composition respected (`:124`).

### B. Block-level menus

#### Block side menu: "+" and ⠿ handle (kind: gesture + menu, BlockNote SideMenu)

- Opens from: hovering a block (mouse) or tapping near a block (touch); calm-handles fade them (`calmHandles.ts`). Controller `PageEditor.tsx:941-945` with `dragHandleMenu={AppDragHandleMenu}`. BlockNote labels **[BN]**: "Add block" (add_block_label), "Open block menu" (drag_handle_label). Drag-and-drop reorder is BlockNote's.
- Main file: `src/editor/CardMenu.tsx:189` (`AppDragHandleMenu`).
- Features (menu order `CardMenu.tsx:192-201`):
  - Edit picture (only on an `image` block) `CardMenu.tsx:153-168,193` -> crop/turn/cut-out editor (`openPictureEditor`)
  - Draw on picture (arrow) (same condition) `:169-175,183-186` (sets pen `drawAs:'arrow'` and selects Pen tool)
  - Make card (only for paragraph, heading, bullet, numbered, check, quote, callout) `:30-46`, `cardActions.tsx:17-35`
  - Add to Summary (text blocks only; hidden on templates because `reviewCards` unset) `summaryActions.tsx:41-59`
  - Duplicate (block + everything nested; new ids, so a copied Card gets a fresh schedule) `CardMenu.tsx:49-65`, `blockActions.ts:22-28`
  - Copy (all formats) `CardMenu.tsx:86-91` -> `copyAs(...,'all')`
  - Copy as… submenu: Markdown / Rich text / Plain text / HTML (`:78-83,92-123`)
  - Mark line: {label} x4 (Key point, Keyword, Must know, Formula; only blocks with inline content) `:129-150`, labels `reviseMarks.ts:39-42`, `recall.ts:226-229`
  - Delete **[BN]** `dict.drag_handle.delete_menuitem` `:199`
  - Colors **[BN]** (BlockNote's block colour submenu) `:200`
  - **Not included** (BlockNote defaults dropped): "Header row" and "Header column" table items (`DragHandleMenu.tsx` default has them; ours omits them, and they would be hidden anyway because `tables.headers` is false).
- Gating: `reviewCards` only on non-template pages (`PageView.tsx:437`); mark/card items type-based.
- Empty/error: `copyAs` toasts "Copied" / "Your browser did not allow copying. Select the text and press Ctrl+C." (`clipboard.ts:331`); summary result toast: "Added to the Summary" / "This line is already in the Summary" / "This Summary has 500 lines. Remove some first." / "There is no text on this line to add" (`addToSummary.ts:42-45`).
- Keyboard/Pencil: handle hidden on touch 3 s after last tap (`calmHandles.ts:12,48-50`).

#### Right-click / long-press / press-and-hold menu (kind: menu)

- Opens from: **no custom right-click menu exists in the page editor** (grep of `contextmenu` shows only `holdMenuWatch.ts:204` here, which only _suppresses_ the system menu). The only custom pointer menu is press-and-hold: under the **Text tool**, press still ~1.5 s (setting `advanced.holdMenu`, choices 0.6-3 s, `builtinSettings.ts:533-552`) with finger, Pencil or mouse button (`holdMenuWatch.ts:129-173`).
- Main file: `src/editor/HoldMenu.tsx:13` (menu), watcher `src/editor/holdMenuWatch.ts:99`; wiring `PageEditor.tsx:812,923-932`.
- Features (`HoldMenu.tsx:41-110`):
  - Paste (📋) `:43-57`: places caret at the press point if no selection, then pastes plain text from clipboard (`pastePlainFromClipboard`)
  - Select (▭) `:58-68`: selects the word at the press (`rangeForTaps(...,2)`)
  - Select all (▦) `:69-79`
  - Cut (✂) and Copy (⧉) `:81-90`, only when text is selected, via `document.execCommand`
  - Insert (＋) submenu: Picture (🖼) -> picture chooser `:96`; Line (—) -> divider via `addExtraLine` `:97-105`
- Gating: only `activeTool === 'text'` (`holdMenuWatch.ts:132,158`); ignores presses on `a, button, input, textarea, select, [data-no-hold-menu], [data-ui]` (`:134-141`); Pencil only when within 12 px of the caret/selection and not within 3 s after typing (`:22-29,57-71,144-147`); press must stay within 5 px and pen pressure rise ≤ 0.2 (`:20,174-183`); after selecting, the mini toolbar opens (`HoldMenu.tsx:30-38`, `PageEditor.tsx:927`).
- Visual: a ring fills from 0.4 s (`holdMenuWatch.ts:26,74-97,165-171`).
- Empty/error: clipboard read refused -> toast `paste.blocked` "Your browser did not allow reading the clipboard. Press Ctrl+Shift+V (Cmd+Shift+V on a Mac) instead." (`clipboard.ts:389`).
- Keyboard/Pencil: Esc/outside closes via `MenuPopover` (shared `src/app/ui`); system callout and context menu cancelled for 1.5 s after opening (`:194-199`).

#### Nested-card chooser (kind: dialog)

- Opens from: "/card" typed inside a Card's answer when `review.nestedCard` = ask (`PageEditor.tsx:649-660`).
- Features: title "New card inside an answer"; message "Put the card inside this answer, or on the next line below the card?"; buttons "Inside this answer", "Next line" (primary); remembers via `settingId` (`PageEditor.tsx:650-658`).

### C. Text-selection and formatting bars

#### Floating selection toolbar (kind: bar, BlockNote FormattingToolbar)

- Opens from: selecting text with mouse/keys (BlockNote). Shown when not reading and (bar not docked or mode `computer`) (`PageEditor.tsx:935-939`).
- Main file: `src/editor/SelectionToolbar.tsx:112` (`AppFormattingToolbar`).
- Features (`SelectionToolbar.tsx:114-123`): BlockNote's defaults (`getFormattingToolbarItems`, `FormattingToolbar.tsx:28-55`): Block type select (Paragraph, Headings, Quote, Toggle/Numbered/Bullet/Check list, Code Block as allowed), Merge cells (hidden: `splitCells` off), File caption / Replace / Rename / Delete / Download / Preview (on file-like blocks), Bold (Mod+B) / Italic (Mod+I) / Underline (Mod+U) / Strike (Mod+Shift+S), Align left / center / right, Colors, Nest block (Tab) / Unnest block (Shift+Tab), Create link (Mod+K), comment buttons (inactive: no comments extension); plus ours:
  - Edit picture (single selected image) `:70-88`
  - Four mark buttons: Key point / Keyword / Must know / Formula, toggling; pressed state shown `:91-110`
  - Blank (tooltip "Make blank (hide these words in review)" / "Remove blank") `:30-50`
  - Card (tooltip "Make card") only on convertible blocks `:52-67`
  - Summary (tooltip "Add to Summary") only when `reviewCards` set `summaryActions.tsx:18-38`
- Gating: see line 935; template pages lack Summary.
- Empty/error: none.
- Keyboard: BlockNote shortcut set (shown as hints in the tray, `barActions.tsx:52-53`).

#### Link toolbar (kind: popover, BlockNote default - not customised)

- Opens from: clicking/hovering a hyperlink in text (`BlockNoteDefaultUI.tsx:126-130`; not disabled in `PageEditor.tsx`). Labels **[BN]**: "Edit link", "Open in new tab", "Remove link", fields "Edit title" / "Edit URL".

#### Docked main bar (kind: bar shell + trays), `DockedBar.tsx`

- Opens from: always present when `bar.placement !== 'floating'`, not read-only and not reading (`PageEditor.tsx:397,933`); mounted into the app's top/bottom/free slots through portals (`DockedBar.tsx:476-495`).
- Main file: `src/editor/DockedBar.tsx:95` (`DockedBar`), `:119` (`Bar`).
- Features:
  - Short (main) bar row from `MainBarRow` (undo/redo, tools, ⋯ menu), `shortPill` `:283-308`; its tools/pill contents live in `src/app/tools/main/*` (outside this audit); a `ContextBar` strip under it (`:306`).
  - Ribbon ("All tools") pill `:261-280`, `aria-label` "Ribbon" when both bars show, else "Formatting".
  - ⋯ **Toolbar menu** tray (`panel==='menu'`, label "Toolbar menu") `:369-459`, rows: **Edit toolbar…** (accent) `:376`; **All tools** (only if ribbon not open) `:379-383`; **Search all features** (opens control panel) `:384-392`; **Done** (clear selection + close) `:393-395`; **Bar settings…** (opens Edit-bar panel) `:396-398`; **Copy as plain text** `:400-408`; **Copy as Markdown** `:409-417`; **Paste as plain text** (hint ⌘⇧V / Ctrl+Shift+V) `:418-427`; **Copy page with pictures** `:428-436`; **Reset bar position** (only if a bar is `free`) `:437-447`; then every bar action row (section C.2 list) `:449-457`.
  - **More** tray (`panel==='more'`, opened by tool registry entries `toolRegistry.ts:259,403`) `:319-359`: BlockNote `BlockTypeSelect` `:326`; every bar action row `:328-336`; BlockNote file buttons Edit caption / Replace / Rename / Download / Toggle preview / Delete `:341-346` (visible only on a picture/file selection); "Edit toolbar…" `:349-357`.
  - **Colour** tray -> `ColorPanel` (C.4) `:360-368`.
  - **Edit toolbar sheet** (lazy dialog `EditToolbarSheet` in `src/app/tools/main`) `:460-464`.
  - **Edit bar panel** tray -> `EditBarPanel` (C.3) `:465-469`.
  - Pop-ups outside the bar's own scope: `MiniToolbar`, `MicroBar` (selection mini bars), iPad `KeyboardBar` when keyboard inset > 150 px, `MiniKeys` `:496-505`; options pill `OptionsPill` `:507-511`; `HoverName` `:506`.
- Gating: auto-hide ("Hide while not selecting or typing") hides docked bars unless editor focused / focus in bar / selecting / tray open (`:194-197,484`); never for the free bar; shortcut hints when setting on **or a hardware keyboard seen** (`deviceModeSettings.ts:133`); `phone:` bar padding (`:294`); Tray min-height >= 44 px (`:324,535,557`).
- Empty/error: tap on blank page space clears selection and keyboard (`:214-230`); a `ToolsBoundary` guards selection bar and options pill from crashes (`:496,508`).
- Keyboard/Pencil: ← → between bar buttons (`:237-247`, `data-bar-btn`), ↑ ↓ and wrap inside the Toolbar menu tray (`:632-643`), Esc closes tray and returns focus to the ⋯ button (`:205-209`), **Ctrl/Cmd+F1** toggles "Tools stay open" / "Tools close after use" with toasts `ribbon.stayOn` / `ribbon.stayOff` (`:162-171`); `onMouseDown preventDefault` keeps selection and iPad keyboard (`:85,351,...`).

##### C.2 Bar actions list (`barActions.tsx`, also in More/Menu trays and the Edit toolbar sheet)

- Bold / Italic / Underline / Strike / Code (faces B I U S `</>`; names + hints from BlockNote dictionary) `barActions.tsx:78-84,121-133`
- Four mark labels (Key point, Keyword, Must know, Formula), id `mark:<label>` `:134-146`
- Blank (Make blank / Remove blank) `:147-157`
- Make card (disabled unless convertible) `:158-164`
- ↵ New line (adds empty paragraph after caret; extra variants via long-press: heading level 2, checklist, divider, `lineActions.ts:73-105`) `:165-170`
- Align left / center / right ("Align text left/center/right") `:86-90,171-182`
- Colors (opens colour tray) `:183-188`
- Nest block (Tab) / Unnest block (Shift+Tab) (disabled when impossible) `:189-210`
- Create link (BlockNote `CreateLinkButton`, Mod+K) `:211`, rendered `DockedBar.tsx:573-583`
- Order = `allActionIds()` `:93-106`

##### C.3 Edit bar panel / tray (`EditBarPanel.tsx`)

- Opens from: Toolbar menu -> "Bar settings…" (`DockedBar.tsx:396`) or settings tool (`toolbarSettings.ts:220`).
- Features: title "Edit bar" + hint "Choose the tools on the main bar, the options of each tool and their order. Saved on this device." (`:191-193`); "Main bar" reorder list: drag handle ⠿, tick, ↑ ↓, at least 1 stays (`:55-158,197-204`); "Options of" select + "Arrange these options…" (opens `EditPillSheet`) + per-tool option list (`:205-238`); "Pinned tools" list with untick/↑ ↓ (`:239-287`); "Ribbon tabs to show" checkboxes (last visible tab cannot be hidden) (`:289-316`); "Size" (compact/normal/large) (`:318-333`); "Position" (top/bottom/free; "floating" appears only if currently selected) (`:334-351`); "Hide while not selecting or typing" (`:352-360`); Reset (back to mode preset, clears pill place) and Done (`:361-380`).
- Gating: saved per device; Reset follows current device mode (`resetBarSettings(mode)` `:367`).

##### C.4 Colour picker / panel (`ColorPicker.tsx`, `colorData.ts`)

- Opens from: Colors row/tray in the docked bar (`DockedBar.tsx:360-368`, `MoreRow` `:558-572`). The floating toolbar uses BlockNote's own colour button instead.

##### C.5 BarStack (free-floating bar shell, `BarStack.tsx`)

- Features: top/bottom/free placement, shared height CSS vars `--dock-top/--dock-bottom` (`:84-99`); free stack clamped inside visible screen incl. keyboard and safe areas, repositioned on resize/scroll/orientation (`:101-143`); drag handle ⠿ (aria "Move the bar (drag, or use the arrow keys)") (`:270-290`); arrow keys move 16 px, Shift 64 px (`:198-211`); double-click handle resets position (`:284`); tip "This bar now floats. Drag ⠿ to move it." closes on tap, Esc or 12 s (`:213-229,296-312`).

### D. Pictures

#### Picture handles, action bar, size presets (kind: popover bar + gesture)

- Opens from: tap/click a picture (`PictureHandles.tsx:82-104`), or auto after paste/place (`takePictureSelect`, `:107-124`). Mounted only when editable and not read-only (`PageEditor.tsx:989-991`).
- Main file: `src/editor/PictureHandles.tsx:54`.
- Features:
  - Four corner resize handles (NW/NE/SW/SE) with labels "Resize picture, top left/right…, bottom left/right corner"; 24 px, 44 px on coarse pointers (`:41-46,491-509`); live width drag with rAF, capped to one sheet's writable height, dashed ghost "This picture would move to the next sheet" (`:274-328`, `pic.s.hint`); width saved in `previewWidth`, one Undo (`:326`); lost-pointer/background cancels (`:176-200`).
  - Action bar (toolbar "Picture actions") with 9 buttons: Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Delete, Smaller, Bigger, Resize (toggle handles), Border (cycles none -> thin -> thick -> none), Deselect (`:426-436,449-476`, border logic `:419-425`). Copy/Cut write the image to the clipboard; Cut never removes if copy failed (`:370-393`).
  - Size presets glide 180 ms (instant under reduced motion): smaller/bigger/fit width/original/fit to page (`:331-368`).
  - Repairs saved widths that are 0/negative/NaN on load (`:126-138`).
  - Registers the "Picture" context strip with the bar: Smaller, Bigger, Fit width, Fit to page, Original size, Resize; Crop and rotate, Replace; Align left/centre/right; Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Delete, Deselect (`PictureHandles.tsx:141-158`, strip `src/app/tools/context/stripItems.ts:87-110`). Border exists only in the floating action bar; align/fit/natural/crop/replace only in the strip.
- Gating: handles shown only for editable pages; `pointer:coarse` larger targets.
- Empty/error: `sizeHint()` message at max size (`pictureMax.ts`); replace/crop go through `openPictureChooser`/`openPictureEditor`.
- Pencil/touch: `touch-none` handles, pointer capture.

#### Picture add panel / chooser / placement (adjacent, outside scope bodies)

- `FilePanelController filePanel={PicturePanel}` (`PageEditor.tsx:940`): for image blocks BlockNote's upload/link panel is replaced by the chooser "Add a picture" with Photos / Files / Camera / Paste rows (`src/images/PicturePanel.tsx:15-47`; keys `pic.add.*`). Other media would keep BlockNote's panel. "Place the picture" overlay (`PicturePlacement`, `PageEditor.tsx:984-988`) with Place picture / Cancel picture; "Place or cancel the picture first". `PictureEditorHost` (crop sheet) `PageEditor.tsx:978`.
- Camera path: `takePhoto` -> file input `capture=environment` -> placement (`PageEditor.tsx:666-702`).

### E. Tables

#### Table block inside a page (kind: inline block + handles + grips)

- Opens from: slash "Table" (2x3), Insert tool (3x3, `src/app/tools/editorCommands.ts:94-103`), paste As table / tab rows / markdown table (`pasteFlow.ts:321-329,435-440`).
- Main files: block `schema.tsx:217-243` (adds `rowHeights` prop); grips `src/editor/tableResize.ts:83`; handles `calmHandles.ts`; BlockNote `TableHandlesController` (default UI, `BlockNoteDefaultUI.tsx:153-155`).
- Features:
  - BlockNote row/column handle menu (⋮⋮ grips) - with the repo's settings (no `tables` option passed to `useCreateBlockNote`, `PageEditor.tsx:339-378`; defaults `BlockNoteEditor.ts:471-474` = `splitCells:false, cellBackgroundColor:false, cellTextColor:false, headers:false`) the menu contains only: **Delete row/column**, **Add row above / below**, **Add column left / right** (`TableHandleMenu.tsx:23-38`, `DeleteButton.tsx`, `AddButton.tsx`). Header row/column toggles, Background colour, Split cell, Merge cells are all **hidden** (checks in `TableHeaderButton.tsx:45,100`, `ColorPicker.tsx:100-103`, `SplitButton.tsx:39`, `TableCellMergeButton.tsx:32`).
  - "+" edge extend buttons (BlockNote `ExtendButton`) add a row/column at the table edge; show on hover only (`calmHandles.ts:9`).
  - Row-height grip pills on every row border (mouse: only the border near the pointer; touch: all, hide after 4 s) drag = row height 24-800 px, saved in `rowHeights` (`tableResize.ts:10-14,157-188,297-317`, `schema.tsx:223-241`).
  - Column-width grip pills on column borders for touch/Pencil, drag = width 60-600 px, writes `colwidth` to every cell in the column in one transaction (`:191-222,243-293`); BlockNote's own `column-resize-handle` still works for mouse.
  - Double-click a column handle (mouse) or double-tap a column grip (350 ms) = fit column to content (`:223-242,250-256,346-359`).
  - Ctrl/Cmd+A steps: cell text -> whole table (cell selection) -> all blocks -> page (`selectAllSteps.ts:15-79`).
  - Context strip for a table lists only "Copy as Markdown" + Duplicate + Delete (`stripItems.ts:123-125`).
- Gating: touch grips armed by `pointerdown` that is not a mouse (`tableResize.ts:330-338`), max 200 styled rows (`:17,29`).
- Empty/error: none; `/` menu behaviour inside cells see section A1.
- Keyboard: Tab/Shift+Tab move between cells (BlockNote/ProseMirror tables); Ctrl+A as above.

Block menu Colors **[BN]** applies text colour.

Paste trees become code blocks (`pasteFlow.ts:331-344`). `[[`/`#` triggers disabled inside code (`linkActions.ts:83`, `tagActions.ts:43`).

Insert via slash "Database" -> `DatabasePicker` (`PageEditor.tsx:728-771`): title "Add a database", "Search your databases…", "Your databases", "Not in a notebook", "Create new" (starter databases), empty "You have no databases yet." / `No database matches “{query}”.` (`src/databases/DatabasePicker.tsx`); after insert a new empty paragraph gets the caret so no text toolbar opens over it (`PageEditor.tsx:764-769`).

Drag a branch onto another to move it. (`:30`). Slash inserts it for today (`slashMenu.tsx:130-135`). No edit controls.

(`:515-543`); **Start at** time field (accepts 12:34, 1m30s…), size Small/Medium/Full radio group (`VideoOptions` `:44-103`); while playing: **Insert timestamp** (adds a "▶ 12:34" link line, or asks "Time in the video" with Insert if the player has not reported a time; bad input "Type a time like 12:34 or 1:02:03.") `:418-449,549-579`, **Paste screenshot** (picture captioned with the time; toasts "There is no picture on the clipboard." / "The clipboard could not be read. ### J. Paste, copy and clean-up

#### Paste chooser (kind: sheet/dialog) and "Pasted as" chip (kind: chip)

- Opens from: a rich paste when setting `general.pasteMode` is "ask" (default; others keep/plain, `builtinSettings.ts:739-757`) and there is more than one choice, or when Clean-up is offered (`pasteFlow.ts:597-652`). Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+V bypasses it (`pasteFlow.ts:601-605,670`).
- Main files: `src/editor/PasteChooser.tsx:31` (UI), `src/editor/pasteFlow.ts:117` (planner).
- Features:
  - Dialog "Paste as" listbox; first row is default (badge "default"), each row shows a one-line preview (`PasteChooser.tsx:128-157`, `pasteFlow.ts:252-287`: "Table, {rows} rows, {cols} columns", "Database, {rows} rows (first column = title)", "{n} headings", "{n} tables", "{n} list items", "Folder tree kept with its spacing", "A new box at the paste spot", "Your AI rebuilds the structure (shows the cost first)").
  - Choices (`pasteFlow.ts:195-217`): **Keep original format** (always; for an address: **Embed (video)** or **Link card**), **Link** (address only), **Plain text** (rich pastes; for an address **Plain URL**), **As table** (when rows exist from tabs or Markdown pipes), **Clean up with AI** (AI on + key + 40+ chars rich / 200+ plain, max 24,000), **As text box** (only on paper pages for rich non-address, non-database) `pasteFlow.ts:213-215`.
  - Checkbox "Always use this, do not ask again" saves keep/plain as `pasteMode` (only for those two) (`PasteChooser.tsx:159-167`, `pasteFlow.ts:628-633`). Footer "Enter = default, Esc = cancel the paste".
  - After a paste a chip "Pasted as" lists the choices (minus cleanup/box) to swap in one Undo (`PasteChooser.tsx:65-89`, `pasteFlow.ts:541-568`); fades after 8 s (`:49-53`).
  - Planner kinds: html, markdown, tree, table, database (Notion tables), url, text (`pasteFlow.ts:43-44`); sources detected: notion, gdocs, word, apple, chat, web (`pasteSources.ts:10-17`); sanitiser allow-list `clipboard.ts:70-93,146-186`; web pictures copied locally first (`pasteFlow.ts:530-538`).
- Gating: `box` choice only when `boxHost(editor)` exists (paper page) `pasteFlow.ts:608`; `mode==='plain'` auto-picks plain.
- Empty/error: **As text box**: `applyAsBox` (`pasteFlow.ts:402-414`) discards the Undo function that `pasteIntoNewBox` returns, so the "toast Undo" promised in `docs/features/copy-paste.md` is not wired (see section P).
- Keyboard: ↑ ↓ Enter Esc (`PasteChooser.tsx:99-111`), outside click cancels (`:116-118`).

#### Clean up with AI dialog (kind: dialog)

- Opens from: choosing "Clean up with AI" (`pasteFlow.ts:571-594`, `PasteChooser.tsx:55-61`).
- Main file: `src/editor/CleanupDialog.tsx:41`.
- Features: title "Clean up with AI"; `AiOffCard` when AI off; intro "Your own AI key rebuilds headings, lists and tables from this messy text. Every fact is kept; anything it could not place is listed at the end."; editable "Instructions (you can edit them)" prompt (saved in the library, `loadCleanupPrompt`/`saveCleanupPrompt`) `:157-166`; "Cost before sending" line (`:172-176`); **Cancel** / **Clean up** (shows "Cleaning up…") `:184-202`; one-time privacy step with provider host and "I understand" `:128-153`; preview `<pre>` + "Not placed: check these lines" list (unplaced lines and missing figures `#n`) + **Insert** `:204-236`.
- Gating: blocked with `ai.allowOff` "AI is off for this notebook. Turn it on in Settings › AI." / `ai.notReady` "Turn on AI and add a key in Settings › AI first." (`:78-82`); "This text is too long for one clean-up. Paste less." (`:167-170`); failure "Clean-up failed: {reason}" (`:94-98`).
- Keyboard: Esc cancels unless busy (`:121-123`).

#### Copy formats (kind: gesture + menu rows)

- Ctrl/Cmd+C/X of whole blocks writes four formats: clean Markdown (text/plain), rich HTML, our lossless `blocknote/html`, and a lone picture also as image (`copyFormats.ts:451-482`, installed `PasteChooser.tsx:37-47`).
- Block menu: **Copy** (all) and **Copy as…** Markdown / Rich text / Plain text / HTML (`copyFormats.ts:557-583`, `CardMenu.tsx:78-123`). Docked ⋯ menu: Copy as plain text, Copy as Markdown, Paste as plain text, Copy page with pictures (`DockedBar.tsx:400-436`). Page ⋯ menu "Copy & paste": Copy page with pictures, Copy page as plain text, Copy page as Markdown, Paste as plain text (`PageView.tsx:359-387`). Toasts "Copied", "Copied (text only, your browser blocked pictures)", "Your browser did not allow copying. Select the text and press Ctrl+C." (`clipboard.ts:331,354`).
- Paste as plain text key: Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+V (`clipboard.ts:399-412`, `PageEditor.tsx:442-445`).

A watching window refreshes live (`:225-238`). Per-box locks for extra paper boxes (`:206-210`).

Nothing can be changed until you unlock it." / "Unlocked. You can edit again."
