### M. Gesture layers that shape the writing surface (no visible menu, but behaviour to keep)

#### Pencil / touch selection and safety (kind: gesture)

- Tap select (Pencil, typing mode): double-tap = word, triple = visual line, quadruple = paragraph, then the mini toolbar (`tapSelect.ts:10-32`, constants `:37-43`; callback `PageEditor.tsx:803-810`). Finger keeps native double-tap.
- Hold select: Pencil resting ~0.5 s (setting 0.3-1.5 s, `builtinSettings.ts:511-531`) enters selection mode and extends on drag (`holdSelect.ts:12-20`). Pencil drag select under Lasso (`penSelect.ts:10-27`, `penMode.ts:23-31`). Drag across pictures selects whole blocks (`dragSelect.ts:7-18`).
- Pen guard: refuses deletes/replaces within 500 ms of Pencil input, 600 ms after a tap-select, big (>=20 chars) removals toast `pen.guard.removed` "Text was removed right after a Pencil touch. Undo?" with action Undo (`penGuard.ts:9-42`, `PageEditor.tsx:814-819`); setting "Pencil writes text (Scribble)" `advanced.pencilWrites` (default off) (`builtinSettings.ts:493-505`).
- Keyboard policy: finger tap/keys show keyboard, Pencil tap under Text places caret with inputmode none (⌨ on bar shows it), "Pencil: tap twice to start typing" rule (`keyboardPolicy.ts:6-43`, `penMode.ts:33`).
- Own selection decorations survive focus loss (`ownSelection.ts:16-45`).
- Page mode/policy: ink tools make the page non-editable (`editPolicy.ts`, `PageEditor.tsx:862-871`); gesture edit locks (`editLock.ts`).
- Calm handles: grips/side menu fade in/out with pointer, 3 s on touch (`calmHandles.ts:15-64`).
- Scroll policy: scroll only when the caret would be hidden (`scrollPolicy.ts:3-12`, wired `PageEditor.tsx:355`).
- Write pad (adjacent): `WritePadLazy` shown while its session is on this editor (`PageEditor.tsx:996`); not audited (folder `src/editor/writePad`).
- History: Undo/Redo go to one per-page journal; ProseMirror history depth 300, 800 ms pause (`historyExtension.ts:5-43`); system "historyUndo" from shake/edit menu redirected (`:21-27`); `runHistory` (`historyActions.ts:21-40`).
- Autosave, stash, live apply of remote edits (`autosave.ts`, `pendingStash.ts`, `PageEditor.tsx:483-572`).

| 1   | paragraph        | BlockNote                                       | inline                      | textColor, backgroundColor, textAlignment         | default / Paragraph                   |
| 2   | heading          | BlockNote                                       | inline                      | level (1-6), isToggleable, colours, align         | slash Heading 1-6, toggle headings    |
| 3   | bulletListItem   | BlockNote                                       | inline                      | colours, align                                    | slash                                 |
| 4   | numberedListItem | BlockNote                                       | inline                      | + start                                           | slash                                 |

Inline content: `link` (BlockNote), `noteLink` (pageId, blockId, label) `schema.tsx:169`, `tag` (tagId, label) `schema.tsx:188`. Styles: bold, italic, underline, strike, code, textColor, backgroundColor (BlockNote), plus `blank`, `mark`, `hand` (`schema.tsx:288`).

### (c) Surprising, duplicated or half-built

1. **Table header / colour / split / merge are not reachable.** No `tables` option is passed to `useCreateBlockNote` (`PageEditor.tsx:339-378`), so BlockNote's defaults hide Header row/column, cell background/text colour, Split cell, Merge cells (`BlockNoteEditor.ts:471-474`). `AppDragHandleMenu` also drops the header items. Ribbon's `insertTable` comment says "the first row is the header" (`editorCommands.ts:94`) but nothing marks a header. Slash Table is 2x3, Insert tool is 3x3.
2. **Slash menu opens inside table cells** because our `SuggestionMenuController` has no `shouldOpen` guard (BlockNote's default has it, `BlockNoteDefaultUI.tsx:132-135`). Verify in browser.
3. **Code block has no language selector or highlighting** although the slash subtext says "Code block with syntax highlighting" (`createCodeBlockSpec()` without `supportedLanguages`). Default language mismatch: registry `'plaintext'` vs BlockNote `'text'`.
4. **Callout emoji cannot be changed** (no picker anywhere; prop exists `schema.tsx:36-40`).
5. **File block `caption` prop is never shown or editable** (`FileView.tsx` never reads it); BlockNote's File caption/rename/replace/download/preview buttons in the bar tray (`DockedBar.tsx:341-346`) and floating toolbar may also appear for our custom `file` block since it has a `url` prop (unverified interplay).
6. **"As text box" undo is not wired**: `applyAsBox` ignores the function returned by `pasteIntoNewBox` (`pasteFlow.ts:402-414`, `pasteBox.ts:94-126`), while `docs/features/copy-paste.md` and a comment in `pasteFlow.ts:550` say a toast Undo exists.
7. **Duplicated lists**: the "More" tray (`DockedBar.tsx:319-359`) and the "Toolbar menu" tray (`:369-459`) both list every bar action; copy-as options are split across three places with different sets (block menu: Copy + Markdown/Rich/Plain/HTML; ⋯ bar menu: plain/Markdown/pictures/paste plain; page menu: pictures/plain/Markdown/paste plain). "Rich text" and "HTML" exist only in the block menu.
8. **Picture actions are spread over five places** with different sets: floating action bar (9, includes Border), context strip (adds Fit width/Fit to page/Original/Align/Crop/Replace, lacks Border), block menu (Edit picture, Draw on picture), selection toolbar (Edit picture), BlockNote file buttons (caption/replace/rename/delete/download/preview).
9. **Mixed hard-coded and translated slash groups**: "Link to page" uses the literal group `'Pages'` (`slashMenu.tsx:202`) while New sub-page uses `t('slash.group.pages')` (`:194`); fallback group `'Basic blocks'` is hard-coded in several items (`:78,99,119,127,139,148,158`). BlockNote's own menus are English for Hindi (`languages.ts:27-28`).
10. **`src/editor/editLock.ts` is a different thing** from the user-facing "Lock editing" (`core/editLock`, `app/lock/editLockMenu.ts`): it owns `contenteditable` during pen/lasso gestures. Easy to confuse in a redesign.
11. **`docs/features/editor-and-blocks.md` is stale**: says the registry has "14 types"; the registry and schema both list 21 (`builtinBlockTypes.ts:13-109`). `PageView.tsx:778` comment "Button in the page action bar; soon = shown but disabled" no longer matches `Banner`.
12. **`pageLink` block is only made by "New sub-page"**; there is no slash item for a block-style link to an existing page (the "Link to page" item makes an inline link), and the block hides itself when its page is deleted.
13. **No custom right-click menu** in the page editor (desktop users get the browser's); the only custom pointer menu is the 1.5 s hold menu, text tool only, limited to Paste / Select / Select all / Cut / Copy / Insert (Picture, Line).
14. **Two toolbars at once on computer mode**: all device presets dock the bar at top, and Computer mode also keeps BlockNote's floating selection toolbar (`PageEditor.tsx:935`), so the same formatting actions can show twice.
15. **Mind-map and Write pad are in `src/editor` but outside this inventory's depth**; their menus (branch menu, pad panels, themes, AI canvas) need their own pass, as do the bodies of `MainBarRow`, `MiniToolbar`, `MicroBar`, `OptionsPill`, `ContextBar`, `KeyboardBar`, `EditToolbarSheet`, `HistoryDialog`, `ExportDialog`, `PromptsDialog`, `CardListView`, `DatabaseBlockView`, `PicturePlacement`, `PictureChooser`, `PictureEditor`.
16. **Docs vs code on slash Image**: BlockNote's Image slash item opens its file panel and hides the toolbar; ours replaces it with the app chooser so "no empty block is left behind" (`slashMenu.tsx:30,61-64`); a redesign must keep that behaviour (owner reported an empty block on iPad, `PicturePanel.tsx:11-13`).
17. **Embed `size` radio** (Small / Medium / Full) applies to Vimeo/Loom/YouTube cards but not to link cards (`EmbedView.tsx:232-263` has no size).

# APPENDIX C (03-mindmap-media-pdf.md)

## Audit 03: Mind map, Write pad, Pictures, PDF tools, Audio, Capture, Memory helpers

Read-only inventory of the repo at `c:\Users\pc\Downloads\learning-notes`, read 12 Nov 2026. All paths are relative to the repo root. Line numbers were checked against the code. Labels are the real English strings from `src/i18n/en/*` (Spanish and Hindi translations also exist in `src/i18n/es`, `src/i18n/hi`).

Global finding for the whole scope: **there is no experience-level (Beginner / Professional) gating and no device-mode gating in any of these files.** The "E / A" tags exist only in `docs/plans/reader-inventory-2026-11-11.md`. The only conditions in code are: read-only / edit-lock, pointer type (touch / pen / mouse), `(pointer: coarse)`, PDF-vs-EPUB format, AI settings, browser support (speech, MediaRecorder, Scribble) and the per-notebook "extra page tabs".

Short forms: "i18n key -> label". `Shell` = `src/reader/pdfTools/Shell.tsx`.

## PART 1. MIND MAP

#### Mind map block (editor block with its own toolbar)

- Opens from: Insert > Mind map tool (`src/app/tools/toolRegistry.ts:425-429`, command `src/app/tools/editorCommands.ts:154-158`, label `tool.mindMap` -> "Mind map" `src/i18n/en/toolbar.ts:336`); slash menu "/" entry "Mind map" with aliases mind map, mindmap, map, tree, brainstorm, diagram (`src/editor/slashMenu.tsx:96-106`, subtext "A tree of ideas you can rearrange" `src/i18n/en/editor.ts:32`); also created by AI notes import (`src/capture/importNotes.ts:107-113`) and NotebookLM import.
- Main file: `src/editor/mindMap/MindMapView.tsx:59` (hosted by `src/editor/mindMap/MindMapHost.tsx:17`, block spec `src/editor/mindMap/mindMapBlock.tsx:8-19`; props `data` and `theme` at :11; no inline content).
- Features:
  - "+ Branch" button: adds a child of the selected node and starts typing (`MindMapView.tsx:341-347`, `add()` :320-325).
  - "+ Beside" button: adds a sibling after the selected node and starts typing (:348-354).
  - "Delete branch" button: removes the selected node and its branch; disabled when the centre is selected (:355-366, disabled :358).
  - "Style" select with three themes: "Calm", "Bold", "Paper" (:367-382; names :44-48; `MIND_THEMES` `src/editor/mindMap/model.ts:23`). Look of each theme is in `src/editor/mindMap/palette.ts:10-28` (Calm: accent centre, soft pills; Bold: filled coloured pills, ink centre; Paper: transparent dashed outlines, 2 px border). Six branch colours cycle by top-level branch (`palette.ts:3-10`); line colour `palette.ts:65`.
  - "Save as picture" button: renders the map to a 2x PNG with the page's real colours and adds a picture block (:383-391; `src/editor/mindMap/toPicture.ts:9-69`; long text is cut with an ellipsis :62-65; font 14 px :44). In the editor the picture block is inserted right after the map block (`MindMapHost.tsx:35-43`). Toasts: "The mind map was added below as a picture." / "The picture could not be made." (`MindMapHost.tsx:43,45`).
  - Hint line under the map: "Tap a branch, tap again to type. Tab adds a branch, Enter adds one beside it, Delete removes it. Drag a branch onto another to move it. Alt+arrows reorder." (`MindMapView.tsx:525-529`, key `mm.hint`).
  - Centre node placeholder "Central topic", other nodes "Branch" (:490, :506); empty node aria-label "Empty branch" (:443); edit input aria-label "Branch text", max 200 characters (:488-489).
  - Auto layout: left to right columns, parents centred beside children, node height 36, width 64-280 by text length (`model.ts:303-376`). Curved connector lines 2 px (`MindMapView.tsx:409-430`). Canvas scrolls inside a 70vh box (:395).
  - Limits: 500 nodes, 200 chars per node (`model.ts:29-30`); damaged data is repaired on load (`model.ts:39`, orphans/cycles hang under the root).
  - Selection ring (accent, 2 px) and drop-target ring (3 px) on nodes (:461-466); the dragged node fades to 40% and a floating "ghost" label follows the pointer (:467, :514-522).
  - Tree accessibility: `role="tree"`, `treeitem`, `aria-level`, `aria-selected` (:396-445).
- Gating: toolbar and hint are hidden when `editable` is false (read-only page, edit-lock, reader) (:339, :525); the map is still selectable by tap (:268-275). Toolbar buttons are 44 px high (`min-h-11` :319). No experience level.
- Empty / error / loading: a block with no data shows a fresh centre node and saves nothing until edited (`model.ts:33` newMindMap, doc `docs/features/mind-map.md:25`). Picture failure toast above.
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: see next section.

#### Mind map canvas: keys and pointer gestures

- Opens from: focus inside the mind map block (it is `tabIndex=0`, `role="group"`, `MindMapView.tsx:327-337`); keys are caught natively so the editor underneath never sees them (:98-226).
- Main file: `src/editor/mindMap/MindMapView.tsx:99-316`
- Features:
  - Tab: add a child and type (:183-192; ignored with Shift :184).
  - Enter: add a sibling and type (:193-202).
  - F2: type in the selected node (:203-207).
  - Delete or Backspace: remove the selected branch; on the centre it does nothing (never deletes the block) (:208-220). Note the hint only names Delete.
  - Arrow Down / Up: select next / previous node in tree order (:163-170).
  - Arrow Right: select first child; Arrow Left: select parent (:171-182).
  - Alt+Arrow Up / Down: move the node among its siblings; Alt+Arrow Right: indent (becomes child of previous sibling); Alt+Arrow Left: outdent (:145-161; `model.ts:189-221`).
  - While typing: Enter = save and add sibling, Tab = save and add child, Escape = keep the text and leave (:110-133); Shift+key is ignored (:115); blur saves (:494-500).
  - Keys pressed on the toolbar select or buttons are not handled by the map (:135-138).
  - Mouse: first click selects, second click on the selected node types (:313-315). Drag a node onto another to re-parent it (6 px threshold :289; centre cannot be dragged :288; drop test :248-254, move :297-302, `moveUnder` `model.ts:168`). Drag only re-parents; reordering siblings is by Alt+Up/Down only.
  - Finger: one tap on a node types (:303-304).
  - Apple Pencil: one tap selects (no keyboard pops up), double-tap within 350 ms types (:41-42, :305-312).
  - Palm guard: touches are ignored while the Pencil is down (`ignoreTouch`, `src/app/palmGuard.ts:64`, used :271, :286).
  - Nodes have a hidden 44 px touch-height hit area when node height is below 44 (:472-483); `touchAction: none` while editable (:468); cursor "grab" on non-root nodes (:469).
  - After typing ends, focus returns to the map so keys keep working (:228-236).
- Gating: edit actions need `editable` (:145, :184, :194, :204, :210). Selection and arrow keys work read-only.
- Empty / error / loading: none beyond placeholders.
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: as above.

#### Mind map page tab (extra page tab)

- Opens from: the page's segment bar when the notebook has the "Mind map" tab switched on (`src/notebooks/pageTabs.ts:13-20`; tab label "Mind map" `src/i18n/en/late/sidebar.ts:35`; segment bar and "Tabs" chooser `src/app/summary/PageTabs.tsx:45,83`, `src/pages/PageView.tsx:594-607`; tab content rendered `src/pages/PageView.tsx:633-641`; per-notebook list `Notebook.pageTabs`, device default in Settings `src/settings/PageTabsSection.tsx:31`, pref `pageTabs.default` `pageTabs.ts`). Off by default.
- Main file: `src/pages/tabs/MindMapTab.tsx:22`
- Features:
  - Lists every `mindMap` block of the page, each as the full `MindMapView` (same toolbar, keys, themes) (:90-124). Edits write straight to the block (`db.blocks.update` :98-104).
  - "Save as picture" in the tab adds an image block through `addBlocks` (no reference block, so not "below the map") and toasts "The mind map was added below as a picture." / "The picture could not be made." (:105-120).
  - "Make one from the headings" button (shown only when editable and the page has no map): builds a map from the page's headings, levels 1-3, at most 300 headings, centre = page title (:54-70, :76-85; label key `tabs.mindMap.fromHeadings`; outline builder `src/pages/tabs/mindMapFromHeadings.ts:22-37`). The new block uses theme calm and source "import".
  - Text "This page has no mind map yet." when none (:75).
- Gating: whole tab only exists when the notebook enabled it and the page is a normal page (`withTabs` `src/pages/PageView.tsx:169`); `editable = !editLockBy` (PageView.tsx:637); templates and database pages have no tabs.
- Empty / error / loading: toast "Add headings to the page first, or bring in a mind map from NotebookLM." when no headings (:56, key `tabs.mindMap.noHeadings`); returns null while loading (:52).
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: same as the block. The notes editor is reloaded when leaving the tab if anything changed (:34-44).

## PART 2. WRITE PAD (handwriting input panel)

#### Write pad entry points

- Opens from: Insert > Write pad (tool `writePad`, icon pencil, group media: `src/app/tools/toolRegistry.ts:340-349`, label "Write pad" `src/i18n/en/late/toolbar.ts:112`; shown in the main bar's Insert pill `src/app/tools/main/mainTools.ts:92`, option label `main.opt.insert.writePad` "Write pad"); "Handwriting to text" chooser (`tool.handwriting`; choice "pad" calls `runTool('writePad')` `src/app/tools/HandwritingChooser.tsx:26`, other choices Lasso and Scribble :27-35).
- Main file: `src/editor/writePad/padState.ts:50` (`toggleWritePad`), pad mounted `src/editor/PageEditor.tsx:996` as lazy `WritePadLazy` (`src/editor/writePad/WritePadLazy.tsx`).
- Features: pressing the tool again or the X closes it; `closeWritePad` first makes any live draft final (`padState.ts:64-73`, `WritePad.tsx:395-403`). Opening also triggers the first-use tip `firstUse('writePad')` (`padState.ts:54`).
- Gating: needs an open page editor, otherwise toast key `tool.needsPage` (`toolRegistry.ts:347`). Leaving the page closes the pad (`PageEditor.tsx:439`, `padState.ts:73`). If no cursor yet, the pad writes at the end of the page (`padState.ts:35-48`).
- Empty / error / loading: lazy chunk, nothing shown while loading (`WritePadLazy.tsx`).

#### Write pad panel: top controls

- Opens from: the entry above. A docked portal at the bottom of the window, `z-[60]`, height 160 px to 70% of window, default 340 (`src/editor/writePad/WritePad.tsx:565-571`, `MAX_FRACTION` :69, `padPrefs.ts:31-33`), lifted above the on-screen keyboard (`useKeyboardLift` :201-219).
- Main file: `src/editor/writePad/WritePad.tsx:227`
- Features:
  - Drag grip along the top edge to resize ("Drag to resize the pad"): pointer drag, plus ArrowUp / ArrowDown change height by 24 px when the grip is focused (:572-594, keys :586-589).
  - Title line "Write pad", or, when the Scribble engine is not supported on this device, the hint "On iPad, write here with Apple Pencil (Scribble)" (:597-601; `engine.ts:20-31`).
  - "Read with" segmented switch: "Scribble" / "AI" (:602-610, labels `writePad.engine*`). Switching keeps the text (:451-469).
  - "A−" button (label "Smaller writing") and "A+" button (label "Bigger writing") with the zoom % between; zoom steps 100, 150, 200, 300 % (:611-629; `PAD_ZOOMS` `padPrefs.ts:31`); display only (CSS font size).
  - Close button (X icon, label "Close the Write pad") (:630-637).
- Gating: AI mode needs the user's own AI key (see AI canvas). Scribble works on iPad / Mac with touch (`engine.ts:22-31`); on other devices the box is a normal text box.
- Empty / error / loading: none here.
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: grip is a focusable `role="separator"`; all pad buttons use `onPointerDown preventDefault` so they do not steal focus from the text box (:105, :144, :180). Hold-for-help tips on each control (`Tip` ids `writePad.*`, strings `help.tip.writePad.*`).

#### Write pad action row

- Opens from: second row of the pad (placed high so the iPadOS floating bar cannot hide it) (`WritePad.tsx:640-712`, `data-write-pad-actions`).
- Main file: `src/editor/writePad/WritePad.tsx:640`
- Features:
  - "␣" (label "Space"), "↵" (label "New line"), "⌫" (label "Delete"): edit the text box at the caret without taking focus away, each is its own Undo step (:507-524, buttons :644-664).
  - "Eraser" toggle (AI mode only): the same Eraser as the main bar; first use sets it to Precise + Thin (:493-504, button :665-674).
  - "Clear": removes pad text, writing area and draft, one Undo step; disabled when both are empty (:472-478, :675-682).
  - "Replace": swaps the words selected on the page for the pad text; disabled when pad empty or page read-only (:375-390, :684-691).
  - "Done": inserts and closes the pad (:692-702).
  - "Insert" (accent button): inserts at the page cursor, stays open, empties the box (:703-711, `apply.ts`).
- Gating: `canApply` = text not empty and editor editable (:559). Read-only page: Insert / Replace / Done disabled; a live draft stays as a saved draft (:395-396).
- Empty / error / loading: nothing inserted for empty text (`apply` returns false :376-377).
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: Ctrl/Cmd+Z and Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+Z in the text box walk the pad's own Undo list (:941-947); the main bar Undo / Redo buttons walk one shared pad-and-page list (`padHistory.ts`, merge 600 ms `:36`, limit 200 `:37`, `src/editor/DockedBar.tsx:154`).

#### Write pad preview line

- Opens from: always visible under the action row (`WritePad.tsx:713-744`, `data-write-pad-preview`, `aria-live="polite"`).
- Main file: `src/editor/writePad/WritePad.tsx:713`
- Features: shows "Your words appear here" when empty (:722-725); otherwise the text in the chosen look with line breaks as " ↵ " (:558, :726-743); in AI mode each word piece carries its weight (:726-740); shows the AI error line (role=alert) "The AI could not answer: {reason}" (:718-721).
- Gating / states: as above.

#### Write pad Scribble text box (default engine)

- Opens from: Scribble mode of the pad (`WritePad.tsx:898-966`).
- Main file: `src/editor/writePad/WritePad.tsx:899`
- Features: one big plain `<textarea>` (label "Write pad text", placeholder "Write here"), in the chosen font at 22 px x zoom, line height 1.3 (:930-964). A mirror layer behind it draws a "↵" mark at each line end (:901-929). Auto-capitalise sentences, spellcheck off (:955-956). Tapping it saves the page selection (:949-954). The box is controlled, so the browser undo is replaced by the pad's own (:941-947).
- Gating: iPad Scribble turns Pencil writing into text here; no pointer handlers on the box (or Scribble breaks). On PC it is a normal text box.
- Empty / error / loading: draft is saved per page in `localStorage` key `learning-notes:writePad.draft.<pageId>` and restored on open (`src/editor/writePad/draft.ts`, `WritePad.tsx:330-360`); auto-update waits while a draft exists (`src/main.tsx:173-176`).

#### Write pad AI canvas (second engine)

- Opens from: choosing "AI" in "Read with" (first time shows the explainer below).
- Main file: `src/editor/writePad/AiCanvas.tsx:35`
- Features:
  - Writing canvas (label "Writing area: write here with the pen"): records strokes with pen pressure; mouse / finger count as pressure 0.5; line width 1 + 3p (`AiCanvas.tsx:110-114`, :92, :170-225).
  - "Read" button (shows "Reading…" while busy): sends a picture of the strokes to the user's own AI, adds the returned words to the pad text, weights follow pressure (:151-164, :235-245; `src/ai/padRead.ts`, `src/editor/writePad/pressure.ts`). Disabled with no strokes.
  - "Use a saved prompt (optional)" / "Prompt: {name}" / "Use the normal reading" chooser for the read prompt (`ReadPromptChoice` mounted :232-234, `src/ai/ReadPromptChoice.tsx`).
  - Hint "Write, then tap Read. Pen pressure sets each word's weight." (:227-231), or "This tool does nothing in the pad. Choose the Pen or the Eraser." when the main bar tool is Lasso or another non-writing tool (:228-230, `WritePad.tsx:480-491`).
  - Kept text above the canvas (read-only box, max 6rem) (`WritePad.tsx:829-837`).
  - Eraser chips (when Eraser active): modes "Precise", "Standard", "Whole stroke"; sizes "Thin", "Medium", "Thick" (:838-866; strings `ink.eraser.*`, `ink.thickness.*`). Erase code `padErase.ts`.
  - Opening the AI canvas with Lasso active switches the tool to Pen (:318-320).
- Gating: needs the user's AI key and notebook AI permission (checked inside `padRead`). Pad pointer events never reach the page (:176-186).
- Empty / error / loading: busy label; errors go to the preview line (WritePad.tsx:895).
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: `touch-none` canvas (:224); pointer capture; palm guard is not used here.

#### Write pad "Discard writing?" bar and explainer dialog

- Opens from: switching away from AI mode with unread strokes (`WritePad.tsx:458-463`) and the first Scribble-to-AI switch (:464-467).
- Main file: `src/editor/writePad/WritePad.tsx:746-825`
- Features:
  - Ask bar: "Your writing on the canvas is not read yet. Switching would throw it away." with "Keep writing" and "Discard" (:746-779).
  - Explainer dialog "What happens to your text" with "Switch to AI", "Don’t show again", "Cancel" (:781-825; body key `writePad.explain.body`). "Don’t show again" stores `explainerOff` (`padPrefs.ts`).
- Gating: explainer appears until dismissed forever.

#### Write pad look rows

- Opens from: bottom of the pad (`WritePad.tsx:968-1019`, padding clears the iPadOS dock bar via `bottomGap` :222-225).
- Main file: `src/editor/writePad/WritePad.tsx:969`
- Features:
  - "Colour" swatches (the BlockNote text colours plus a "×" default) (:974-979).
  - "Highlight" swatches (background colours plus "×") (:980-985).
  - "Size": 100%, 125%, 150%, 200% (:986-992; `handStyle.ts:15`).
  - "Font": "Normal", "Caveat", "Kalam (Hindi)" (:993-999; `handStyle.ts:11`).
  - "Weight": "Light", "Normal", "Bold" (:1000-1006; `handStyle.ts:13`). Caveat has no real Light (looks Normal).
  - Checkbox "Show my writing on the page as I write" (live copy, default on): draws the pad text as a dashed draft at the cursor (:1007-1018; ghost `src/editor/writePad/ghost.ts`).
  - Defaults: Caveat, Normal, 125, zoom 1.5, height 340 (`padPrefs.ts:33-39`). All remembered on this device in pref `writePad.prefs` (`padPrefs.ts`).
- Gating: none. Swatch buttons are 36 px (`size-9`), Seg buttons `min-h-9`, below the 44 px used elsewhere.

## PART 3. PICTURES AND IMAGE TOOLS

#### Add a picture sheet ("Add a picture")

- Opens from: Insert > Picture (tool `picture` `src/app/tools/toolRegistry.ts:338`), "/image" slash item (`src/editor/slashMenu.tsx:61`), the empty picture block's Add button (replacement panel `src/images/PicturePanel.tsx:20-47`), the Picture strip's "Replace" (`src/editor/PictureHandles.tsx:408-413`). Wiring `src/editor/PageEditor.tsx:711-724` (`choosePicture`), sheet mounted :981 (lazy).
- Main file: `src/images/PictureChooser.tsx:22`
- Features (each row has a title and a sub-line):
  - "Photos" / "Photo Library, or take a photo": hidden file input `image/*` (:63, :125-148).
  - "Files" / "Choose a picture file": accepts `.png,.jpg,.jpeg,.webp,.gif,.heic,image/*` (:140-144).
  - "Camera" / "Take a new photo": file input with `capture="environment"` (:129).
  - "Paste" / "Use a picture you copied": reads the clipboard; if blocked shows a read-only box with the hint "Press and hold → Paste" and a paste target (:49-53, :108-117, `readClipboardPicture` `src/images/pictureChooserStore.ts:88-103`); a normal paste into the dialog also works (:54-60).
  - "Cancel" button, Escape key, backdrop click (:73, :82-87, :118-124).
  - First button gets focus; focus returns to the opener (:24-30).
- Gating: lazy chunk. A picture chosen here never becomes a block until placed (see next surface).
- Empty / error / loading: `saveImage` refuses other types with "Pictures must be PNG, JPEG, WebP or GIF files." and over 25 MB with "This picture is too big (the limit is about 25 MB)." (`src/images/imageService.ts:27-28`, `src/images/imageRef.ts:20-28`, strings `image.badType`, `image.tooBig`).
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: on iPad the three file inputs are clicked straight from the tap (required by iPadOS).

#### Picture placement preview (check / cross bar)

- Opens from: any file chosen in the chooser, and the Camera tool (`src/images/pictureChooserStore.ts:40-47` `beginPlacement`; `PageEditor.tsx:682`; mount :986).
- Main file: `src/images/PicturePlacement.tsx:47`
- Features:
  - Drag the preview picture to another line; it snaps under the block it is over; where it is dropped sets alignment: left third, centre, right third (:327-341, :375-389).
  - A real gap opens in the text flow at the drop point; mid-line it splits the line like a caret (`src/images/placementGap.ts`, :146-149, place() split :265-289).
  - Four corner handles (44 px) resize the preview, capped to the largest size for one sheet (:342-353, :354-374, :428-448; labels "Resize picture, top left corner" etc.).
  - "Place picture" (check icon, Enter) places it as one Undo step (:455-463, :265-289). "Cancel picture" (X icon, Escape) throws it away (:464-472, :290-293).
  - Auto-scrolls the page near top and bottom edges and stays inside the page area (`src/images/placementDrag.ts`, :166-263).
  - Shows a size hint "Largest size for one page" when capped (:371, `src/images/pictureMax.ts:41-54`).
  - Initial size: picture's own width, at most 60% of the page and the one-sheet cap (:133-143).
- Gating: it is a mode: page not editable, ink and text selection off (`data-placing`), tool change is undone with toast "Place or cancel the picture first" (:59-76). Fills an existing empty block when `blockId` is given (:94).
- Empty / error / loading: the preview is hidden until the image loads (:320-325).
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: capture-phase Enter and Escape (:300-314); pointer events stopped so Lasso / ink do not see the drag (:329); holds pagination while dragging (:338); stays in place across app switch (:166-170).

#### Picture action bar and corner handles (selected picture)

- Opens from: tap or click a picture in the page; a picture just placed or pasted is selected at once (`src/editor/PictureHandles.tsx:82-124`, `src/images/pictureSelect.ts`). Mounted in the editor.
- Main file: `src/editor/PictureHandles.tsx:54`
- Features:
  - Floating toolbar (aria "Picture actions") above the picture, below when no room (:426-476), buttons: "Copy", "Cut" (never cuts if the copy failed :379), "Duplicate", "Delete", "Smaller" (40% less), "Bigger" (1.5x), "Resize" (toggle for the corner handles, pressed state), "Border" (cycles none, thin, thick, none; pressed when set; one Undo each :419-425), "Deselect".
  - Four corner handles: 24 px, 44 px on coarse pointers (:491-509, size :504), labelled "Resize picture, top left corner" etc.; drag away from the centre = bigger; writes the width once per frame and commits on release as one Undo (:244-328; maths `src/editor/pictureResize.ts:47-61`, minimum 48 px :12).
  - Ghost outline "This picture would move to the next sheet" when the size would push it to the next sheet (:477-490).
  - Smaller / Bigger / Fit width / Fit to page / Original size glide for 180 ms, instant with Reduce motion (:331-368).
  - Repairs bad saved widths (0, negative, NaN) on load (:128-138, `pictureResize.ts:91-102`).
- Gating: a drag is cancelled if the app goes to the background or the pointer is lost (:176-200, :503). Handles hidden after "Resize" is toggled off (:491). Selected picture shows a dashed orange outline (docs/features/pictures.md).
- Empty / error / loading: Copy / Cut use `navigator.clipboard.write` (:370-381), failures are silent (no toast).
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: pointer events, so finger, Pencil and mouse all work; handles are `tabIndex=-1` (no keyboard resize).

#### Picture strip (ribbon context tab "Picture")

- Opens from: automatically when a picture is selected (`setContextActive('picture')` `PictureHandles.tsx:141-144`; strip content `src/app/tools/context/stripItems.ts:65-95`, caption field `src/app/tools/context/ContextStrip.tsx:19-41`).
- Main file: `src/app/tools/context/stripItems.ts:66`
- Features (groups in order):
  - Size: "Smaller", "Bigger", "Fit width", "Fit to page", "Original size", "Resize" (handles toggle).
  - "Crop and rotate" (opens the picture editor), "Replace" (opens the chooser for this block).
  - "Align left", "Align centre", "Align right" (pressed state) (applies `textAlignment`, `PictureHandles.tsx:414-417`).
  - "Copy", "Cut", "Duplicate", "Delete", "Deselect".
  - Caption field "Caption and alt text" (max 500 chars, saves on blur or Enter) (`ContextStrip.tsx:19-41`, `PictureHandles.tsx:150-156`).
- Gating: only while a picture is selected (`pictureControl` memStore `src/editor/pictureControl.ts:31`).

#### Picture editor sheet ("Edit picture")

- Opens from: block menu (grip menu) > "Edit picture" (`src/editor/CardMenu.tsx:153-168`), selection toolbar button "Edit picture" when exactly one image is selected (`src/editor/SelectionToolbar.tsx:70-87`), Picture strip "Crop and rotate". Host `src/images/pictureEditor.tsx:17`, store `src/images/pictureEditorStore.ts:34`.
- Main file: `src/images/CropSheet.tsx:69` (native `<dialog>` shown modally :93-95, 92vh x 96vw max 820 x 760 :252)
- Features:
  - "Turn left", "Turn right" (quarter turns, resets the crop to the current shape) (:255-268, `turn` :129-139).
  - "Straighten" slider -45 to +45 degrees, step 0.5 (:269-281).
  - Zoom: "Zoom out" (-), live % readout, "Zoom in" (+) (x or / 1.25), max 400%; Ctrl/Cmd+mouse-wheel zoom (x1.1); two-finger pinch on the stage (:283-310, :318-323, :161-186, `MAX_ZOOM` :67).
  - Crop box: drag to move it; four 44 px corner handles (sliders, labels "Crop corner, top left" etc.); arrow keys move the focused corner 1% (Shift = 5%) (:339-377, :217-229).
  - "Crop shape" chips: "Free", "Original", "1:1", "4:3", "3:4", "16:9" (:53-65, :383-398).
  - "Reset" (disabled when nothing changed and zoom is 100%) (:399-406).
  - Footer: "Restore original" (only when an earlier original exists), "Cancel", "Cut out as new picture", "Save" (:409-438). Save and Cut out are disabled until something changed.
  - Save replaces the picture in the note (one Undo); Cut out inserts the result as a new picture after it (`pictureEditor.tsx:76-87`); Restore puts the original back (:65-69).
- Gating: only pictures kept in this app (an `lnimg:` reference) can be edited: otherwise toast "Only pictures kept in this app can be edited." (`pictureEditor.tsx:33-37`). Palm guard on stage and handles (:169-170, :197).
- Empty / error / loading: toast "This picture could not be edited." on load or save failure (:50-53, :91); success toasts "Picture changed. Undo brings the old one back.", "Cut-out added below the picture.", "Original picture restored." (:69, :80, :86).
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: Escape cancels (native dialog `onCancel` :251); stage `touchAction` is `none` at 100% and pan at higher zoom (:317).

#### Block menu items for pictures

- Opens from: the drag grip menu of an image block (`src/editor/CardMenu.tsx:153-193`).
- Features: "Edit picture" (:165-168) and "Draw on picture (arrow)" (sets the pen to the arrow shape and selects the Pen, `drawOnPicture` :183-186; label `pic.draw`).
- Gating: only for blocks of type image (:158).

#### Camera tool

- Opens from: Insert > Camera (`src/app/tools/toolRegistry.ts:384-388`, label "Camera"), slash item "Camera" with aliases camera, photo, picture, scan (`src/editor/slashMenu.tsx:158`, subtext "Take a photo and add it as a picture" `src/i18n/en/media.ts:45`).
- Main file: `src/editor/PageEditor.tsx:666`
- Features: opens a file input with `capture="environment"` (:670-680); the photo goes through placement preview then `placePicture` (:682-700). If the camera permission is denied, toast "The camera is off for this site. On iPad: Settings › Safari › Camera, then choose Ask or Allow." (:667-673, key `camera.refused`).
- Gating: a computer shows a normal picker.

#### Picture storage and insertion (no UI, behaviour that matters)

- `src/images/placePicture.ts:29-106`: saves via `saveImage` + `shrinkImage` (longest side 1600 px, WebP, GIF kept; `src/images/shrinkImage.ts`), fills an empty line or inserts after / before the chosen block, moves the cursor to the line below, adds a new empty paragraph if last, and selects the new picture so the strip shows (:100). A picture added while a video plays gets the video time as caption (`PageEditor.tsx:703-709`).
- Web pictures: pasted web images are copied into the app once, others show a "Load remote image" card until tapped; SVG is drawn to a bitmap, never kept (`src/images/remoteImages.ts:1-58`, card text :48).

## PART 4. PDF TOOLS (in the book reader)

#### PDF menu button and menu

- Opens from: the reader bar button "PDF" (aria "PDF tools"), visible only for PDFs (`src/reader/BookReader.tsx:1110-1123`, popover `:1422-1427`, items `:789-824`).
- Main file: `src/reader/BookReader.tsx:795`
- Features (menu items, with icon names): "Organise pages…" (grid), "Combine files…" (duplicate), "Scan to PDF…" (camera), "Make searchable (OCR)…" (search), "Export with my marks" (save), "Export clean copy" (copy) (:795-824). Each calls `openPdfTool`, which first stops inking and clears selection (:789-794).
- Gating: `format === 'pdf'` and book data loaded (:1110, :1428). Not shown for EPUB.
- Other PDF reader extras live in the same file but are outside this scope's folders (reader inventory doc).

#### PDF tool frame (Shell)

- Opens from: every PDF sheet below.
- Main file: `src/reader/pdfTools/Shell.tsx:30-66`
- Features: full-screen modal (z-80), title, "Close" X button (44 px), scrolling body, optional footer; Escape closes unless busy (:41-43, :49-58). Shared button classes `btnClass`, `mainBtnClass` (:70+).
- Gating: `busy` disables close and Esc.

#### Organise pages sheet

- Opens from: PDF menu > "Organise pages…" (`src/reader/pdfTools/PdfTools.tsx:136-159`).
- Main file: `src/reader/pdfTools/OrganiseSheet.tsx:85`
- Features:
  - Grid of page thumbnails (132 px, drawn when scrolled into view, :18-77; grid :288-352). Tap a page to pick it (multi-pick, `aria-pressed`) (:310-348). A page shows its new number and "Page {n} (was page {old})" in its label (:316-320).
  - Toolbar (aria "Page tools", sticky) (:248-282): "Turn left" and "Turn right" (picked pages, disabled with none picked) (:253-264); "Move earlier" and "Move later" (picked pages shift by one, :151-168, :265-266); "Delete" (refuses to delete all pages with toast "A PDF needs at least one page." :169-173); "Add blank page" (after the last picked, else at the end :174-177, :268); "Extract…" (makes a new Library PDF from the picked pages; blank pages alone refused "Blank pages alone cannot be extracted." :178-191, :269); "Pick all" / "Pick none" toggle (:270-280); "Undo" (history of the last 30 plans :133-144, :281).
  - Drag a thumbnail onto another to reorder (HTML5 drag and drop, :298-308); there is no pointer-event fallback, the Move earlier / later buttons are the touch route.
  - Footer: "{count} pages · {n} picked", "Cancel", "Save" (disabled until the plan differs from the original; shows "Saving…") (:220-246, dirty :147).
  - Save writes the new file, moves highlights, notes and ink with their pages, closes, and toasts "Saved: {count} pages. Your highlights, notes and drawing moved with their pages." with an "Undo" action (`PdfTools.tsx:63-74, 141-148`; undo `pdfEditService.ts:104`, toast "Put back as it was.").
  - Extract results in a new book: toast '"{title}" was added to your Library.' with "Open" (`PdfTools.tsx:76-84, 149-157`).
- Gating: none beyond PDF format. Blank tiles are dashed white boxes (:340-341). Turn animates with a 200 ms transform (:332-338).
- Empty / error / loading: "Loading…" (:285-286); "This PDF could not be changed. It may be locked with a password or damaged." (:283-284); failures toast "That did not work. Nothing was changed." or the PdfEditError message (:186-199).
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: Escape via Shell; tiles are buttons (Space / Enter toggle).

#### Combine files sheet

- Opens from: PDF menu > "Combine files…" (`PdfTools.tsx:160-175`).
- Main file: `src/reader/pdfTools/CombineSheet.tsx:24`
- Features: ordered list starting with this book, each row shows number, name, "{mb} MB", buttons "↑" (aria "Move {name} up"), "↓" ("Move {name} down"), "✕" ("Remove {name}", disabled for the only item) (:121-169). "Add PDF files…" opens a multi-file picker for PDFs (:170-191). Footer: "Together {mb} MB", "Cancel", "Combine" (disabled with fewer than 2 files) (:94-117). Result is a new Library PDF named "{title} + {count} files" (:73-86; `PdfTools.tsx:165-173`); originals are unchanged.
- Gating: a file that is not a PDF (checked by its first bytes) is skipped with "{name} is not a PDF, so it was left out." (:48-51); over the book size limit: "That is more than the app can hold in one file." (:52-56, `MAX_BOOK_BYTES`).
- Help text key `pdftools.comb.help` (:120).

#### Scan to PDF sheet and corner editor

- Opens from: PDF menu > "Scan to PDF…" (`PdfTools.tsx:176-211`).
- Main file: `src/reader/pdfTools/ScanSheet.tsx:163`
- Features:
  - Help text, then "Take a photo" (single capture input `capture="environment"` :263-275, :337-345) and "Choose pictures…" (multi-pick :276-288, :346-354). Both disabled while reading or busy.
  - Status "Looking for the sheet…" while pictures are read and the sheet corners are auto-detected (:356-360, :186-210; `detectCorners` `src/reader/pdfTools/scanGeometry.ts:138`; fallback inset quad 4% :26). A picture that cannot be read toasts "{name} could not be read as a picture." (:200).
  - Page chips "Page {n}" numbers, tap to switch (:361-385).
  - Corner editor: picture with a blue quad and four 44 px round corner buttons ("Top-left corner", "Top-right corner", "Bottom-right corner", "Bottom-left corner"); drag with pointer or move with arrow keys (step 1% of the longest side) (:69-156, names :101-106, keys :141-151). Caption line "Sheet found. Drag a corner to adjust." or "Drag the four corners to the corners of the sheet." (:393-395).
  - "Find the sheet again" (re-run detection; toast "No clear sheet was found. Drag the corners to the sheet." if none :228-236, :397-399); "Move earlier", "Move later", "Remove this page" (:400-423).
  - "Look of the pages" radio group: "Colour" (as taken), "Grey", "Document" (dark text on white paper), default Document, applies to all pages (:425-447, `enhance` `scanGeometry.ts:326-345`).
  - Footer: checkbox "Make it searchable (on this device)" (default on, runs OCR after) and "Create PDF ({count})" (disabled with no pages) (:298-331). Output pages are straightened JPEG 0.85, longest side up to 2200 (:60-66, `outputSize` `scanGeometry.ts:234`); input pictures shrunk to 2400 px (:17, :31-45).
  - While saving with OCR, status "Reading page {page} of {total} ({percent}%)" is shown in the footer (`PdfTools.tsx:185-193`). If OCR fails the scan is saved without text with the toast "The text could not be read, so the scan was saved without it." (:197-200). New book named "Scan: {title}" opened via the same Library toast (:202-208).
- Gating: busy state blocks close (`Shell busy`).
- Empty / error / loading: as listed.
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: corners use pointer capture, `e.buttons` guard, `touchAction: none` (:92-100, :137).

#### Make searchable (OCR) sheet

- Opens from: PDF menu > "Make searchable (OCR)…" (`PdfTools.tsx:212-227`).
- Main file: `src/reader/pdfTools/OcrSheet.tsx:32`
- Features:
  - Help text; radio "Pages to read": "Only pages with no text ({count} of {total})" (default) or "All {total} pages" (:243-265, `scannedPages` `ocr.ts:37`).
  - Section "On this device (free)": help (English printed text, first page slower), button "Read the pages" (:267-284). If every page already has text: "Every page already has text. Choose "All pages" to read them anyway." (:279-283).
  - Section "With AI (optional)": help text, the AI-off card (`AiOffCard`), then once AI is ready: cost line "About {cost} for {count} pages, sent to {provider} ({host}). It stops at your monthly limit.", button "Read with AI…", which turns into "Send pages and pay about {cost}" + "Cancel" (a two-step confirm) (:286-333; `src/reader/pdfTools/aiOcr.ts:11-40`).
  - While busy: progress bar and "Getting ready…" / "Reading page {page} of {total} ({percent}%)" (:335-360); footer button "Stop and keep what is read" (:220-228).
  - Success: the book is re-saved with an invisible text layer, toast "Done: {count} pages are now searchable." with Undo (:218-225 in `PdfTools.tsx`).
- Gating: AI path obeys AI off, the notebook switch, locked pages and the monthly limit: toasts "Turn on AI in Settings first.", `ai.lockedOff`, `ai.allowOff` (:131-147). Device OCR is English only (`src/reader/pdfTools/ocr.ts:114`); AI prompt transcribes any script (`aiOcr.ts:13`).
- Empty / error / loading: "Stopped. Nothing was changed.", "No readable text was found. Nothing was changed." (:114-123, :187-191); pages already read by AI are kept on stop or error (:192-206). Close is never blocked (Shell `busy={false}` :218) and aborts the work (:214-217).

#### Export with my marks / Export clean copy (no sheet)

- Opens from: PDF menu items of the same names (`PdfTools.tsx:87-130`).
- Main file: `src/reader/pdfTools/PdfTools.tsx:87`
- Features: "Export clean copy" downloads the open file's bytes unchanged as "{title} (clean).pdf" and toasts "Saved a clean copy, without your marks." (:93-97). "Export with my marks" toasts "Making the PDF…", saves ink first, flattens highlights, notes and visible ink layers into the pages (notes list headed "Notes"/"Page") and downloads "{title} (annotated).pdf" (:98-123, `flattenMarks` `pdfEdit.ts:379`).
- Gating/errors: toast of the error message or "That did not work. Nothing was changed." (:125).

#### Page Flip bar (reader extras)

- Opens from: the reader's "Page Flip" toggle button (`src/reader/BookReader.tsx:1045-1051`); in full screen a centre tap shows / hides the bar and Page Flip together (:698-708). Locking the reader hides it (:1136).
- Main file: `src/reader/extras/PageFlip.tsx:11`
- Features: a slider along the bottom ("Position in the book"); PDF shows a 96 px page picture (drawn after the slider rests 120 ms) and "Page {page} of {total}"; EPUB shows "{percent}% of the book" (:66-103, :131-174). Committing: after a 450 ms pause, on pointer up or on key up (:58-64, :95-102). "Back to {where}" returns to where scrubbing began (":p. N" or "N%") (:106-118, reader wiring `BookReader.tsx:1319-1350`). "Close Page Flip" (:119-125).
- Gating: none; slider range is page count for PDFs, 1-1000 for EPUBs (:41-43). Slider is 44 px tall.

#### Time left line and screen-direction lock (reader extras)

- Opens from: automatic. Progress line at the bottom of the reader (`BookReader.tsx:620-665`, shown :1174-1181): "About {chapter} left in chapter · {book} in book" or "About {book} left in book", using the measured reading speed (default 220 words per minute) stored in `localStorage` key `learning-notes:readerWpm` (`src/reader/extras/timeLeft.ts:9-12`, `SpeedTracker` for steady reading only). Format "5 min", "1 h 20 min", "Done".
- Screen direction lock: Settings > Reading > "Lock screen direction" with options "Off", "Upright", "Sideways" (`src/settings/readingSettings.ts:223`, strings `settings.reading.orient.*`, `reading.opt.orient.*`). Applied by `src/reader/extras/orientation.ts:22-42` while a book is open (`BookReader.tsx:379-390`). If refused (iPad always), toast "This screen can't be locked from here. On an iPad, open Control Centre (swipe down from the top right corner) and tap the rotation lock."

## PART 5. AUDIO

#### Audio recording tools (Insert and View entries)

- Opens from: Insert > Audio (record / stop for the open page; `src/app/tools/toolRegistry.ts:368-376`, label "Audio"); View > Audio list (tool `audioPanel`, label "Audio list", toggles the panel `toolRegistry.ts:552-557`).
- Main file: `src/audio/audioActions.ts:40-57` (`toggleRecording`).
- Features: Record starts at once and opens the panel; calling it while recording stops and saves (:44-47, :54-55). Toasts when it cannot start: "Open a page first, then record." (:51), "This browser cannot record sound.", "A recording is already running.", "No microphone was found.", the denied message, "The recording could not start." (:28-34, strings `audio.*`).
- Gating: needs an open page; MediaRecorder support; microphone permission (Permissions-Policy `microphone=(self)` in `public/_headers`).

#### Audio panel (left panel, "Audio")

- Opens from: automatically when recording starts (`audioActions.ts:55`), the recording chip's "Audio" button, View > Audio list, the page Audio tab's "Open the Audio panel", or a replay request from a drawing (`src/audio/AudioDock.tsx:321-323`). Mounted app-wide `src/App.tsx:2027`.
- Main file: `src/audio/AudioDock.tsx:159` (panel), `:306` (dock)
- Features:
  - Fixed left panel, `min(22rem, 92vw)` wide, with a single tab "Audio" and a close button ("Close the audio list") (:203-231).
  - Big "Record" button; while recording it reads "Stop · {m:ss}" in red; disabled while saving or while another page is recording (:233-247). Hint "…" `audio.recordHint`, or "Recording another page. Tap Stop to save it." (:248-250).
  - List of the page's recordings (oldest first), each `RecordingRow` (:251-262): name (default "Recording 6 Nov, 14:32"), length, "Play" (loads the sound only then, then a native `<audio controls autoPlay>`), "Delete" (aria "Delete: {name}") (:58-156). "This recording could not be played here." on error (:149-153). Empty: `audio.list.empty` text (:260-262).
  - Delete moves it to Trash-like list and toasts "Recording deleted." with "Undo" (:183-189).
  - Collapsible "Deleted recordings ({count})": each with "Restore" and "Delete forever", which asks "Delete this recording forever?" then blanks the sound (:263-295, :190-200).
  - Ink replay: a recording row started by "Play the recording from here" (Lasso menu) seeks to the second of the stroke (`seekAndPlay` :43-55, effect :91-108).
- Gating: only the current page's recordings. A recording that outlives its page stays tied to its page id (`recorder.ts`).
- Empty / error / loading: toasts "Recording saved to this page.", "Nothing was recorded.", "The recording stopped because of an error.", "The recording reached 3 hours and was stopped and saved.", save failure text (`src/audio/recorder.ts:101-111, 131-159`; one chunk per second; 3-hour cap `MAX_RECORDING_MS`).
- Keyboard and Pencil / touch: all buttons 44 px. Playback speed: only whatever the browser's own audio control offers; the app adds no speed control for recordings.

#### Recording chip

- Opens from: automatic whenever a recording runs and the panel is closed (`AudioDock.tsx:332-363`).
- Features: red dot, "Recording {m:ss}", button "Audio" (aria "Open the audio list") and button "Stop" (aria "Stop recording") (:343-361). Shows even when another page is open.
- Gating: `recorder.status === 'recording' && !open`.

#### Page "Audio & video" tab

- Opens from: the page tab "Audio & video" when enabled for the notebook (`src/notebooks/pageTabs.ts:13`, `src/pages/PageView.tsx:642`); also the Audio tab in the left panel (`src/app/panel/LeftPanel.tsx:507-509`).
- Main file: `src/pages/tabs/AudioTab.tsx:21`
- Features: button "Open the Audio panel" (:48-55); section "Audio" with the same `RecordingRow` list and delete with Undo (:59-74, :38-44); section "Video" listing the page's video files with an inline player (`InlineVideo` :75-85). Empty text `tabs.audio.empty` (:56-58).
- Gating: a video not stored here shows `tabs.audio.notHere` ("This video is not on this device.") by key, but that string is not used in the tab (see findings).

#### Book Read aloud panel (left panel Audio tab for a book)

- Opens from: left panel > Audio tab while a book is open (`src/app/panel/LeftPanel.tsx:503-505`). (The notes-page Reading mode has a different, richer Read aloud panel, see findings.)
- Main file: `src/reader/aloud/AloudPanel.tsx:27`
- Features: heading "Read aloud"; "⏮" ("Previous sentence"), main button "Read this page" / "Pause" / "Resume", "⏭" ("Next sentence"), "■" ("Stop reading") (:55-100); "Speed" select 0.5×, 0.75×, 1×, 1.25×, 1.5×, 1.75×, 2× (`SPEEDS` `src/app/reading/readingPrefs.ts:178`, :102-116), stored in the shared reading speed setting; "Voice" select with "Device default" and the device's voices "{name} ({lang})" (:117-133); note "Uses your device voice (free). The page follows and the sentence is marked." (:134).
- Gating: if the browser has no speech: "This device has no built-in speech." (:39-45). Back / forward / stop disabled while idle (:59, :87, :96). Engine `src/reader/aloud/bookAloud.ts` (starts at the first words on screen :88-96, goes to next chapter or page when finished :62-70, stops the notes reader first :90); text/highlight source `src/reader/aloud/bookText.ts`.

#### Capture screen

Rendered `src/App.tsx:1633-1644`.

  - Fields: "Link (optional)" (url input, placeholder "https://…"), "Title" ("Name this source"), "Text, transcript or notes" (10 rows) (:85-126). Fields prefill from the address (:36-38).
  - Live notes: "YouTube video {id}, starts at {time}." for a YouTube link (:97-104, `src/capture/youtube.ts:59-85`) and "Looks like a {kind}." with kinds Article, Video, Pasted text (:127-131, `captureKind` `captureService.ts:30`).

The bookmarklet sends title, link and up to 8,000 characters of selection through the address (`src/capture/bookmarklet.ts:8-23`).

#### Notes from your own AI panel (Copy prompt, Paste notes, zip)

- Opens from: inside the Capture screen (`CaptureScreen.tsx:146-151`) and inside the source page's "Paste notes" dialog (below).
- Main file: `src/capture/NotesPanel.tsx:27`
- Features (heading "Notes from your own AI", help "Copy a ready prompt, paste it into Gemini, Claude or any AI, then paste its answer back here. Nothing is sent from this app."):
  - "Prompt preset" select ("No extra instructions" + saved presets) (:132-154); "Extra instructions" text area (max 2000 chars, placeholder "e.g. Name every tool icon you see") (:155-166); preset name field (max 80) (:168-176); "Save preset" (disabled with no name or at 20 presets; at the limit toast "You can keep up to 20 presets." :54-63, :177-185); "Delete preset" (only when the name matches a saved preset :186-196). Presets live in pref `capture.presets` (:43).
  - "Copy prompt": copies the prompt built from the link, title, pasted text (cut at 120,000 chars) + the Learning Notes Markdown format + extra instructions (:198-205, `src/capture/promptBuilder.ts:11-58`). The toast key `capture.promptCopied` ("Prompt copied. Paste it into your AI.") exists, but this button only runs the clipboard task (see findings).
  - "Paste notes" text area (8 rows, "Paste the AI’s answer here") and "Make a page from these notes" (disabled when empty or busy) (:206-231). Result: toast "Made a page with {count} cards." and the page opens (:65-95); inline errors "No notes found in that text." / "That is too big (the limit is 2 MB)." (:85-90).
  - "Or choose a zip with notes.md and images" file chooser (accepts `.zip`); images become stored pictures; errors "That zip could not be used: it needs a notes.md file." (:97-118, :232-246; limits `src/capture/zipNotes.ts:12-15`: 200 MB, 500 entries, 300 MB expanded, 200 pictures).
  - Format: `docs/features/ai-notes-format.md`, parser `src/capture/notesFormat.ts` (2 MB, 500 cards; sections Key points, Steps, Mind map, Cards (Q:/A:/Cloze:), Screenshots, any other heading kept), writer `src/capture/importNotes.ts:90-148` (blocks marked `source: 'ai'` with one batch id; mind map -> mind map block `:107-113`; screenshot slots "📷 {time} {what}" `:137-148`).
- Gating: none; works with the user's own AI outside the app.

#### Source page header actions (Copy prompt, Paste notes, AI badge)

- Opens from: the header of a source page (a page with `page.source`), next to the book header's actions (`src/App.tsx:1823-1851`).
- Main file: `src/capture/PageAiActions.tsx:25`
- Features: "Copy prompt" (builds the prompt from the page's own text, up to 120,000 chars, `pageSource.ts:11-19`) (:42-48, :81-88); "Paste notes" opens a dialog containing the Notes panel for this page (Esc closes, "Close" button) (:89-97, :127-155); once an AI answer exists, a chip "Made with your AI · edited by you" opens a menu: "Keep as mine" (toast "Kept as yours. It is no longer marked as AI-made.") and "Remove this AI result" (danger, toast "Removed the AI result." with "Undo") (:99-126, :50-80).
- Gating: only on source pages; the chip only when an AI batch exists.

#### Memory helpers dialog (Major system and Acronym builder)

- Opens from: Templates list in the sidebar, last row "🧠 Memory helpers" (`src/templates/TemplatesSection.tsx:137-150`, dialog mounted :151-155; section mounted `src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:680`).

Make your own." when none (:96-100), and an own-word field "My word" (Enter saves, max 40 chars) (:117-132). Picks are kept in settings key `memory.majorPicks`, newest first, at most 5 per digit string (`majorSystem.ts:196-216`). English-only built-in word list.

It creates a `card` block "What does {acronym} stand for?" with the items as bullet children and syncs recall cards (:40-59). Text "Open a page first. The card goes there." when no page (:169-173).

## SURPRISING, DUPLICATED OR HALF-BUILT

1. No experience-level or device-mode gating anywhere here. If the redesign wants a simple default plus advanced section, every item above is currently always visible.
2. Mind map: Backspace deletes a node (not in the hint); F2, Alt+arrows and the Pencil double-tap are not mentioned in the hint; no collapse, zoom, pan, per-node colour or long-press / right-click menu; drag only re-parents (siblings reorder with Alt+Up / Down only); root cannot be moved or deleted. "Save as picture" lands directly after the block in the editor but through `addBlocks` (position not tied to the map) in the tab. `ComingReason` still lists `'mindmap'` (`toolRegistry.ts:60`) although the tool is live. i18n key `tabs.mindMap.made` is defined but never used by the UI (only in `src/app/toastNoise.test.ts:24`).
3. Write pad: `engine.ts:10,59` declares a `handwriting-api` engine and `hasHandwritingApi()` but only Scribble and AI exist. Closing the pad (X or the Insert tool) commits any live draft into the page when "Show my writing on the page as I write" is on (`WritePad.tsx:395-403`). Two Undo systems merge in the main bar. Look-row buttons are 36 px (below the 44 px used elsewhere). Eraser button appears only in AI mode. Spec says the main bar Insert pill, but `main.opt.insert.writePad` also exists in "Handwriting" chooser: three routes lead to the same pad.
4. Pictures: two overlapping action sets: the floating bar (Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Delete, Smaller, Bigger, Resize, Border, Deselect) vs the ribbon Picture strip (adds Fit width, Fit to page, Original size, Crop and rotate, Replace, Align x3, Caption; lacks Border). "Edit picture" exists in three places (block menu, selection toolbar, strip as "Crop and rotate"). "Draw on picture (arrow)" is only in the block menu. Camera has two code paths: the chooser's "Camera" input vs `PageEditor.takePhoto` (extra permission toast only on the latter). The "Files" input accepts `.heic` (`PictureChooser.tsx:142`) but `isImageType` allows only PNG, JPEG, WebP, GIF (`imageRef.ts:20-25`), so a HEIC file is rejected with "Pictures must be PNG, JPEG, WebP or GIF files." Copy / Cut failures are silent. `image.added`, `image.missing` strings are unused. Corner resize logic exists twice (placement preview and `PictureHandles`) but shares `pictureResize.ts`.
5. PDF: Organise reorders by HTML5 drag and drop only (no pointer fallback), so touch users rely on Move earlier / later. Scan "Look" is one setting for all pages. OCR on device is English only. "Export clean copy" is just the original bytes. The OCR sheet can be closed while working (it aborts). PDF tools exist only for PDFs, not EPUB.
6. Audio: the panel is built as a tab strip but has exactly one tab ("Audio", `AudioDock.tsx:209-219`); docs mention future Pages / Outline tabs. No rename, no download / export and no speed control for recordings. `audio.otherPage` and `tabs.audio.notHere` strings are unused (the missing-video case is not messaged in `AudioTab`). Two different Read aloud panels exist: the book one (`src/reader/aloud/AloudPanel.tsx`: skip a sentence, speed, voice) and the notes Reading-mode one (`src/app/reading/ReadingPanels.tsx:152` with sleep timer, skip mode seconds / sentence, voice ranking); their string sets (`reader.aloud.*` vs `reading.aloud.*`) are parallel.
7. Capture: `capture.promptCopied` ("Prompt copied. Paste it into your AI.") is defined but `copy()` in both `NotesPanel.tsx:51-52` and `PageAiActions.tsx:42-48` gives no confirmation (runTask only). Bookmarklet and Shortcut recipe are text on the screen only.
8. Memory helpers have one entry point only (Templates list in the sidebar) and an English-only word list.

---

## ALL KEYBOARD SHORTCUTS IN THIS SCOPE

Mind map (focus in the map, `MindMapView.tsx:99-226`)

- Tab: add child (while typing: save and add child)
- Enter: add sibling (while typing: save and add sibling)
- F2: type in selected node
- Escape (while typing): keep text and leave
- Delete / Backspace: delete selected branch (not the centre)
- Arrow Up / Down: previous / next node; Arrow Right: first child; Arrow Left: parent
- Alt+Arrow Up / Down: move among siblings; Alt+Arrow Right: indent; Alt+Arrow Left: outdent

Write pad

- Ctrl/Cmd+Z, Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+Z in the pad box: pad Undo / Redo (`WritePad.tsx:941-947`)
- Arrow Up / Down on the resize grip: height +/- 24 px (:586-589)

Pictures

- Placement preview: Enter = place, Escape = cancel (`PicturePlacement.tsx:300-314`)
- Picture editor: Escape = cancel (native dialog); Arrow keys on a focused crop corner move it 1% (Shift: 5%) (`CropSheet.tsx:217-229`); Ctrl/Cmd+mouse wheel = zoom (:318-323)
- Chooser sheet: Escape = close (`PictureChooser.tsx:82-87`)

PDF

- Escape closes a PDF sheet unless busy (`Shell.tsx:41-43`)
- Scan corner buttons: Arrow keys move the focused corner (`ScanSheet.tsx:141-151`)
- Page Flip slider: arrow keys move, release commits (`PageFlip.tsx:99-102`)

Memory helpers

- Enter in an own-word field saves it (`MemoryHelpers.tsx:125-130`); Escape closes the dialog (native `<dialog>`)

Audio / Capture

- Escape closes the "Paste notes" dialog on a source page (`PageAiActions.tsx:133`)
- Enter in the Capture form submits it (native form, `CaptureScreen.tsx:78-84`)
- No dedicated shortcut for Record / Stop (the Insert > Audio tool has no `shortcut` entry in `toolRegistry.ts:368-376`)

# APPENDIX D (04-databases.md)

## 04 - DATABASES UI inventory (read-only audit)

Paths are relative to the repo root (`c:\Users\pc\Downloads\learning-notes`). All line numbers verified against the code read on 2026-11-12.
Abbreviations: `DV` = src/databases/views, `DB` = src/databases, `DASH` = src/databases/dashboard. Labels are the English strings from src/i18n/en/databases.ts (`en`), src/i18n/en/late/databases.ts (`late`), src/i18n/en/home.ts + late/home.ts (calendar), src/i18n/en/importExport.ts (csv), src/i18n/en/recall.ts (dashboard._, rollup._), src/i18n/en/library.ts (gallery.*).

**Global gating finding:** nothing under src/databases/** or src/pages/tabs/DatabaseTab.tsx reads experience level, device mode or settings. The only conditions are: Study-tracker vs normal database (`database.kind === 'studyTracker'`), touch/pen vs mouse (drag code), CSS breakpoints (`phone:` and `pointer-coarse:` / `any-pointer-coarse:` variants) and the per-notebook "Database" page tab switch (src/notebooks/pageTabs.ts:12-35). The Databases hub link sits in the sidebar "Quick access" group, only in the Notebooks space (src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:429-481).

---

### A. Entry points / containers

#### Databases hub - "All databases" list (kind: screen)

- Opens from: sidebar "Databases" button (src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:465-479, label `sidebar.allDatabases`), App `openDatabases` (src/App.tsx:1265-1268), also "All databases" back link of a database. Route `{kind:'databases'}` (src/app/router.ts:15). Rendered lazily at src/App.tsx:1697.
- Main file: src/databases/DatabasesHub.tsx:32 (`DatabasesScreen`)
- Features:
  - Heading "Databases" (`databases.title`) - DatabasesHub.tsx:61-63
  - "+ New database" button (aria-haspopup menu, wrapped in Tip `databases.new`) - :64-73. Opens the starter menu (below).
  - "×" Close button, labelled "Close" (`databases.close`) - :74-81. Only shown on phone width (`hidden ... phone:flex`).
  - One card per database: title, a "Study tracker" badge for tracker databases (`databases.tracker`, :95-99), "N rows" / "N lessons" count (`databases.rows`/`databases.lessons`, :100-106), then "in <notebook>" or "not in a notebook" (`databases.inNotebook`/`databases.loose`, :108-110). Click opens the database (:91).
  - Rows count is computed per database with `listRows` for every database on every change (:44-55) (performance note, not UI).
- Gating: none (any user). "×" phone-only (:78).
- Empty / error / loading: empty text "No databases yet. Make one to keep a list of anything: drugs, words, books, habits." (`databases.empty`, :83-85). No loading state (list is blank until the live query returns).
- Keyboard / Pencil / touch: cards are plain buttons (min-h-14); no custom shortcuts.

#### New database starter menu (kind: menu)

- Opens from: "+ New database" in the hub (DatabasesHub.tsx:117-132)

#### Single database screen (full page) (kind: screen)

- Opens from: a hub card, the "Open" toast after a CSV import into a new database, "Open ↗" in an inline database block, or any `hub {kind:'database'}` route (src/App.tsx:1706-1719, wrapped in `DatabaseGate` for the lock check).

Export toast `csv.exported` "Saved “{name}”" (:230). Import toasts: `csv.doneNew`, `csv.doneReplace` (with Undo action "Undo" = `review.undo`, which re-trashes the added rows and restores the old ones, then toast `csv.undone`) (:312-326), `csv.doneUpdate`, `csv.done`, with `csv.doneColumns` suffix.

- Gating: part of the notebook's tab template (can be hidden/renamed by tab settings).

#### Inline "/database" block inside a note (kind: block / section)

- Opens from: slash menu item "Database" (subtext "Show a database in this note", group `slash.group.databases`, aliases database/board/tracker) - src/editor/slashMenu.tsx:165-168; asks which database via the picker (below). Also the "Database page" start option on a blank page (`newPage.database`, src/pages/PageView.tsx:682-697 -> `makeDatabasePage`, src/databases/databasePage.ts:17: a full-width database block + empty line; the page is named after the database).
- Main file: src/databases/DatabaseBlockView.tsx:346 (`DatabaseBlockView`) and :359 (`DatabaseBlock`)
- Features:
  - Database title as h3 (:458).
  - View switcher group "Views" (Tip `db.blockViews`): 6 buttons Table, List, Dashboard, Board, Calendar, Gallery with their icons (TYPES list :328-335; button :466-476). Switching only changes the block's own view type (`setBlockViewType`, blockViews.ts:53-59), keeping its own sorts/filters/charts config.
  - "⇔ Full width" toggle (`databaseBlock.fullWidth`, aria-pressed, Tip `db.blockWide`) - :480-496; grows block to the main area width, max 1400 px (MAX_WIDE :338; measuring :392-410, uses ResizeObserver).
  - "Open ↗" link (`databaseBlock.open`, Tip `db.blockOpen`) - :497-507, only if the editor supplies `app.openDatabase`. Opens the full-screen database.
  - The chosen view component for the block (:509-517).
- Gating / states: locked database shows a lock (DatabaseGate compact, :353). Own-view creation `ensureBlockView` (made as a Table the first time, blockViews.ts:29-50). "This database is shown inside one of its own rows. Open it from the Databases list instead." (`databaseBlock.loop`, :420-429). "This database was deleted. You can restore it from the Trash." (`databaseBlock.gone`, :430-439). Placeholder 16px box while loading (:417-419).
- Notes: the block switcher has 6 types (includes List); the main DatabaseView add-view menu lists the same 6 (viewTypes.ts:326-366). The block view cannot be renamed/deleted (no ⋯ menu); `deleteView` does not guard the last view for block views (databaseService.ts:797-803).

#### Database picker for "/database" (kind: dialog)

- Opens from: slash item "Database" (src/editor/PageEditor.tsx:728-739, rendered :1030).
- Main file: src/databases/DatabasePicker.tsx:26
- Features:
  - Modal `<dialog>` titled "Add a database" (`databaseBlock.choose.title`) - :100-111.
  - Search combobox "Search your databases…" (`databasePicker.search`) matching database title and notebook name (:112-138, :66-73).
  - Section "Your databases" (`databasePicker.yours`): databases sorted by last update with right-aligned notebook name or "Not in a notebook" (`databasePicker.loose`) - :45-64, :145-155.
  - Section "Create new" (`databasePicker.create`): the six starters (same list/labels as the hub menu), filtered by the search - :70-73, :156-164.
  - Values returned: `db:<id>` or `new:<starter>` (:11).
- Empty/error: "You have no databases yet." (`databasePicker.empty`) / "No database matches “{query}”." (`databasePicker.none`) (:149-155).
- Keyboard: ArrowDown/ArrowUp move, Enter chooses, Esc cancels (dialog `onCancel`), click on backdrop cancels (:104-106, :125-136); listbox/option ARIA with `aria-activedescendant`.

### B. View container, switcher and view options

#### Database view container: view switcher (tabs) (kind: bar)

- Opens from: shown by every database screen/tab/block-less mount (`DatabaseView`, src/databases/views/DatabaseView.tsx:29).
- Features:
  - Segmented group (aria-label "Views" = `database.views`) of one button per view: icon + view name; `aria-pressed` for current (DV/DatabaseView.tsx:98-117). Per-type hover tips (`db.viewTable/Dashboard/Board/Calendar/Gallery`, :251-269; List has no tip).
  - "+ View" button (`database.addView`, aria-haspopup menu, Tip `db.addView`) - :118-128, opens Add-view popover.
  - "⋯" "View options" button (`database.viewOptions`, aria-haspopup dialog, Tip `db.viewOptions`) - :129-140, opens View options popover.
  - Optional database title heading (`showTitle`) - :95-97 (all current callers pass false).
- Opening-view rule: picked "Always open on" view, else Dashboard once the database has rows (Table while empty), else first view (src/databases/views/openingView.ts:11-23). Chosen once when opened so adding the first row does not switch view (DatabaseView.tsx:64-71). While the app is open the last shown view per database is remembered in a module-level map (`lastViewShown`, openingView.ts:25-27, used :57, :82). If the route carries a view id it wins (:58-59, :88-90).
- Gating: none. A new database gets 3 views: Table, Dashboard, Board (databaseService.ts:85-100). Study tracker gets the same three (newViews, :174).
- Loading: "Loading…" (`notebook.loading`, :72-74).

#### Add-view menu (kind: popover)

- Opens from: "+ View" (DatabaseView.tsx:143-165)
- Features: popover "Add a view" (`database.addViewLabel`, width 220) with one item per registered view type, icon + localized label: Table ▤, Dashboard ◔, Calendar ▦, Gallery ◫, Board ▥, List ☰ (viewTypes.ts:326-366; label keys `database.view.*`, List key lives in late/databases.ts:3). New view name = type label, made unique ("Board 2", uniqueViewName :271-278), then shown (:155-161).
- Not offered: no copy/duplicate view, no reorder views, no per-view icon.

#### View options popover (kind: popover)

- Opens from: "⋯" next to the view tabs (DatabaseView.tsx:179-237)
- Features:
  - Heading "Which view this database opens on" (`database.openHint`) :185
  - "Open on Dashboard once there are rows" (`database.openAuto`, check mark when no explicit pick) :186-193
  - "Rename this view…" (`database.renameView`, icon ✎) :195-202 -> NameDialog "Rename view" / label "View name" / confirm "Rename" (:166-178), trims; empty name ignored.
  - "Delete this view" (`database.deleteView`, trash icon) :203-222. Refuses the last view with toast "A database keeps at least one view." (`database.lastView`, :209-212). Otherwise soft deletes and toast "View “{name}” deleted" with Undo (`restoreView`) :213-220.
  - One "Always open on “{name}”" item per view with icon and check on the chosen one (`database.openOn`, :224-235).

#### Group-by picker (Table and List) (kind: bar control)

- Opens from: always-visible row above Table and List (src/databases/views/GroupSelect.tsx:11)
- Features: native select labelled "Group by" (`group.label`, late/databases.ts:9) with "No grouping" (`group.none`) plus every column except Relation, Rollup, Note link (rowGroups.ts:14-20, `groupByColumns`). Hidden when no eligible column (GroupSelect.tsx:20). Saves `config.groupBy` and clears collapsed state (TableView.tsx:440-447; ListView.tsx:88-95).
- Group heading rows: "▾/▸ name count" button, "Collapse {name}" / "Expand {name}" labels (`group.collapse`/`group.expand`), "No value" for empty key (`group.noValue`) - TableView.tsx:601-634, ListView.tsx:150-177. Choice groups ordered by option order, no value last (rowGroups.ts:70-92). Collapsed state is not persisted (local state).
