### C. Table view

#### Table view (kind: screen/view)

- Opens from: view tab "Table" (DatabaseView), default for empty databases.
- Main file: src/databases/views/TableView.tsx:121 (`TableView`)
- Features (toolbar and chips):
  - Group-by select (above).
  - Chip bar (only shown if a sort, filter or hidden column exists, :448-517):
    - Sort chip "Sorted: {column} {direction}" with "×" "Remove sort" (:450-464, keys `table.sortChip`/`table.removeSort`)
    - One filter chip per filtered column, e.g. "Confidence is at least ★★★☆☆", "×" "Remove filter: {name}" (:466-489, `filterLabel` columnFilters.ts:148-158)
    - "◌ N hidden columns" button (`table.hiddenCount`) opens Hidden-columns popover (:490-501)
    - "Clear all" link (`table.clearAll`) when sort or filter exists (:502-510)
    - "{count} of {total} rows" live counter when filters exist (`table.shown`, :511-515)
  - Header row: sticky title column header + one header button per column + "+ Column" cell (:535-593).
    - Header button: type icon (`columnIcon`), name, "auto" badge for tracker auto columns (`database.auto`, :263-267), ↑/↓ arrow when sorted (:268-272), "⏷" mark when filtered (:273-277); aria-haspopup dialog; opens Column menu (:247-254). `aria-sort` set on `th` (:539-545, :558-564).
    - Column resize handle on every header edge (ColumnResizer, below).
  - "+ Column" button (`table.addColumn`, Tip `db.addColumn`, aria-haspopup dialog) opens Add-column menu (:574-590).
  - Body rows (:600-706): sticky first cell (row title). Title cell: row grip (only when no sort, :666-668), title button that starts inline rename (:669-684), and "Open ↗" button (`table.openRow`, aria-label "Open “{name}”") that opens the row's page (:685-694). One cell per visible column (:698-702) and an empty filler cell (:703).
  - Cell editing by column type (renderCell :283-416, editKind cellEditing.ts:36-45): see "Cell editors" section.
  - Group rows (header with count) when grouped (:601-634).
  - "+ New row" button at the bottom (`table.newRow`; "+ New lesson" for a Study tracker `table.newLesson`; Tips `db.newRow`/`tracker.newLesson`) - :720-728. Creates a row (or, for tracker, a top-level page in the notebook, :226-232) and starts renaming its title in place.
  - Help line under the table (`HelpLine db.table`, or `tracker.table` + `tracker.levels` for a tracker) :731-737.
  - Windowing: all rows up to 200, above that only rows near the screen + 12 each side with spacer rows (src/databases/views/rowWindow.ts:8-11, useRowWindow; TableView :155, :595-599, :707-711).
- Column widths: defaults 208 (title) / 128 (others), filler min 96 (TableView.tsx:65-68); user widths saved per view in `config.widths`, min 80, max 800 (viewConfig.ts:322-361).
- Gating: grip hidden while a sort exists (:666). Tracker specifics: Status cell is a picker with a pill and 📌 mark, Levels cell is a bar, Cards due and others read-only (:288-318).
- Empty / error / loading / toasts: "No rows yet." (`database.empty`) when no rows at all, "No rows match these filters." (`table.noMatch`) when filters hide everything (:715-719). Loading "Loading…" (:171-173). Bad typed value toast "That doesn’t fit this column: {detail}" (`table.badValue`, :222, :334).
- Keyboard / Pencil / touch: Enter saves, Esc cancels inline edit (TableCells.tsx:74-85). Column resize via drag or ArrowLeft/ArrowRight (16 px steps). Row reorder via grip (below). Header and cell buttons are real buttons with labels. No arrow-key cell navigation, no Tab-to-next-cell, no multi-cell selection or copy/paste of cells.

#### Column header menu (kind: popover)

- Opens from: clicking a column header button (TableView.tsx:739-770 -> `ColumnMenu`, src/databases/views/ColumnMenu.tsx:26). Also on the title column (column = null).
- Features:
  - Heading "Sort" (`column.sort`); two items whose labels depend on kind (columnFilters.ts:112-125): text "A → Z" / "Z → A"; number "Low → high" / "High → low"; date "Oldest first" / "Newest first"; checkbox "Unticked first" / "Ticked first"; choice "Option order" / "Reverse order"; others as text. Click toggles (clicking active sort clears it) and closes the menu (ColumnMenu.tsx:72-75, 108-120). Single-column sort only (viewConfig.ts:260-266).
  - Heading "Filter" + form (:122-248): description "Show only rows where {column}…" (`filter.where`); operator select (label list below); value input by operator input type; live "{count} of {total} rows match" (`filter.matches`, :223-227); buttons "Apply" (`column.apply`, disabled until valid) and "Clear filter" (`column.clearFilter`) (:228-247). Enter in the form applies.
  - Operator sets per column kind (columnFilters.ts:42-88):
    - Text/Web link/Tag/Note link/Relation: contains, does not contain, is, starts with, is empty, is not empty
    - Number: is, more than, less than, at least, at most, is empty, is not empty (star columns relabel empty/not-empty as "has no rating"/"has a rating", :106-110; star filter input is a clickable ★ row, ColumnMenu.tsx:147-175, default op "at least" :56-58)
    - Date: is, is before, is after, is on or before, is on or after, is empty, is not empty (date picker input, :191-199)
    - Checkbox: is ticked, is not ticked
    - Choice/Choices/Status: is any of, is none of (checkbox list of options, max-h-48), is empty, is not empty
    - Formula/Rollup/other "any": contains, is, more than, less than, is empty, is not empty
    - Unused: operator `isNot` ("is not") exists in the query engine and label list but no column kind offers it (columnFilters.ts has no isNot).
  - "Hide column" (icon ◌, `column.hide`, not for the title column) - :250-259.
  - "Edit column" (icon ✎, `column.edit`; hidden for tracker auto columns) - :260-269 -> Column editor dialog. For the title column it opens the dialog in rename-only mode.
  - For tracker auto columns: explanatory text `column.autoHint` (Status/Learning/Mastered rules) in place of Edit (:270-277).
  - Popover label `table.columnMenu` "{name} column options" (:105).
- Gating: filters are one per column (replaces previous on same column, viewConfig.ts:269-288). The "match all/any" option is read from config (`config.match`, viewConfig.ts:243) but there is no UI to set it - filters always AND together (half-built).
- Keyboard: Popover autofocuses first input, Esc closes and returns focus to the header button (Popover.tsx:25-59).

#### Add-column menu (kind: popover)

- Opens from: "+ Column" (TableView.tsx:771-812)
- Features: heading "Column type" (`column.type`). Basic items with icon, name and hint (typeLabel :103-109): Text; Number ("%, ₹/$ or ★ rating"); Date; Choice ("pick one, coloured pill"); Choices ("pick several"); Checkbox; Web link; Tag ("your #tags"); Note link ("points to a page"). Then separator and "More types ›" (`column.moreTypes`, hint "Formula, Relation, Rollup") which expands in place to Formula ("writes an answer in each row"), Relation ("link rows to another database"), Rollup ("count or total across a relation") (TableView.tsx:72-94, 788-810).
- Picking a type opens the Column editor dialog in "new" mode (:782-785).

#### Hidden-columns popover (kind: popover)

- Opens from: "◌ N hidden columns" chip (TableView.tsx:813-831)
- Features: one item per hidden column "Show “{name}”" (`table.showColumn`) with its type icon; closes after the last one.

#### Column resize handle (kind: control)

- src/databases/views/ColumnResizer.tsx:26. role=separator, tabIndex 0, aria-label "Resize column “{name}”" (`table.resize`), aria-valuemin/max 80/800. Mouse/pen/finger drag (pointer capture, RTL aware, :61-72) or ArrowLeft/ArrowRight (16 px, :73-79). Saved once at end of drag (`onDone` -> `withWidth`, TableView.tsx:205-208). Touch target width 12px (w-3).

#### Row reorder grip (Table and List) (kind: control)

- Main files: src/databases/views/RowGrip.tsx:31, useRowReorder.tsx:70, reorderRows.ts, DragGhost.tsx
- Features: six-dot grip button, label "Move row" (`reorder.grip`), visible on row hover/focus and always at 70% opacity on coarse pointers (`pointer-coarse:opacity-70`, RowGrip.tsx:33). Drag with any pointer (handle mode, 3 px slop, touch-action none). Keyboard: Space picks the row up, Up/Down move, Space or Enter drops, Tab drops, Esc cancels (useRowReorder.tsx:234-258). Screen reader texts: `reorder.help`, `reorder.pickedUp`, `reorder.position`, `reorder.moved`, `reorder.cancelled`. Rows stay inside their group (idsByGroup). Undo-by-placing-back per docs/features/database-views.md.
- Gating: enabled only while the view has no sort (`enabled: !query.sorts?.length`, TableView.tsx:165-170; ListView.tsx:65-71). Grip not even rendered when sorted (TableView.tsx:666, ListView.tsx:191).

### D. Cell editors (per column type)

#### Cell editors (kind: cell editor, per type)

Edit kinds (src/databases/views/cellEditing.ts:21-45): `none` (computed or unknown type), `inline` (text box), `checkbox`, `stars`, `picker` (popover). The same editors are reused in Table (TableView.tsx:283-416), Board title (BoardView.tsx:509-519) and Row properties panel (RowProperties.tsx:123-244).

- Title (row name): inline text editor, saves on Enter/blur, Esc cancels (TableCells.tsx:28-89; select-all on focus :64-66).
- Text: inline text editor.
- Number: inline editor with `inputMode=decimal`, only digits . , - kept while typing (`numberTyping`, columnFilters.ts:170-178); percent columns take whole percent typed and store fraction (typedNumber :164-168); empty stores null; wrong text toasts `table.badValue`.
- Date: browser `<input type=date>` or `datetime-local` when "Include a time" is on (TableCells.tsx:49-56; local time converted to ISO, cellEditing.ts:94-101).
- Checkbox: checkbox cell with optimistic state (TableCells.tsx:95-119).
- Number with "★ Rating" format: row of clickable stars (1 to `max`, default 5, up to 10); click the current star to clear (StarCell :122-153).
- Choice: Option picker popover; click picks and closes; picking the current clears (OptionPicker TableCells.tsx:198-344, `single` :216, toggle :266-273).
- Choices: same picker, multi toggle (tick state), stays open.
- Tag: picker shows `#path` tag choices via `findTagChoices`; can create a new tag from typed text via `findOrCreateTagPath` (:221-224, :245-251, :277-279).
- Note link: picker searching pages by title (`findLinkPages`), shows current links first when empty (:225-235, :252-256); placeholder "Type to find a page" (`table.findPage`).
- Relation: picker over the related database's rows, first 50 matches (:257-264); both sides settable (cellEditing.ts:39).
- Web link: inline text editor (`type=url`) + "🖼" picture button (aria "Choose a picture from this device", PictureButton :350-398, accepts PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF, shrinks and stores `lnimg:` ref) + "↗" "Open link" anchor when safe (TableView.tsx:395-412); a stored picture shows as "🖼 Picture" (:391-393).
- Formula / Rollup / back-relation: read-only text (editKind none).
- Tracker auto columns (Study tracker only): Status = pill with picker (see below); Levels = stacked bar + counts; Cards due = text; Last revised, % correct = read-only; "Confidence" is a normal 1-5 star number column (databaseService.ts:162-168) and stays editable.
- Picker contents (OptionPicker): search box "Find…" (`table.find`, Enter picks first match or creates), option list with colour dots, "Create “{name}”" (`table.create`) for Choice/Choices/Tag when no exact match (new option colour cycles through TAG_COLORS, :274-285), "Nothing found." (`table.noOptions`), and "× Clear" (`table.clear`) when something is set (:329-340).
- Pills: `CellPills` shows tag-chip pills coloured by option colour; tags prefixed "#" (TableCells.tsx:156-185).
- Gating: Study tracker Status cannot be typed; unknown column types read blank but keep data (columnTypes.ts header comment).
- Keyboard: Enter/Esc as above; picker is a Popover (Esc closes). Stars are buttons (Tab/Enter/Space).

#### Study tracker Status picker (kind: popover)

- Opens from: Status cell (Table), Status in the Row properties/Review tab, src/databases/views/TrackerCells.tsx:189
- Features: "Auto (now: {status})" with ⟲ icon (`tracker.status.auto`, hands status back to the app); heading "Set it yourself" (`tracker.status.setByYou`); four items Not started, Learning, Mastered, Review each with colour dot and 📌 (TrackerCells.tsx:210-236); help line `tracker.statusRule` (:238-241). Writes via `setLessonStatus`. Cell shows 📌 PinMark "Set by you" (`tracker.status.pinned`) when overridden (:174-183).
- Level bar (`LevelsCell`, :255-288): coloured segmented bar plus per-level counts with hover tips for New/Learning/Familiar/Mastered; "No cards" when none (`tracker.levels.none`).

---

### E. Column editor dialog

#### Column editor dialog (kind: dialog)

- Opens from: Add-column menu (new column), "Edit column" in the column menu, Board "Edit options of …"/"+ Choice column", Board empty state button "+ Choice column" (BoardView.tsx:413-431), Calendar none (it adds a Date column directly).
- Main file: src/databases/views/ColumnDialog.tsx:137 (`ColumnDialog`)
- Features:
  - Modal `<dialog>`; title "New {type} column" for new (`column.newTitle`, :364-367) or "Edit column" (`column.editTitle`).
  - "Name" input (`column.name`), auto-selected (:200-203, 390-396). For the title column a hint "This column is each row’s page title." (`column.titleHint`, :397-399); title column dialog only renames (:254-255).
  - "Column type" select (only when editing a non-relation column): Text, Number, Date, Choice, Choices, Checkbox, Web link, Tag, Note link, Formula, Rollup (EDIT_TYPES :56-68, select :401-420). Relation cannot be chosen when editing and relation columns show "Relations keep their type. Add a new column instead." (`column.typeLocked`, :422-424). Changing type does not rewrite values (changeColumnType).
  - Number settings: "Show as" select Number / Percent / Money / ★ Rating (:426-440); Money adds "Currency code" 3-letter uppercase input with hint "For example INR, USD or EUR" (:441-458); ★ Rating adds "Most stars" number 1-10 (default 5) (:459-477).
  - Date setting: "Include a time" checkbox (:481-490).
  - Choice/Choices settings: "Options" list with colour dot, rename input and "Remove “{name}”" × per option (:492-529); "Add an option" input (Enter or blur adds, case-insensitive duplicates ignored, colour cycles through TAG_COLORS, :296-309, 530-543). Hidden when the type is being changed (options are derived from old values, :222-238). There is no colour picker, no drag-reorder of options, no option delete confirm.
  - Formula: see Formula editor below.
  - Relation (new only): "Links to" database select (all workspace databases, current one marked "(this database)", :726-742) + checkbox "Also show the link in that database" (`column.twoWay`, :743-752, not for the back-side column; toggling on edit calls `setRelationTwoWay`, :275-278).
  - Rollup: if no relation exists -> "Add a Relation column first: a rollup sums up the rows it links to." (:754-759). Otherwise "Through" (relation column), "Column" (title or a column of the related database), "Show" function select (:761-811). Functions (`ROLLUP_FUNCTIONS`, databaseService.ts:340-352; labels rollup.* in recall.ts:240-250): The values (showOriginal), Count of rows, Count of values, Count of different values, Sum, Average, Smallest, Largest, % ticked, Earliest date, Latest date.
  - Error line (role alert) for save failures (:815-819).
  - Buttons: "Delete column" (danger, left, edit only; toast "Column “{name}” deleted" with Undo -> `restoreColumn`, :286-294, 821-829); "Cancel"; "Add column" (new) / "Save" (edit) (:830-843). Submit disabled when a rollup has no relation or a formula cannot be built (:368-370).
- Gating: Study tracker auto columns are not editable (menu hides Edit). Type list excludes relation and all tracker types.
- States: discard guard `useDiscardGuard` asks before closing a dirty dialog (:154, :378-380 backdrop click); Esc -> `onCancel`.
- Keyboard: form submit with Enter; Esc closes; modal focus trap via `<dialog>`.
- Missing: no column reorder anywhere in UI (service `moveColumn` databaseService.ts:588 has no caller outside tests), no duplicate column, no column description, no column-colour/icon choice.

#### Formula editor (inside Column editor) (kind: dialog section)

- Opens from: Column editor with type Formula (ColumnDialog.tsx:547-724)
- Features:
  - Simple sentence mode (default when formula is empty or expressible, :190-194, 579-698): "If [column select] [condition select] [value] write [text] otherwise write [text]". Column select lists the title + all other columns (:180-185, 583-598). Condition select depends on column type (formulaSentence.ts:56-65): number = is below, is above, is at most, is at least, is, is not, is empty, is not empty; checkbox = is ticked, is not ticked; date = is empty, is not empty; others = is, is not, is empty, is not empty. Value is a select of option names for Choice columns or an input (decimal keyboard + % suffix for percent columns) (:622-663). Initial rule is "If <percent/stars/number col> is below 75% / 3 / 10 write Redo otherwise OK" or "If <first col> is not empty write Yes otherwise No" (startRule :109-130; labels formula.builder.*).
  - "Advanced: write a custom formula" link (`column.advanced`, :690-696) switches to text mode; "Back to the simple sentence" link (`column.simple`) when the typed formula can be shown as a sentence or is empty (:569-577).
  - Advanced mode: monospace "Formula" input (:551-558), line "Means: …" plain-English translation (`formula.means`) or hint "Column names go in [brackets]." (`column.formulaHint`) (:559-563), and the "Columns and functions" helper (below).
  - Live preview line (aria-live): "For “{row}”, {inputs}, so it writes {result}." etc. computed on the first row, or "Add a row to see a preview." (`column.previewNoRows`) (:699-705; previewFormula/previewSentence in formulaPreview.ts).
  - "Examples" (:706-722): up to three clickable examples built from the database (DAYS(date, TODAY()), IF(score < limit, Redo, OK), score * 1.5), shown as sentences.
- Formula language (src/databases/formula/*, limits 2000 chars, depth 64: formulaParser.ts:29-31): column references `[Name]`; operators `+ - * / ^ & = <> < > <= >=`, unary; functions (formulaEval.ts, FORMULA_FUNCTIONS :525-528):
  - Logic: IF, IFERROR, AND, OR, NOT, ISBLANK (lines 172-263)
  - Math/aggregate: SUM, AVERAGE, MIN, MAX, COUNT, ROUND, FLOOR, CEILING, ABS, SQRT, POWER, MOD (266-328)
  - Text: LEN, UPPER, LOWER, TRIM, TEXT, VALUE, LEFT, RIGHT, MID, CONCAT, JOIN, CONTAINS, SUBSTITUTE (342-413)
  - Date: TODAY, NOW, DATE, YEAR, MONTH, DAY, DAYS, DATEADD (425-458)
  - Errors shown in the cell (`formula.error.*`): wrong number of values, self-reference cycle, division by zero, not a date, not a number, syntax, too big, too deep, too long, unknown column, unknown function.
- Gating: none. Simple builder only handles one IF; anything else forces Advanced.

#### "Columns and functions" helper (kind: disclosure panel)

- Main file: src/databases/views/FormulaHelper.tsx:11; shown only in Advanced formula mode (ColumnDialog.tsx:564-568)
- Features: `<details>` summary "Columns and functions" (`formula.helper`). "Tap a column to add it:" chip per column (title first) inserts `[Name]` at the END of the formula (not at cursor) (:27-39). "Tap a function to add it:" monospace chip per function in the list above inserts `NAME(` (:40-52).

### F. Board view

#### Board view (kind: screen/view)

- Opens from: view tab "Board" (default 3rd view).
- Main file: src/databases/views/BoardView.tsx:107
- Features:
  - Toolbar pills: "Grouped by {column} ▾" (`board.groupedBy`, Tip `db.groupedBy`) and "Card shows ▾" (`board.cardShows`, Tip `db.cardShows`) - :554-589.
  - Columns (one per option of the group column, plus a "No {column}" column shown only while it has cards): header shows a coloured option pill + card count (:618-634); width w-64 (78vw on phone) (:614). Study tracker status headers carry status tips (StatusTip :875-889).
  - Card (li): title button (opens the row page), "⋯" options button ("Options for “{row}”", `board.moveLabel`), 📌 for pinned tracker status, and up to 3 chosen values (default `DEFAULT_CARD_VALUES = 3`, boardGroups.ts:14): pills for choice/tag/note link/relation/status, "Column text" otherwise; stars print plain (:456-480, 482-552).
  - Per-column "+ New row" ("+ New lesson" for tracker) with screen-reader suffix "in {group}" (`board.newIn`) - :670-682. Creates the row with that group's value and starts inline title rename (:196-204).
  - "Show N more" per column (`board.showMore`) 50 cards at a time (PAGE_SIZE 50, :51, :657-669).
  - Last dashed column: "Add options" button (accent) with text "“{column}” has no options yet." when the group column has no options, or small "+ Add option" otherwise; opens Column editor for the group column (:686-710). Not shown for tracker Status (fixed options, :454, :687).
  - Tracker-only help line above the board (`HelpLine tracker.board`, :592-594).
- Drag and drop: mouse/pen after 5 px (MOUSE_SLOP :64), finger after 350 ms hold (TOUCH_HOLD :66; moving > 8 px before that scrolls), gap placeholder where the card will land, floating rotated ghost card, edge auto-scroll 48 px zone / 12 px per tick (EDGE :69-70, autoScroll :248-264), `placeRow` writes group value + position in one step (:266-287). Sorted view -> only group changes (:280). Long-press context menu suppressed (:489-494). Click after drag suppressed (:326-331). Text selection disabled on cards.
- Dialogs/menus: Group-by popover, Card-shows popover, Card "⋯" popover (below), Column editor dialog.
- Gating: group column must be a Choice or tracker Status column (boardGroups.ts:19-24). Falls back to the first Choice column if the saved one is gone.
- Empty state (no Choice column): dashed box "A board puts rows in columns by a Choice column, like To do · Doing · Done. Add one to start." with "+ Choice column" button (`board.noChoice`/`board.addChoice`, :407-431).
- Not present: no filter or sort UI in Board (config is read via `readRowQuery` but nothing writes it, :131-139); no column (option) reorder by drag; no collapse of columns; no WIP counts; no drag of whole columns.
- Keyboard: no keyboard drag. "Move to" menu is the keyboard path. Card title button focusable; Esc cancels inline rename.

#### Board "Grouped by" popover (kind: popover)

- Opens from: pill "Grouped by …" (BoardView.tsx:732-773)
- Features: "Edit “{column}” options" (`board.editOptionsOf`, icon ✎, not for tracker) opens Column editor; heading "Group by" (`board.groupBy`); every Choice/Status column with icon and check; "+ Choice column" (`board.addChoice`) opens new Choice column dialog.

#### Board "Card shows" popover (kind: popover)

- Opens from: pill "Card shows ▾" (BoardView.tsx:774-807)
- Features: heading "Card shows"; every column except the group column as a toggle item (aria-pressed, icon); saved in `config.cardColumns` (`withCardColumn`).

#### Board card menu (Move to) (kind: popover)

- Opens from: "⋯" on a card (BoardView.tsx:808-844)
- Features: "Open" (`board.open`, icon arrow) opens the page; "Rename" (`board.rename`, icon ✎) starts inline title edit; heading "Move to" (`board.moveTo`) + one item per group (check on current), writes the group's value (`moveTo` :191-195). Not offered: delete, duplicate.

### G. Gallery view

#### Gallery view (kind: screen/view)

- Opens from: view tab "Gallery" (added via + View).
- Main file: src/databases/views/GalleryView.tsx:259
- Features:
  - "Gallery options ▾" pill (`gallery.options`, Tip `db.galleryOptions`) opens options popover (:374-388).
  - Counter "N tiles" (`gallery.count`), hint "Drag a tile to move it (or Alt + arrow keys)." (`gallery.dragHint`) when more than 1 tile (:389-394). Hidden role=status announces "Moved to position N" (`gallery.moved`).
  - Tile grid (`repeat(auto-fill, minmax(11rem,1fr))`, :402-405): picture (http(s) address or `lnimg:` local image; lazy, no-referrer; "◫" placeholder otherwise - TileImage :594-615), title (or "Untitled"), up to 3 values (`DEFAULT_VALUES = 3`, galleryTiles.ts:5) as muted truncated lines. Click opens the row page.
  - "Show more" button (`search.more`) 60 tiles at a time (PAGE_SIZE 60, :252, :535-543).
  - No "new row" button in Gallery (rows only via other views).
- Options popover (:544-588): heading "Picture from" (`gallery.image`); "No picture" item; one item per Web link column (icon link); heading "Card shows"; toggle item per non-picture column (saved `cardColumns`). Default picture column = first Web link column unless explicitly cleared (:311-314).
- Drag: mouse/pen after 6 px, finger after 400 ms hold (10 px move before hold = scroll) (:417-450); insertion bar (inset shadow) before/after the hovered tile; keyboard: Alt+Arrow keys step the focused tile (:486-503, galleryTiles `stepPlace`). If the view has a sort, a confirm sheet "Place tiles by hand?" with "Cancel" and "Remove the sort and move" (`gallery.manual.*`, `ui.choose`, :321-343) is shown before moving.
- Empty: "Nothing to show yet." (`gallery.empty`, :399-401).
- Gating: none. Sorting/filtering config is honoured (`readRowQuery`) but there is no UI in Gallery to set it.

### H. List view

#### List view (kind: screen/view)

- Opens from: view tab "List" (key `database.view.list` lives in late/databases.ts:3).
- Main file: src/databases/views/ListView.tsx:33
- Features:
  - Group by select (shared GroupSelect).
  - "Sort by" select (`list.sortBy`): "Manual order" (`list.sortManual`), the title column, and every column (:96-123).
  - Direction toggle button "Ascending ↑" / "Descending ↓" (`list.ascending`/`list.descending`) when a sort is chosen (:124-141).
  - One line per row: optional row grip (only in manual order), title button (opens the page), and up to 4 muted values joined with " · " from visible columns, excluding group column (SHOWN_VALUES 4, :26, :178-205).
  - Group headers with collapse (:150-177).
  - "+ New row" (`table.newRow`) button: adds a row and opens its page directly (:215-223) (differs from Table which edits the title in place).
  - Windowing like Table (>200 rows, :55).
- Empty: "No rows yet." (`database.empty`, :212-214). Loading: "Loading…".
- Gating: respects saved filters and hidden columns of the view but offers no UI to set filters or hide columns (hidden columns can only be set from a Table view of the same view config - note each view has its own config, so a List view effectively cannot filter at all).
- Keyboard: grip drag/keys as in Table.

### I. Calendar view (inside a database)

#### Calendar view (database) (kind: screen/view)

- Opens from: view tab "Calendar" (lazy-loaded chunk, src/databases/views/LazyCalendar.tsx:12).
- Main file: src/databases/views/CalendarView.tsx:110
- Modes (button group aria-label "Calendar range", `calendar.mode`): Month (default), Week, Year - MODES = month/week/year (:52, :554-570). Saved in `config.mode`. (Day mode is NOT in this view; it exists only on the global Calendar screen, below.)
- Features:
  - Title: month name+year (monthTitle), "Week of {day}", or the year number (:398-405).
  - Navigation: "‹" Previous, "Today", "›" Next (aria labels `calendar.previous`/`calendar.next`, Tip `db.calendarNav`) - :527-553; step by month/week/year.
  - "Date column" select shown when more than one date-like column exists (date, Last revised, formula, rollup via `dateColumns`) (:571-584, chartSpec.ts:96, 103).
  - "End date column" select (`calendar.endColumn`) with "No end date" (`calendar.noEnd`), only for real Date columns and only when another Date column exists (:585-599). Makes multi-day spans laid out in lanes per week.
  - Month/Week grid: 7 columns, min width 640 with horizontal scroll (:612-626); weekday header; day cells (min-h-24 month / min-h-40 week) dimmed when outside the month; today's number in an accent circle; chips per row (title, 22px tall, truncated; span chips continue across days, no repeated label); max visible lanes 3 (month) / 12 (week) (MAX_LANES :51).
  - "+ N more" button per crowded day (`calendar.more`, aria "Show all rows on {day}") opens the Day list popover (:501-515).
  - "+" add button on each day (aria "Add a row on {day}", `calendar.addOn`), visible on hover/focus-within, only for a real Date column and not for tracker databases (`canAdd`, :406, :476-491). Creates a row with that date and opens its page.
  - Year mode: see Calendar year below.
  - Undated count line "{N} rows have no date and are not shown." (`calendar.undated`, only outside Year mode, :627-631).
- Drag to move a row's date: mouse/pen after a few px, finger after 350 ms hold (mode 'hold', useDragGesture.ts:30-37, :258), floating ghost with target day text, target day highlighted (ring); start+end shift together; toast "“{name}” moved to {day}." with Undo (`calendar.moved`, :190-207), failure toast "“{name}” could not be moved." (`calendar.moveFail`). Apple Pencil on iPad also handled via touch events with `touchType === 'stylus'` (useDragGesture.ts:204-231). Esc cancels a drag (:160).
- Keyboard move: focus a chip, Space picks it up, Arrow Left/Right = -/+ 1 day, Up/Down = -/+ 7 days, Space or Enter drops (toast with Undo), Tab drops, Esc puts it back (`calendar.cancelled`) (:325-349, KEY_DAYS :79-84); live region announcements (`calendar.pickedUp`, `calendar.keyMoved`); sr-only hint `calendar.chipHelp` (:632-636). Changing outside the shown month/week moves the view along (:299).
- Empty / error: no date column -> "A calendar needs a Date column." with "Add date column" button (adds a Date column named "Date", `calendar.noDate`/`calendar.addDate`/`calendar.dateName`, :374-394). Loading text via Suspense fallback and `notebook.loading`.
- Gating: dragging, adding and the end-date select need a real `date` column (`writable`, :149); a computed date column (Last revised, formula, rollup) is read-only. Span longer than a year is cut; dates outside years 1000-9999 are not moved (docs).
- Touch: chips `touch-callout none`, context menu suppressed when writable (:436). Week/Month grid scrolls sideways on narrow screens.

#### Calendar "+N more" day list (kind: popover)

- Opens from: "+ N more" in a day (CalendarView.tsx:640-651; src/databases/views/CalendarDayList.tsx:14)
- Features: width 280 popover labelled "Rows on {day}", header with the day, windowed list of all rows of that day (title buttons; "Untitled" if empty); click opens the row page. Rows are only openable here (no drag from this list).

#### Calendar year grid (kind: view mode)

- Main file: src/databases/views/CalendarYear.tsx:15
- Features: 12 small month calendars (auto-fill grid min 210px); month name button (aria "Open {month}") jumps to that month in Month mode; weekday letters; day buttons (aria "{day}: N rows" via `calendar.yearDay`) with a dot whose opacity grows with count (min 1, 0.45 + 0.18n); today in an accent circle; clicking a day sets the anchor and switches to Month mode (CalendarView.tsx:602-611). Region label "Months of {year}" (`calendar.yearLabel`). No dragging in Year mode.

### J. Global Calendar screen (not a database view, but built on database rows)

#### Calendar screen (hub) (kind: screen)

- Opens from: sidebar "Calendar" (src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:450-463), `openCalendar` (src/App.tsx:531-534), route `{kind:'calendar'}` (router.ts:18); mounted at App.tsx:1688-1695.
- Main file: src/databases/CalendarScreen.tsx:230
- Features:
  - Heading "Calendar" and "Close" button (`calendar.screen`, `calendar.close`) - :293-300. Help paragraph `calendar.screenHelp` (:301).
  - Title (aria-live) for the shown day/week/month (:279-286).
  - "‹" Previous, "Today", "›" Next with Tip `calendar.nav` (:309-333).
  - Range switch group "Calendar range": Day, Week, Month (MODES :210; labels `calendar.day/week/month`) (:334-350). The last choice is remembered on this device (pref `calendar.mode`, readPref/writePref :247-253).
  - Week/Month grid (min-w 640): day-number button per cell that opens or starts that day's journal (aria "Open the journal for {day}", `calendar.openDay`, :389-399); chips for dated database rows and journal days (journal chips have an accent left border; locked items prefixed with a lock emoji, `chipText` :219-223); max chips 3 (month) / 12 (week) with a non-clickable "+ N more" text (:420-424, 287). Chip tooltip = database name.
  - Hint when range is Day: `calendar.dayHelp` (:355-357).
  - Empty: "Nothing is dated in this period." (`calendar.empty`, :431-433) in Week/Month.
- Data: all dated rows of every database (and journal days) via `calendarEntries(db, workspaceId, from, to)` (src/databases/calendarEntries.ts).
- Gating: none. Read-only (no dragging, no add buttons) unlike the database Calendar view. No year mode here although docs/features/calendar-day.md says "Year view stays parked" (Year exists only in the per-database view).
- Phone: `phone:px-3` (:292); touch buttons min-h-11.

#### Calendar Day card (kind: card)

- Main file: src/databases/CalendarDayCard.tsx:456; swipe rules src/databases/swipe.ts:7-8 (>= 60 px sideways within 700 ms, mostly horizontal)
- Features: weekday + " · Today" (:504-507), the date in 34px type (:508-510), button "Open this day’s journal" / "Start this day’s journal" (`calendar.openJournal`/`calendar.startJournal`, :511-517), heading "Dated rows" with a button per dated database row (title + database name, lock prefix) (:518-541). Move by day: swipe left = next day, right = previous (touch and pen only; mouse ignored, :480-495); ArrowLeft/ArrowRight when the card itself has focus (:496-500); `touch-action: pan-y` keeps vertical scroll (:501).
- Empty: "No database row is dated on this day." (`calendar.dayEmpty`, :521-523).

### K. Dashboard view

#### Dashboard view (kind: screen/view)

- Opens from: view tab "Dashboard" (default opening view once rows exist).
- Main file: src/databases/dashboard/DashboardView.tsx:38
- Features:
  - 12-column grid of chart cards; sizes small (6/12 cols, 3 on lg), medium (12, 6 on lg), wide (12) (SPAN :26-30).
  - Each card: title (tracker history charts have a Tip), "⋯" chart options button (aria "“{name}” chart options", Tip `db.chartMenu`) - :105-131.
  - "+ Add chart" dashed button (`dashboard.addChart`, Tip `db.addChart`) - :142-150.
  - Starter charts until the user changes something: `suggestCharts` (chartSpec.ts:241-345) builds: row count tile; up to 3 tiles per number column (average for stars/percent/% correct, else sum); donut of the first choice-like column; bar of the first number column per row; wide/medium progress bar of the first choice-like column; tracker adds Study streak (number), Answers right per week (line), Cards answered per day (heatmap); non-tracker with a date column adds a wide heatmap on the first non-formula date column (chartSpec.ts:331-340). First change saves them all (save :71-72).
  - Per-chart state: "Show as table"/"Show as chart" toggle kept in local state only (:53, :171-185).
- Chart options popover (width 220, :152-210): "Edit chart" (icon ✎, not for history charts), "Show as table"/"Show as chart" (▤), "Move earlier" (←, not for first), "Move later" (→, not for last), separator, "Delete chart" (trash). Delete shows toast "Chart “{name}” deleted" with Undo that restores the whole list (:83-90).
- No drag-reorder of charts (only the two Move items); no resize on the card (size set in the dialog); no duplicate chart.
- Loading: "Loading…". Charts pointing at deleted columns are dropped (`knownChart`, chartSpec.ts:179).
- Gating: history charts need the Study tracker (loadReviewHistory only runs for `kind === 'studyTracker'`, :42-48).

#### Chart dialog (kind: dialog)

- Opens from: "+ Add chart" or "Edit chart"; src/databases/dashboard/ChartDialog.tsx:112
- Features:
  - Title "New chart" / "Edit chart" (`chart.newTitle`/`chart.editTitle`).
  - Study tracker only, new chart only: fieldset "Review history" with three one-click buttons: "Study streak" (number, small), "Answers right per week" (line, medium), "Cards answered per day" (heatmap, wide) and note "From your card answers; added as they are." (:167-198, HISTORY_PRESETS :74-78). Clicking saves immediately.
  - "Chart type" tiles (aria-pressed, icon + name + hint, :200-225; KIND_ICONS :32-39): Number "One big number" (#), Bars "Compare groups or rows" (☰), Line "Change over time" (⟋), Donut "Parts of a whole" (◔), Progress bar "Parts of a whole, in one bar" (▭), Calendar (heatmap) "How much happened on each day" (▦). CHART_KINDS in chartSpec.ts:21-28. Switching kind keeps settings and fills needed group/date columns (`switchKind` :88-105).
  - "Show" select (measure): "Number of rows" plus, for each number-like column (number, formula, rollup, Cards due, % correct - NUMERIC chartSpec.ts:89-95) the measures Total/Avg./Min/Max `{column}` (`chart.measureOf.*`); Donut, Progress and Calendar only offer count and sum; sum of percent columns is hidden (:227-263). Note "Parts of a whole add up counts and sums only." (`chart.wholeOnly`) for donut/progress.
  - "Group by" select (Bars, Donut, Progress): "{title column} (one bar per row)" or any column except date, Last revised, Web link (:265-284; NOT_GROUPS chartSpec.ts:97).
  - "Date column" select (Line, Calendar heatmap): date, Last revised, formula, rollup columns; if none exists a notice "This database has no Date column yet. Add one in the Table view (+ Column › Date)." (`chart.noDateColumn`, :286-329). "One point per" select Day/Week/Month for Line only (:309-327; PERIODS chartSpec.ts:37).
  - "Only rows where" filter (:331-405): column select ("All rows" default), operator select, and for "is" an option select. Operator sets: checkbox = is ticked / is not ticked; choice, choices, tracker status = is / has a value / is empty; others = has a value / is empty (filterOps :61-71). Only one filter per chart.
  - "Size" segmented: Small, Medium, Wide (:407-425).
  - "Title" input (max 200; placeholder shows the automatic title; sr-only hint "Leave empty for an automatic title", :426-444).
  - "Preview" panel: the live chart with real data (:447-461).
  - Buttons: "Cancel", "Add chart" (new) / "Save" (edit) (:463-477).
- Auto titles (chartSpec.ts `chartTitle` :375): "{what} by {group}", "{what} per day|week|month ({date})", "{group} progress" (en: `chart.title.*`).
- Limits (chartData.ts:68-77, docs/features/dashboards.md): bar max 12 groups, donut 6, progress 6, rest folded into "Other"; line points 31 days / 12 weeks / 12 months; heatmap 18 weeks; weeks start Monday.
- States: discard guard (:130, :154-156); modal `<dialog>`.
- Keyboard: Esc closes via dialog cancel; type tiles and size buttons are buttons.

#### Chart renderers (kind: chart body)

- Main file: src/databases/dashboard/ChartBody.tsx:21 (`ChartBody`)
- Per chart type:
  - Number: 30px value, optional note (e.g. "Best: N days") and "from N rows" / "from N lessons" for non-count measures (:41-58).
  - Bars (:79-89, `Bars` :180): horizontal bars with truncated labels (title tooltip), value text, value share; folded "Other"; "+ N more rows/lessons" note.
  - Donut (:272-352): SVG ring with centre total, legend with "{value} · {percent}" (`dashboard.share`), role=img with textual label.
  - Progress (:355-392): one stacked bar split by group, role=img with a text label, legend.
  - Line (:425-610): SVG, y ticks (niceTicks), points tooltip on pointer move/down; focusable (tabIndex 0) with Left/Right arrow keys to move the active point (:482-510); aria-label lists all points.
  - Heatmap (:612-680): 18-week calendar grid with per-day tooltip (hover/pointer enter, `dashboard.heatLabel`), "Less/More" legend and "{value} in the last 18 weeks" total (aria-live).
  - Table alternative (`DataTable` :142): headers "Group/Value" or "When/Value" (`dashboard.col.*`).
  - Empty/notice texts: "No data yet" (`dashboard.noData`), "No rows with a date yet" (`dashboard.noDates`), "Pick a column: ⋯ › Edit chart" (`dashboard.pickColumn`), "Empty", "Ticked/Not ticked", "Other", "Untitled", "Week of {day}".
- No chart colour customisation; no axis/legend toggles; no export of a chart image (grep found none).

### L. Row open / row properties

#### Row open (kind: navigation)

- There is no row peek, side panel or modal. Every view opens a row as its full page via `onOpenPage(pageId)`: Table "Open ↗" button (TableView.tsx:685-694), Board card title (BoardView.tsx:521-530) or menu "Open", Gallery tile (GalleryView.tsx:477-485), List line (ListView.tsx:194-205), Calendar chip, Calendar day list, Calendar screen chips. Rows are ordinary pages with `databaseId`/`values`, so they can hold notes, cards, links and drawings (docs/features/database.md).

#### Row properties panel (kind: panel above the row page's notes)

- Opens from: automatically at the top of a row page, or (Study tracker) in the Review tab as "Study status" when `where='review'`; src/databases/RowProperties.tsx:48
- Features: section titled "Properties" (`properties.title`), one line per column (8.5rem label + editor) using the same editors as Table (RowProperties.tsx:123-244); empty values show a muted "Empty"-style placeholder (`properties.empty`); read-only text for computed columns.
- Study tracker mode (:260-286, 126-174): collapsible summary row "Study status: {status} · {n} due · {pct}%" with toggle (state saved in pref `review.statusOpen`, device only, :37-41) and an "ⓘ" button (`study.status.why`) that shows the rule explanation (`help.line.tracker.statusRule`); Status pill with 📌 and a "Back to auto" link (`tracker.status.backToAuto`); Levels bar with the meaning of each level (New/Learning/Familiar/Mastered).
- Gating: shows nothing for ordinary pages and for a database with zero columns (:105); top panel is hidden for tracker rows (they live in the Review tab) (:107).

---

### M. CSV import / export

#### CSV import dialog (kind: dialog)

- Opens from: Import/Export menu "Import CSV…" (not Study tracker); src/databases/CsvImportDialog.tsx:209
- Features:
  - Title "Import CSV" (`csv.title`) and intro "Adds the file’s rows to this database as new rows. Nothing that is already here is changed." (`csv.intro`) - note this intro text is stale because replace/update modes exist and the default mode is "New database" (:309-310, :234).
  - "Choose a CSV file" file button (`csv.file`, accepts .csv, text/csv, text/plain) via FileButton (:311-316).
  - After a file: "N rows found." (+ "It was cut to the first rows the app can import." when truncated) (:324-327); mapping table with columns "CSV column", "First rows" (first 3 rows joined by " · ", PREVIEW_ROWS :200) and "Becomes" select (:328-392). Select options: "Title ({name})", "Column “{name}”" for each existing importable column, "New {type} column" for each IMPORTABLE_TYPES type, "Leave out" (:370-386). Defaults guessed by `guessMapping`/`guessType` (csvTransfer.ts:121-165). IMPORTABLE_TYPES at csvTransfer.ts:81 (excludes formula/relation/rollup).
  - Mode radio group "What should happen to the rows already in this database?" with four options (MODES :197): "New database (recommended)" (default, makes a separate database, source untouched), "Add rows", "Replace rows" (old rows go to Trash, Undo from the result toast), "Update matching rows" (same title, ignoring case; blank CSV cells keep values) - each with a hint (:393-417).
  - Hint "No column is the title, so new rows start without a name." when no column is mapped to the title (:418-420).
  - Buttons: "Cancel"; primary "Import N rows" (`csv.import`) or "Pick a file first" (`csv.importNone`) disabled until a file is read or while busy (:423-444).
  - Replace mode with existing rows opens a ConfirmDialog "Move the {count} rows in this database to Trash and import {incoming} rows from the file?" with button "Replace rows" (:445-458).
- Errors: "This file is too big to import (the limit is about 30 MB)." (`csv.tooBig`, :254-257), "This file has no rows to import." (`csv.empty`, :259-262), role=alert (:317-321).
- Limits: 30,000,000 chars, 100,000 rows, 200 columns (csv.ts:8-10); chunks of 500 rows (csvTransfer.ts:313). Separator auto-detect comma/semicolon/tab (csv.ts:12-15). Export formula-injection guard `protectCell`/`unprotectCell`.
- Discard guard: closing after a file is chosen asks first (`useDiscardGuard`, :225).
- Gating: not for Study tracker (menu item omitted DatabasesHub.tsx:234-242).

#### CSV export (kind: action)

- "Export as CSV" in the Import/Export menu (DatabasesHub.tsx:223-233): writes the title column plus every column as plain text; downloads `<database title>.csv`; toast "Saved “{name}”". Available for Study tracker too. No column/row selection, no export of the current filtered view, no other formats.

---

### N. Not found in the code (asked for in the brief but absent)

- Bulk selection bar / multi-row select: none in any view (grep for selection UI in src/databases found only combobox selection in DatabasePicker).
- Row context menu / row delete / duplicate row / row-level move-to-trash: none (only Board's "⋯" with Open/Rename/Move to). Rows are deleted by deleting their page elsewhere; `trashRows`/`trashRowIds` are used only by CSV replace/undo and the prompt library.
- Free-text search over a database's rows: none (only the global search indexes row text, docs/features/database.md "Search"). Pickers search options only.
- Multi-column sort, "match any" filter UI, saved filter presets, filter groups: none.
- Column reorder, column pin/freeze (only the first title column is sticky), column duplicate, conditional formatting, summary/footer row (aggregations at the bottom of Table): none.
- Formula editor with syntax highlighting or autocomplete: none (buttons insert at the end).
- Hub features: no search, sort, folder/notebook grouping, rename/delete/duplicate from the list, no database icons/covers.

## Tables

### Column types (registered in src/databases/columnTypes.ts:359-391, UI wiring above)

| Type id                                                                                                                                                                                     | Visible name | Stored as                                                   | Settings in editor                        | Edit control            | In "+ Column" menu | In Edit-type select | Filter kind |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------- | ------------------ | ------------------- | ----------- |
| text                                                                                                                                                                                        | Text         | string (100,000 chars)                                      | none                                      | inline                  | basic              | yes                 | text        |
| number                                                                                                                                                                                      | Number       | number (formats: number, percent fraction, currency, stars) | Show as, Currency code, Most stars (1-10) | inline / stars          | basic              | yes                 | number      |
| date                                                                                                                                                                                        | Date         | "YYYY-MM-DD" or UTC ISO with time                           | Include a time                            | date / datetime-local   | basic              | yes                 | date        |
| choice                                                                                                                                                                                      | Choice       | option id                                                   | Options list                              | picker (single)         | basic              | yes                 | choice      |
| choices                                                                                                                                                                                     | Choices      | option ids                                                  | Options list                              | picker (multi)          | basic              | yes                 | choice      |
| checkbox                                                                                                                                                                                    | Checkbox     | boolean                                                     | none                                      | checkbox                | basic              | yes                 | checkbox    |
| url                                                                                                                                                                                         | Web link     | http/https/mailto text or `lnimg:` ref                      | none                                      | inline + picture + open | basic              | yes                 | text        |
| tag                                                                                                                                                                                         | Tag          | tag ids                                                     | none                                      | picker (create tags)    | basic              | yes                 | text        |
| noteLink                                                                                                                                                                                    | Note link    | page ids                                                    | none                                      | picker (search pages)   | basic              | yes                 | text        |
| formula                                                                                                                                                                                     | Formula      | computed (no storage)                                       | sentence builder or advanced text         | read-only               | More types         | yes                 | any         |
| relation                                                                                                                                                                                    | Relation     | row ids (two-way partner `back`)                            | Links to, Also show link in that database | picker (first 50)       | More types         | NO (locked)         | text        |
| rollup                                                                                                                                                                                      | Rollup       | computed                                                    | Through, Column, Show (11 functions)      | read-only               | More types         | yes                 | any         |
| trackerStatus                                                                                                                                                                               | Status       | computed/pinned (Study tracker only)                        | none (auto)                               | Status picker           | no                 | no                  | choice      |
| trackerLastRevised                                                                                                                                                                          | Last revised | computed                                                    | none                                      | read-only               | no                 | no                  | date        |
| trackerCardsDue                                                                                                                                                                             | Cards due    | computed                                                    | none                                      | read-only               | no                 | no                  | number      |
| trackerCorrect                                                                                                                                                                              | % correct    | computed                                                    | none                                      | read-only               | no                 | no                  | number      |
| trackerLevels                                                                                                                                                                               | Card levels  | computed                                                    | none                                      | read-only bar           | no                 | no                  | text        |
| (Tracker types are not in the registry; they are created only by `ensureStudyTracker`, databaseService.ts:138-184, and read via databaseQuery. Confidence is a normal number-stars column.) |

### View types (src/databases/views/viewTypes.ts:326-366)

| Type      | Icon | Label     | Component               | Config keys saved                       | In new database | In inline block switcher | Notes                                |
| --------- | ---- | --------- | ----------------------- | --------------------------------------- | --------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------ |
| table     | ▤    | Table     | TableView.tsx           | sorts, filters, hidden, widths, groupBy | yes (1st)       | yes                      | only view with filter/sort/hide UI   |
| dashboard | ◔    | Dashboard | DashboardView.tsx       | charts                                  | yes (2nd)       | yes                      | default opening view once rows exist |
| calendar  | ▦    | Calendar  | CalendarView.tsx (lazy) | dateColumnId, endColumnId, mode         | no              | yes                      | month/week/year, drag to move        |
| gallery   | ◫    | Gallery   | GalleryView.tsx         | imageColumnId, cardColumns              | no              | yes                      | no add-row button                    |
| board     | ▥    | Board     | BoardView.tsx           | groupBy, cardColumns                    | yes (3rd)       | yes                      | choice/status only                   |
| list      | ☰   | List      | ListView.tsx            | groupBy, sorts                          | no              | yes                      | not in the Tip map (VIEW_TIPS)       |

### Calendar modes

| Where                  | Day | Week | Month         | Year |
| ---------------------- | --- | ---- | ------------- | ---- |
| Database Calendar view | no  | yes  | yes (default) | yes  |
| Calendar screen (hub)  | yes | yes  | yes (default) | no   |

### Chart types

Number, Bars, Line, Donut, Progress bar, Calendar (heatmap); measures count/sum/average/min/max (donut, progress and heatmap: count and sum only); sizes small/medium/wide; line periods day/week/month; history presets (tracker): streak, correct, days.

| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------ | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| Enter / Esc                                                                                                        | inline cell editor                   | save / cancel                    | TableCells.tsx:75-84                              |
| Enter                                                                                                              | option picker search                 | pick first match or create       | TableCells.tsx:297-302                            |
| Esc                                                                                                                | any Popover                          | close, focus back to trigger     | Popover.tsx:38-43                                 |
| Enter                                                                                                              | column filter form                   | Apply                            | ColumnMenu.tsx:93-98                              |
| Enter                                                                                                              | new option field in column editor    | add option                       | ColumnDialog.tsx:535-540                          |
| Enter                                                                                                              | database title input                 | save name                        | DatabasesHub.tsx:206                              |

| Space, Up/Down, Space/Enter/Tab, Esc                                                                               | row grip (Table, List)               | pick up, move, drop, cancel      | useRowReorder.tsx:234-258                         |
| Space, Arrows (Left/Right = 1 day, Up/Down = 7 days), Space/Enter/Tab, Esc                                         | calendar chip                        | pick up, move date, drop, cancel | CalendarView.tsx:79-84, 325-349                   |

| Left / Right arrows                                                                                                | line chart (focused)                 | move active point                | ChartBody.tsx:482-510                             |
| ArrowUp / ArrowDown / Enter / Esc                                                                                  | database picker                      | move, choose, cancel             | DatabasePicker.tsx:125-136                        |
| Esc                                                                                                                | drag in calendar/rows                | cancel drag                      | useDragGesture.ts:160                             |
| Enter in dialogs                                                                                                   | Column / Chart dialogs (form submit) | save                             | ColumnDialog.tsx:383-388; ChartDialog.tsx:159-164 |
| (No global keyboard shortcut opens the hub, adds a row or switches views; Ctrl+K is global search, outside scope.) |

### Pen / Pencil / touch summary

- Drag helper treats pen like mouse (starts after a few px) and uses touch events for iPad Pencil (`touchType === 'stylus'`) where pointer events are cancelled (useDragGesture.ts:11-17, 204-231).
- Finger drag requires a hold: Board 350 ms (BoardView.tsx:66), Calendar 350 ms (useDragGesture.ts:35), Gallery 400 ms (GalleryView.tsx:429); moving before that scrolls.
- Calendar Day card swipe: touch and pen only, mouse ignored (CalendarDayCard.tsx:481).
- Row grip always visible (70%) on coarse pointers; hover-only on mouse (RowGrip.tsx:33).
- Hit-size boosts for coarse pointers exist only in the page Database tab chips and the sidebar items (`any-pointer-coarse:min-h-11`); other buttons are fixed min-h-8/9 (about 32-36 px, below 44 px) - e.g. Table header buttons (TableView.tsx:254), chip close buttons min-h-7 (:429), Calendar nav buttons min-h-8 (CalendarView.tsx:408), Board "⋯" min-h-8 (BoardView.tsx:545).
- Hover tips (`Tip` ... `touchInfo={false}`) are hidden for touch on most database controls.

---

## Surprising, duplicated or half-built

1. Unused i18n keys in databases.ts: `databases.newTitle`, `databases.newMessage` (dead "New database" dialog text), `table.autoLater` ("Filled in from your reviews (coming soon)."), `tracker.streak.best_one/best_other` partly (best uses `tracker.streak.best`). Several `column.type.*`, `chart.kind.*` etc. show as unused only because they are built dynamically (`t(\`...\${x}\`)`) - those are fine.
2. Docs vs code: docs/features/csv-import-export.md says the default mode is "add"; code defaults to "New database (recommended)" (CsvImportDialog.tsx:234) and the dialog intro (`csv.intro`) still describes add-only behaviour. docs/features/database-views.md lists List in database.md only; viewTypes registers `list` (viewTypes.ts:361). docs/features/databases.md says a rollup picks "Show" for the column and "Calculate" for the function; UI labels are "Through", "Column", "Show". docs/features/calendar-day.md says Year view is parked but it is built in the database Calendar view.
3. Two different components named `DatabaseTab` (src/pages/tabs/DatabaseTab.tsx for pages; src/notebooks/NotebookScreen.tsx:345 for notebooks) with different controls (scope chips vs page filter + database chips).
4. `config.match` ("any"/"all") and filter operator `isNot` are in the engine but have no UI. Board, Gallery and List honour saved sorts/filters but only Table (sort/filter/hide) and List (sort) write them, so a saved filter in a Table view cannot be edited from another view type.
5. `moveColumn` service exists with no UI; there is no way to reorder columns, rows cannot be deleted or duplicated from any database view, and there is no bulk selection - a redesign should decide whether to add these or keep parity.
6. The view-type Tip map (DatabaseView.tsx:251-257) lacks `list`; the add-view menu and inline block switcher both include List.
7. Folding behaviour inconsistency: Table "+ New row" edits the title in place; List "+ New row" opens the new row's page; Calendar "+" opens the page; Board per-column "+ New row" edits in place.
8. FormulaHelper inserts at the end of the formula, not the cursor (ColumnDialog.tsx:567).
9. Inline block `deleteView` has no last-view guard for block views by design (service :797-803) and the UI offers no rename/delete for a block's view.
10. Hub rows count loads every database's rows on each change (DatabasesHub.tsx:44-55) and has no loading/empty distinction while the live query is pending.
11. Study tracker detail: `isAutoColumn` omits the Confidence star column (columnLook.ts:540-546), so Confidence is editable and filterable like normal; its tip is attached via `headerTip` (trackerTips.ts:312-313).
12. Calendar chips on the global Calendar screen are not draggable and "+ N more" there is plain text (not clickable), while the database Calendar has a popover list (CalendarScreen.tsx:420-424 vs CalendarView.tsx:501-515).

---

# APPENDIX E (05-cards-templates-tags.md)

## Audit 05: Recall cards, Anki, Revise, Summary, Templates, Tags, Prompt library, Journal

Read-only inventory of the current code, written for the redesign. Paths are relative to the repo root. `file:line` was checked in the source.
English labels come from `src/i18n/en/*.ts` (and `src/i18n/en/late/*.ts`). Where a label key is quoted, the visible text is quoted next to it.

**General gating finding (checked with grep over all scoped folders):** none of these files reads an experience level, a "simple/advanced" mode or a device mode. The only responsive/touch conditions are: Tailwind `phone:` classes, `any-pointer-coarse:min-h-11` (bigger tap targets), `<Tip touchInfo={false}>` (help tip is not shown on touch), 44 px (`min-h-11`) buttons. Real gating that does exist is listed per surface (page is a Template page, notebook kind, AI settings, `onReview` available, etc.).

---

## PART 1. RECALL CARDS

Data model summary (for orientation, not a surface): a card is either a **Card block** (`type 'card'`: its own text is the question, the blocks nested inside it are the answer) or a **blank** (a text style `blank` whose value is an id; each blank id is its own card; `src/editor/blankActions.ts:15`, `src/editor/schema.tsx:126-135`). There is no other card type in the editor. Q/A, "fill in the blank" (cloze), highlight-cards (either of the two, plus `sourceAnnotationId`), vocabulary cards (Q/A), Anki imports (Q/A or cloze), AI cards (qa / cloze / truefalse / reverse, but those are written into the notes as the two real types, see Prompt section).

#### Card block (inline editor in a note)

- Opens from: typing "/card", Make card (toolbar, block menu, ribbon), Anki import, template starter, AI/skill save.
- Main file: `src/editor/schema.tsx:93-109`
- Features:
  - Question line: bold text editable inline, preceded by a small non-editable "Q" label (`card.question` = "Q", `src/editor/schema.tsx:100-103`, `src/i18n/en/recall.ts:6`).
  - Answer: any blocks nested (indented) under the card: paragraph, lists, images, etc. (`cardActions.tsx:8-10`). `card.answer` = "A" label key exists (`recall.ts:3`) but is not used in `schema.tsx` (the A label is not drawn in the editor; CSS in `index.css`, not checked). Possible dead key.
  - Block type id `card`, label "Card" in the block catalogue (`src/blocks/builtinBlockTypes.ts:67`).
- Gating: an empty Card (no question) is saved but left out of reviews and lists (`docs/features/recall-cards.md` Edge cases; `hasQuestion` in `src/recall/cardService.ts`). Template pages have no cards/Summary buttons (`summaryActions.tsx:325`, `reviewCards` unset).
- Empty/error: empty-question cards can be found and removed in Search (see "Empty-card cleanup").
- Keyboard/Pencil: Ctrl+Z undoes a conversion in one step (`cardActions.tsx:8-11`).

#### Selection toolbar (floating bar over selected text) (bar)

- Opens from: selecting text (also appears on touch, `SelectionToolbar.tsx:22-26`).
- Main file: `src/editor/SelectionToolbar.tsx:112-125`
- Features (order as rendered, lines 114-124):
  - BlockNote's own formatting buttons (`getFormattingToolbarItems`, line 115).
  - "Edit picture" (only when one picture block is selected) `pic.edit`, `SelectionToolbar.tsx:70-88`.
  - One button per Revise label: Key point, Keyword, Must know, Formula, plus any user labels (names from `markName`), pressed state when active; pressing again removes (`:91-109`, `markActions.ts:11-18`).
  - **Blank** button (`blank.button` = "Blank"; tooltip "Make blank (hide these words in review)" or, when inside a blank, "Remove blank"; `:30-50`, `library.ts:3-5`). Toggles `toggleBlank` (selection snapped to whole words, one blank id per selection; touching an existing blank removes every blank touched; across several blocks does nothing; `blankActions.ts:25-95`).
  - **Card** button (`card.button` = "Card", tooltip "Make card"; `:52-67`). Hidden when the cursor block cannot become a card (`canMakeCard`).
  - **Summary** button (`summary.addButton` = "Summary", tooltip "Add to Summary"; `summaryActions.tsx:320-340`). Hidden on pages without `reviewCards` (template pages).
- Gating: Card button only for block types paragraph, heading, bulletListItem, numberedListItem, checkListItem, quote, callout (`cardActions.tsx:17-28`). Code blocks etc. are refused (`makeCard` returns false).
- Keyboard/Pencil: works with touch; mark/blank buttons call `editor.focus()` after.

#### Edit bar / docked bar buttons (bar)

- Opens from: the Edit bar panel (customisable list of buttons), also the iPad "short bar".
- Main file: `src/editor/barActions.tsx:85-175`
- Features (ids in bar order, `allActionIds` lines 93-107): text styles, one per Revise label (`markActionId`, `:97` area), `blank` (label "Make blank..." / "Remove blank", face "Blank", shows active state, `:149-157`), `card` (label and face "Make card", disabled when `!canMakeCard(block)`, `:158-164`), `newLine`, alignment, colour, indent, outdent, link.
- Same actions exist as ribbon commands: `blank` and `card` in `src/app/tools/editorCommands.ts:140-148` (card is a no-op if the block is not convertible); ribbon labels `tool.blank` "Make blank", `tool.card` "Make card" (`toolbar.ts:276,283`); registry: `card` under Insert › study group with a second placement in the Review tab (`toolRegistry.ts:436-442`).

#### "/" slash menu: Card and Card list (menu items)

- Opens from: typing "/" in a note.
- Main file: `src/editor/cardActions.tsx:102-137` (Card), `src/editor/slashMenu.tsx:84-95` (both)
- Features:
  - **Card**: title "Card", subtext "Question with a hidden answer, for review", group "Recall", aliases card / flashcard / question / recall / anki, icon 🃏 (`cardActions.tsx:106-111`, `recall.ts:7-9`). Inserts an empty Card with an empty answer line and puts the cursor in the question (`insertCardHere`, `:67-75`). Lazy-loads BlockNote helper.
  - Inside another card's answer it asks where the new card goes (see next surface). If the line was a typed "/card" empty paragraph and "next line" is chosen, the empty line is removed (`insertCardAfter` `:81-95`).
  - **Card list**: title "Card list", subtext "Live list of the cards on this page", aliases cards / list / flashcards / deck, group Recall (`slashMenu.tsx:85-93`, `recall.ts:17-18`). Inserts a `cardList` block (prop `includeSubpages`, default false; `schema.tsx:112-122`).
- Gating: `onPlaceNestedCard` only passed from PageEditor (`PageEditor.tsx:958`); "Insert template…" and others are in the same menu but out of this section.

#### Nested card placement dialog (choice dialog)

- Opens from: "/card" typed inside a card's answer, when the setting is "Ask every time".
- Main file: `src/editor/PageEditor.tsx:640-660`
- Features:
  - Title "New card inside an answer", message "Put the card inside this answer, or on the next line below the card?" (`recall.ts:154-158`).
  - Options: "Inside this answer" and "Next line" (primary). Carries a remember-this checkbox through `settingId: nestedCardPlacement.id` (`PageEditor.tsx:658`; the "remember" UI itself lives in the generic `choose` dialog, not read).
  - Cancel returns null, nothing inserted (`:659-660`).
- Gating: setting `review.nestedCard` (synced) with options "Ask every time" / "Always next line" / "Always inside" (`builtinSettings.ts:303-318`, `settings.ts:147-150`; label "New card inside an answer"). Default `ask`.

#### Block (⋮⋮) menu (context menu on the drag handle)

- Opens from: hover / tap the block's drag handle (⋮⋮).
- Main file: `src/editor/CardMenu.tsx:189-203`
- Features, in rendered order:
  - "Edit picture" and "Draw on picture (arrow)": only for image blocks (`:152-186`, `media.ts:128-129`). Draw sets pen to arrow and selects the Pen tool.
  - **Make card** (`card.make`): only for convertible blocks (`:30-46`).
  - **Add to Summary** (`summary.addMenu`): only if the page has `reviewCards` and the block has text content (`summaryActions.tsx:343-361`).
  - **Duplicate** (`block.duplicate`) the block with everything nested (`:49-65`).
  - **Copy** (`copy.all`) and submenu **Copy as** (`copy.as`) with Markdown / Rich text / Plain text / HTML (`copy.as.markdown|rich|plain|html`; `:68-126`). Keys exist in `importExport.ts` (not re-checked for exact English).
  - **Mark line: <label>** for each Revise label (`revise.markLine` "Mark line: {label}"; `:129-150`): marks or unmarks the whole line; hidden for blocks with no inline content.
  - BlockNote's Delete (`dict.drag_handle.delete_menuitem`) and Colors (`:199-200`).
- Gating: items hide themselves when not applicable (see above). Hover-only menu; touch users use the selection toolbar (comment `SelectionToolbar.tsx:24`).

#### Card list block (inline live list)

- Opens from: "/Card list" in a note; also inside starter templates ("Lesson" templates).
- Main file: `src/recall/CardListView.tsx:17-93`
- Features:
  - Header: 🃏 + title "Cards on this page" or, when `includeSubpages`, "Cards on this page and its sub-pages" + count ("1 card"/"N cards") (`:40-43`, `recall.ts:10-11,22-23`).
  - Button **Review these cards** (`cardList.review`), only when the page has `reviewCards` and there is at least one card (`:44-52`). Runs `app.reviewCards(includeSubpages)`.
  - Row per card: question text (blank cards show "..." in place of the blank), optional page title for sub-page cards (max 35% width), status label (`:63-79`). Clicking a row calls `openBlock(pageId, blockId)` which scrolls to the card.
  - **Show all N cards** after the first 100 rows (`FIRST_ROWS`, `:11,82-90`).
- Gating: non-editable block (`contentEditable={false}`, `:36`); reads live and refreshes every minute (`useMinuteClock`).
- Empty: "No cards yet. Type "/card", or select words and choose Blank. They appear here as you make them." with a cards icon (`:54-60`, `recall.ts:12-13`). Loading: blank 4rem spacer (`:30`).
- Status label per row (`CardStatusLabel.tsx:10-28`): "Due" (accent, medium), "New", "Suspended", or for later cards the formatted due time ("in 10 minutes", "tomorrow", "in 3 days", then short date; `cardList.ts:118-140`).
- Keyboard/Pencil: rows are 40 px buttons; no special keys.

#### Notebook "Cards" tab (tab)

- Opens from: notebook tab bar (default tab set: notes, summary, database, cards, revise; `src/databases/notebookTabs.ts:20-31`). Tab can be renamed or hidden from the tab menu (`NotebookScreen.tsx:122-142`). Router has `{kind:'cards'}` (`router.ts:16`).
- Main file: `src/recall/CardsTab.tsx:39-209`, mounted at `src/notebooks/NotebookScreen.tsx:294-300`
- Features:
  - Heading with count ("1 card"/"N cards") and caption "from this notebook's notes" (or "from all your notebooks") (`:100-105`, `recall.ts:26-29`).
  - **Sort: <name> ▾** button, opens the sort menu (below). Persisted per device (`usePref('cardsTabSort')`, default `best`; `:51`). Wrapped in help Tip `cards.sort`.
  - **Review N due** / **Review 1 due** or **Practice** when nothing is due (`cards.review`, `cards.practice`; `:152-160`). Calls `onReview(scope)` with scope notebook or all. Wrapped in Tip `cards.review`.
  - List of result rows (same row component as Search, `ResultRow`, with `plain` styling; `chip` only in "All cards" which shows notebook › page; `:164-182`). Row shows question text, location, red "failed N times" count (`search.fails`) and the status label (`SearchResults.tsx:140-155`); empty cards show the italic "Card with no question" and "Empty" (`:118-148`).
  - **Show more** (`search.more`) adds 50 rows at a time (`PAGE_SIZE = 50`, `:22,183-191`).
  - Footnote: "Cards stay inside your notes; this tab gathers them. Search › Cards can also filter by tag and status." (`:192`, `recall.ts:32-33`).
- All-notebooks-only controls (when `notebookId === null`): **Search cards…** text box (`cards.search`, `:108-118`) and a **Notebook** select ("All notebooks" + every normal notebook; templates/databases/journal kinds excluded because `!n.kind`; `:119-134`). A deleted notebook silently stops narrowing (`:67-70`).
- Query: type card, filters default with `templates:false` (template pages never count; `:71-80`).
- Gating: sort + review buttons only appear when there is at least one card (`:137`).
- Empty: "No cards yet. Turn a line of a note into a card (select text › Card), and it shows up here." (`:164-165`, `recall.ts:30-31`). Loading: "notebook.loading" text (`:94-96`).

- Opens from: the "Sort: ... ▾" button (`CardsTab.tsx:144`).

#### "All cards" screen (whole-app Study hub, page)

#### Review home (decks) (section; brief, belongs to the approved Review mock-up)

- Opens from: top of "All cards" › Review.
- Main file: `src/recall/ReviewHome.tsx:23-201`
- Surfaces / features: heading "Decks" (`:50-53`); **New deck** button (`decks.newDeck`, `:55-62`); tiles grid 1/2/3 columns: built-in "All cards" tile + one tile per deck (colour bar on the start edge) (`:65-86`). Each tile (`DeckTile` `:203-284`): name, ✎ edit (aria "Edit {name}") and trash delete (aria "Delete {name}") (not on All cards), three counts New · Learning · Due (`decks.state.new|learning|due`), **Study** button (disabled when all counts are 0; aria "Study {name}"). Empty: "No decks yet. A deck is a saved filter: pick where the cards are and which ones you want. It refills by itself." with action `empty.decks.action` (`:87-96`). **Saved searches** list with **Make deck** per search, and one-time offer "Turn your saved searches into decks?" with **Make decks** / **Not now** (stored in pref `decksFromSearches.done`; `:98-150`). **Paused** list with hint "Paused notebooks and pages stay out of reviews. Nothing is lost." and **Resume** per item (`:152-177`). Delete deck confirm "Delete the deck "{name}"? Your notes and cards stay as they are." with button "Delete deck" (`:187-198`).
