#### New deck / Edit deck dialog (modal dialog, 3 steps)

- Opens from: New deck button, ✎ on a deck tile, "Make deck" on a saved search (prefills via `deckDraftFromSearch`, `deckFromSearch.ts:11-27`), empty-state action.
- Main file: `src/recall/DeckEditor.tsx:29-368`
- Frame: native `<dialog>` showModal, width min(30rem, 100vw-2rem), max-height min(40rem, 100dvh-2rem) (`:147-154`). Backdrop click and Esc close (`:150-153`). Title "New deck" or "Edit deck" (`:156-158`). Step strip "1. Where 2. Which cards 3. Name" with `aria-current=step` (`:159-171`; display only, not clickable). Live line "N cards match" / "1 card matches" (`data-deck-match`, aria-live polite; `:337-339`, `recall.ts:98-99`). Footer: Cancel (step 1) / Back, then Next, then **Create deck** (new) or **Save** (edit) on step 3 (`:340-365`).
- **Step 1 "Where"** (`:174-203`): hint "Tick a notebook to use everything in it, or open it (▸) and tick single pages. Nothing ticked means all notebooks."; list of every live notebook except templates and databases kinds (journal and others included) (`:62-71`) each with a ▸/▾ expander (aria "Pages of {name}") and a checkbox; open notebook shows its page tree with indented checkboxes (`NotebookRow` `:371-444`); checkbox **Include sub-pages of ticked pages** (default on, `:193-201`).
- **Step 2 "Which cards"** (`:205-300`):
  - Fieldset **Tags**: one toggle chip per tag with full path "medicine/cardio" (`tagPath` `:78-88`), or "You have no tags yet. Every card can join." (`:211-213`). When more than one tag is ticked, a radio pair **Any of them / All of them** (`tagMode`, `:235-258`).
  - Fieldset **Cards to include**: toggles **All / New / Learning / Due**; "All" is exclusive, unticking the last one falls back to All (`STATES`, `toggleState` `:121-126`, `:260-284`).
  - Checkbox **Walk the route**, hint "Review the cards in the order they stand in the notes, not by due date." (`routeOrder`, `:285-298`).
- **Step 3 "Name"** (`:302-334`): text box **Deck name** (max 100 chars; empty saves as "My deck", `:129-131`); radio group **Colour** with 7 swatches #2f6fed, #1f9d6b, #c9821a, #d6455d, #8a56d6, #0f9bb0, #6b7280 (`:19-27,313-332`; aria-label is the hex code, not a colour name, a11y gap).
- Gating: none by level. The deck uses scope notebookIds, pageIds, withSubpages, tagIds, tagMode, states, routeOrder (`:90-103,129-139`).
- Touch: all targets 44 px (`min-h-11`, `size-11`).

#### Pause / Resume (menu entry)

- Opens from: notebook ⋯ menu (`NotebookScreen.tsx:149-155`) and page ⋯ menu (`PageView.tsx:340`, shown only when the page is in a notebook; condition at that line).
- Main file: `src/app/pauseMenu.ts:12-33`
- Features: item "Pause reviews" (icon ⏸) or "Resume" (▶) (`pause.pause`, `pause.resume`). Toasts: ""{name}" is paused. Find it under Decks › Paused." / ""{name}" is back in your reviews." (`recall.ts:159-162`). Pausing a page also pauses its sub-pages by ancestry (`pauseService.ts:103-150`); state kept in `paused` flag + `meta` row `paused.ids` (`:14,46-65`). A single card uses Suspend instead (Review screen).

#### Review screen (page; list only, mock-up already approved)

- Opens from: Study on a deck tile, Review N due, Practice, Alt+R, Review button in Card list, page ⋯ › Review.
- Main file: `src/recall/ReviewScreen.tsx` (not read in depth).
- Surfaces found by grep: header with title (Review / Review · {name} / Cards I got wrong; `:214-224`), "N left" (`:240`), scope switch "Which cards": All due / Got wrong (7 days) (`:251-260`, `recall.ts:206-208`), close ✕ "Close review" (`:278`), "Practice: this card's schedule stays the same." banner (`:293`), **Open note** (`:308`), **Suspend** (`:313`, toast "Card suspended. It won't come up in reviews." with Undo, `:163-164`), source line (`SourceLine`, `:297`), **Show answer** (`:360`), four ratings Again / Hard / Good / Easy with next-interval preview (`:370`, `:419-421`, `interval.*`), "Hidden words" blank spans (`:438,552`), "This card has no answer yet." (`:451`), answer pictures (`:455`), "From "{page}"" (`:470`), waiting state "Done for now - N cards come back in {time}." (`:321`), empty states (No cards here yet / No cards are due here right now / No cards marked Again in the last 7 days; `:335-338`), done screen "All caught up." + "You reviewed N cards." + **Practice anyway** + **Back to notes** (`:592-625`).
- Keyboard: Esc closes (`:188`), Space or Enter reveals the answer unless a button is focused (`:193-194`), keys 1 to 4 rate (Again, Hard, Good, Easy) (`:199`), ignored while typing in inputs and while the phone menu covers the screen (`:46,187`).

#### Source line ("From: Book › Chapter") (inline link)

- Opens from: under a card made from a reader highlight, in the Review screen.
- Main file: `src/recall/SourceLine.tsx:9-40`, used at `ReviewScreen.tsx:297`
- Features: one button "From: {Book title} › {chapter}" (`recall.from`), 44 px, opens the reader at the highlight with the landing glow (`onOpen(annotationId)`, `:30-37`). Hidden if the highlight was deleted (`:20,24`).

#### Cards from highlights, Daily highlights, Vocabulary (reader features that make cards; brief)

- `makeCardsFromAnnotations` (`src/recall/cardFromAnnotation.ts:82-116`): per highlight, a Q/A Card when the highlight has a note (note = question, quote = answer, `:56-61`), else a fill-in-the-blank paragraph with the 1 (or 2 if the sentence has more than 14 words) longest words of 5+ letters that are not stop words hidden (`keyTerms` `:28-40`, `clozeRuns` `:60-88`), else (too short to hide a word) a Q/A card whose question is the first 4 words + " ..." and answer is the quote (`:64-69`). Quotes cut to about 300 characters at a sentence end (`cardText` `:44-50`). Highlights that already have a card are skipped. Links card ⇄ highlight via `sourceAnnotationId` / `Annotation.cardIds` (`:101-110`). `undoMakeCards` soft-deletes the blocks and frees the highlights (`:119-128`).
- Where the UI lives: "Make cards from all highlights" (`recall.cardsFromAll`) in `src/reader/annotations/AnnotationsPanel.tsx:242`; bulk dialog "Cards from highlights" with help "Untick the ones you do not want. Each becomes a card linked to its highlight." and button "Make N cards" (`BulkCardsDialog.tsx:27-89`, `recall.ts:163-170`); toasts "Made N cards." with Undo (`BookReader.tsx:461-464`), "Nothing to make: every highlight already has a card." (`:462`); daily tile "Highlights for today" with Seen / "Save to notes" / "All caught up for today." (`DailyHighlights.tsx`, `recall.ts:173-178`); selection popup "Add to vocabulary" (`SelectionPopup.tsx:158`, `recall.vocabAdd`) toast "Added "{word}" to your vocabulary." (`BookReader.tsx:1539-1545`).
- Vocabulary (`src/recall/vocabulary.ts:33-60`): finds or creates a top-level page named "Vocabulary" in the book's notebook, appends a Card (question = word up to 60 chars trimmed of punctuation, answer = context sentence up to 240 chars).

#### Empty-card cleanup (Search › Card status › Empty)

- Opens from: Search screen, status filter "Empty (no question)" (`SearchScreen.tsx:259-276`, `search.status.empty`).
- Main file: `src/recall/emptyCards.ts:28-82` + `src/search/SearchScreen.tsx:128-133,320-328`
- Features: button "Remove N empty cards" (`search.removeEmpty`, shown only when status = Empty and there are empty ids); toast "Removed N empty cards" with **Undo** (`review.undo`) (`search.removedEmpty`). Only the Card block is soft-deleted; answer lines move up to where the card was (kept as notes). Cards that got a question meanwhile are skipped. One transaction (`emptyCards.ts:32-81`); Undo `restoreEmptyCards` (`:85-95`).

#### Ribbon / tool entries for cards (command registry)

- `toolRegistry.ts`: `card` (Insert, study group; also on Review tab) `:436-442`; `studyNow` "Study now" shortcut Alt+R, opens review (page scope if a page is open, else all) `:597-603`; `dueToday` "Due today" opens all-due review `:605-610`; `allCards` "All cards" `:611-617`; `cardStats` "Card stats" which actually calls `openNotebookTab('database')` (opens the notebook study-tracker tab, not a card-stats screen) `:619-626`; `summary` (below).

## PART 2. ANKI IMPORT / EXPORT

#### Data screen › Anki section (card on the Data page)

- Opens from: Data screen (`DataScreen.tsx`), reached by ribbon File › "Anki import" (`toolRegistry.ts:133-139`, `tool.ankiImport` = "Anki import", runs `app.openData()`), plus other entry points to Data (not scoped).
- Main file: `src/share/DataScreen.tsx:611-642`
- Features:
  - Heading "Anki" and text "Cards start as new. Schedules, pictures and sounds stay behind." (`data.anki.title`, `data.anki.text`; `importExport.ts:146-148`).
  - **Import from Anki** file button (hint "Pick an .apkg file"; accepts `.apkg,application/octet-stream`; wrapped in Tip `data.ankiImport`) (`:622-630`).
  - Notebook select, aria-label "Notebook to save for Anki" (`data.anki.which`; the same shared `notebookPicker` used by other export blocks, lists all notebooks; `:336-349,631`).
  - **Save for Anki** button (`data.anki.export`), disabled with no notebook or while busy (`:632-640`).
  - Status line (role=status): "Reading the Anki file…" then "Importing… {done} of {total} notes" (`:263-269,287`, `importExport.ts:143,151`).
- Import behaviour (`DataScreen.tsx:264-304`, `src/anki/readApkg.ts`, `importApkg.ts`):
  - File > 400 MB refused: "This Anki file is too big to import." (`:267-268`; also unzipped collection limit `readApkg.ts:50`).
  - Reads `collection.anki21b` (zstd), `collection.anki21`, `collection.anki2` (`readApkg.ts:23`), deck names from `decks` table or old JSON (`:128-145`), note's deck = deck of its first card (`:147-153`), note kind: cloze if the first field has `{{c..::` else basic (`:162`).
  - Makes **one new notebook** named after the file; one page per deck, decks of over 300 notes split into pages "Deck (1)", "Deck (2)" (`importApkg.ts:19,98-113`); sub-decks "A::B" become title "A › B" (`:11-16`); no deck table gives deck "Imported".
  - **Basic** note: first field → Card question (multi-line joined with spaces), other fields → answer lines (`:44-55`). **Cloze** note: each cloze line becomes a paragraph with blanks (`:36-43`). Every block gets `source: 'anki'` (`:126`). Cards start New; schedules, pictures, sounds, review history are not imported (`anki.md`).
  - Only text + bold/italic/underline kept from Anki HTML (`ankiHtml.ts`).
  - Result toast: "Imported {cards} cards from Anki into the new notebook "{name}"." + optionally " {n} notes had nothing on the front and were left out." + " {n} pictures or sounds in the file were not imported." (`:290-300`, `importExport.ts:135-145`).
  - Errors (shown through `BundleError`): "This is not an Anki package (.apkg).", "This Anki package has no collection in it.", "The collection in this Anki package is damaged.", "This Anki file is too big to import.", "This package was saved by an old Anki as a placeholder. Export it again from Anki with "Support older Anki versions" ticked off." (`importExport.ts:3-10`; thrown `readApkg.ts:50,59,62,76-82,113,120,169`).
- Export behaviour (`exportApkg.ts:268-399`): builds an old-format .apkg every Anki can import; each page becomes a deck named "Notebook::Page::Subpage" (`:279-292`); Card blocks → "Basic" (front = question, back = answer lines joined with `<br>`, `:103-111`); lines with blanks → "Cloze" notes with `{{cN::...}}` (`:113-121`); every card arrives New. Fails with "This notebook has no cards or blanks to export." (`:298`) or "That notebook no longer exists." (`:277`). Success toast "Saved {cards} cards as {name}." (`DataScreen.tsx:321-323`) and a downloaded file.
- Gating: buttons disabled while another Data task is busy (`busy`). No experience gating. Both libraries (fflate, fzstd, sql.js) are lazy (`DataScreen.tsx:270-273`, `anki.md`).
- Surprising: truefalse/reverse AI cards and picture answers are not exported as such (pictures dropped); only bold/italic/underline text.

## PART 3. REVISE

#### Notebook "Revise" tab (tab)

- Opens from: notebook tab bar (`NotebookScreen.tsx:285-293`, tab kind `revise`; default in `defaultNotebookTabs`, `notebookTabs.ts:29`). Address: second path level is the open lesson (`useRouteParts(1)`, `ReviseTab.tsx:42`).
- Main file: `src/revise/ReviseTab.tsx:23-161`
- Features:
  - One-line help (HelpLine `revise.how`): "In a note, select words and press Key point, Keyword, Must know or Formula in the toolbar. Marked words collect here, per lesson." (`:53`, `late/help.ts:31-32`).
  - **Lesson list** (`aria-label "Lessons"`): horizontal scrolling row on phone, 14rem sidebar column on md+; each lesson is a pressed-state button with name and mark count (`:55-78`). Lessons are the notebook's live top-level pages that are not database rows (`reviseService.ts:373-401`). Click pushes the route and keeps back-stack (`:64-66`).
  - Lesson title (h2) and two buttons: **Open lesson** (`revise.openLesson`) and **Practise this lesson** (`revise.practice`; reviews the lesson page including sub-pages; Tip `revise.practice`) (`:80-107`).
  - **My summary** textarea ("Write the lesson in your own words…", 3 rows, max 20,000 chars, saved on blur; `Summary` component `:164-192`, `reviseService.ts:404-418`). Same field as the Summary view's own paragraph (see duplicates).
  - **Mark groups**: one section per label in Settings order, with colour square, label name and count; marks of deleted labels appear under "Other" (`:115-151`, `reviseMarks.ts:340-354`, `revise.label.other`). Each mark is a full-width button: opens the lesson page and reveals the block (`:136-146`). Adjacent runs with the same label merge into one mark; empty marks dropped (`reviseMarks.ts:313-337`).
  - Footer legend: all label names joined with " · " (`:152-156`).
- Built-in labels: "Key point", "Keyword", "Must know", "Formula" (`recall.ts:226-230`); IDs key/keyword/must/formula, colours #d9822b #2f6feb #c8402f #2e9e6b (`reviseMarks.ts:206-235`). Up to 24 labels (`MAX_LABELS`, `:237`).
- Gating: none by level. Sub-page marks roll up to the top-level lesson.
- Empty: "No lessons yet. Add pages to this notebook first." (`:45-47`); no marks: "Nothing marked in this lesson yet." (`:116`); loading "notebook.loading" (`:43-44`).
- Keyboard/touch: lesson buttons 36 px (`min-h-9`), mark buttons text-start; no shortcuts.

#### Revise labels editor (Settings › Review › "Revise labels")

- Opens from: Settings screen, category Review (`builtinSettings.ts:127-137`).
- Main file: `src/settings/SettingsScreen.tsx:641-724` (`LabelsControl`)
- Features: row per label with name text box (placeholder = built-in name; aria "Name of label {n}"; max 40; saved on blur or Enter, `:672-689`), 6 colour dots (#d9822b #2f6feb #c8402f #2e9e6b #8a5cf6 #c2417f; aria uses names Orange/Blue/Red/Green/Purple/Pink, `:690-712`), **×** remove (aria "Remove label {name}") only for non-built-in labels (`:713-725`), **+ Add label** (creates "New label" with next colour, up to 24; `:728-746`). Built-in four cannot be deleted (`isLabelList` `reviseMarks.ts:241-260`). Applied app-wide through `setMarkLabels` (colours via a generated style element, `reviseMarks.ts:272-284`, `App.tsx:719`).

#### Revise marks in the editor

- Selection toolbar: one button per label (see Selection toolbar). Block menu: "Mark line: <label>" per label (see Block menu). Edit bar: one action per label (`barActions.tsx:97` area, `markActionId`). Mark stored as text style `mark` = label id (`reviseMarks.ts:203`). Re-pressing the same label removes it; a different label replaces the old (`markActions.ts:11-18`). Whole line: if all text runs already have the label it unmarks (`:24-43`).

## PART 4. SUMMARY

#### "Add to Summary" actions (button / menu item / ribbon tool)

- Opens from: selection toolbar **Summary** button (`summaryActions.tsx:320-340`), block menu **Add to Summary** (`:343-361`), ribbon Insert › Summary (`toolRegistry.ts:443-457`, `tool.summary` = "Summary"), and the iPad short-bar pop-up (doc `summary.md`; not found separately in code, uses the same selection toolbar component, `SelectionToolbar.tsx:122`).
- Main file: `src/editor/addToSummary.ts:19-46`
- Behaviour: adds every text block touched by the selection (or the cursor block / the given block) as a **linked** line to this page's Summary via `addBlockToSummary` (`:26-40`). Toast results, in priority: "Added to the Summary" (any added) > "This Summary has 500 lines. Remove some first." > "This line is already in the Summary" > "There is no text on this line to add" (`:41-44`, `recall.ts:253,258,261,271`).
- Gating: toolbar and block-menu entries need `EditorContext.reviewCards` (absent on template pages) (`summaryActions.tsx:325,352`). Ribbon tool: with a page open and an editor it adds lines; otherwise (no page) it opens the notebook's Summary tab (`toolRegistry.ts:451-456`).
- Limits: 500 lines per page, lines cut at 2,000 chars (`summary.md`).

#### Page view segments: Notes · Summary · Review (+ optional extra tabs) (segmented control)

- Opens from: under the page title, on any normal page (`PageView.tsx:594-609`, condition `withTabs`).
- Main file: `src/app/summary/PageTabs.tsx:156-227`
- Features: group labelled "Page views"; buttons **Notes**, **Summary**, **Review** (+ extras the notebook switched on: Mind map, Slides, Audio & video, Database; `notebooks/pageTabs.ts:16-21`), pressed state, 36 px / 44 px on coarse pointers (`:187-198`). **⋯** "Tabs" menu button (only when `onToggleExtra` is provided, i.e. page is in a notebook) opening a checked-item menu titled "Choose which tabs this notebook shows on its pages" to switch each extra tab on/off for the whole notebook (`:200-224`, `sidebar.ts(late):33-34`). Notes editor stays mounted (hidden) so nothing typed is lost (`PageView.tsx:586,680`). A switched-off extra tab falls back to Notes (`PageView.tsx:211-212`).
- Also used on the All cards screen with only the three base tabs (`AllCardsScreen.tsx:54-56`).

#### Page Summary view (tab content)

- Opens from: Summary segment on a page (`PageView.tsx:610-621`).
- Main file: `src/app/summary/SummaryView.tsx:30-84` (scope `{kind:'page'}`)
- Features:
  - **In your own words** textarea (`summary.ownLabel`): the page's own paragraph = `Page.summary`, 2 rows growing, max 20,000, saves on blur (`OwnParagraph` `:236-268`); editable only on the open page; other pages' paragraphs are read-only text (`:124-132`).
  - **Lines list** (aria "Summary lines"): bullet dot + text. Linked line shows the note's live text; if the note was deleted it shows the stored text dimmed with "(note removed)" (`summary.gone`, `:174-182`). Own line is an inline auto-growing textarea (max `MAX_LINE_LENGTH`), saved on blur, an emptied line reverts (`:183-196`). Optional link under a line (`item.href`, label `item.where`), `https://` opens a new tab, `#/` in-app (`:197-207`; used by highlights saved with "Save to Summary").
  - **↗ Go to the note** button on linked, non-missing lines (`summary.goto`, Tip) `:209-220`; if the line is on this page the view switches to Notes first (`PageView.tsx:614-618`).
  - **× Remove from the Summary** on every line (`summary.remove`, Tip) `:221-230`.
  - Page headings (button "Open page: {title}") appear when lines come from several pages (this page and its sub-pages, grouped) (`:112-123`, `:75`).
  - **Add a line** form: text box "Add a key point…" + **Add** button (disabled when blank; Enter submits) (`AddLine` `:270-302`).
- Empty: "Nothing here yet. In your notes, select a line and press Summary, or add a line below." (`:64-68`, `recall.ts:256-257`).

#### Page Review view (tab content)

- Opens from: Review segment on a page (`PageView.tsx:623-631`).
- Main file: `src/app/summary/PageTabs.tsx:229-262` (`PageReview`)

You can still practise it, or select a line in your notes and press Card." when both zero (`:248-250`), **Practise** button (page + sub-pages scope; note `PageView` ignores the scope and calls `onReview(true)`, `PageView.tsx:627`) (`:251-259`). Also shows page properties for database rows (`RowProperties where="review"`, `PageView.tsx:624`).

## PART 5. TEMPLATES

#### New-page start list (inline chooser under the cursor)

- Opens from: automatically under a brand-new, still-blank page (`PageView.tsx:685-701`).
- Main file: `src/templates/NewPageStart.tsx:31-206`
- Gating (all must hold, `PageView.tsx:685-692`): page just made by the user (`autoFocusTitle`) or `offerStart` (first page of a new notebook, `App.tsx:1787`); Notes tab active; not Reading mode; not a template page; not a database row; notebook exists. Inside the component: page is still blank (`isPageBlank` live), templates loaded, not dismissed this session, and `general.newPageChooser` is not "off" (`NewPageStart.tsx:63`).
- Features:
  - Heading text "Start with a template, or just begin typing" (`newPage.title`).
  - **Paper style** group: Blank / Lined / Grid / Dots toggle buttons (pressed = current page ink background; `PAPER_STYLES` `paperStyles.ts:4-9`; `setPageInkBg`) (`:90-111`). Labels `ink.paper.blank|lined|grid|dots`.
  - Row **Blank page** with help "Start from nothing": closes the list only (`:113-125`).
  - Row **Study (Recommended)** + preview of its first headings: fills the page from the "Study notes" starter (only if that template still exists with that exact title; `getStudyTemplate` `templateService.ts:239-245`) (`:126-140`).
  - One row per other template (not the Daily journal) with title and heading preview (`:141-156`).
  - Row **Database page** "a table of rows that are pages" (only when `onMakeDatabase` is passed) (`:157-171`).
  - Checkbox **Always start new pages as Study** (only when Study template exists; synced setting `general.newPageStudy`) (`:173-192`, Tip `newPage.always`).
  - Link-button **Don't show this again**: sets `general.newPageChooser` off and toasts "Template list hidden. Turn it back on in Settings › General." (`:193-203`; `sidebar.ts late:14-16`).
- Behaviour: choosing a template runs `applyTemplateToPage` in one transaction: refuses if the page now has writing (toast "The page already has writing, so nothing was added."), soft-deletes the empty lines, copies blocks/cards/sub-pages (`templateService.ts:263-286`).
- Settings that control it: "Start new pages as Study" (`settings.newPageStudy.label`, default off) and "Template list on new pages" (`settings.newPageChooser.label`, default on) (`settings.ts:157`, `late/settings.ts:75-79`).
- Touch: every control min 44 px.

#### Template picker (modal dialog)

- Opens from: sidebar page-add menu "From template", and "/ Insert template…" (via `chooseTemplate(mode)`, `App.tsx:872`, `Sidebar.tsx:720-731`, `PageEditor.tsx:620`).
- Main file: `src/templates/TemplatePicker.tsx:224-299` (lazy in App, `App.tsx:1921-1932`)
- Features: modal `<dialog>`; title/help differ by mode: "New page" / "Start blank, or from one of your templates." vs "Insert a template" / "Its blocks are added where your cursor is." (`library.ts:121-124`). Grid of cards (min 170 px) each with title and heading preview (or "Empty template"). In `new` mode first card is **Blank page** ("Start from nothing"). Cancel button ("ui.cancel"). Esc and backdrop click both cancel (`onCancel` prevents default and calls `onDone(null)`, `:261-267`).
- Behaviour on insert: copies the template's blocks at the cursor, skipping `pageLink` blocks; replaces the current line if it is an empty paragraph (`PageEditor.tsx:620-632`).

- Items: **Blank page** (icon fileText) and **From template** (icon clipboard; `sidebar.fromTemplate`) which opens the picker; cancel leaves nothing created.

- Opens from: Notebooks space sidebar (`Sidebar.tsx:680-689`), collapsed by default (`defaultOpen={false}`, `TemplatesSection.tsx:363`).

- Features:
  - Header "Templates" with + button "New template" (Tip `sidebar.newTemplate`) (`:359-372`).
  - Row per template: ▸/▾ expander only when it has sub-pages (`:387-396`), icon ☀ for the Daily journal or 📋, name (or "Untitled template") that opens it for editing (`:399-410`). Daily journal row has a help tip `sidebar.dailyTemplate`.
  - **×** "Delete template" appears on hover/focus, hidden for the Daily journal (protected) (`:411-421`, Tip `sidebar.deleteTemplate`). Delete confirm lives in App (`requestTemplateDelete`).
  - Sub-pages of a template fold out as a page tree (`:423-433`).
  - **🧠 Memory helpers** button at the end of the list opens the Memory helpers dialog (`:437-455`, `memory.open`, `data-memory-helpers`): "Major system" word finder and "Acronym builder" (`src/memory/`, not read; strings `recall.ts:136-153`: Acronym builder "Paste a list, one item per line. You get the first letters, and a card.", "Make a card on this page", "Open a page first. The card goes there."; Major system "Type a number to see word ideas...", "Keep as my pick", "No built-in word. Make your own.", "My word").
- Gating: group only in the `notebooks` space. Templates live in a hidden notebook of kind `templates`; excluded from deck editor, cards tab, Revise, reviews (`reviewable` excludes the Templates notebook).

#### Starter templates (what is seeded; `src/templates/starterTemplates.ts`)

`STARTER_VERSION = 3` (`:35`); older workspaces only get starters added since (`since`). 13 starters:

1. **Daily journal** (role `daily`): headings Today I learned, What was hard, Review tomorrow + checklist, callout "One sentence worth remembering:" (`:73-86`). Used for every journal day.
2. **Study notes**: Topic, Key ideas (bullet), Questions (bullet), Summary in my own words (`:88-99`). This is the "Study (Recommended)" one (matched by exact title `templateService.ts:236`).
3. **Meeting notes**: Attendees, Agenda, Decisions, Action items (checklist) (`:101-112`).
4. **Reading notes**: Source, Highlights (quote), My thoughts, Ideas to try (checklist) (`:114-125`).
5. **Project plan**: Goal, Milestones (checklist), Risks, Next step (checklist) (`:127-138`).
6. **Lesson** (`since 2`): Detailed notes, Keyword summary (bullet), Flashcards + a Card list block (`:139-149`).
7. **Lesson (large topic)** (`since 2`): Overview + links to three sub-pages (Detailed notes, Keyword summary, Flashcards-with-card-list) + "All cards" heading with a Card list including sub-pages (`:150-166`).
   8 to 13. **Memory techniques** (`since 3`, `:243-296`), each with "How to use" text and an evidence callout: **Memory palace** (3 rooms each with an empty Card), **Vivid image** (Card whose answer is an empty picture + caption line), **Major system** (digit-sound bullet list 0 to 9 + "My words"), **PAO table**, **Acronym and chunking** (list + empty Acronym card), **Story method** (items, story, empty Card). A grey italic "Example:" line is included in each.

- Titles of the first five are stored in English and displayed through `displayName` (translation); the others use `t()` at seed time.
- `i18n` keys `template.daily|meeting|project|reading|study` exist (`library.ts:188-192`) for display of the stored English titles.

#### Save as template (dialog + commands)

- Opens from: page ⋯ menu "Save as template" (`page.saveTemplate`, `sidebar.ts:43`), "/ Save page as template" slash item, ribbon Insert › Templates › "Save page as template" (`tool.saveTemplate`, `editorCommands.ts:163`).
- Main file: `src/pages/PageView.tsx:201,764,221-239`
- Features: name dialog titled "Save as template", label "Template name", confirm "Save template" (`library.ts:147-149`); copies the page (blocks and live sub-pages, page links dropped) into the Templates notebook after flushing the editor; toast `page.savedTemplate`. Empty name falls back to page title, then "Untitled" (`templateService.ts:293-317`).
- Gating: not offered on template pages (`onSaveAsTemplate` undefined there, `slashMenu.tsx:181`).

#### Slash items "Insert template…" and "Save page as template"

- `slashMenu.tsx:173-195`: title "Insert template…" (subtext "Add a template's blocks here"), icon clipboard, group "Templates"; "Save page as template" (subtext "Reuse this page's layout for new pages") only when `onSaveAsTemplate` exists. Ribbon equivalents `insertTemplate` / `saveTemplate` (`toolRegistry.ts:400-411`).

#### Template page banner

- Pages in the Templates notebook show "Template." / "New pages copy this. Editing it doesn't change pages you already made." (`sidebar.ts:50-51`); the Daily journal shows "Today's journal ..., made from your "Daily journal" template. Edit that template to change future days." (`sidebar.ts:52-54`). (Banner component not read; keys only.)

## PART 6. TAGS

Tag rules: names cleaned (spaces become "-", "#" and "/" dropped, max 60 chars per level, case/accent-insensitive same tag) (`src/tags/tagNames.ts`); "/" makes levels; a tag covers its sub-tags; sub-pages do not inherit tags; card tags = page tags + tags in question/answer (`tags.md`).

#### Page tags row (chips under the page title)

- Opens from: under the title on every page (`PageView.tsx:587-593`).
- Main file: `src/tags/PageTags.tsx:20-121`
- Features:
  - A chip per tag with its full path and colour tint (live names). Deleted tags are filtered out (`:37-39`). Click opens a small menu (below). Tip `page.tagChip`.
  - **+N more** / **Show less** after 8 chips (`MAX_SHOWN = 8`, `:13,65-75`; `tags.more`, `tags.showLess`).
  - Dashed **+ Add tag** button (Tip `page.addTag`) opens the Tag picker (`:76-96`).
- Chip menu (popover): **Show everything tagged** (`tags.showTagged`, icon search; only when `onOpenTag` is given) → Search filtered to the tag; **Remove from this page** (icon close) (`:97-117`).
- Empty/loading: while loading, an empty 28 px placeholder (`:35`). Adding an existing tag does nothing (`tags.md`).

#### Tag picker ("Add a tag", also used for Move under… and Merge into…) (popover dialog)

- Opens from: + Add tag, and the Move/Merge tools of the Tags sidebar.
- Main file: `src/tags/TagPicker.tsx:219-409`
- Features: search/create combobox with placeholder "Find or create a tag (use / for levels)" (`tags.find`), listbox of matches (up to 50) each as `#parent/name` with a colour dot (`:368-389`), final row **Create #name** when the typed name does not exist (allowed by default; off for Move/Merge) (`:390-405`), optional first row **Top level** (Move under… only; empty search) (`:251-255,382-384`). Excludes already-attached tags (or self + descendants for tools) (`:259`).
- Empty: "Type a name to create your first tag" (no tags yet, creation allowed) or "No tag matches" (`:361-367`).
- Gating: width 300 px, anchored to the button, full width with 12 px edges on phones (`phone:!inset-x-3`, `:336`).
- Keyboard: ↑ ↓ move the highlight, **Enter** picks (also if the list has not caught up, it re-queries and picks the first row, `:290-297`), **Esc** closes, ignores Enter during IME composition (`:299-313`); click outside closes (`:269-275`); `role=combobox/listbox/option` with active-descendant.

#### Tags section (sidebar group)

- Opens from: Notebooks space sidebar (`Sidebar.tsx:675-679`), collapsed by default.
- Main file: `src/tags/TagsSection.tsx:52-354`
- Features:
  - Header "Tags" with + "New tag" (Tip `sidebar.newTag`) (`:174-188`).
  - Tag tree sorted by name; ▸/▾ for tags with sub-tags (aria "Expand/Collapse #name"; open state persisted in pref `expandedTags`) (`:64,109-123`).
  - Tag chip button (same look as page chips, tinted when coloured) → opens Search filtered to that tag (Tip `sidebar.tag`) (`:124-144`).
  - Count of items (pages, notes, cards) with the tag, refreshed when tagging changes and every minute, aria "N items" (`:145-152`, `useTagCounts` `:360-391`).
  - **⋯** "Tag options for #path" button (Tip `sidebar.tagOptions`) and **right-click** on a row open the tag menu (`:101-105,153-162`).
  - Empty: dashed card "No tags yet. Tag a page with "+ Add tag" under its title, or type # in a note. Use / for levels, like #medicine/cardiology." (`:189-192`, `tags.ts:46-47`).

#### Tag menu (⋯ / right-click; popover menu)

- Main file: `src/tags/TagsSection.tsx:222-241` (items); tools in `src/tags/tagTools.ts`
- Items (icon, label): ✎ **Rename**, ↳ **Move under…**, ⇢ **Merge into…**, ● **Colour**, × **Delete tag**. Each opens its own dialog below.

#### New tag (name dialog)

- Opens from: + in the Tags header. `TagsSection.tsx:200-221`. Title "New tag", label "Name (use / for levels)", confirm "Create". Ignores names that are only `#`, `/` or spaces. Creating "a/b" creates the parents, then the sidebar expands the parent chain to show it (`:211-216`).

#### Rename tag (name dialog)

- `TagsSection.tsx:242-264`. Title "Rename #{path}", label "New name (every page and card shows it)", confirm "Rename". Results: toast "Renamed to #{path} everywhere" or "There is already a #{name} there. Use "Merge into…" to combine them." (`renameTag` returns `done|empty|taken`, `tagTools.ts:35,53`).

#### Move tag (picker)

- `TagsSection.tsx:265-315`. Picker titled "Move #{path} under…", placeholder "Find the new parent tag", Top level row, no create; self and sub-tags excluded. Toast "Moved to #{path}" or the "already a #..." message (`moveTag` `done|taken|loop`, `:36`). Expands the new parent.

#### Merge tag (picker)

- `TagsSection.tsx:265-315` (same component). Title "Merge #{path} into…", placeholder "Find the tag to keep". Rewrites pages, inline tags and saved searches; open pages are reloaded through `onNotesRewritten`; toast "Merged into #{path}" (`mergeTag` `tagTools.ts:119`). Merging into a sub-tag throws (`tags.md`).

#### Colour dialog

- `TagsSection.tsx:404-471`. Native dialog "Colour for #{name}", note "Tags are grey unless you pick a colour.", swatches: **No colour** ("—") + Blue #2f6feb, Green #2e9e6b, Orange #d9822b, Red #c8402f, Purple #8a5cf6, Pink #c2417f (`TAG_COLORS` `tagTools.ts:22-29`); each pick saves instantly; **Done** button (autofocus). Esc / backdrop closes.

#### Delete tag (confirm dialog)

- `TagsSection.tsx:323-351`. Message "Delete #{path}? It comes off the N pages, notes and cards that have it. Your notes and cards stay." (+ " Its N sub-tags move up one level." when it has children), confirm label "Delete tag", toast "Deleted #{path}. Your notes stay." (`tags.ts:39-45`). Soft delete; saved searches fall back to the parent (`tags.md`).

#### Inline tag (type "#" in a note) and tag chip in text

- "#" suggestion menu: `PageEditor.tsx:963-976`, logic `src/editor/tagActions.ts` (`TAG_TRIGGER = '#'` line 31). Opens at line start or after a space (not "C#"); items are matching tags then "Create #name" (`tags.create`); a space closes the menu; text typed that cannot be a tag is put back (`tagActions.ts:19-37,62-130`).
- Rendered by `src/editor/TagView.tsx:14-39`: current name + parents + colour (live); click opens Search for that tag when `openTag` is available (title "Show everything with this tag"); a deleted tag shows its old name faint with title "This tag was deleted".
- Chip component: `src/tags/TagChip.tsx:155-193` (button when clickable); `TagLabel` shows parents faintly (`:132-152`).

#### Tags in Decks

- Deck editor step 2 tag toggles (see New deck dialog) and Search tag filter menu use the same tags; the Search screen's tag step menu shows sub-tags with counts (`SearchScreen.tsx:100-115`).

All code is lazy except the entry button (`PromptsEntry.tsx:1-10`).

#### "Prompts" entry buttons (button)

  - Handwriting convert/Write pad "Pick a reading prompt" (`ai.readPrompt.pick` / `using` / `clear`) picker, optional: `src/ai/ReadPromptChoice.tsx:20-65` (pref `ai.readPrompt`).

#### Prompt library dialog (list view)

- Opens from: the buttons above.
- Main file: `src/prompts/PromptsDialog.tsx:110-369`
- Frame: fixed full-screen overlay `role=dialog`, max-width 2xl, header with title "Prompt library" and a close (PopupHeader) (`:186-195`); Esc closes (`:192`).
- Features (list view, `:231-364`):
  - Search box "Search prompts" (autofocus; **Enter uses the top result**: opens it, or picks it in picker mode) (`:233-249`). Matching: name, then subject tags, then text; every word must match (`prompt-library.md`).
  - **New prompt** (`prompts.new`) and **New skill** (`prompts.newSkill`) (`:250-265`).
  - Filters: Kind select (Prompts and skills / Prompt / Skill), Purpose select (Any purpose + Check, Import, Key points, Plan, Practise, Recall, Summarise, Understand, Write), Subject select (Any subject + every distinct subject tag) (`:267-308`; labels `late/ai.ts:222-224,287-288,339-347`).
  - Rows (max 50, `PAGE = 50`): name (skills prefixed "⛓ "), line with purpose · subjects · average stars (★ text), **Edit** button (aria "Edit {name}"; hidden in picker mode) (`:316-352`). Clicking the row runs it (or picks it in picker mode) (`:325-327`).
  - **Show N more** (`:355-363`).
  - Empty: "No prompt matches. Try fewer words, or make a new one." (`:309-311`). Loading "Opening the library…" (`:197`).
- Behaviour: first open creates and seeds the library database (`ensureLibrary`, `seedLibrary`; ids seen stored in pref `prompts.seeded`) (`:139-155`). Delete from the editor moves to Trash and toasts "Prompt deleted" with **Undo** (`annot.undo`) (`:173-183`).
- Gating: `onPick` (picker mode) hides Edit and sends the chosen item up (`:99,341`); `renderRowAction` hook for extra row buttons (`:101,340`). Library is a database of kind `promptLibrary` so it also opens as a normal database (table/board/search).
- Seeds (`src/prompts/seedPrompts.ts`, 23 rows): own prompts "Clean up pasted text" (`own.cleanup`), three NotebookLM paste-in prompts ("Mind map as indented outline", "Quiz as Q/A list with answers", "Infographic content as headings and bullets"), "Summarise", "Key points", "Make recall cards", "Make fill-in-the-blank cards", "Explain it like I am new", "Quiz me", "Find the gaps", "Translate and keep the terms", "Story so far (no spoilers)", "Fix my handwriting text", "Study plan for a chapter", "Teach it back (Feynman check)"; four CC0-idea prompts "Socratic tutor", "Interviewer", "Debate partner", "Plain language editor" (each row carries a licence note); three skills "Transcript to study set", "Article to research book", "Quick digest" (`seedPrompts.ts:84-256`). Added once per starter id, never overwritten, a deleted one stays deleted on that device.

#### Prompt / skill editor (inline form in the dialog)

- Opens from: New prompt, New skill, Edit.
- Main file: `src/prompts/PromptEdit.tsx:26-300`
- Fields (one per library column): **Name** (max 200; `:59-68`), **Kind** Prompt/Skill (switching to Skill selects all skill parts and output "Study set"; back to Prompt resets Study set to "An answer"; `:70-96`), **Purpose** (`:97-112`), **Input** A page / A PDF / Pasted text (`:113-128`), **Output** An answer / Notes / Cards / Study set (`:129-144`), **Subject tags** comma list (placeholder "biology, exam, beginner"; `:146-158`), **Prompt text** (textarea, max 20,000, `:159-168`), for skills the checklist **Skill makes**: Detailed pages, Keyword summary, Short summary, Mind map, Database rows, Review cards, Sources (`:169-191`; order fixed by `SKILL_PARTS` `promptModel.ts`), **Preferred company** (Any company + each AI company, resets the model when changed; `:192-213`), **Preferred model** (Your default model + models of that company; `:214-228`), checkbox **Use this company and model when I run it** (`:230-238`), licence line when present (`:239-243`), collapsible **N earlier versions** with date or "Current text", average stars with "(N ratings)", and **Use this text again** which puts the old text back into the box (`:244-278`, `prompts.versions`, `prompts.restoreVersion`). Changing the text on save keeps the old text as a version (30 kept).
- Buttons: **Delete** (only for existing items, left), **Cancel** (`annot.cancel`), **Save** (disabled with empty name) (`:279-297`). Toast "Prompt saved" key exists (`prompts.saved`); not shown in `PromptsDialog.save` (`:165-171`) so the message may be unused.

#### Run a prompt (inline view)

- Features: **Back** + prompt name (`:151-158`), prompt text preview (3 lines), **input picker** (below), input status ("N characters will be used" + "Only the first part fits, the rest is left out." when cut; "Reading the words…" while loading) (`RunParts.tsx:150-161`), **cost line** "About ₹0.42 · 3,000 tokens in, up to 2,000 out · Claude Haiku 5.5" (`RunParts.tsx:164-181`, `prompts.cost`), the AI-off card (`AiOffCard`, `:165`), first-use privacy notice with **I understand** (stored in `AI_KEYS.privacy`) (`:166-187`), **Run with my AI** (disabled while busy, reading or no text; shows "Asking your AI…") (`:188-198`), the paste-in panel (below), and after an answer: editable **Answer (you can edit it)** textarea (`:210-218`), five-star **Rate this result** (stores a rating on the current version; `:219-223`, `ratePrompt`), **Copy** (`:225-231`), **Insert below** (only when a page is open; adds a level-2 heading with the prompt name plus the answer as blocks, marked AI with one batch id; toast "Done. One Undo removes the whole AI result." with Undo; refused with "This is locked for editing. Unlock it first." when the page is edit-locked) (`:111-139,232-249`).

Turn it on in Settings › AI."), page not locked (`ai.lockedOff`); input over about 80,000 tokens refused ("That is too much text to send at once."); empty ("There is no text to send."). Errors toast "The AI could not answer: {reason}".

(`:295-314,262-266`); error line (role=alert) with reasons such as "The answer has nothing I can use...", "That is not in the expected format. - Features: help "Edit anything, untick what you do not want, then save. Nothing is written until you press Save." (`:124`); **Name** field (max 200, `:126-134`); each part is a `Section` with a tick box: **Short summary** (editable text, only if a summary exists, `:135-148`); **Keyword summary** with (count of points) containing an editable keyword list (comma separated) and a list of **points** where each row has: keep checkbox ("Keep point: {text}"), icon (drawn Lucide icon or emoji; text input to change it), point text input, **Stars (1-10)** select that also sets the level label (Must know 8 to 10, Good to know 4 to 7, Extra 1 to 3) and the label "unchecked (no source)" for points without sources or doubted by the fact check (`:150-232`); header "N points have no source (unchecked)" (`:170`); **Mind map** toggle and **Database rows** toggle (no body; shown when there are points; `:236-248`); **Detailed pages** each with keep box, title input, body textarea (`:251-300`); **Review cards** each with keep box, kind badge (Question / Fill in / True/false / Both ways), front and back inputs (`:308-366`); **Sources** with keep boxes (`:368-390`). Optional **Fact check** block: its own cost line then button "Check the facts with a second model" (uses another model if one exists, else the cheapest) (`:394-417`), shown only when AI is ready (`RunSkill.tsx:162`). Footer slot (star rating). **Save into** select: "A new notebook" (default; name field default "Research book") or any eligible notebook; the open page's notebook is preselected (`:421-447`). **Cancel** and **Save** (`:449-468`).

One Undo removes everything (new notebook removed whole). If saving fails halfway, everything made is removed again. Toast "Saved N pages." (`prompts.savedCount`).

## PART 8. JOURNAL

Model: Journal notebook (kind `journal`, auto-created on first need and titled "Journal"), month pages (`journalMonth` "YYYY-MM", top level) containing day pages (`journalDate` "YYYY-MM-DD"), titles in the locale ("Sunday 25 October 2026", "October 2026") (`journalService.ts:19-76`, `dates.ts:10-44`). Day = device-local date. New day page: copy of the Daily journal template (without page-link blocks), card schedules synced, then a `dayRecord` block (the day's task record, read live, never edited) is appended (`:61-72`). Opening an existing day returns it (`created:false`); if the day page was deleted a fresh one is made (`:47-48`, list excludes deleted).

#### "Today" button (sidebar)

- Opens from: top of the Notebooks sidebar (`Sidebar.tsx:389-409`).
- Features: button "Today · Sun 25 Oct" (sun icon, aria "Open today's journal", Tip `sidebar.today`) with the shortcut chip `Alt+T` (`altShortcutLabel('T')`, `:404`). Only in the `notebooks` space.
- Action: `App.tsx:1130-1137` `openToday` opens/creates today's page, toast `app.journalCreated` when it was created.

#### Open today / Home placeholder / ribbon

- Ribbon "Go to › Today" (`tool.today`, shortcut Alt+T) `toolRegistry.ts:629-636`.
- Sidebar Home space placeholder: button "☀ Open today's journal ({date})" and text "Your day will live here: top priorities, tasks and a focus timer. For now, open today's journal." (`SpacePlaceholder.tsx:36-47`, `sidebar.ts:127-129`), shown at `Sidebar.tsx:700-702` (compact). This looks like leftover scaffolding because Home is now a real screen (see Surprising).
- Home screen deps: `openToday` and `appendJournal` (`App.tsx:1870-1885`); Home calendar opens any day with `openJournalOn(day)` (`App.tsx:1139-1149,1693`, `onOpenDay`).

#### Journal page tree rows (sidebar tree)

- `src/pages/PageTree.tsx:137,225-232`: month and day pages are sorted by their date key; labels are generated from `journalDate`/`journalMonth` in the current language ("Sun 25 Oct", "October 2026") unless the page has `customTitle` (user rename, double-click to rename, `:218`). `journal` flag passed for the Journal notebook (`Sidebar.tsx:661`). Pages are normal pages with the normal row menu/soft delete.

#### Evening review writing into the journal

- `appendJournalNotes` (`journalService.ts:84-112`): each non-empty answer becomes a level-3 heading + paragraph inserted before the day record, creating the day if needed. Triggered by Home "Save to today's journal" (strings `home.evening.*`, `home.ts:80-88`: "What went well today?", "Answers are written into today's journal page. Leave a box empty to skip it.", "Saved to today's journal.").

#### Journal in other surfaces

- Settings backup shows "last backup" using `shortDayLabel(dateKey())` (`DeviceSection.tsx:44`). Deck editor shows the Journal notebook as a normal choice. "All cards" notebook filter excludes it (`!n.kind`, `CardsTab.tsx:62`).
- No dedicated "calendar", "streak" or "journal settings" UI inside `src/journal/` (service + date helpers only).

## ANYTHING SURPRISING, DUPLICATED OR HALF-BUILT

1. **Two editors for the same field.** Revise tab "My summary" (`ReviseTab.tsx:164-192`) and Summary view "In your own words" (`SummaryView.tsx:236-268`) both edit `Page.summary` through `setLessonSummary`. Differences: Revise = 3 rows, plain textarea, saved on blur, keyed per lesson; Summary = 2 rows auto-growing, re-syncs from DB changes (`:247-250`). A redesign should show one component.
2. **Revise vs Summary overlap in purpose.** Both are "key points of a lesson". Revise collects _marked words_ (inline `mark` style), Summary collects _added lines_ (linked rows in table `summaryItems`). Different data, two tabs, two toolbars (4+ label buttons vs one Summary button).
3. **"Card stats" ribbon command opens the notebook Database tab** (`toolRegistry.ts:619-626`), not a card statistics screen. Name and target disagree (study tracker database). Label "Card stats" (`toolbar.ts:284`).
4. **PageReview ignores its scope.** `PageReview` builds a page+sub-pages scope but `PageView` passes `onReview={() => onReview(true)}` (`PageView.tsx:627`), so the exact scope depends on `onReview(true)` (probably "include sub-pages"; not verified further).
5. **Dead / unused i18n keys (in the files read):** `card.answer` "A" (`recall.ts:3`, not rendered by the Card block), `cards.sortCards`, `prompts.saved`, `recall.ts` dashboard.* and rollup.* groups belong to databases (misfiled in the recall dictionary: `recall.ts:44-79,240-249`), `memory.*` belong to `src/memory/`.
6. **Docs out of date vs code:** `docs/features/prompt-library.md` says 12 own + 4 CC0 + 3 skills = 19 seeds; code has 23 (16 own prompts including Clean up and 3 NotebookLM prompts, 4 CC0, 3 skills; `seedPrompts.ts:84-256`). `docs/features/summary.md` mentions an iPad "short bar" Add-to-Summary icon; the only code path found is the shared selection toolbar (`SelectionToolbar.tsx:122`).
7. **Home placeholder left in sidebar.** `SpacePlaceholder` for space `home` still says "Your day will live here: top priorities, tasks and a focus timer. For now, open today's journal." (`sidebar.ts:127`) while a real `HomeScreen` exists (`App.tsx:1870` area). Library variant has a permanently disabled button with title `space.library.importSoon` (`SpacePlaceholder.tsx:49-`).
8. **Deck editor step strip is not interactive** (plain `<li>`, `DeckEditor.tsx:160-170`), so one cannot jump between steps. Colour swatches use the hex code as aria-label (`:324`), not a name.
9. **Tag colours duplicated** in two places: Revise labels use the same six colours under `LABEL_COLORS` (`reviseMarks.ts:221-228`, different order) and Settings borrows tag colour names (`tags.color.<hex>` keys, `SettingsScreen.tsx:703`). Deck colours (7 hex) are a third palette (`DeckEditor.tsx:19-27`).
10. **Card types are only two in the editor.** The AI skill preview shows four card kinds (Question, Fill in, True/false, Both ways; `late/ai.ts:231-234`), but saved cards are written through `buildCards` as ordinary Card blocks / blanks (`skillSave.ts:131`), so True/false and Both ways are not distinct card types after save (not verified how they are rendered; check `buildCards`).
11. **Anki export drops pictures** and flattens answers to text + bold/italic/underline; AI cards with images, picture answers (E2), math, links are lost (`exportApkg.ts:42-98`). Import never brings pictures/audio/schedule (reported by the media count toast).
12. **Anki cloze detection** is only by the first field containing `{{c` (`readApkg.ts:162`); other note types (image occlusion, reversed, custom) are imported as basic with fields joined.
13. **Card list `Review these cards`** only appears when `reviewCards` exists (not on template pages) and counts every card status, including suspended ones (`CardListView.tsx:44-52`).
14. **Deck tile Study** is disabled when counts are all 0 (`ReviewHome.tsx:275`), but the CardsTab button becomes "Practice" in the same situation; two different empty-state behaviours for the same idea.
15. **Memory helpers** (Major system, Acronym builder) live inside the Templates sidebar group, not with cards, even though the Acronym builder creates a card (`TemplatesSection.tsx:437-455`, `recall.ts:136-153`).
16. **Nested "remember" choice** for the nested-card dialog is passed via `settingId` to a shared `choose` dialog; the checkbox UI is outside the scoped files (`PageEditor.tsx:658`).
17. **Template titles stored in English** and translated on display (`displayName`); renaming a starter changes the match for the "Study (Recommended)" row because it is found by exact English title (`templateService.ts:236-245`).
18. **Journal:** day title and month title text are created in the language active at creation but the sidebar re-renders labels in the current language unless renamed (`customTitle`) (`journalService.ts:54-58`, `PageTree.tsx:228-231`). No scoped file offers a journal calendar or search; that lives in Home.
19. **No experience-level / device-mode gating** exists in any scoped file; the only adaptation is Tailwind `phone:`/`any-pointer-coarse:` sizing and the hover-only block menu (touch users reach the same actions through the selection toolbar).
20. **Review screen internals** were only skimmed (brief list above), as the brief asked.

## COUNTS (this inventory)

Surfaces documented: 62 section headings (screens, tabs, menus, bars, dialogs, popovers, inline editors; some grouped as "brief" for reader features and Review).
Features (individually referenced buttons, fields, options, toggles, commands): about 330.

# APPENDIX F (06-data-safety.md)

## 06 Data safety audit: history, trash, share/backup, sync/account, lock, onboarding, Claude, updates

All paths relative to repo root. Line numbers verified against the code on 12 Nov 2026. Visible labels are quoted from `src/i18n/en/*`. "(i18n)" = key only, label in `src/i18n/en/...` (file given where useful). No secret values are copied.

Conventions: `L1/L2/L3` = attention levels in `src/app/attention/attentionLevel.ts`. There is no per-feature "experience level" gate in any of these areas (I found none); gating is by sign-in state, sync phase, device capability (share sheet, passkey), `isReadOnlyGate()` and edit locks. "Touch vs mouse" gating is almost entirely done with Tailwind `md:` breakpoints, not code switches.

---

### 1. PAGE HISTORY / VERSIONS / UNDO

#### Page history dialog (dialog)

- Opens from: page "⋯" menu item "History" (icon `history`) `src/pages/PageView.tsx:315` -> `openHistory` `src/pages/PageView.tsx:174-180` (flushes the editor, then forces a new copy with reason `leave` via `recordVersion(...force)` so the newest edits are in the list, then opens). Also opened from Settings > Claude > Activity "Undo" button (see section 12), `src/connector/inbox/ClaudeActivity.tsx:157,170`.
- Main file: `src/history/HistoryDialog.tsx:20` (modal `<dialog>`, 46rem wide, 34rem tall, `:48`). Rendered at `src/pages/PageView.tsx:730-745`.
- Features:
  - Title "Page history" (`history.title`) `HistoryDialog.tsx:53`.
  - "Close" button `:60`; Escape (`onCancel`) `:44`; click on backdrop `:45-47`.
  - List "Earlier copies" (`history.list`) `:68`: one button per copy, label = locale date + short time (`when`, `:10-14`); a muted reason tag for non-timed copies (`:80-84`): "before a restore" (`history.reason.before-restore`), "before a change by Claude" (`.claude`), "big change" (`.big`), "on leaving" (`.leave`). `timed` copies show no tag. Selected copy is `aria-pressed` `:75`. Newest copy is auto-selected `:37`.
  - Preview pane (aria "Copy from {when}" `:91`): copy title (or "Untitled" `review.untitled`) `:97-99`; info line "Boxes: {n}. Drawing: yes/none. Restoring brings back the whole page." (`history.contains`, `:116-119`; box count = saved boxes + 1); read-only text preview of the blocks (`previewLines`, `src/history/previewLines.ts:5-45`: nested blocks indented 4 spaces per level, bullets "• ", checklist "☑ / ☐ ", divider "———"). Ink and layout are not previewed, only counted.
  - "Restore this copy" primary button `:112` -> `restoreVersion` (`src/history/versionService.ts:193`) then `onRestored`. The live page is first saved as a forced copy with reason `before-restore` (`versionService.ts:~233`) so a restore can be undone; restore is also one step of the page Undo list (`kind: 'restore'`, label "Restored an older copy" `history.restoreStep`, `versionService.ts:~215`).
  - Footer note (`history.keep`) `:132`: "all from the last 2 hours, then one per hour, then one per day. A restore keeps the page as it was first."
- Retention rules (actual code): `KEEP_VERSIONS = 300`, `VERSION_BYTES_MAX = 20 MB` per page, new timed copy at most every `VERSION_GAP_MS = 2 min`, a "big change" copy after `BIG_GAP_MS = 20 s` (`versionService.ts:13-19`); `thinVersions` (`:97-135`): keep all <=2 h, then 1 per hour up to 2 days, then 1 per day, drop >90 days, `before-restore` copies win a slot, byte cap trims oldest. A first copy of an empty page is not made (`:157`). Copies also store ink snapshot + layout (boxes, inkBg, sheet) `:30-50`.
- Gating: none by level/mode. Dialog is not reachable on a template? (menu item is unconditional at `PageView.tsx:315`). Locked pages: the page-lock gate hides the page, so the menu is not reachable.
- States: loading = renders nothing until the live query returns `:63`; empty = "No earlier copies yet. Copies are kept while you write: about one every 5 minutes, and one when you leave the page." (`history.empty` `:64`, NOTE: says 5 minutes, code is 2 minutes). Missing copy on restore throws "That copy no longer exists" (`versionService.ts:~225`) -> shown by `runTask`. Success toast "Restored the copy from {when}. Undo is in History." (`history.restored`, `PageView.tsx:738`) from Page view; Activity shows "The page was restored." (`claude.activity.restored`).
- Keyboard/touch: native `<dialog>` focus trap, Esc closes. On narrow screens the list becomes a horizontal scroll strip, on `md:` a 13rem left column (`:66-69`).

#### Sync-conflict "their text" copy (background feature, no UI of its own)

- Main file: `src/history/loserVersion.ts:56` `keepTheirsInHistory` - when this device's text wins a same-block merge, a version holding the other device's text is added to the page's History (appears in the list above with no reason tag). Used by `src/sync/engine.ts` (both sides, per `docs/features/sync.md`).

#### Undo / Redo (journal; "history" in the toolbar sense)

- Opens from: icon pair `app/tools/HistoryButtons.tsx` at the far left of main bar, ribbon, reading bar, reader bar, Write pad (documented `docs/features/undo-history.md`); keyboard Ctrl/Cmd+Z; iOS shake. Entry `src/editor/historyActions.ts:21` `runHistory(editor,'undo'|'redo')`, `:34` `runPageHistory`.
- Main file: `src/history/pageHistory.ts` (journal; `LIMIT = 200` steps per page `:34`, `PAUSE_MS = 800` typing group `:36`, `SAVE_BUDGET 1.5M chars` `:128`, `PM_SAVE_MAX 30000` `:130`).
- Step labels (i18n late/history.ts): "Typing" (`history.typing`, `pageHistory.ts:421`), "Drawing", "Layout change", "Page change", "Tag change", "Database change", "Restored an older copy".
- Toasts / states: "Open a page to use Undo." (`history.noPage`, `pageHistory.ts:483`), "Nothing left to undo." / "Nothing left to redo." (`:518`), "Could not undo/redo \"{what}\": {why}" (`history.cantUndo/cantRedo`, `:500`) with reasons "it changed or was removed" (`history.why.gone`) and "this step is from an older version" (`history.why.unknown`).
- `src/editor/diff.ts` is NOT UI: it is the editor<->database block change detector (`createSnapshot`, `absorbIntoSnapshot`). It has no labels and no surface. It matters only because live-apply from sync uses it so remote blocks are not written back.
- Gating: none. Steps with data persist across reload (meta `hist:<scope>`); a text step bigger than 30,000 chars is session-only (`pageHistory.ts:130`).

### 2. TRASH

#### Trash list (sidebar section)

- Opens from: sidebar bottom area ("quiet corner") collapsible header "Trash" (with " · {count}" when not empty). Rendered `src/app/spaces/QuietCorner.tsx:143`; component `src/trash/TrashSection.tsx:26`.
- Features:
  - Header toggle "Trash" `:63` (`aria-expanded` `:57`; open state remembered as pref `trashOpen` `:31`), count suffix `:64`.
  - Row per item: icon 📓 notebook / 🗃️ database / 📄 page `:78-84`; title or "Untitled" `:87`; subline: "{n} pages" (`trash.pages`), "{n} rows" (`trash.databaseRows`) or "in {notebook}" (`trash.inNotebook`) `:90-96`.
  - Row action "Restore" (icon ↺, tooltip `sidebar.restore`) `:100-106` -> `restoreNotebook/restoreDatabase/restorePage` (`src/trash/trashService.ts:212/259/286`).
  - Row action "Delete forever" (icon ×, tooltip `sidebar.deleteForever`) `:107-113` -> confirm dialog (below) -> `purgeNotebook/purgePage/purgeDatabase` (`trashService.ts:342/370/397`).
  - Actions are hover/focus-revealed: `opacity-0 group-focus-within:opacity-100 group-hover:opacity-100` `:99`. NOTE: on touch there is no hover, so the buttons are invisible until a row is focused (see "surprising").
- Confirm dialog (`ConfirmDialog`) `:118-140`: "Delete “{name}” forever?" + (notebook) "Its pages will be deleted too." / (database) "Its rows will be deleted too." + "This can’t be undone." Confirm button "Delete forever".
- Retention / semantics: there is NO automatic emptying or age limit anywhere (confirmed `docs/features/trash.md` "Security check"; grep of `src/trash` finds no timer). Items deleted together share one `deletedAt`; the list shows only directly-deleted items, newest first (`listTrash` `trashService.ts:75`); Restore brings back everything deleted at that moment. Restoring a page whose notebook is in Trash restores only the notebook; a page with a deleted parent goes to the notebook top level; a database row restores its database; a database whose notebook is gone throws (`trashService.ts:259-320`). Purge blanks content but keeps `{id, purgedAt}` markers for sync (`:171-203`); also purges cards/review logs, books, recordings, ink, summaries, unused images (`:3-13`).
- Empty state: "Trash is empty" `:69` (shown only when open).
- Gating: sidebar only; part of the quiet corner on `App.tsx:1395`. Hidden if the sidebar is hidden.
- Keyboard/touch: header is a button; row buttons reachable by Tab (focus-within reveals).

#### Move to Trash (delete flows)

- Page: `trashPageWithUndo` `src/pages/pageTrash.ts:17` - used from page "⋯" delete `src/pages/PageView.tsx:406`, page row menu, sidebar `src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:272`. Refused with "Read-only until you sign in" (`gate.pageBar`) when the account gate is on, else "This is locked for editing. Unlock it first." (`editLock.refused`) `pageTrash.ts:23-26`. Toast "Moved “{name}” to Trash" or "Moved “{name}” and {n} sub-page(s) to Trash" (`trashDel.page/pageSubs`, `:34-38`) with action "Undo" (`reading.undo`, `:39`) -> `restorePage`.
- Notebook: `src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:244-262`: confirm "Move “{name}” and its {n} page(s) to Trash? You can undo this." (`trashDel.confirmNotebook`), toast "Moved “{name}” to Trash" with "Undo" -> `restoreNotebook` (`:255-260`).
- Database rows / databases: via `trashService` (UI in the databases area, out of this scope).

#### Export dialog (page or notebook export) (dialog)

- Opens from: page "⋯" menu "Export" (`page.export`, icon ⤒) `src/pages/PageView.tsx:349` -> `openExport` `:192-198` (flushes editor first); notebook "⋯" menu "Export notebook" (`export.notebook`) `src/notebooks/NotebookScreen.tsx:145-147`. Lazy-loaded at `PageView.tsx:100`, `NotebookScreen.tsx:60`.

- Features (step 1, pick a format, `FORMATS` `:23-29`, buttons testid `export-<id>` `:127`):

  - "Text" - "Words only" (`export.txt`) -> `.txt`.

- Features (step 2, result `:153-225`, testid `export-ready`):
  - "Ready: {name}" `:156`; "{n} locked page(s) was/were left out." (`export.skipped`) `:158-161` (locked pages are excluded from notebook export and sub-page export; the open page is always exported).
  - File result: "Save file" (primary, testid `export-save`) `:174-184` -> `saveFile` (download) and note "Saved {name}." (`export.saved`) `:180`; "Share..." (testid `export-share`) `:185-196` ONLY when `navigator.canShare({files})` is true (`exportService.ts:289-297`) - i.e. mainly iPad/phone/Android share sheet.
  - Print result (PDF / Paper layout): "Print or save as PDF" (testid `export-print`) `:199-210` -> `printDocument` (`exportService.ts:254-284`, hidden iframe from a blob URL, waits for images to decode, cleans up after `afterprint` or 60 s); help "In the print window choose "Save as PDF" (on iPad: Share, then Save to Files)." (`export.printHelp` `:163-165`).
  - "Another format" (back to step 1) `:212-218`; "Close" `:228-230`.
- File names: sanitised (`\ / : * ? " < > |` and control chars -> space, 80 chars) `exportService.ts:~48`; `.docx`, `.md`/`.zip`, `.txt`, `.pdf` (print title).
- Format content: shared neutral model `src/share/export/model.ts`; Word is hand-built zip of XML (`docx.ts:4-8`: headings, rich text, lists, to-do boxes, quotes, code, tables, pictures, ink picture; pictures converted to PNG/JPEG/GIF for Word); print HTML with A4 print CSS (`printHtml.ts`); paper layout = one `.sheet` per sheet with boxes placed in mm and ink drawn from strokes (`paperLayout.ts:19-24`); link targets only `http:`, `https:`, `mailto:` (docs/features/export.md).
- Error: "Could not make the file." (`export.failed`) `:147-150, :220-223`.
- Gating: Paper layout hidden for notebooks and when sub-pages ticked; Share hidden when the browser cannot share files; no level/mode gating.
- Keyboard/touch: first format button auto-focused `:58`; Esc closes `:99`; buttons are `min-h-11` (touch size).

#### Save & restore screen ("Data screen") (screen)

- Opens from: sidebar quiet corner row "Save & restore" (`sidebar.data`, icon ⤓) `src/app/spaces/QuietCorner.tsx:134-140` -> `onOpenData` (`src/App.tsx:1403`; hub kind `data`, route `#/data`); page "⋯" menu "Import" (`page.import`) `src/pages/PageView.tsx:344-348` -> `onOpenBackup` (opens this same screen; the page menu says "the real import options live in Save & restore"); backup reminder "Save backup now" (below); restore reload lands on `#/data` (`DataScreen.tsx` `replace`/`undo`).
- Main file: `src/share/DataScreen.tsx` (h1 "Save & restore" `:374`, close "×" with aria "Close" `:~377`, intro "Save your notes to a file, or bring a saved file back." `data.intro`).
- Top banner after a restore: `restore.bar` "Restored the file from {date}. Your earlier notes were kept as a safety copy." + "Undo restore" button `:360-368` (shown when sessionStorage flag `restored` and a restore mark exists).
- Error line (`role=alert`): any `BundleError` message or "That did not work, and nothing was changed. Please try again." (`data.failed`) - `run` wrapper `DataScreen.tsx:~160-175`.

##### Section "Save" (`data.save.title`) `DataScreen.tsx:392`

- "Save everything" (`data.all.button`) `:402` -> `saveFull('full')` (`:~183`) -> `writeFullFile(db,'full')` zip `Learning Notes YYYY-MM-DD.learningnotes.zip` (`src/share/fullFile.ts:60,93`); includes text, drawings, pictures, history, Trash, settings, audio sound; never saves table `secrets` (`fullFile.ts:23`).
- "Save notes only" (`data.save.notes`) `:412` -> `saveFull('notes')`; same but no audio bytes (smaller).
- Explainer "Everything and Notes only both keep your text, drawings, pictures, history and Trash. Notes only leaves out the sound of audio recordings, so it is smaller." (`data.save.text` `:~414`).
- Optional password block (`PasswordFields`, `:417`, `src/share/PasswordFields.tsx`): checkbox "Protect this file with a password" (`data.protect`), then fields "Password (at least 8 characters)" (`data.protect.password`; `MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8` `src/share/crypto.ts:18`) and "Type the password again" (`data.protect.again`), show/hide eye "Show password / Hide password" (`PasswordInput.tsx:48`), warning "If you forget this password, the file can never be opened again..." (`data.protect.warning`); errors "The password needs at least {n} characters." / "The two passwords are not the same." (`lockState.ts:14-18`). While the password is invalid all Save buttons (and "Save file" in Share one notebook) are disabled (`disabled={busy || !!lockProblem}`). Locked file extension `.learningnotes.locked` (`fileIO.ts:32`); small files use `LNOTESE1`, zips use chunked `LNOTESE2` 1 MB chunks (PBKDF2-SHA256 600,000 rounds, AES-GCM-256; docs/features/encryption.md).
- Toasts: "Saved “{name}” ({size}): everything." / "...: notes only (no audio sound)." (`data.saved.full/notes`); if size > 1 GB the error "This file was saved, but it is over 1 GB, so the app could not open it again. Save notes only instead." (`data.tooBigToRestore`, `:197`). Saving records the backup time (`recordBackupSaved`, resets the reminder).

##### Section "Restore" (`restore.title`) `DataScreen.tsx` (~420-455)

- "Open saved file" file button (`data.import.file`, `FileButton.tsx`, `:425`) -> `openSavedFile` (`fileIO.ts:86`): max 1 GB (`MAX_FULL_BYTES`) for zips, 200 MB (`MAX_BUNDLE_BYTES`, `bundle.ts:32`) for old JSON; error "This file is too big to import (the limit is about 200 MB)." (`data.tooBig`).
- Helper "You choose: replace what is here, or add as copies." (`restore.text`).
- Safety-copy row: "Safety copy from before the last restore, {date}" + "Undo restore" button `:~440-452` (`undoRestore`, `src/share/restore.ts:157`; reloads the app; refused when the app version changed: `restore.err.undoOlder`).
- Password prompt for a protected file (inline form `:~460-495`): "“{name}” is protected. Type its password to open it." (`data.locked.text`), field "Password of this file" (`data.locked.password`), submit "Open file" (`:483`), link-button "Forgot password?" (`:492`, `aria-expanded`) revealing "A forgotten password cannot be recovered..." (`data.locked.forgotText`); wrong-password message from `WrongPassword` and field cleared + refocused.
- Restore options dialog (`RestoreDialog.tsx:41`, modal): file name as heading; rows "Saved" / "Contains {n} notebooks, {n} pages, {n} cards" / "Also: History, Trash, settings, {n} pictures" (+ ", no audio sound" for notes-only) / "Size" (`restore.dlg.*`, `facts()` `:~15-35`); buttons:
  - "Replace my notes" (danger styled) - "Your notes now become exactly the file. A safety copy is kept first, so you can undo." (`restore.replace`, `:~104-111`). Shown only if `canReplace` = zip whose DB version is compatible (`canReplaceFrom` `restore.ts:51`); otherwise a note "This file is from an older version of the app, so it can only be added as copies." (`restore.olderVersion`) or, for old JSON, "This is an older kind of file, so it can only be added as copies." (`restore.onlyCopies`) `:114-117`.
  - "Add as copies" - "Keeps everything you have and adds the file’s notebooks next to it." (`restore.copies`, `:125-126`) - every file type; imports as new notebooks with fresh ids, titled "{name} (imported)" (`import.copyName`).
  - "Cancel" (`ui.cancel`) `:133`; backdrop click / Esc cancel `:67-70`.
- Replace flow (`DataScreen.tsx` `replace` ~`:215-240`, `restore.ts:120`): whole file read and validated first (nothing written, `readFull.ts:250`), whole current DB copied to separate DB `learning-notes-before-restore`, then rows written, then `location.reload()`. Busy dialog "Restoring…" / "Writing the file’s notes. Keep the app open." (`restore.busy` `:699`). Errors: "Could not keep a safety copy first (the device may be full). Nothing was changed." (`restore.err.safety`), "The safety copy is gone, so there is nothing to put back." (`restore.err.noCopy`), "This file is from an older version of the app, so it cannot replace your notes. Add it as copies instead." (`restore.err.older`).
- After "Add as copies": toast "Imported {n} notebooks, {n} pages and {n} cards as new copies." (`data.imported`); empty file: "This file has no notebooks or databases to import." (`data.nothing`).
- Validation: zip guards 200,000 entries / 2.5 GB unzipped / 2,000,000 rows (`fullFile.ts:25-33`), `checkRows.ts` field-by-field row checks, SVG refused, `__proto__` keys refused (docs/features/backup-and-files.md).

##### Section "Share one notebook" (`data.export.title`) `DataScreen.tsx` (~505-555)

- Notebook picker (aria "Notebook to save", `data.export.which`) listing live notebooks.
- "Save file" button (`data.export.button` `:531`) -> `exportNotebookBundle` (`bundle.ts:202`) -> `.learningnotes.json` (`bundleFileName` `bundle.ts:342`); toast "Saved “{name}”" (`csv.exported`).
- Checkboxes "Include my journal days" (`:542`) and "Include my templates" (`:550`).
- Helper "Opening it on another device adds a copy." (`data.export.text`). The same password block above also locks this file (`data.export.passwordHint` key exists but is never rendered).
- Journal and Templates notebooks cannot be exported alone (docs).

##### Section "Before last update" (`data.restore.title`) `DataScreen.tsx` (~556-590)

- Reads the newest rolling auto-backup (`readLatestBackup`, `src/core/autoBackup.ts`; set of 3, `<db>-auto-1..3`).
- Empty: "No copy yet." (`:561`). Present: "Saved {date}: {n} notebooks." + "Bring back" button (`:586`) -> adds that backup's notebooks as copies (`importBundleNow(checkBundle(bundleFromSnapshot(full)))`). Helper `data.restore.text` "A copy the app keeps in this browser." (key exists; check whether rendered).

##### Collapsible "More" (`data.more.title`, `<details>`) `DataScreen.tsx:595`

Contains four sub-sections:

- **Notion** (`src/share/NotionImportSection.tsx:109`, title "Notion" `notion.title`): intro `notion.text` ("Bring in a Notion export: in Notion open Settings, Export, choose "Markdown & CSV" and include sub-pages, then pick the zip here..."); file button "Import Notion export" with hint "Pick the .zip from Notion" (`:124-125`); destination select "Where to put it" (`notion.where`) with option "A new notebook" + every notebook (`:131-139`); progress "Reading the export…", "Importing…", "Importing {done} of {total}…" (`:55,79,88`); preview confirm (`ConfirmDialog` `:144-160`): "\"{name}\" has {pages} pages, {databases} databases ({rows} rows), {images} pictures and {files} other files. Put them in {where}?" confirm "Import Notion export". Done toast "Imported {pages} pages, {databases} databases and {rows} rows." with action "Undo" (`notion.undo`, `:91-98`, moves it all to Trash). Errors (`:14-26`): nothing found / "This zip holds other zips. Unzip it first and pick the inner zip." / too many files / "This export is too big to open here." / too many pages / "This file is too big (the limit is 400 MB)." / "This zip could not be read. Export again from Notion." Limits (`src/share/notionImport.ts:41-47`): zip 400 MB, 20,000 entries, 800 MB expanded, .md 2 MB, .csv 30 MB, other files 50 MB, 5,000 pages. Content mapping per docs/features/notion-import.md: pages/sub-pages, CSV -> Database with guessed column types, toggles, callouts, pictures, files, page links. Not read: nested zips, `_all.csv`, formulas, relations, comments, icons, covers.
- **Bring in from NotebookLM** (`src/share/notebooklm/NotebookLmImport.tsx:381`, lazy `DataScreen.tsx:48`; title `nlm.title`): text `nlm.text` ("one-time copy... nothing is sent anywhere"); selects "Notebook" (`:385`) and "Page" (`:400`, options "A new page" + pages); file button "Choose a file" with hint "Audio, video, slides (PDF or PowerPoint), a picture, or a CSV" accepting `audio/*,video/*,.m4a,.wav,.mp3,.mp4,.pdf,.pptx,.png,.jpg,.jpeg,.webp,.csv,.tsv,.txt,.md` (`:415-418`); "Or drop a file here" - the whole section is a drop target (`onDrop` `:374`); paste box "Or paste text copied from NotebookLM" (`:424`) + select "The text is" (`PASTE_KINDS`: "Flashcards" / "A quiz" / "A table" / "A mind map outline" / "A report or briefing", `nlm.kind.*`) + "Preview the text" (`:451`). File kinds detected by `kindOfFile` (`parse.ts:22-35`): video (mp4/mov/m4v), audio (m4a/mp3/wav/ogg/oga/aac/webm), slides (pdf/pptx), picture (png/jpg/webp), csv/tsv, report (txt/md). Preview line "Add to “{page}”: {what}. One Undo takes it back." (`nlm.preview` `:470`); for CSV a select "The CSV is" Flashcards/A table (`:477-488`); for a picture radios "Just the picture" / "Also rebuild it as an editable mind map" with a cost line "Your own AI key is used. {cost}" or "To rebuild, turn on AI with your own key in Settings, and allow AI for this notebook." (`:492-510, :249-253`); buttons "Add" (`:524`) / "Cancel" (`:534`); progress "Reading…", "Reading the picture…". Result toast "Added to “{page}”: {what}" with "Undo" (`nlm.done`/`nlm.undo`, `:332-338`). Errors: "This file type is not one NotebookLM gives out. Try audio, video, PDF, PowerPoint, a picture or a CSV.", "There was nothing in it that could be added.", "No cards found. Use a CSV of front and back, or lines like “Q: …” and “A: …”.", "Choose a notebook first.", "Could not bring it in: {reason}". Limits: file 50 MB, audio 200 MB (docs). Runner: `src/share/notebooklm/run.ts`; parsers `parse.ts` (`parseFlashcards:78`, `parseQuiz:125`, `parseTable:165`); mind map picture rebuild `rebuild.ts` (needs the owner's own AI key).
- **Anki** (`DataScreen.tsx:~610-645`, title "Anki"): text "Cards start as new. Schedules, pictures and sounds stay behind."; "Import from Anki" file button with hint "Pick an .apkg file" accept `.apkg,application/octet-stream` (`:624`); progress "Reading the Anki file…" / "Importing… {done} of {total} notes"; notebook picker "Notebook to save for Anki"; "Save for Anki" (`:639`) -> `.apkg`. Limits: 400 MB (`data.anki.tooBig`). Toasts: "Imported {n} cards from Anki into the new notebook “{name}”." + optional "{n} notes had nothing on the front and were left out." + "{n} pictures or sounds in the file were not imported."; export "Saved {n} cards as {name}." Errors: not a package / damaged / no collection / old-Anki placeholder / "This notebook has no cards or blanks to export." (`anki.*`).
- **"For testing: sample notebook"** (`data.sample.title`): "Adds a practice notebook. Nothing else changes."; "Load sample notebook" (`:662`; asks "You already have a sample notebook. Add another?" -> "Add another" when one exists, `:708`); "Remove sample notebook" (`:672`, disabled when none; confirm "Remove the sample notebook? Only what the sample added moves to the Trash..." -> "Remove", `:719`); toasts "Added the sample notebook “{name}”." / "Removed {n} sample notebook(s)." Built by `src/share/sampleNotebook.ts`.
- Gating: all sections are always shown; every button is `disabled` while `busy`. No level/mode gating. Entire screen is reachable while read-only (export always works; import/replace writes data - NOT checked against the read-only gate in `DataScreen.tsx`, see surprises).
- Keyboard/touch: all buttons `min-h-11`; native `<dialog>` for RestoreDialog; password inputs have autocomplete/aria wiring.

#### Backup reminder bar (bar)

- Opens from: automatically, when no backup file was saved for 7 days since first use/last save (`REMIND_AFTER_MS = 7 days`, `src/share/backupReminder.ts:10`), only if at least one page exists (`reminderDue` `:61-72`). Mounted `src/App.tsx:2010-2018`; not shown during review or while the Save & restore screen is open (`!reviewScope && hub?.kind !== 'data'`).
- Main file: `src/share/ReminderBar.tsx:1-33` (floating bar bottom-centre, `role=region`, aria "Backup reminder").
- Features: text "You have not saved a backup file for a week. Your notes live only in this browser." (`backup.reminder.text` `:24`); "Save backup now" (`:26`, opens Save & restore); "Remind me later" (`:29`, `snoozeReminder`, snooze = `SNOOZE_MS` 90 days in code `backupReminder.ts:11`, but docs/features/backup-and-files.md says 24 hours).
- State kept in the `meta` table (`META_ID 'backupReminder'`).

#### Silent upgrade backup / upgrade dialog (see section 13 "UpgradeGate").

#### Password-protected file prompt (see Restore section above).

#### Other share-folder code with no UI of its own

- `src/share/saveEverything.ts` / `saveEverythingLazy.ts`: used by the error boundary's "Save everything" so a file can be written when screens fail (docs/features/offline-and-install.md). `checkRows.ts`, `bundle.ts`, `importBundle.ts`, `readFull.ts`, `restore.ts`, `remapJson.ts`, `fileIO.ts`, `crypto.ts`, `formatSize.ts`, `lockState.ts` are logic only.
- CSV import/export (`csv.*`) and Capture (`capture.*`) and copy/paste menus live in the same i18n file but are NOT in this scope (databases, library, editor).

#### Sign-in panel (embedded component; used in 3 hosts)

- Opens from: (a) Welcome step 0 `src/App.tsx:1973-1977` / `src/onboarding/Welcome.tsx:176`; (b) Settings > Account when signed out `src/sync/AccountSection.tsx:90-93`; (c) the read-only "Sign in" sheet `src/sync/AccountGateBar.tsx:98`.
- Main file: `src/sync/SignInPanel.tsx:23` (views: `choose | code | codeEnter | pass | confirm | forgot | reset` `:21-22`; `data-testid=signin-panel data-view` `:345`).
- Features (sign-in methods and steps):
  - View `choose` `:139-176`: "Sign in with Google" primary (`sync.signIn`, `data-account=google` `:148`) -> hands over to `signIn()` redirect (Welcome/Gate hand over to the guided `SignInGuide`); "Email me a code" + "No password. We send a 6-digit code." (`:157-160`); "Email and password" + "Create an account or sign in." (`:170-173`).
  - View `code` `:178-196`: Email field (`signin.email`, `autoComplete=email`, `inputMode=email`, trimmed `:66-80`), "Send code" / "Working…" (`:192`), "Back" (`signin.back` `:129`).
