(`signin.codeSent`) or, after sign-up, "We emailed a 6-digit code to {email} to confirm it. Enter it below." (`signin.confirmSent`) `:210-213`; "6-digit code" field `:83`; in `reset` an extra "New password" field; "Send a new code" (`signin.resend` `:240`); submit "Continue" (`signin.verify`) or "Set new password" (`signin.resetGo`) `:248-251`.
  - View `pass` `:276-345`: tabs (role=tablist) "Sign in" / "Create account" (`:292-311`); Email; Password; when creating, live password rule line "At least 15 characters. A short sentence works well. No other rules." (`signin.passHelp` `:116`) and errors "Use at least 15 characters." / "Use at most 128 characters." (`signin.pass.short/long` `:118`; rules `src/sync/passwordRules.ts`); submit "Create account" or "Sign in" (`:322-328`); "Forgot password?" (`:336`, only on the sign-in tab).
  - View `forgot` `:256-275`: "We will email a 6-digit code to reset your password." + "Email me a reset code" (`signin.resetSend`).
  - Error line (`role=alert`, testid `signin-error` `:123`) from `errorKey` (`src/sync/signInErrors.ts`): "That email address does not look right." / "This email already has an account. Sign in instead." / "Something went wrong. Try again." / "That did not work. Check the code or password and try again. A code works for one hour." / "Could not reach the server, or too many tries. Check your connection, wait a minute and try again." / "Too many tries. Wait a minute and try again." / "The email service refused to send the code. Try again in a minute." / "This email cannot be used yet. The app is in private testing." / "Choose a longer password (15 or more characters)."

- Gating: Google button always shown; email options need the connector methods (`auth.passwordSignUp?.` optional chaining `:282-289`).
- Offline: in Welcome, Google button offline shows "You need to be online to sign in. Check your connection and try again." (`welcome.account.offline`, `Welcome.tsx:147-151,178-182`); in the read-only sheet the whole panel is replaced by "You are offline. Reading, search and export still work. Sign in when you are back online." (`gate.sheet.offline`, `AccountGateBar.tsx:100-107`).

#### What syncs (settings block)

- Main file: `src/settings/WhatSyncs.tsx` (rendered `SyncSection.tsx:92`). Title "What syncs" (`syncWhat.title` `:52`), intro `:53`, one switch per group "Sync {group} settings" (`syncWhat.switch` `:68`) for groups in `src/settings/syncGroups.ts`: General, Appearance, Review, Reading, AI settings, Lock, AI keys (ids `general, appearance, review, reading, ai, lock, aiKeys`, `syncGroups.ts:26-63`) with descriptive lists (`syncGroup.*.list`); AI keys switch only when signed in and tracking (`canKeys` `:35`) else suffix "Sign in to use." (`:91`); "Reset this device to synced values" (`:104`) -> "This device now follows your synced settings." / "This device has no settings of its own." and "Undo" (`:121`).
- AI-keys-on-by-default migration toast: "Everything now syncs, including AI keys (locked end to end, never in backups). You can change it in Settings." + "Review" (`syncAllMigration.ts`).

#### Per-setting sync badge (chip/menu)

- Main file: `src/settings/SettingSyncBadge.tsx:49` (rendered per setting row `SettingsScreen.tsx:369`). Chip "This device" (non-synced settings; tooltip `settings.scope.device.hint` "Only changes this device.") or a cloud chip for settings that follow you (tooltip `settings.scope.synced.hint`); menu "Sync options for {name}" (`setting.menu.label`) with "Use this device’s value everywhere" (`setting.menu.everywhere`), "Reset to synced value" (`setting.menu.reset`), "Keep on this device only" (`setting.menu.keep`) `:75-125`. Keys `setting.tag.all` ("All devices") and `setting.tag.only` ("This device only") exist; check whether rendered (the chip file uses `setting.tag.device` at `:60`).

#### Sync status dot (chip)

- Opens from: always visible next to "Settings" in the sidebar quiet corner `src/app/spaces/QuietCorner.tsx:126` and in Sync section header `SyncSection.tsx:184`.
- Main file: `src/sync/SyncDot.tsx:15`, state from `useSyncLabel.ts:~8-44`, logic `syncDisplay.ts:~25-43`.
- Features: states "Synced {time}"/"Synced" (green, `sync.state.syncedAt/synced`), "Syncing" (accent, pulsing unless reduced motion), "Offline" (muted; navigator offline or problem offline), "Needs attention" (red; needsSetup/needsKey phases, any other problem, or `needsDriveTap`). Not rendered when signed out (null). Text always accompanies the colour (`aria-live=polite`).

#### Alert banner system (Attention banner) (banner)

- Opens from: automatically; one banner at a time (highest level not snoozed). `src/app/attention/AttentionGate.tsx` mounted `src/App.tsx:2021`.
- Main file: `src/app/attention/AttentionBanner.tsx` + rules `attentionLevel.ts:28-61`.
- Features (text -> action): L2 "Pictures are waiting: Drive backup needs Google." -> "Connect Google for Drive backup" (`:28-30`); L2 "Drive is disconnected. Pictures are waiting." -> "Reconnect Drive" (`:38-40`); L2 "Google Drive is full. Sync is paused." -> "Open Drive storage" (`:49-51`); L2 "This Google account can't sync." -> "Switch account" (`:62-64`); L2 "Your device clock looks wrong. Sync is paused." -> "Details" (`:70-72`); L2 "Sync signed out. Changes are not syncing." -> "Sign in" (`:78-80`); L2 "Sync keeps failing." (after 3+ failures in a row) -> "Try again" runs `syncNow` (`:86-90`); L2 "Backups are failing." (2+) -> "Open backups" (`:95-97`); L2 "Update to keep syncing." -> "Update" (`update.now`, `:103-105`); L3 "Storage is almost full." -> "Manage storage" (`:111-112`, goes to Settings > data); L3 "Finish setting up sync." -> "Set up" (`:116-118`); L3 "A new version is ready." -> "Update" (`:124-126`); dismiss button aria "Dismiss" (`attention.dismiss` `:196`). Snooze: L2 until next app start, L3 for 7 days, returns if "worse" counter grows (`attentionLevel.ts:92-118`); L1 is never dismissed here.
- Keys `attention.fixed` ("Drive reconnected") and `attention.locked.*` used by sibling components.

#### "Your notebooks are locked" alert (Level 1 modal + banner)

- Opens from: `AttentionGate` returns `LockedAlertGate` when level 1 (`AttentionGate.tsx:71`) -> `src/sync/LockedAlertGate.tsx:18` -> `src/sync/LockedAlert.tsx`. Condition `isLocked` (`lockedStatus.ts`): signed in to sync but phase `needsKey`, or problem `needCode`/`unreadable`.
- Features: one `alertdialog` per session (sessionStorage `attn.locked.shown`) after the person stops typing, not while Settings > Sync is open (documented); title/body "Your notebooks are locked" / "Your synced notes are encrypted. Enter your passphrase or recovery code to see them on this device." (`LockedAlert.tsx:99-103`); buttons "Later" (`:111`, leaves the banner), "Use recovery code" (`:118`), "Enter passphrase" (`:126`); resulting red banner "Your notebooks are locked ·" + "Unlock" (`:146-155`, testid `locked-banner`). Keys `attention.locked.title/text/later/code/enter/banner/unlock`.

#### Read-only account gate (bar + sheet)

- Opens from: automatically when no sign-in is stored on the device (rule `src/sync/gateRule.ts:31-36`, no session and not switched off by localStorage `learning-notes:accountGate.off`); `AccountGate` mounted `src/App.tsx:2023`, tiny eager part `src/sync/AccountGate.tsx:23`; also opened when an edit is refused (`onSignInRequired` `AccountGate.tsx:34`).
- Main file: `src/sync/AccountGateBar.tsx:28`.
- Features: top bar (`role=status`, testid `account-gate-bar`, reserves 44px via `--attn-h`) "Sign in to keep editing and sync your notes" + "Sign in" button (`gate.banner`, `gate.signIn` `:41-48`); sheet (`<dialog>`, testid `account-gate-sheet`) "Sign in to keep editing" (`gate.sheet.title` `:93`) containing the Sign-in panel (online) or offline text, and "Not now" (`gate.sheet.later` `:115`); Esc closes via `onCancel` `:86-89`. Companion text "Read-only until you sign in" shown on page bars/refused edits (`gate.pageBar`), help `gate.sheet.help` ("Your notes are safe on this device. You can still read, search and export them...") - key exists, check render. Reading, search and export keep working; editing, creating, deleting are refused (`core/readOnlyGate.ts`).
- Hidden while the full-screen "You were signed out" lock is shown (`AccountGate.tsx:37`).
- A stored session counts as signed in even offline with an expired token (stay-signed-in fix, `gateRule.ts` header).

#### "You were signed out" lock (full-screen)

- Opens from: `SignedOutGate` when `endedRemotely && phase==='signedOut'` (set when another device used "Sign out on all devices" or the server refused the refresh token): `src/sync/SignedOutGate.tsx:18-23` -> `src/sync/SignedOutLock.tsx:~45` (testid `signed-out-lock`).
- Features: title "You were signed out" (`sync.ended.title` `:58`), text "Sign in to continue. Your account was signed out from another device. Your notes are safe on this device and hidden until you sign in." (`:61`); buttons "Sign in to continue" (`:74`) and "Start fresh on this device" (`:82`). "Start fresh" step (`:88-126`): title "Start fresh on this device?", text "This saves one last backup file, then removes all notes from this device and opens a new, empty app. Notes already in your cloud account are not touched." + unsent count "{n} change(s) on this device was/were never sent to the cloud. It will only exist in the backup file." or "Every change on this device was already sent to the cloud." (testid `fresh-unsent` `:97-102`); danger button "Save backup and start fresh" / "Saving the backup…" (`:117`); "Go back" (`:126`); error "Could not finish. Close other windows of the app and try again. Check that the backup file was saved." (`:107`).

#### Sync choice dialog ("Changes on this device and in the cloud")

- Opens from: `SignedOutGate` when `status.choice` is set (after signing in again with local changes AND cloud changes): `src/sync/SignedOutGate.tsx:24-29` -> `src/sync/SyncChoiceDialog.tsx:~36` (testid `sync-choice`).
- Features: title "Changes on this device and in the cloud" (`:43`); text "While you were signed out, this device has changes the cloud does not have." (`:46`); counts "Changed on this device: {n}." (testid `choice-here`) and "Changed in the cloud: {n}." (testid `choice-there`) where n is "{n} page(s)" / "{n} item(s)" / "nothing" (`:28-31`); safety line "Nothing is deleted: the version you do not pick is kept in the page’s History." (`:57`); three choices: "Merge both" - "Keeps both. Where the same line differs you can choose Keep both." (`:68-71`, primary), "Use this device’s version" - "The cloud version goes to History." (`:81-84`), "Use the cloud version" - "This device’s version goes to History." (`:94-97`). All disabled while busy.

#### Changed-on-two-devices bars (inline conflict UI)

- Opens from: automatically at the top of a page when a block has a `clash` (`src/editor/ClashBars.tsx`, set by the sync merge, `src/sync/clash.ts`, `merge.ts`).
- Features: one bar per clashed block (`:124-161`, testid `clash-bar`): title "Changed on two devices" (`clash.title`), "The other version: {excerpt}" (`clash.other` `:137`), buttons "Keep mine" (`:146`, clears the mark), "Use other" (`:153`, swaps in the other text), "Keep both" (`:160`, inserts the other as a second block). When more than one: a single bar "{n} blocks changed on two devices. Choose for all of them." with "Keep mine" and "Use other" for all (`:78-114`). Choice clears the clash on every device. The losing text is also in the page History (see section 1).
- Rules: newer hybrid clock wins; Delete forever always wins; review logs append only; a clock >1 day ahead is not followed and raises the clock warning (`src/sync/merge.ts`, `hlc.ts`; docs/features/sync.md).

#### Save-failed notice (bar)

- Opens from: automatically while any editor save failed (`src/app/saveStatus.ts` `markUnsaved/markSaved/hasUnsaved`, listener `onUnsavedChange`); mounted `src/App.tsx:2043` in the bottom-centre stack.
- Main file: `src/app/SaveNotice.tsx:~10-25` (`role=alert`, not a toast, stays until saved).
- Features: text "Not saved yet. Your latest changes are kept on this device and the app keeps trying." (`save.failed`) and "Try again" (`save.retry`) -> `retryUnsaved()` (`saveStatus.ts:25`).

#### Sign-in-guide state flag

- `src/app/signInGuideState.ts:10`: pref `onboarding.guide` (shared flag, no UI).

#### Offline / install behaviour (no dedicated screen)

- Service worker via `vite-plugin-pwa`, offline-first, installable (docs/features/offline-and-install.md). Surfaces: "Offline" dot state, offline messages in sign-in, update pill "No connection" (`upd.offline`), Settings > This device section (`src/settings/DeviceSection.tsx`, storage protection / install steps; outside this audit's file list but referenced by the doc). ErrorBoundary offers "Go home" and "Save everything".
- DB-will-not-open screen: "Your notes could not be opened" / "They are most likely still safe on this device. Close other tabs of this app, then try again. If this keeps happening, restart the device." / "Try again" (`open.failed.*`, in `src/App.tsx`).

#### Sync engine / connectors (no UI, listed so nothing is lost)

- `src/sync/engine.ts` (push/pull/merge/apply), `syncService.ts` (phases `signedOut | needsSetup | needsKey | ready | syncing`, `syncStatus.ts:9-14`), `outbox.ts`, `merge.ts` (modes `record|blocks|append|file|local`, `tables.ts:14-16`), `realtime.ts` (WebSocket live feed; 10 s polling when closed, 60 s safety net), `crypto.ts` (AES-256-GCM, Argon2id 64 MiB), `setup.ts` (passphrase + recovery code + `replaceRecoveryCode`), `keyVault.ts`, `drivePass.ts` / `driveServer.ts` / `driveDiag.ts` (Google Drive encrypted file store, spare key), `claudeServer.ts` / `connectServer.ts` (Claude connector server calls), `remoteSignOut.ts`, `deviceCheck.ts`.

### 5. APP LOCK / PAGE LOCK (privacy lock)

#### Settings > Lock (settings category)

- Opens from: Settings category "Lock" `src/settings/SettingsScreen.tsx:174`; toast action "Open Settings" from lock menus (`openSettings('lock.idleMinutes')`, `src/app/lock/lockMenu.ts:44,84`).
- Main file: `src/app/lock/LockSection.tsx:~60-423` (testid `lock-section`; title "Privacy lock" `:86`).
- Features:
  - Explanations `lock.explain` / `lock.explain2` (privacy lock, not encryption) `:89-90`.
  - "Ways to unlock" (`lock.methods` `:92`): **PIN** row (`:94`, hint "4 to 6 digits."): status "is set / is not set", button "Set up" / "Change" (`:304`), "Remove" (`:308`); **Password** row (`:109`, hint "At least 6 characters.", `PASSWORD_MIN = 6` `src/core/lock/lockCrypto.ts:24`); **Face ID, Touch ID or Windows Hello** row (`:124`, WebAuthn platform passkey; hint "Uses this device's own lock (a passkey). It works on this device only." or "This browser or device cannot do this. Use a PIN or a password." `:127-128`), set up/remove with toasts "Device lock is on. Your PIN or password still works if it fails." / "Could not set up the device lock." (`:138-140`) and detailed failures (`lock.device.cancelled/refused/notSetUp/unsupported/unavailable`).
  - Set/change form (`:330-420`): "New PIN (4 to 6 digits)" / "New password" input (`inputMode` numeric for PIN `:369`, `autoFocus` `:367`), "Type it again" (`lock.confirm` `:382`), mismatch "The two do not match." (`:345`), Save button (`dialog.save` `:418`), toast "Saved. Only a scrambled fingerprint of it is kept."; when Sync is on a note appears "Sync is on, so this code travels with your synced settings. Use 12 or more characters..." (`lock.sync.pin/password`, testid `lock-sync-note` `:401`) plus "Longer is safer: aim for 12 or more characters." (`:406`).
  - If nothing set: "Set up at least one way to unlock before you lock anything." (`:158`).
  - "Locked now" (`lock.locked` `:160`) list of locked pages/notebooks/databases (testid `locked-list` `:165`) with "🔒 {title} ({page|notebook|database})" and a per-item "Remove lock" (`:206`) -> toast "Lock removed."; empty "Nothing is locked." (`:162`).
  - "Remove the lock completely" (`:220`) -> confirm "Remove every lock and every code ({n} locked)? Your notes stay exactly as they are, and anyone with this device can read them." -> "Remove the lock" (`:251-252`), toast "The lock was removed. Your notes are unchanged."
  - "Lock again after" dropdown (setting `lock.idleMinutes`, registry `src/settings/lockSettings.ts`, choices 1, 2, 5, 10, 15, 30 minutes, default 5, `src/core/lock/lockState.ts:30-31`); synced under the "Lock" sync group.
  - Changing or removing any way, or removing locks, first opens the authorization dialog (`AuthDialog`, `LockDialogs.tsx:171`): "Confirm it is you" / "Enter your current code, or use this device, to change the lock." The first way to unlock can be set without asking (`LockSection.tsx:66`).
- Gating: items need at least one method before locking; device-lock row disabled when the browser cannot do WebAuthn.

#### Lock screen (full-screen/inline gate)

- Opens from: opening a locked page / notebook / database (gates `LockGate`/`PageGate`/`DatabaseGate`; `src/app/lock/LockScreen.tsx:~38`, testid `lock-screen`).
- Features: title (lock + page title or "Untitled" `:45-47`), "This page/notebook/database is locked." (`lock.locked.<kind>` `:49`), then the unlock form (`src/app/lock/UnlockForm.tsx`): "Unlock with this device" (`lock.unlock.device`, testid `unlock-device` `:107-110`; "Face ID, Touch ID or Windows Hello"), method select "Unlock with" when PIN and password both exist (`:118`), PIN/password input (numeric keypad for PIN `:164`, placeholders "Type your PIN here" / "Type your password here"), "Unlock" (`:177`), errors "That is not right. Try again." (`lock.wrong` `:191`), "Too many tries. Wait {n} seconds." / "...about {n} minutes." (`:188-189`; 5 wrong tries then 30 s doubling), device failure "The device lock did not confirm. Use your PIN or password instead.", "Forgot?" link (`:202`).
- Forgot dialog (`ForgotDialog`, `LockDialogs.tsx:60-165`): title "Forgot your code?", honest explanation `lock.forgot.what`; if device lock set: text + "Unlock with this device" (`:123-130`) then toast "Unlocked. Set a new PIN or password in Settings › Lock."; "Locked now: {n}." (`:141`); acknowledgement checkbox "I understand that every lock and code will be removed and my notes will be readable by anyone with this device." (`:150`); "Remove the lock" (`:158`).
- Auto-relock (`src/app/lock/useLock.ts`): on leaving the locked place (`:~30-48`), when the app is hidden/closed (`visibilitychange` hidden and `pagehide` `:56-66`; also blanks the app via `html[data-app-hidden]` for the app switcher snapshot), and after the idle minutes with no `pointerdown/keydown/wheel/touchstart` (`:75-90`).
- Search/review/sidebar behaviour for locked items documented in docs/features/lock.md (search shows title with 🔒 only; review excludes cards).

#### Lock menu items (⋯ menus)

- Page ⋯ (`src/pages/PageView.tsx:250` `useLockItem`), notebook ⋯ (`src/notebooks/NotebookScreen.tsx:94`), database ⋯ (`src/databases/DatabasesHub.tsx:164`): label "Lock this page/notebook/database" or "Remove lock from this ..." (`lock.menu.lock.*`/`lock.menu.remove.*`, `src/app/lock/lockMenu.ts:54`, icon 🔒). Lock when no method: toast "Set up a PIN, a password or Face ID in Settings › Lock first." + "Open Settings" (`:41-46`). Success toast "Locked. It opens with your PIN, password or device lock." / "Lock removed."
- Also in the ribbon File > Protect and the control panel (docs).

#### Edit lock (separate from privacy lock)

- Menu items: "Lock editing" (page) / "Lock editing (all pages)" (notebook) / "Unlock editing" with icons 🔏/🔓 (`src/app/lock/editLockMenu.ts:15-33`); used at `src/pages/PageView.tsx:321` (only when not already notebook/account locked `:317-321`), `PageRowMenu.tsx:74`, `NotebookScreen.tsx:158`, `NotebookRowMenu.tsx:62`. Toasts "Locked. Nothing can be changed until you unlock it." / "Unlocked. You can edit again." Bars "Locked — tap to unlock" / "Notebook locked — tap to unlock"; refusal "This is locked for editing. Unlock it first." (`editLock.refused`). Edit-lock source `'account'` is the read-only sign-in gate.

### 6. ONBOARDING

#### Welcome sheet (first-run wizard dialog)

- Opens from: automatically on the first start of a device when `onboarding.pending` is set and `onboarding.done` is not (`src/onboarding/onboardingState.ts:25-27`, `welcomeNeeded`); again from Settings > This device "Show the welcome again" (`settings.welcomeAgain`, `src/settings/DeviceSection.tsx:65`, store `welcomeOpen` `onboardingState.ts:30`). Mounted `src/App.tsx:1969-1984` (hidden while the SignInGuide is open). Existing users never get it on update.
- Main file: `src/onboarding/Welcome.tsx:98-300` (portal `<dialog>` testid `welcome`, aria "Welcome"). Escape: `if (step > 0) skip()` - step 0 has no way out (`:163`).
- Steps (`STEPS = 3` counts steps 1-3 only):
  - Step 0 (only when not signed in; skipped if already signed in `:99-101`): "Sign in or create an account" (`signin.title`) + "One account keeps your notes on all your devices. After the first time, this device stays signed in, even offline." (`signin.help` `:174-175`) + the Sign-in panel (`renderSignIn` `:176`); offline Google attempt -> "You need to be online to sign in..." (`:147-151`). No Skip, no Next at step 0 (`:268,287`). Accounts required since 9 Nov 2026 (CLAUDE.md, owner-approved).
  - Step 1 "How will you write?" (`welcome.way.title` `:189`) + "This sets up the toolbar for you. You can change it any time." (`:191`): radio-like choices Keyboard ("Type with a keyboard and mouse or trackpad."), Finger ("Tap and type on a phone or tablet with your finger."), Apple Pencil ("The Pencil writes and draws; your finger scrolls and taps the tools.") `:194-217`. Pre-selected by `suggestKind(browserFacts(...))` (`:42-46`). On Next, `applyWay` sets the device mode (computer / touch / pencil) and the "chosen kind" (computer / tablet / phone) and marks the device picker done; Pencil also triggers `offerLastToolOnce()` (`:111-121`).
  - Step 2 "Language and theme" (`:227`): segmented buttons for Language (`settings.language.label`) and Theme (`settings.theme.label`) via `SettingButtons` `:224-238`, applied immediately.
  - Step 3 "Where do you want to start?" (`:242`) with help "The Start here notebook has five short pages that teach the app by doing. You can delete it later." and two buttons: "Start with the Start here notebook" (`data-start=sample` `:254`) and "Start empty" (`data-start=empty` `:262`).
  - Footer buttons: "Skip" (steps >0, `:274`), "Back" (steps >1, `:284`), "Next" (steps 1-2, `:293`); step counter "Step {n} of 3" (`:168`).
- "Start here" notebook (`src/onboarding/startHere.ts`): five pages built lazily in the app's language: "1. Write on paper", "2. Make a box", "3. Lasso and convert", "4. Write pad", "5. Make a card and review it" (keys `startHere.p1-p5`, title "Start here" in `src/i18n/en/late/help.ts:647-669`). Created by `startHere()` via `onFinish(true)` (`App.tsx:1979`).
- Skip semantics: Skip or Finish sets `onboarding.done`, clears `onboarding.pending`, and `devicePickerDone` (`:123-126`); Skip does NOT apply the step-1 choice.
- Related flag: `onboarding.accountAsked` (`onboardingState.ts:35`) - the doc says the first screen offered "Use without an account", which no longer exists (see surprises).

### 7. CLAUDE CONNECTOR, INBOX, ACTIVITY

#### Settings > Claude (settings category, tabbed)

- Opens from: Settings category "Claude" `src/settings/SettingsScreen.tsx:185-189`.
- Main file: `src/connector/ClaudeArea.tsx:23` - tab list `role=tablist` aria "Claude": "Settings" (`claude.tab.settings`), "Inbox" or "Inbox ({count})" with the live count of pending items (`:25-40`, from table `claudeInbox`), "Activity" (`:33-43`); panel `role=tabpanel` `:71-81`. Tabs are lazy-loaded. Testids `claude-tab-<id>`.

#### Claude > Settings tab (on/off, privacy, scope, connected apps)

- Main file: `src/connector/ClaudeSection.tsx` (testid `claude-section`; title "Let Claude work with my notes" `claude.title` `:240`; description `claude.what` `:242` - Claude on claude.ai can search/read chosen notebooks and add notebooks, pages, recall cards and databases; can never delete; small changes to existing pages apply with the earlier version kept in History, bigger ones and card changes go to the Inbox).
- Features: privacy block "What this means for your privacy" with 3 bullets (`claude.privacy.1-3` `:244-248`: key given to the server once; the server (and whoever controls it) can open chosen notebooks; off by default, turning off deletes the key copy, switches off by itself after 30 days without use).
- State machine (`:252-396`): not signed in -> "Sign in to your account first (Settings › Account)." (`:254`); sync phase `needsSetup` -> "Set up sync first (Settings › Sync): Claude uses the same key." (`:258`); loading -> "Checking…"; error -> message ("Could not reach the server. Check your internet and try again." / login / "This is not set up on the server yet.") + "Try again" (`:266-284`; no retry when unavailable); server reports unavailable -> `claude.error.unavailable` `:290`.
  - **On** (`:294-366`): "On" (testid `claude-state`); "Last used {when}. It switches itself off on {expires} if it is not used before then." (`:300`); notebook choice (`claude.choice.title` "Which notebooks can Claude use?" `:120`: radios "All my notebooks" `:131` / "Only the notebooks I pick" `:142` + checklist of notebooks `:154-156`, error "Pick at least one notebook." `:162`); "Save choice" (`:318`, disabled when unchanged or invalid via `scopeToSend`/`sameChoice` in `src/connector/claudeChoice.ts`) -> status "Saved."; "Turn off" (`:327`, testid `claude-off`) -> confirm "Turn off Claude? The server deletes its copy of your key and every Claude connection stops now." -> "Turn off" (`:390-391`); "Connected apps" list (`claude.apps.title` `:339`) with "{name}, last active {when}" and per-row "Revoke" (aria "Revoke {name}") `:352-364`, empty "No app is connected yet. Add the connector in claude.ai under Settings, Connectors." (`:342`).
  - **Off** (`:374-386`): "Off"; the same notebook choice; an unlock form (passphrase only, `UnlockForm` kinds=['passphrase']) titled "Turn it on", help "Type your sync passphrase to confirm. The passphrase stays on this device; only the key it opens is sent, once.", submit "Turn on" (testid `claude-unlock`).

#### Claude > Inbox tab

- Main file: `src/connector/inbox/ClaudeInbox.tsx` (testid `claude-inbox`; title "Inbox" `:212`; help `claude.inbox.help` `:214` "Changes Claude proposed to pages you already wrote. Nothing here has been applied yet. Accept saves the page in History first, then makes the change."); empty "Nothing is waiting. When Claude proposes a big change, or one that touches recall cards, it appears here." (`:217`).
- Item card (`:97-190`, testid `claude-inbox-item`): "Changes to \"{page}\"" (`:130`); counts "{n} added, {n} replaced" (`:134`); reason chips "More than 30% of the page" / "Touches recall cards" / "The page changed after Claude read it" (`claude.inbox.reason.big/cards/conflict` `:145`); "Show changes"/"Hide changes" toggle (`:152-155`, `aria-expanded`) revealing a diff list (`DiffView :30-90`, testid `claude-diff`): per row "Replaces" ... "Now" / "Claude wants (type)" or "New (type), at the start / after \"{text}\" / at the end", "(empty)", or "This block is no longer on the page."; "Accept" (aria "Accept changes to {page}", testid `claude-accept` `:171-176`, disabled when busy or `failed`) -> toast "Accepted. The page as it was is in History."; "Reject" (testid `claude-reject` `:181-186`) -> toast "Rejected. Nothing was changed."; errors in-card: "This cannot be applied: a block Claude wanted to change is gone. Reject it." / "This page is no longer in your notes." / "Something went wrong. Nothing was changed." (`:115-119,164`).
- "Decided ({count})" history (`:228-240`): "Changes to \"{page}\", Accepted {when} / Rejected {when}".
- Service: `src/connector/inbox/inboxService.ts` (accept/reject/apply, takes a `claude`-reason version first).

#### Claude > Activity tab

- Main file: `src/connector/inbox/ClaudeActivity.tsx:76` (testid `claude-activity`; title "Activity" `:108`; help "Everything Claude wrote in your notes: when, and where. The text itself is never listed here." `:110`).
- Features: loading "Checking…" (`:112`), error "Could not reach the server..." (`:117`), empty "Claude has not changed anything yet." (`:123`); rows (testid `claude-activity-row`) "{what}: {where}" + time; `what` from tools: "Added recall cards", "Added a page", "Changed the look", "Created a database", "Created a notebook", "Changed a page", "Proposed a change (waiting in the Inbox)" (`:24-31, :129-135`); `where` = page/notebook title or "something that is no longer in your notes" (`:45-74`); "Undo" button (aria "Undo: {what}") only for an applied `refine_page` whose page still exists (`canUndo` `:137`, `:153-160`) -> opens the Page history dialog (the copy labelled "before a change by Claude" is there); footnote "To remove this, open the page and use \"Remove this AI result\"." (`claude.activity.noUndo` `:164`); toast "The page was restored." on restore. Data comes from the server (`claudeActivity(accessToken)`), so it needs sign-in + network.

#### Connect page (OAuth-style consent screen)

- Opens from: link from claude.ai to `#/connect?...` (`isConnectRoute`, `src/connector/connectRoute.ts:2-3`).
- Main file: `src/connector/ConnectPage.tsx:~40-164`. Title "Connect Claude" (`:101`); working "Connecting…" (`:103`); consent: "{name} wants to use your Learning Notes. It never sees your password." (`:110`), "It will be able to:" with scopes "Read your notebooks, pages and recall cards" and "Create notebooks, pages, cards and databases, and add to pages. It can never delete anything." (`:112-122`), "After you choose, you go back to {host}." (`:123`); buttons "Allow" (`:131`) and "Not now" (`:138`, `decide('deny')`); redirect only if `isClaudeAddress(redirect)` (`:83`). Signed-out: "Sign in to Learning Notes in this browser first (Settings › Account), then connect again from Claude." + "Open Learning Notes" (`:145-147`). Errors "This connection link is not valid. Start again from Claude." (`:153`) / "Could not connect. Check your internet and try again." (`:158`).

#### Connector spec / MCP (no UI)

- `src/connector/spec.ts` (848 lines) and `spec.schema.json`: the tool contract shared with the server (tool names seen in Activity: `add_cards, add_page, apply_style, create_database, create_notebook, refine_page`). `inboxService.ts`, `claudeSettings.ts`, `claudeChoice.ts` are logic.

### 8. UPDATES

#### Update pill (chip)

- Opens from: automatic, whenever the update status is not `idle` and no mandatory update is showing (`src/App.tsx:2034-2038`). Also driven by Settings > About "Check for updates now".
- Main file: `src/app/UpdatePill.tsx` (`role=status aria-live=polite` `:63-64`). Texts by phase (`:19-55`): "Checking for updates…" / "Downloading the update…" or "Downloading {loaded} / {total} MB ({pct}%)" (+ " · slow connection, about {s} s left") / "Installing…" / "Update ready" / "Restarting into the new version…" / "Updated to version {version}" / "You have the latest version" / "No connection" / "The update did not finish. It will try again." Transient ("brief") phases auto-clear after 4 s (`:12-16`).

#### Settings > About check button

- `src/settings/AboutSection.tsx:40-75`: status line "Checking…" / "Update ready. Use the Update button at the top." / "Up to date." / "Could not check. Are you offline?" and button "Check for updates now" (`about.check`, disabled while checking). Also version "Version {commit}, built {date}" and "Last checked for updates: {time}" / "Not checked for updates yet."
- Setting "Update automatically" (`settings.autoUpdate.label`, `src/settings/builtinSettings.ts:454`): applies a new version by itself when safe (nothing unsaved, no pen down, idle a few seconds); Off = an "Update" button instead. Logic `src/app/autoUpdate.ts` (idle 1.5 s, foreground 1.5 s, backup max 8 s, splash max 5 s).

#### Update risk bar (bar)

- Opens from: `updateQuestion` in the bottom-centre stack `src/App.tsx:~2050` when an update is ready but the safety copy could not be made / device low on space / unsaved changes.
- Main file: `src/app/UpdateBar.tsx:5-40` (`role=status`). Texts: "A new version is ready, but some changes could not be saved yet." / "...this device is low on space, so no safety copy was made." / "...the safety copy of your notes could not be made." (`update.risk.unsaved/space/backup` `:18-21`); buttons "Update anyway" (`:34`) and "Not now" (`:37`).

#### Mandatory update (blocking dialog)

- Opens from: automatic when a required (database-changing) version is detected: `src/App.tsx:2029-2033` (`mandatoryUpdate` store set by `startMandatoryUpdate`, `src/app/mandatoryFlow.ts:86`, started at `src/main.tsx:186`).
- Main file: `src/app/MandatoryUpdate.tsx:~10-110` (`role=alertdialog aria-modal`, `#root` set `inert` while open `:19-24`, primary button auto-focused `:28-30`).
- Phases: "Update required" with body "This update changes how your notes are stored, so it is needed to keep going. Your notes stay safe." -> countdown "...Downloading starts in {s} s." (`COUNTDOWN_S = 5`, `mandatoryFlow.ts:17`) -> "Downloading the update… {pct}%" with progressbar (`:74-78`) -> "Installing" / "Please keep this window open. Your notes are not touched." (`INSTALL_MAX_MS 30 s`, `FORCE_RESTART_MS 6 s`) -> "paused": "The download was stopped. The app stays paused until it is updated." -> "failed": "The update did not finish. Check your connection and try again. Your notes are safe." or "Some changes are not saved yet, so the update is waiting. Your notes are safe. Try again." Buttons: "Cancel" (`dialog.cancel`, `:94`, during countdown/download) and "Retry" (`update.req.retry`, `:104`, when paused/failed).

#### Upgrade gate (database upgrade / backup dialog)

- Opens from: wraps the whole app at start `src/main.tsx:329` (`UpgradeGate`). Shows nothing while checking (`:81`), nothing (children) when ready.
- Main file: `src/app/UpgradeGate.tsx:74-127`. The silent rolling backup (`runAutoBackup`, last 3 copies `<db>-auto-1..3`, `:42-47`) runs first; the dialog (`role=dialog`) appears only when it failed or space is under 25 MB: title "Updating how your notes are stored", "Your notes need a quick one-time update.", detail one of "A backup copy of everything was saved in this browser first. You can also save a backup file to your computer." / "The backup copy in this browser could not be saved (the browser may be out of space). Please save a backup file before updating." / "The app could not check your saved notes ({message}). Updating will still try to open them." (`:101-104`); buttons "Skip the file, just update" (`:112`) and (when a snapshot can be read) "Save backup file and update" (autofocus, `:118-124`; downloads the snapshot via a temporary `<a>` then continues).

#### Other bits in updates folder (no UI)

- `updates.ts` (offer logic; check every 10 min, throttle 2 min `:249-250`), `updateChannel.ts`, `updateProgress.ts`, `updateStatus.ts`, `mandatoryState.ts`, `mandatoryFlow.ts`.

### SURPRISING / DUPLICATED / HALF-BUILT

1. Docs vs code drift on history: `docs/features/page-history.md` says 30 copies and one per 5 minutes; code is `KEEP_VERSIONS = 300`, `VERSION_GAP_MS = 2 min` (`src/history/versionService.ts:13-17`) and the UI strings `history.empty` ("about one every 5 minutes") and `history.keep` (2 h / hourly / daily tiers, which matches code) are inconsistent with each other. `history.empty` should say about 2 minutes.
2. Backup reminder snooze: code is 90 days (`backupReminder.ts:11`), `docs/features/backup-and-files.md` says 24 hours.
3. Trash row buttons are hover/focus-revealed only (`TrashSection.tsx:99`): on a touch device the Restore and Delete forever buttons are invisible until a row gets focus. Trash has no automatic purge or retention limit at all (by design per docs, but users may expect 30 days).
4. Restore/Import in Save & restore is not guarded by the read-only account gate (no `isReadOnlyGate` in `DataScreen.tsx`); an unsigned device can still "Replace my notes" or import, which edits data. Verify intent.
5. Unused / orphan i18n keys: `data.export.passwordHint` (never rendered), `update.auto.done` (replaced by `refresh.auto.done` in `App.tsx:964`), `setting.tag.all` / `setting.tag.only` (badge file only renders `setting.tag.device` and a cloud icon chip; confirm), `data.restore.text` and `gate.sheet.help` need a render check.
6. "Use without an account" is gone (accounts required since 9 Nov), but `docs/features/sync.md` still describes it, and the Welcome flag `onboarding.accountAsked` plus the comment in `onboardingState.ts:32-34` still refer to it. The sync message `sync.notAllowed` still says "Only the owner’s Google account can sync." and the sign-in error `signin.err.refused` says "The app is in private testing" even though email accounts are open.
7. The About section tells the user "Use the Update button at the top" (`about.ready`) but updates are now offered through the Attention banner ("Update", L2/L3) and the Update pill; there is no persistent top "Update" button in this scope.
8. Duplicate locations for the same job: three hosts of `SignInPanel` (Welcome step 0, Account, read-only sheet) hand off differently (`SignInGuide` vs inline); "Sign in" appears also in Sync (link), the banner, the lock-out screen and the Claude section. Sync-now exists only in Account, not in the Sync section. Page "Import" menu item just opens Save & restore (no page-level import).
9. Two different lock concepts both called "lock": privacy lock (`locked`, PIN/password/passkey; Settings > Lock) and edit lock (`editLocked`, "Lock editing") plus the account read-only gate that reuses the edit-lock source `'account'`.
10. Password minimums differ in three places: file password 8 (`share/crypto.ts:18`), privacy-lock password 6 (`core/lock/lockCrypto.ts:24`), account password 15 (`sync/passwordRules.ts:6`), sync passphrase 12 letters (`sync.tooShort`); PIN 4-6 digits.
11. `src/editor/diff.ts` is listed in the brief under history but is the editor-database change detector, not a user-facing diff; the only user-facing diff is the Claude Inbox `DiffView` (`ClaudeInbox.tsx:30`). The History dialog shows a plain text preview with no highlighting of what changed versus the live page.
12. History preview does not render ink, tables, pictures or Card blocks beyond text; it only counts boxes and ink.
13. The sidebar "Trash" is not available on any other surface: no Trash screen on phone width beyond the sidebar; Data screen does not link to it.
14. Share sheet: the only native share sheet use is the Export dialog "Share..." (file share). There is no "share link" or collaboration feature (project rule: no collaboration). The "Share one notebook" section is a file export (named "share" but sends nothing).
15. NotebookLM import accepts `.webm` as audio but video detection is by `.mp4/.mov/.m4v` or MIME `video/*`; a `.webm` video file with MIME video/webm is classed video, a `.webm` audio file by extension only.
16. Account-level tooling hidden in Settings search only via `accountSettings.ts`: the Account rows are discoverable by search keywords but have no direct deep links except `account.signIn` (`data-setting-id`, `AccountSection.tsx:~80`).

# APPENDIX G (07-shell-system.md)

## 07 - App-wide shell and system UI inventory

Read-only audit of `c:\Users\pc\Downloads\learning-notes` (2026-11-12). All paths are relative to the repo root. Line numbers were verified in code (App.tsx = `src/App.tsx`, 2,093 lines; main.tsx = 338 lines; ui.tsx = 698 lines).

Short answers up front:

- There is NO shortcuts help screen and NO standalone Help screen. "Help" in the quiet corner just opens Settings at "Show help" (`src/App.tsx:1431`, `src/app/spaces/QuietCorner.tsx:170`).
- The nearest thing to a command palette is the one Search popup (`src/search/SearchPopup.tsx`, Ctrl/Cmd+K and Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+K) with its **Features** chip, which lists every tool in `src/app/tools/toolRegistry.ts` (70 tool ids, 16 with shortcut labels) plus every setting (docs/features/control-panel.md).
- There is NO alert-dialog primitive and NO bottom-sheet primitive in `src/app/ui.tsx`. Dialogs are native `<dialog>` elements (26 files use `<dialog`). "Sheets" in this app are just styled dialogs (NameSheet, LassoMenuSheet, SaveToSheet ...).
- There is NO browser Notification / push / app-badge code (grep for `Notification`, `showNotification`, `setAppBadge`, `PushManager` found nothing in `src`). "Reminders" = the in-app Backup reminder bar only. "Badges" = in-app due-count pills on Sidebar rows.

### PART B - System surfaces

#### Toast system (toast)

- **Opens from:** `AppUi.toast(message, action?)` from `AppUiContext` (`src/app/appUi.ts:36-37`) or `getAppActions().toast` (`src/App.tsx:1254`). ~261 call sites in 93 files (grep `toast(`).
- **Main file:line:** state + timer `src/App.tsx:857-897`; component `src/app/ui.tsx:401-471`; timing `src/app/toastTiming.ts:1-23`; mounted `src/App.tsx:2068-2075`.
- **Features:**
  - One kind only: a message string plus optional ONE action button `{label, onSelect}` (`appUi.ts:7-10`, `ui.tsx:460-468`). There are no severity types (no success/error/warn variants, no icon). The "kinds" are by usage: plain message, message + Undo, message + Restore (`search.trashed` `App.tsx:552-560`), message + "Open Settings" (`lock.needSetup`, `lockMenu.ts:42-46,82-86`), message + "Turn on" (`handwriting.scribble.turnOn`), "Open" (`pdftools.open`), "Change" (`sync.allOn.change`).
  - Action labels found near toast calls (approx. counts): `annot.undo` 10, `review.undo` 8, `reading.undo` 4, `search.restore` 2, `paper.box.undo` 2, `lock.openSettings` 2, `audio.undo` 2, plus one each `mode.undo`, `ink.fav.undo`, `sync.allOn.change`, `pdftools.open`, `handwriting.scribble.turnOn`. Choosing the action also closes the toast (`App.tsx:881-887`).
  - Duration rule: `1800 + 45 ms * characters`, clamped 2,500 to 8,000 ms; with an action at least 6,000 ms (`toastTiming.ts:2-16`).
  - Hold: pointer enter or pointer down keeps it open; pointer leave (not touch) or a tap elsewhere releases; after release it closes in 1,500 ms (`toastTiming.ts:7`, `App.tsx:863-869`, `ui.tsx:414-448`).
  - Stacking: NONE. A new toast replaces the current one (`setToastMessage`, `App.tsx:889`) and restarts the timer. There is no queue.
  - Position: `fixed bottom-5 left-1/2 z-40`, centred by `centreEl` (whole-pixel margin, `centreEl.ts`), `max-w-[calc(100vw-32px)]`, raised above the on-screen keyboard plus its 44 px bar when keyboard inset > 40 px (`toastBottom`, `toastTiming.ts:19-23`, `ui.tsx:456`). `role="status"`, `data-toast`, `data-popup-trigger` (a tap on a toast is not an "outside" tap for open pills, `ui.tsx:451-454`).
  - Built-in toasts raised by the shell: `attention.fixed` (Drive reconnected, `App.tsx:938`), `upd.updated` (after update restart, `App.tsx:952`), `refresh.auto.done` (`App.tsx:964`), `storage.full` (at most once a minute, `App.tsx:970-978`), `nav.gone` (`App.tsx:1024`), `app.journalCreated` (`App.tsx:1135`), `app.newPageFromTemplate` (`App.tsx:1084`), `fav.added/removed` (`App.tsx:1307`), `tool.needsPage` (`App.tsx:1271,1289,1302`), auto device-mode switch `mode.autoSwitched` + Undo (`useAutoDeviceMode`, `App.tsx:899`).
- **Gating:** none; `toast` is a no-op in the default context (`appUi.ts:44`).
- **States:** shown / held / released. No exit animation; unmounts.

Other "notice" surfaces that are NOT toasts (separate components, can sit at the same time):

- `SaveNotice` red persistent alert "Not saved yet..." + Try again, never auto-closes (`src/app/SaveNotice.tsx:9-27`, mounted `App.tsx:2043`).
- `SettingsNotice` ("Settings now follow you. {n} changed ... Undo / OK"), own React root appended to `document.body`, `fixed inset-x-0 bottom-4 z-50` (`src/settings/settingsNotice.tsx:11-35`, mounted `src/settings/settingsNoticeMount.ts:5-12`, started `src/main.tsx:124-133`).
- `UpdateRiskBar` (update could lose data: Update anyway / Not now) in the bottom-centre stack (`src/app/UpdateBar.tsx`, `App.tsx:2045-2051`).
- `FirstUseTip` one-line tip with close, auto-dismiss 12 s (`src/help/FirstUseTip.tsx:21-24`).
- `BackupReminder` bottom bar (`src/share/ReminderBar.tsx`, `App.tsx:2011-2019`).
- `UpdatePill` bottom-left status (checking / downloading with % bar / installing / ready / updating / updated / current / offline / failed; brief answers vanish after 4 s) (`src/app/UpdatePill.tsx:7-79`).
- `PencilOffer` card "Use Pencil mode?" Yes / Not now (`src/settings/PencilOffer.tsx:12-44`).
- `NowPlayingPill`, `TimerChip` (bottom corner pills; phone: `bottom` above the tab bar) (`src/app/reading/NowPlayingPill.tsx:35`, `src/home/TimerChip.tsx:83`).

#### Confirm / name / choice dialogs (dialog)

- **Opens from:** `<ConfirmDialog>` rendered directly by features (17 usages, 16 files); `appUi.choose(request)` (39 call sites, 17 files); `appUi.askName` (5 sites); `<NameDialog>` (8 usages, 7 files).
- **Main file:line:** `src/app/ui.tsx:473-531` (Confirm), `533-629` (Name), `631-698` (Choice); host in `src/App.tsx:2002-2010` (choice), `2054-2067` (name sheet).
- **Features:**
  - `ConfirmDialog` `{message, confirmLabel, cancelLabel?, onConfirm, onCancel}`: native `<dialog>` `showModal()`, `w-80`, backdrop click = cancel, Esc = cancel. Confirm button is ALWAYS red (`bg-red-600`, `ui.tsx:524`) even when the action is not destructive. Default focus: Confirm on mouse devices, Cancel on touch (`autoFocus={touchUi()}` `ui.tsx:514,522`). Cancel default label `ui.cancel` "Cancel" (`ui.tsx:518`). `data-palm-guard` (ui.tsx:502).
  - `NameDialog` `{title, label, initialValue, confirmLabel}`: single input `id="name-dialog"`, text pre-selected, Enter submits (trimmed), buttons `dialog.cancel` / confirm; backdrop click closes only if untouched, else opens an inner ConfirmDialog "Discard your changes?" [Discard / Keep editing] (`ui.tsx:557-629`). This is a hand-copied version of `useDiscardGuard` (see below).
  - `ChoiceDialog` `ChoiceRequest {title, message, options[{value,label,primary?}], settingId?}`: title `<h2>`, message, buttons in order; primary has focus (Enter); optional left-aligned underlined link `dialog.changeInSettings` "Change in Settings" that cancels and calls `openSettings(settingId)` (`ui.tsx:668-679`, `appUi.ts:13-20`). Esc/backdrop resolve `null`.
  - NO alert-only dialog exists (no single-OK "alert"). The only native `window.prompt` fallback is in the Pencil lab when copy fails (`src/app/lab/PencilLab.tsx:128`).
  - Dialog text keys: `dialog.cancel` Cancel, `dialog.changeInSettings`, `dialog.discard` "Discard your changes?", `dialog.discardButton` Discard, `dialog.keepEditing`, `dialog.save`, `ui.cancel`, `ui.delete`, `ui.name`.
- **Gating:** none. Choice/template/name requests are single-slot state in App; a second `choose()` while one is open replaces the first request without resolving it (`App.tsx:868-870`, see Part F).
- **States:** open, answered, cancelled (null).

#### MenuPopover (generic menu primitive) and its variants (menu)

- **Opens from:** 26 usages in 21 files (sidebar add menu, row menus, page menu, notebook menu, device mode, reader controls, page tabs, NotesViews, etc.).
- **Main file:line:** `src/app/ui.tsx:181-395` (type `MenuItem` `181-199`).
- **Features:**
  - Item fields: `label`, `icon`, `onSelect`, `disabled`, `hint` (right-side small text e.g. shortcut), `description` (second line), `checked` (radio tick -> `menuitemradio`), `submenu` (shows `▸`, nested popover width `w-64`), `danger` (red, forced to the end of the list), `inPlace` (red but keeps its position) (`ui.tsx:181-199, 316-319`).
  - Placement: portal to `document.body`, `fixed z-50`, `w-52` (nested `w-64`), glass, scroll inside if tall; measured in `useLayoutEffect` against the visual viewport, flips above the anchor near the bottom, stays 8 px inside every edge (`ui.tsx:237-261`).
  - Keyboard: first enabled item focused on open; ArrowUp/ArrowDown wrap (`ui.tsx:272-292`); Esc closes and returns focus to the opener (`ui.tsx:263-270`).
  - Close rules come from `usePopup` (`src/app/popups.ts:76-163`): outside pointerdown, Esc (newest popup only), user scroll (ignored for 400 ms after open and when the scroll was not user-initiated, 1,200 ms window), `hashchange`, `popstate`; only one popup open at a time except a child opened from its parent (`opener`).
  - Header `PopupHeader` with a close button (`src/app/tools/PopupClose.tsx`, `ui.tsx:314`).
  - Variants (same primitive, different item lists): sidebar add-page menu (Blank page / From template, `src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:707-733`); notebook row menu; page row menu; NotebooksHome card menu; NotebookScreen tab menu (`NotebookScreen.tsx:305`); page ⋯ menu (`src/pages/PageView.tsx:489`); DeviceModeButton / DeviceModeMenuRow; ReaderControls (`src/app/reading/ReaderControls.tsx:232`); PageTabs (`src/app/summary/PageTabs.tsx:81`); NotesViews View/Layout menus (`NotesViews.tsx:190,197`).
  - Other menu primitive (separate, not MenuPopover): `AnchoredMenu` for ribbon/bar menus (`src/app/tools/AnchoredMenu.tsx`, `useAnchoredMenu.ts`), used by ThemePicker.
- **Gating:** items may be disabled (`disabled`), e.g. Delete when `editLocked` (`NotebookRowMenu.tsx:80`, `PageRowMenu.tsx:89`).
- **States:** closed, open, submenu open.

#### Sheets (sheet) - there is no shared sheet primitive

The word "sheet" is used for styled native dialogs only. List: NameSheet (below), `LassoMenuSheet` (`src/ink/LassoMenuSheet.tsx`, mounted `App.tsx:1986-1990`), `HandwritingChooser` (`src/app/tools/HandwritingChooser.tsx`, `App.tsx:1991-1995`), `EditListSheet` (`src/app/tools/EditListSheet.tsx`), `AaSheet` (`src/app/reading/AaSheet.tsx`, `src/reader/AaSheet.tsx` - two files with the same name, see Part F), `CropSheet` (`src/images/CropSheet.tsx`), `SaveToSheet`, `NoteSheet`, `ScanSheet`, `TemplatePicker` (`src/templates/TemplatePicker.tsx`, `App.tsx:1920-1932`), `LayerGallery`. The phone "drawer" (sidebar) is the only slide-in sheet (see Sidebar below).

#### Name sheet (NameSheet) (dialog)

- **Opens from:** `askName(title, initial)` (touch/Pencil only): new page (`App.tsx:1074-1080`), new notebook (`App.tsx:1100-1105`), rename in sidebar (`PageTree.tsx:113-119`, `Sidebar.tsx:167-175`).
- **Main file:line:** `src/app/NameSheet.tsx:15-91`; lazy wrapper `src/app/LazyNameSheet.tsx:7-19`; host `src/App.tsx:2054-2067`.
- **Features:** centred native modal `<dialog data-testid="name-sheet" data-palm-guard>`, portal to body, `w-[min(26rem,100vw-2rem)]`; title `<h2>`; large input (min-height 56 px, text-xl, `aria-label` `ui.name`, autocomplete off, autocapitalize sentences) selected on open; buttons `dialog.cancel` Cancel / `bar.done` Done (submit). Enter = Done; empty or unchanged name = same as Cancel; Esc cancels (`NameSheet.tsx:49-52`). Title keys: `nameSheet.newPage`, `nameSheet.newNotebook`, `sidebar.rename.title`.
- **Gating:** only when `touchUi()` (any-pointer: coarse) is true; mouse devices rename inline (`RenameInput`, `ui.tsx:104-136`; Enter/blur saves, Esc cancels).
- **States:** open, done, cancelled.

#### Error boundary screen and error catalogue (error)

- **Opens from:** render error in a screen (inline) or anywhere (full screen). Three boundaries: app-level `src/main.tsx:328` (`fullScreen`, `onHome=goHome` resets prefs + hash + reload `main.tsx:308-314`), main-area `src/App.tsx:1593` (`onHome=openPage(null)`, `resetKey`), tool boundary `src/app/tools/ToolsBoundary.tsx:28-79` (silent: drops half gestures, retries after 800 ms up to 3 times; renders null, no UI). `PageEditor.tsx:274` throws to reach the boundary.
- **Main file:line:** `src/app/ErrorBoundary.tsx:27-112`.
- **Features:** `role="alert"`; title `error.title` "This page could not be shown"; text `error.text` "Your notes are still stored on this device. Go home to carry on, or save a backup file first."; buttons `error.home` "Go home" (autofocus) and `error.saveAll` "Save everything" (disabled while saving); status line `error.saved` "Backup file saved." / `error.saveFailed` "The backup file could not be saved." (`ErrorBoundary.tsx:83-109`). The failure is logged by `reportError` (`errors.ts:35-40`).
- **Error message catalogue (user-facing, en):**
  - File/import errors (`src/i18n/en/common.ts:24-38`): `err.backupDamaged` "This backup file is damaged." / `err.fileDamaged` / `err.imageDamaged` / `err.listDamaged` / `err.newerFile` "This file was made by a newer version of the app." / `err.notJson` / `err.notLocked` / `err.notOurFile` / `err.noteDamaged` / `err.pageDamaged` / `err.tagsDamaged` / `err.tooBig` / `err.tooBigFile` / `err.unknownKind` / `err.wrongPassword` "Wrong password. Try again." (15 keys).
  - App-level: `open.failed.title/message/retry` (`App.tsx:1351-1371`), `save.failed`/`save.retry`, `storage.full`, `nav.gone`, `upgrade.checkFailed/browserFailed`, `update.req.failed/failedSave`, `upd.failed`, `error.*`, `lab.needNotebook`, `lab.copyFailed`, `editLock.refused`.
  - Plumbing (`src/app/errors.ts`): `onStorageFull` (QuotaExceededError chain, 1 listener), `onSignInRequired` (`SignInRequiredError` opens the sign-in sheet via AccountGate), `reportError`, `runTask` (catch + log). `window` `unhandledrejection` -> `reportError` (`App.tsx:979-984`).
- **Gating:** `resetKey` change (user navigates) clears the failed state (`ErrorBoundary.tsx:34-37`).
- **States:** ok, failed, saving, message.

#### Top bar (bar)

- **Opens from:** always visible above every screen; wrapped with the tool bar in one glass layer (`App.tsx:1554-1584`).
- **Main file:line:** `src/app/TopBar.tsx:15-138`.
- **Features (left to right):**
  - Panel toggle (`lp.open` "Panel: pages, outline, audio"; `aria-pressed`; only while a page or reader is open) (`TopBar.tsx:51-62`, `testid panel-toggle`).
  - Phone: menu button `nav.openMenu` opens the drawer (`TopBar.tsx:64-73`, `hidden phone:flex`). Wide: `nav.hideMenu` / `nav.showMenu` toggles sidebar (`TopBar.tsx:74-83`, `flex phone:hidden`).
  - Back `←` (`nav.back`, disabled when position = 0) and Forward `→` (`nav.forward`, disabled until Back used) via `useCanGoBack/Forward` (`TopBar.tsx:84-105`, `navHistory.ts:78-82`).
  - Optional Refresh app button `nav.refresh` when setting `showRefresh` = on (`TopBar.tsx:106-118`, `testid topbar-refresh`).
  - Title slot: screen name (`barTitle`, `App.tsx:1330-1349`) or the page's own path (pages portal into the slot via `TopBarSlotContext`, `TopBar.tsx:119-124`).
  - Device-mode button (monitor/hand/pencil icon, `Device mode: <mode>`) (`TopBar.tsx:125`, `DeviceModeMenu.tsx:17-50`).
  - "Show tool bar" pill (`bar.show`) when the bar is off (`TopBar.tsx:126-135`, `toolbarVisibility.ts:88-91`).
  - Below it, a `data-bar-slot` row for page bars (`App.tsx:1578-1583`).
- **Gating:** panel button needs `currentPage || hub.kind==='read'` (`App.tsx:1568-1575`); refresh button needs setting; show-tool-bar needs bar placement `floating` (= off).
- **States:** back/forward disabled; drawer open.

Screen titles (`barTitle`, `App.tsx:1330-1349`): Settings `settings.title`, `review.sidebar`, `sidebar.allCards`, `sidebar.data`, `calendar.screen`, `capture.title`, reader = null, databases `sidebar.allDatabases`, saved search name or `search.title`, notebook title.

#### Quick pages (Favourites + Recent) (sidebar section)

- **Opens from:** Sidebar > Notebooks space > "Quick access" group (`src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:433-480`).
- **Main file:line:** `src/app/QuickPages.tsx:112-155` (Favourites), `136-155` (Recent), `PageList` `36-109`.
- **Features:** Favourites heading `sidebar.favourites` (folding, pref `sidebar.favouritesOpen` default open, `QuickPages.tsx:15`), rows open page, per-row `×` button `fav.removeNamed` "Remove {name} from favourites" (`QuickPages.tsx:93-102`), hidden when empty (`:116`); Recent `sidebar.recent` (pref `sidebar.recentOpen` default closed, `:14`) shows last `RECENT_SHOWN` pages (`pageTitle.ts`), `readRecentPages()` from `search/recentPages`; current page `aria-current` bold; tips `sidebar.favourites`, `sidebar.recent`. Toggling a favourite: page menu `fav.add/fav.remove` (`PageRowMenu.tsx:59-65`), app action `toggleFavourite` (`App.tsx:1301-1309`), logic `src/app/favourites.ts:43-54` (max 200, synced via `Page.favourite` `{on,at}`, `favouriteSync.ts`).

#### Device-mode menu (menu)

- **Opens from:** top bar button; quiet corner "Device mode" row; page ⋯ menu "Device mode ▸".
- **Main file:line:** `src/app/DeviceModeMenu.tsx:17-86` (button + row), `src/app/deviceModeItems.ts:12-43` (3 rows with description + tick), `src/app/deviceMode.ts` (presets `41-62`, detection `105-130`, auto switch `140-170`, hardware keyboard `205-251`).
- **Features:** three rows: Computer ("Mouse and keyboard"), Touch ("Finger"), Pencil ("Apple Pencil"), tick on the current one, applying via `applyDeviceMode` (`deviceModeItems.ts:31`). Preset table: computer = top / compact (36 px) / tips on / hints on; touch = top / normal (44) / tips off / hints off; pencil = top / large (54) / off / off; auto-hide off for all (`deviceMode.ts:41-62`). Auto switch (`pen`/`both`/`off`, quiet 1,500 ms, `deviceMode.ts:150-170`) toasts "Switched to {mode} mode" + Undo. Tip `topBar.deviceMode`.
- **Gating:** none; auto switch via setting `advanced.autoSwitch`.
- **States:** closed/open; current mode.

#### Attention banner / gate (banner)

- **Opens from:** `<AttentionGate>` always mounted (`App.tsx:2021`).
- **Main file:line:** gate `src/app/attention/AttentionGate.tsx:57-84`; banner `AttentionBanner.tsx:137-209`; decision `attentionLevel.ts:41-62,121-131`; signals `attentionSignals.ts`.
- **Features:** ONE problem at a time (highest level not snoozed). Levels: 1 = locked pop-up (`LockedAlertGate` -> `src/sync/LockedAlert.tsx`, once per session, "Later" leaves a banner); 2 = bright accent banner; 3 = calm banner. Codes and buttons (`AttentionBanner.tsx:18-131`): `locked` L1; `driveTap` L2 "Drive is disconnected. Pictures are waiting." [Reconnect Drive / Connect Google if email-only]; `driveFull` L2 [Open Drive storage]; `notAllowed` L2 [Switch account]; `clock` L2 [Details]; `signInAgain` L2 [Sign in]; `updateNeeded` L2 [Update]; `syncFailing` L2 (3+ fails in a row or 30 min) [Try again]; `backups` L2 (2+ failed silent backups) [Open backups]; `storage` L3 (>90% quota) [Manage storage]; `setup` L3 [Set up]; `update` L3 "A new version is ready." [Update]. Dismiss `×` (`attention.dismiss`): L2 until next app start, L3 for 7 days, comes back sooner if it got worse (`attentionLevel.ts:92-119`). Banner is `fixed inset-x-0 top-0 z-40`, sets `--attn-h: 44px` which pushes the whole app shell down (`AttentionBanner.tsx:148-154`, `App.tsx:1385`). Appears only after 2 s without typing (`AttentionGate.tsx:30-50`).
- **Gating:** hidden while the device is read-only (account gate owns the spot, `AttentionGate.tsx:70`).
- **States:** none, L1 pop-up, L2, L3, snoozed.

#### Account gate and signed-out gate (banner/sheet)

`AccountGate` (`src/sync/AccountGate.tsx:17-48`, mounted `App.tsx:2023`): a device with no sign-in is read-only (owner decision Q3a, `startAccountGate()` `src/main.tsx:151`); shows `AccountGateBar` and a sign-in sheet when a refused edit fires `onSignInRequired`. `SignedOutGate` (`App.tsx:2022`).

#### Pencil lab (screen + panel)

- **Opens from:** Settings > Advanced > Pencil lab (hash `#/lab/pencil`).
- **Main file:line:** `src/app/lab/PencilLab.tsx` (entry `34-85`, panel `88-228`), `labPage.ts`, `labStats.ts`; mounted `App.tsx:1585-1588` (panel while the lab page is open) and `App.tsx:1651-1654`.
- **Features:** finds or makes a "Pencil lab" page in the first notebook with 3 sample lines; failure text `lab.needNotebook`. Panel `aside` fixed bottom-end, `w-[320px]`, collapsible (`lab.hide`/`lab.show`): Scribble-wrote counter `lab.scribbleWrote` (red if > 0), page mode, per-pointer-type counters (down/move(coalesced)/up/cancel/lostCapture), touchstart, hover, viewport, focus, three switches (`lab.sw.policy`, `lab.sw.penTap`, `lab.sw.touch`; restored on unmount `PencilLab.tsx:104-111`), "Copy results" (`lab.copy`/`lab.copied`; falls back to `window.prompt`).
- **Gating:** only reachable via the setting or the hash; English id `Pencil lab` page title hard-coded (`PencilLab.tsx:65`).
- **States:** open/closed panel, copied.

#### Left panel (panel)

- **Opens from:** Top-bar panel toggle (pref `leftPanel.open`, `App.tsx:838`); the reader's Contents / Notes button forces it open (`outlineRequest`, `src/app/panel/outlineRequest.ts`, `App.tsx:839-842`).
- **Main file:line:** `src/app/panel/LeftPanel.tsx:407-513`; model `panelModel.ts`.
- **Features:** tabs (`role=tablist`): Pages / Outline / Audio; with a book open: pages, outline, audio, annotations (`:46-51,445-476`). Close `lp.close` (`:467-475`).
  - Pages: page thumbnails with title, lock icon, first 3 lines loaded when scrolled into view, "Empty page" (`lp.pages.blank`); max 200 rows, then `lp.pages.more`; empty `lp.pages.empty`; sheet thumbnails strip for long paper pages (`SheetStrip` `:516-554`).
  - Outline: depth select (`lp.outline.h1`, `h12`, `hAll`), "Toggles" and "1, 1.1" chips, fold buttons, jump with glide (`:213-357`); empty `lp.outline.empty`.

- **States:** docked/overlay; tab; loading.

#### Space strip and Quiet corner (nav)

- **Opens from:** always visible; left column 56 px (`w-14`) on computer/iPad; fixed bottom tab bar 56 px (`h-14`) on phone (`SpaceStrip.tsx:29-35`).
- **Main file:line:** `src/app/spaces/SpaceStrip.tsx:9-68`, `QuietCorner.tsx:25-215`, `spaces.ts`.

Behaviour: Home/Library open their main screen; Notebooks opens the shelf (on a phone from another space it opens the list drawer); Review opens the Review space (wide) (`App.tsx:795-812`).
  - Quiet corner (⚙ `corner.label` "Quiet corner", `aria-haspopup`, panel `fixed bottom-2 left-14 z-50 w-64`, phone: `right-2 bottom-16`): rows (1) Settings with `SyncDot` (`:115-128`), (2) Save & restore (`sidebar.data`, `:129-141`), (3) Trash section (`TrashSection`, `:142-144`), (4) Device mode row (`:145`), (5) Layer gallery `layers.open` (`:146-156`), (6) Refresh app `refresh.app` (always shown, `:157-169`), (7) Help `corner.help` (opens Settings at `general.showHelp`, `:170-175`), (8) Day / Night / Sepia theme buttons (`aria-pressed`, `:176-198`). Closes on Esc (unless a `[role=menu]` is open), outside pointerdown, or choosing Settings/Data/Help (`:51-76`).

#### Help tips and first-use tips (help)

- **Opens from:** `<Tip id>` wrappers around features (177 tip ids in `TIPS` `src/help/helpRegistry.ts:24-252`; 11 help lines in `HELP_LINES` `:254-270`).
- **Main file:line:** `src/help/Help.tsx` (Tip `44-76`, TipBox `78-179`, Bubble `186-246`, HelpLine `249-263`), `useShowHelp.ts`, `firstUse.ts`, `FirstUseTip.tsx`.
- **Features:** hover tip after 450 ms (`Help.tsx:42`); keyboard focus tip only when the last key was Tab (`:35-39`); touch: small `ⓘ` button (`help.info` "What is this?", `Help.tsx:161-175`) or press-and-hold 500 ms (`useLongPress`, auto-hides after 3 s, `:99-105`); tip bubble = meaning + "Example: ..." (`help.example`) + optional image (`tipImage`); `role=tooltip`, `fixed z-[70]`, width min(280, viewport-16), repositioned to stay on screen (`:204-228`); Esc / outside tap closes. Texts: `help.tip.<id>.meaning/.example`, `help.line.<id>` (`i18n/en/help.ts`). With Settings > Show help off the wrapper stays (no layout shift) and tips/lines vanish (`Help.tsx:65`, `useShowHelp.ts:6`).
  - First-use tips: ids `lasso`, `writePad`, `layers`, `paperBox` (`firstUse.ts:12-17`); shown once per device (pref `firstUse.seen`), only if Show help is on (`firstUse.ts:34-40`); one line with a close `×` (`popup.close`), auto-dismiss at 12 s (`FirstUseTip.tsx:21-24`); texts `firstUse.<id>` in `i18n/en/late/help.ts:3-8`; triggers: Lasso tool active, Layers card open (`FirstUseTip.tsx:12-17`); the others from their tools. Rendered in the bottom-centre stack (`App.tsx:2040-2052`).
  - Generated review list `docs/HELP-TEXTS.md`.
- **Gating:** `general.showHelp` device setting (`builtinSettings.ts:160`).

#### Discard guard (hook)

- **Main file:line:** `src/app/useDiscardGuard.tsx:11-35`.
- **Features:** returns `dirtyProps` (spread on `<dialog>`, sets touched on any input), `requestClose()` (closes if untouched, else asks), `confirm` node = `ConfirmDialog` "Discard your changes?" [Discard / Keep editing]. Only 3 consumers: `src/databases/CsvImportDialog.tsx`, `src/databases/dashboard/ChartDialog.tsx`, `src/databases/views/ColumnDialog.tsx`. `NameDialog` re-implements the same logic inline (`ui.tsx:557-559,615-626`). Other dialogs (NameSheet, ChoiceDialog, ConfirmDialog) close on backdrop without a guard.

#### Long press and row menus (gesture)

- `useLongPress(onLong, ignoreTips)` `src/app/useLongPress.ts:71-123`: 500 ms (`LONG_PRESS_MS` `:10`), move slop 8 px (`:12`), pointer type not mouse and primary only, swallows the following click, blocks the native iOS/Android context menu while fired. Used by: Sidebar notebook rows (`Sidebar.tsx:183`), PageTree page rows (`PageTree.tsx:78`), Help tips (`Help.tsx:101`), Ink favourites row (`FavouritesRow.tsx:41`), Home TaskRow (`TaskRow.tsx:37`), Library cards (`LibraryScreen.tsx:225`).
- A visible `⋯` `RowMenuButton` (`ui.tsx:77-98`) is shown on `any-pointer: coarse` (always) and when the row container is narrower than 19rem (`@max-[19rem]:flex`, `narrowToo`); `aria-label` `sidebar.rowMenu` "..." with tip.
- There are NO `onContextMenu` (right-click) menus on Sidebar notebook rows or page rows for a mouse; a mouse uses hover icon buttons. The only right-click menus: NotebooksHome notebook cards (`NotebooksHome.tsx:448-460,496`) and ink favourite pills (`FavouritesRow.tsx:56`, deletes the favourite).

#### Notebook row menu (menu: long-press or ⋯ on a Sidebar notebook row)

- **Main file:line:** `src/notebooks/NotebookRowMenu.tsx:11-85`; host `src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:735-760`; lazy `LazyNameSheet.tsx:21`.
- **Rows in order:** (1) `sidebar.addPage.title` "+" Add page - unless journal notebook (`:37-43`); (2) `review.thisNotebook` ⟳ - unless templates notebook (`:44-50`); (3) `sidebar.rename.title` ✎ (`:52`); (4) `lock.menu.lock.notebook` / `lock.menu.remove.notebook` lock toggle (`:53-61`); (5) edit lock `editLock.menu.lockNotebook` "Lock editing (all pages)" / `editLock.menu.unlock` (`editLockMenu.ts:11-34`); (6) `tree.moveUp` ↑ (no-op at index 0) (`:63-68`); (7) `tree.moveDown` ↓ (`:69-74`); (8) `sidebar.deleteNotebook.title` × danger, disabled with hint `editLock.deleteHint` "Locked" when edit-locked (`:75-82`). Delete opens a ConfirmDialog, then soft-deletes with toast + `reading.undo` (`Sidebar.tsx:~250-270`).
- **Hover icon buttons on the same row (mouse):** add page, review, rename, delete (`Sidebar.tsx:~590-640`) with tips `sidebar.addPage`, `sidebar.reviewNotebook`, `sidebar.rename`, `sidebar.deleteNotebook`.

#### Page row menu (menu: long-press or ⋯ on a PageTree row)

- **Main file:line:** `src/pages/PageRowMenu.tsx:14-93`; host `src/pages/PageTree.tsx:316-343`.
- **Rows in order:** (1) `sidebar.rename.title` ✎ (`:53`); (2) `tree.addSub` + if `canAddSub` (not journal) (`:55-57`); (3) if not in a root-scoped tree (`!rootId`): `fav.add`/`fav.remove` ☆/★ (`:58-65`) and `lock.menu.lock.page`/`lock.menu.remove.page` (`:66-70`); (4) edit lock `editLock.menu.lock`/`unlock` - hidden when the lock comes from the notebook or the account (`:73-75`); (5) `tree.moveUp`/`tree.moveDown` if `canMove` (not journal, not root) (`:76-81`); (6) `tree.moveTop` ↰ only for a sub-page allowed to go top-level (`:82-83`); (7) `tree.deletePage` × danger, disabled with hint when edit-locked (`:84-91`).
- **Hover icon buttons on the page row:** move up `tree.moveUp`, move down `tree.moveDown`, move to top `tree.moveTop`, add sub-page `tree.addSub`, delete `tree.deletePage` (`PageTree.tsx:247-303`); double-click name = inline rename (`PageTree.tsx:219`); rows draggable when `canMove` (`:155`) with drop intents; a move gets one Undo step (`moveWithUndo` `:93-111`, `tree.moved`).

#### NotebooksHome card menu (right-click / ⋯ on a shelf card)

`src/notebooks/NotebooksHome.tsx:161-197` items: `nbh.star`/`nbh.unstar` ("Pin to the top"/"Unpin"), `nbh.cover.page`/`nbh.cover.colour` ("Show the first page as the cover"/"Show a colour cover"), `nbh.colour.next` ("Change colour"). Card has its own star button (`StarButton` `:~262-290`, `nbh.starNamed/unstarNamed`) and a `MenuButton`. Layout toggle Shelf/List (`nbh.layout.*`), `nbh.new` button, tabs starred / recent / all.

#### Other notebook / page ⋯ menus (outside the requested files, found while tracing)

- NotebookScreen ⋯ (`src/notebooks/NotebookScreen.tsx:305`): tab rename/hide/export/lock items (`lockItem`, `ExportDialog`).
- Page ⋯ menu (`src/pages/PageView.tsx:272-413`, rendered `:489`): Paper style, Paper size, Review this page (hint Alt+R), Prompts, History, Reader full, favourite, Save as template, Import (grey), Export (grey), Search all features (hint Ctrl+Shift+K), Copy & paste ▸ (copy pictures, copy page plain, copy page Markdown, paste plain with hint Ctrl+Shift+V), Show/Hide tool bar, Delete page, Restore backup.

#### Language switcher (setting)

No dedicated switcher widget. English / Hindi / Spanish (`src/i18n/languages.ts:9-16`, names in their own script) are a segmented control in Settings > General (`builtinSettings.ts:139-154`, id `general.language`) and in Welcome step 2 (`Welcome.tsx:227-240`). Applied by `applyLanguage` in an effect (`App.tsx:700-711`): loads `i18n/en/late` plus `hi`/`es`, sets `<html lang>`, loads BlockNote Spanish dictionary for `es` only (`languages.ts:39-55`). Missing keys fall back to English. Dates: `dateStyleSetting` (`App.tsx:688`).

#### Theme switcher (setting)

Day / Night / Sepia (`src/app/theme.ts:9`). Entry points: Quiet-corner buttons (`QuietCorner.tsx:176-198`); ribbon/bar Theme button cycles (app action `cycleTheme` `App.tsx:1297`) with the arrow list `ThemeArrow` showing swatches (`src/app/tools/ThemePicker.tsx:15-93`, label `theme.pick`); Settings > General segmented (`builtinSettings.ts:108-124`); Welcome step 2; hidden design page. Applied by `applyTheme` -> `data-theme` on `<html>` (`App.tsx:711-712`, `theme.ts:24-26`); first paint is handled by a script in `index.html` using the same key. Editor colour scheme `editorColorScheme` (`theme.ts:19`). Related: glass reduce/auto setting `reduceGlassSetting` -> `data-glass="lite"` (`src/design/glass.ts`, `App.tsx:713-719`).

#### Notification / reminder / badge mechanisms

- Browser notifications: none.
- Backup reminder: `src/share/backupReminder.ts` (due after 7 days since first use or last saved file, `REMIND_AFTER_MS` `:11`; "Remind me later" snoozes 90 days despite the doc comment saying "tomorrow" - `SNOOZE_MS` `:12`, see Part F); UI `src/share/ReminderBar.tsx:8-33` (`backup.reminder.text`, `.save` "Save backup now", `.later` "Remind me later"); mounted unless reviewing or on the Data screen (`App.tsx:2011-2019`).
- Review due badges: global due count pill on Sidebar "Review" (`Sidebar.tsx:317`), per-notebook due pill (`:594-603`; re-counted every 60 s `Sidebar.tsx:~196-215`), NotebooksHome card `Meta due` (`NotebooksHome.tsx:~100`), ReviewHome counts (`ReviewHome.tsx:30`). Aria label `review.dueCount`.
- Sync status dot (`src/sync/SyncDot.tsx:15-37`) shown in the Quiet-corner Settings row: Synced / Syncing / Offline / Needs attention.
- Update reminder: bottom pill + attention L3 + risk bar; auto update (`autoUpdate.ts`).
- Home habit reminders: explicitly none (`home.routine.noReminders`, `src/home/Routines.tsx:436`).

#### Other shell machinery worth keeping in the redesign

- Router/history: `router.ts`, `navHistory.ts` (own back/forward counter, session key `learning-notes:nav-last`), `pushRouteParts`/`useRouteParts` (`router.ts:176-236`).
- `src/app/uiPrefs.ts` per-device prefs in localStorage with prefix `learning-notes:`; `usePref.ts`.
- Pop-up rule `popups.ts` (above); `uiGuard.ts` (`modalOpen`, `isUiEvent`: ink ignores pointers on `[data-ui], dialog, [role=dialog], [role=menu], [role=toolbar]`).
- `scrollHints.ts` (edge fade + slim scroll indicator, setting `scrollHintsSetting`, `main.tsx:82-88`), `edgeStretch.ts`.
- Health check / soft refresh / robustness (`healthCheck.ts` 3 probes: flags, editable, listeners; `softRefresh.ts` `refreshNow`, auto at most once per 10 min `AUTO_CAP_MS`), loaded when idle `main.tsx:258-269`; "Refresh app" in the corner.
- Updates: `updates.ts`, `autoUpdate.ts`, `mandatoryFlow.ts`, `updateStatus.ts`, `updateChannel.ts`, `chunkReload.ts`, service worker `registerSW` (`main.tsx:222-243`).
- Input guards: `palmGuard.ts`, `rowPalmGuard.ts`, `penTap.ts`, `inputMachine.ts`, `gestureReset.ts`, `listenerCount.ts`.
- Window scroll lock: document is snapped back to scrollY 0 (`main.tsx:90-103`); app scrolls in `[data-page-scroller]`.
- Design system: `src/design/tokens.css` (427 lines; `@theme static`), `inter.css`, `loadInter.ts` (Inter loaded after first paint on non-Apple devices only), `glass.ts`; stacking scale `--z-bar:20 --z-pill:30 --z-popup:40 --z-toast:50 --z-modal:60 --z-mini:35` (`index.css:23-30`) but components use hard-coded z-index (toast `z-40`, menu `z-50`, tip `z-[70]`, attention `z-40`, mandatory `z-[2147483000]`).

### PART C - Keyboard shortcuts (everything registered under `src`)

Shortcuts help screen: NONE. Command palette: Search popup Features chip (above). Search popup footer prints its own key hints (`↑ ↓ move · Enter open/see all · Esc close`, hidden on phone, `SearchPopup.tsx:454-461`, texts `search.keys.*`). Display helpers: `src/app/keys.ts:2-20` (`isApple`, `shortcutLabel` Ctrl+/⌘, `altShortcutLabel` Alt+/⌥, `shortcutShiftLabel`). Shortcuts use `e.code` so they work on any keyboard layout (docs/features/keyboard-shortcuts.md).

| `phone:` Tailwind variant                                                                                 | `(width < 40rem), (height < 32rem)` i.e. under 640 px wide OR under 512 px tall (so a landscape phone counts) | `index.css:5-9`, mirrored in JS `PHONE_SCREEN` `src/app/appUi.ts:57`                              |
| `wide:` variant                                                                                           | `(width >= 40rem) and (height >= 32rem)`                                                                      | `index.css:12-17`                                                                                 |
| `--frame-gap` 12 px -> 0 px on phone                                                                      | frame pieces (sidebar, bars, paper) touch edges                                                               | `index.css:19-35`                                                                                 |
| `(hover: none), (any-pointer: coarse)`                                                                    | `.tip-info` (ⓘ) displayed; all buttons min 32x44; inputs/selects min-height 44; round buttons min 44 wide     | `index.css:306-340`                                                                               |
| `(pointer: coarse)`                                                                                       | table handles/extend button min 44x44                                                                         | `index.css:880-885`                                                                               |
| `(display-mode: standalone) and (min-device-width: 600px)` + iOS `@supports (-webkit-touch-callout:none)` | `--top-clear: 36px` (room for the iPad status bar) used by TopBar and SpaceStrip padding                      | `index.css:385-395`                                                                               |
| `@media print`                                                                                            | hides ink canvases                                                                                            | `index.css:659-663`                                                                               |
| `prefers-reduced-motion`                                                                                  | multiple blocks (reduce: no animations; no-preference: transitions)                                           | `index.css:288,690,796,803,836,962,1022,1083,1175`; `tokens.css:322,421`                          |
| `prefers-reduced-transparency: reduce`                                                                    | solid replacements for glass                                                                                  | `tokens.css:274,305`                                                                              |
| `any-pointer-coarse:` utility / `[@media(any-pointer:coarse)]`                                            | bigger targets (ui.tsx:92,516,524; NotebooksHome; Sidebar)                                                    | 29 uses across `src`                                                                              |
| Container queries                                                                                         | row `@container`, `@max-[19rem]` shows the ⋯ and hides hover icons                                            | `ui.tsx:92`, `Sidebar.tsx:~520,625`, `PageTree.tsx:186,243`                                       |
| Tailwind `md:` (48rem default)                                                                            | Settings two-column layout; 21 uses in screens (dashboard, library, review ...)                               | `SettingsScreen.tsx:~137`, `AllCardsScreen`, `ReviewHome`, `LibraryScreen`, `NotebookScreen` etc. |
