#### E3. JS width / device checks

- `matchMedia(PHONE_SCREEN)`: `App.tsx:802,807,811,1163,1282`.
- `matchMedia('(any-pointer: coarse)')` = `touchUi()` (`src/app/touchUi.ts:2-7`), used by `ui.tsx:514,522` (confirm focus), `App.tsx:1074,1100` (Name sheet instead of inline title/name), `Sidebar.tsx:167`, `PageTree.tsx:113,207`, `useRowPalmGuard.ts:7`.
- Device mode (`deviceMode`): bar size/position/hints (presets `deviceMode.ts:41-62`, `barButtonPx` min 44 px for touch/pencil `:77`), detection `:105-130` (iPad-as-Mac), `Welcome.tsx:115` (>= 600 px = tablet), `deviceKind.ts:60-76` (shortSide), `useKeyboardInset` (toast/bar lift above keyboard `ui.tsx:417`, `MicroBar.tsx:83`, `BarStack.tsx:76`).
- `window.innerWidth` rules: left panel dock >= 1024 (`LeftPanel.tsx:54,433`), reading contents dock >= 1024 (`contentsTree.ts:12`), paper narrow < 600 px collapses multi-sheet layout (`paper/sheets.ts:41`, `PaperFrame.tsx:463`), sidebar max (`sidebarWidth.ts:15`), menu/tip clamping to viewport (`ui.tsx:241,305`, `Help.tsx:196`).
- `(pointer: coarse)` for Write pad (`WritePad.tsx:223`), `(display-mode: standalone)` (`toolStateReport.ts:19`, `DeviceSection.tsx:17`).

---

### PART F - Surprising, duplicated or half-built

1. Banner buttons point to a settings category that does not exist: "Open backups" (`backups`) and "Manage storage" (`storage`) call `openSettings('category:data')` (`AttentionBanner.tsx:98,112`), but no category with id `data` is registered (registered ids: general, review, journal, toolbar, advanced, apis, ai, permissions, claude, sync, account, lock, reading). `SettingsScreen.tsx:79-82` accepts the id as-is, so Settings opens with no tab highlighted and an empty list. Save & restore is a screen (`#/data`), not a category.
2. `updateNeeded` attention code can never fire: `updateNeeded` is initialised false (`attentionSignals.ts:22`) and nothing calls `setSignals({updateNeeded: true})` (grep: only App.tsx sets `updateReady`/`applyUpdate`, `App.tsx:925-934`). The message `attention.updateNeeded` is dead.
3. Toasts do not stack or queue: a later toast silently replaces an earlier Undo toast, so a quick second action loses the first Undo. Also `choose()` / `chooseTemplate()` / `askName()` are single-slot states: a second request overwrites the first and the first promise never resolves (`App.tsx:868-870`).
4. Bottom-centre collisions: Toast (`bottom-5 z-40`), BackupReminder (`bottom-5 z-30`, `ReminderBar.tsx:22`), SettingsNotice (`bottom-4 z-50`), PencilOffer (`bottom-20 z-50`), the centre stack (`bottom-16 z-40`: SaveNotice + FirstUseTip + UpdateRiskBar), UpdatePill (`bottom-16 left-3`), NowPlaying/TimerChip corners - several can overlap each other (toast over the reminder at the same y).
5. `ConfirmDialog` confirm button is always red (`ui.tsx:524`) even for non-destructive confirms (e.g. "Discard", "Update"); the `danger` look cannot be turned off. No alert-only dialog.
6. Two independent copies of the discard-guard logic: `useDiscardGuard.tsx` (3 users) and the inline version in `NameDialog` (`ui.tsx:557-626`). Most other dialogs have no guard.
7. Two files named `AaSheet`: `src/app/reading/AaSheet.tsx` (371 lines, imports `READING_THEMES`) and `src/reader/AaSheet.tsx` (imports a different `THEMES` from `reader/readStyle.ts`); two reading-mode systems side by side (`src/app/reading/*` and `src/reader/*`).
8. Spaces placeholders are stale: `SpacePlaceholder` (`SpacePlaceholder.tsx`) still has a disabled "Import" button with `space.library.importSoon` and "Coming next: your books", but the Library is built; the library branch in the Sidebar is dead because `libraryPanel` is always provided (`Sidebar.tsx:703`, `App.tsx:1431-1447`). Home placeholder still used in the Sidebar (`Sidebar.tsx:700`) although Home is built. Docs spaces.md still lists them as placeholders.
9. Search popup "Books" and "Highlights" chips still show "arrive with the Library / reader" even though both exist (`SearchPopup.tsx:46,303-307`, `i18n/en/search.ts:25-29`); the `book`/`highlight` chips may be in the `COMING` list incorrectly.
10. Duplicate shortcut handlers: Ctrl+K and Ctrl+Shift+K have two identical capture listeners (`App.tsx:1202-1228`) that do the same thing; comment "control panel is part of it". docs/features/control-panel.md still describes the old split.
11. Two "Refresh app" entries: quiet corner always shows it (`QuietCorner.tsx:157-169`) while the top bar shows it only if setting `showRefresh` is on (`TopBar.tsx:106-118`); same action.
12. `studyTab` (Notes / Summary / Review) lives in `useState`, not the address: `#/cards` always reopens on Review after reload or Back (`App.tsx:413`, route hash `router.ts:76-79`). "All summaries" and "Notes overview" share this route.
13. `Route` has `'deck'` scope in the review route (`router.ts:32`) but `ReviewScope` in `App.tsx` is built via `reviewScope.kind`; "custom" scopes (blocks lists) cannot be reopened - Back goes to a blank state (`router.ts:187-189`). Documented, but a custom review leaves no address (`App.tsx:574-577`).
14. Backup-reminder "Remind me later" snoozes 90 days (`SNOOZE_MS`, `backupReminder.ts:12`) while the doc comment and UI wording say "tomorrow" (`backupReminder.ts:56`, README/doc).
15. No right-click (context menu) for sidebar rows on a mouse; only touch long-press and the ⋯ button (shown on touch or narrow rows). The menu rows exist only in the touch path (`NotebookRowMenu`, `PageRowMenu`). Mouse users rely on hover icon buttons (up to 5 per page row). A mouse right-click on a sidebar row shows the browser's native menu.
16. Native-feature leak: `window.prompt` used as a copy fallback (`PencilLab.tsx:128`) is the only native dialog in the app.
17. Stacking scale tokens `--z-*` exist in CSS (`index.css:23-30`) but are not used by shell components; z-indexes are literal (see B).
18. `HealthCheck` and `softRefresh` heavy robustness machinery loads on idle; auto soft-refresh capped at 1 per 10 min (`softRefresh.ts:26`). Not visible to users except one toast.
19. `nameAsk` state is declared after `appUi` uses `setNameAsk` (`App.tsx:871-873` vs `1033-1038`) - works via closure but is out-of-order.
20. `SpaceStrip` buttons for phone are `h-12` (48 px) while desktop `size-11` (44 px); labels `text-[10px]` on phone (small).
21. `design-tokens` and `connect` pages render in their own React root without ErrorBoundary/UpgradeGate/i18n language application (`main.tsx:316-326`).
22. `src/help/helpRegistry.ts` doc comment says texts live in `i18n/en.ts` but they are in `src/i18n/en/*.ts` (docs/features/help-and-tips.md also outdated); `HELP_LINES` count 11, `TIPS` count 177.
23. Mandatory-update dialog traps Esc and renders outside `#root` with z-index `2147483000` (above everything, including native dialogs only if rendered in top layer - a `showModal()` dialog opened later will still sit above it).

---

### Counts (for the summary)

- Routed screens: 14 route kinds. Space-level screens: 5. Full-screen/blocking: 10. Hidden/lab: 3 (+2 test events). Total distinct screens ~32.
- System surfaces documented in Part B: 27 (toast, confirm/name/choice dialogs, menu primitive, sheets note, name sheet, error boundary, top bar, quick pages, device-mode menu, attention gate, account gate, Pencil lab, left panel, space strip + quiet corner, sidebar, help tips + first-use, discard guard, long-press, notebook row menu, page row menu, notebooks-home menu, other ⋯ menus, language, theme, notifications/badges, shell machinery, plus non-toast notices).
- Toast kinds: 1 primitive (message + optional action); 13 distinct action labels; 9 other notice surfaces that are not toasts.
- Shortcuts: 70 rows (8 app-level + widget/editor ones).
- Empty states: 44 rows (10 via `EmptyState`), 15 error-state rows.

# APPENDIX H (08-paper-ink-tools-reading.md)

## Audit 08: paper, ink, tools, layers, reading mode vs toolbar v8

Read-only audit, 12 Nov 2026. Repo paths are relative to `c:\Users\pc\Downloads\learning-notes`. Line numbers were checked in the working copy on `main`.

### How "covered by v8" was judged

The file `v8/mapping.md` is a TEXT-options-only table (Box style, Format, fonts, lists, Box tab, Layout tab, box menu, 150 rows). It says nothing about ink, paper, zoom, reading or layers. The wider v8 scope comes from three places:

- `v8/design-study.md` (cards for Pen, Shapes, Eraser, Lasso, Text, plus the shared frame of Insert, Elements, More).
- The in-mock feature map `PMAP` (`v8/toolbar-mock-v8.html:3463-3499`) and the audit list `V8.audit` (`toolbar-mock-v8.html:4959-4961`, same text in `v8/v8a.js:6-8`).
- `v8/v8notes.html`.

Together these cover: Pen (all five sections, Layers & paper inside the Pen card), Eraser, Lasso, Selection menu (handwriting, text box, picture, mixed), Text, Insert, "While you type" (Box bar, Mini toolbar, Micro bar, Keyboard bar, Hold menu), Main bar and More (New line, Mic, Reader bar, Settings, All tools row), Box grip menu, Layouts card (toolbar layouts), Toolbar settings sheet, Customise sheet, Help sheet, Reading look sheet, Arrange-the-menu sheet, dialogs (New layout, Rename, Delete, Add a picture, New recall card, Handwriting, Link, Save as preset, Save to notes, Screenshot), Elements card tabs, toast, tooltip.

Legend: **yes** = the mock or the mapping has a named home for it. **partly** = the feature has a home but sub-features, states or the on-page overlay do not. **no** = no named home anywhere in the four documents or the mock.

Note on names: the repo calls the page-grid pop-up "Layout" (`layout.title` = "Layout"). v8 "Layouts card" is a different thing (saved TOOLBAR layouts, `app/tools/main/layoutSync.ts`). Do not mix them.

---

---

## PART B: full sections for every "partly" or "no" surface

Common gating facts (apply to every section unless stated otherwise):

- Device mode (Computer / Touch / Pencil) sets bar size, tips, hints, auto-hide: `app/deviceMode.ts:41-70`. Experience level (Beginner / Professional) only matters for the toolbar cards, not for anything in this Part except where stated.
- Phone: "fit width" under `NARROW_PX = 600` (`paper/sheets.ts:41`) means no sheets, no page scroll bar, boxes stacked (`PaperFrame.tsx:459-463`).
- Read-only pages (open in another window) and Reading mode show ink but do not draw (`docs/features/ink.md` Edge cases).

### B1. Layout pop-up (A1 #1, #2, #3, #4)

- **Opens from**:
  - Fixed Layout button (grid icon) left of the bar, next to Undo/Redo: `LayoutButton.tsx:20-29`, drawn at `MainBar.tsx:176` and `Ribbon.tsx:234`. Only while a paper page is open (`useSheetInfo()` is null otherwise, `LayoutButton.tsx:12`).
  - Box ⋯ menu "Layout" with hint ⌃⌥L (`usePaperBoxes.tsx:350-355`).
  - Text strip "Layout" button (`TextBoxExtras.tsx:53-60`), disabled with no active box.
  - Registry tool `layout` (ribbon Home > More format) (`toolRegistry.ts:307-317`).
  - Keyboard Ctrl/Cmd+Alt+L when focus is inside a box (`usePaperBoxes.tsx:227-236`).
- **Main file:line**: `paper/LayoutDialog.tsx:353-645`; open/close `usePaperBoxes.tsx:205-237,408-415`.
- **Features**:
  - Top bar: ✕ "Cancel" (`LayoutDialog.tsx:362-369`), title "Layout" (371), ✓ "Done" (373-381, disabled while saving).
  - Coach line "Drag a box to move it, pull its right edge to resize. ✓ applies everything, ✕ throws it away." (393).
  - Page preview (aria "Page preview"): live drag of boxes onto rows/columns, right-edge resize handle "Change box width (drag)" (458), tap a box to select, tap empty stage to deselect (`LayoutDialog.tsx:380-480`).
  - Side panel "Layout settings" (482):
    - **Tidy**: "Line up left" (491-494), "Space evenly" (disabled with fewer than 3 boxes, 497-505), "One box" (507-512), details "More layouts" (515) with presets Two columns, Narrow left/wide right, Wide left/narrow right, Three columns, Four boxes, Six boxes (`layout.two/.thirdTwo/.twoThirds/.three/.quad/.six`, 518-528).
    - **Box** (532): None + five named presets ("Outline", "Soft card", "Note", "Callout", "Shadow card") and colour dots for the selected box (536-572). Not for the page's own text ("Page text", `layout.assist.pageText`). Empty state "Tap a box in the preview to set its style and colour." (574).
    - **Text** (577): "Applies to all your notes." with Size select and Line spacing select (580-615). These write `noteSize` / `noteLine`, i.e. a note-wide setting, not per page.
    - **Pages** (618): one button per page of the notebook (title or number); switching keeps each page's draft (`LayoutDialog.tsx:618-637`).
    - Empty state: "This page has only its own text. Tap the page with the Text tool to add boxes." (638-641).
  - Nothing is written until Done; Done applies every changed page in one History group (one Undo) (`LayoutDialog.tsx:318-343`).
- **Gating**: needs an editable page (`usePaperBoxes.tsx:210-213`); on a read-only page it toasts "This page is read-only". Button hidden when no paper page. Side panel is a bottom sheet (45 vh) below the `md` breakpoint, a 20 rem side column at `md` and up (`LayoutDialog.tsx:481-483`). No Beginner/Professional split. No separate phone gate: the unused key `layout.assist.phone` ("Layouts appear on larger screens") shows the old plan had one.
- **Empty / error / toast states**: blank full-screen dialog while the draft loads (`LayoutDialog.tsx:196-210`); read-only toast; "Row is full" toast on grid drops (`PaperFrame.tsx:1655`, key `layout.full`); `layout.notEnough`, `layout.done` keys exist but are not used by this dialog.
- **Keyboard / Pencil**: Esc cancels (170); focus goes to the dialog on open (166); drag works with finger, Pencil and mouse (pointer events, `LayoutDialog.tsx:227-305`). No Pencil-specific gesture.

### B2. Box ⋯ menu, grip, handles, frame (A1 #7, #9, #10)

- **Opens from**: ⋯ on the selected box (`BoxFrame.tsx:277-297`), ⋯ on the Box bar (`BoxBar.tsx:85`), ⋯ on the page's own box (`PaperFrame.tsx:2369-2390` `MainChrome`).
- **Main file:line**: `usePaperBoxes.tsx:306-362` (items), `BoxFrame.tsx:231-466`.
- **Features**:
  - Menu items in order: Box style (palette), Copy, Cut, Duplicate box, Colour ▸ (dots: `BOX_COLOURS` names, "No colour" with ○), Layout (⌃⌥L), Delete box (red). The page's own box (`MAIN_BOX`) never shows the danger item (`usePaperBoxes.tsx:303-304`).
  - Copy/Cut use `document.execCommand('copy')` after selecting all of the box's text (`usePaperBoxes.tsx:296-300`); Cut deletes the box except the main one. Delete shows toast "Box deleted." with Undo (283-284); emptying the page shows "Page emptied." with Undo (155-156).
  - Grip "≡" "Move box (drag)" (`BoxFrame.tsx:268`), shown only on a selected box; replaced by 🔒 when the box is locked or the layout is stacked (262-266).
  - Resize: 8 handles, `data-resize-handle` n/s/e/w/ne/nw/se/sw, 44 px targets, cursor per direction (`BoxFrame.tsx:405-466`); hidden when locked, stacked, dragging or in layout mode (`BoxFrame.tsx:337-343`).
  - Four 10 px "bands" around the frame: a tap selects, a drag moves, text inside stays editable (`BoxFrame.tsx:300-330`).
  - Dashed frame while empty/idle, solid accent when picked, "Type here" placeholder (`textTool.typeHere`, `BoxFrame.tsx:323`).
- **Gating**: Text tool for creation (`PaperFrame.tsx` `onAddBox` checks `activeTool.get()==='text'` at `usePaperBoxes.tsx:~245`); stacked layouts (phone) have no grip or handles; locked boxes (`box.locked`, no UI sets it) cannot be moved, resized or deleted by keyboard.
- **Empty / error / toast**: "Could not copy the text" (`BoxBar.tsx:45`); "N boxes moved." (`paper.box.movedMany`).
- **Keyboard / Pencil**: Esc deselects (`PaperFrame.tsx:671`); Delete/Backspace deletes the picked unlocked boxes when focus is not in a field (`PaperFrame.tsx:675-681`); Shift-tap adds to the pick (`PaperFrame.tsx:742`); Ctrl/Cmd+Alt+L opens Layout.

### B3. Column dividers, group bar, drag, guides, lasso-on-paper (A1 #11-#15)

- **Opens from**: automatic. Dividers appear between the columns of a row; GroupBar when two or more boxes are picked (or a group); guides during a drag.
- **Main file:line**: `GridDividers.tsx:20-193`; `PaperFrame.tsx:1991-2125` (GroupBar, GuideLines); drag controller `PaperFrame.tsx:700-960`.
- **Features**:
  - Dividers: 20 px wide, 44 px on touch, drag resizes the two neighbouring columns (min 25 %), double-tap = equal widths, aria "Change box width (drag)" (`GridDividers.tsx:124,171`; `docs/features/paper-page.md` Box grid).
  - Group bar (toolbar "Group boxes"): buttons "Group" / "Ungroup" (`PaperFrame.tsx:2019-2045`); placed above the picked boxes.
  - Smart guides: magenta 1 px edge/centre/page-centre lines and equal-spacing marks, snap within `SNAP_MM` (`guides.ts:1-30`).
  - Drag: see-through copy follows the pointer, other boxes slide in 150 ms, accent outline marks the landing cell; release is one write and one Undo.
  - Lasso on bare paper picks boxes (mouse and Pencil only: `pointerType === 'touch'` is skipped, `PaperFrame.tsx:1179`).
- **Gating**: not on phone (stacked), not touch for the lasso loop, Lasso tool for the loop, locked boxes excluded.
- **Empty / error / toast**: "Row is full" (`PaperFrame.tsx:1655`).
- **Keyboard / Pencil**: Esc cancels a drag (`PaperFrame.tsx:942-951`).

### B4. Zoom pill, pinch/wheel zoom, scroll bar, sheets (A1 #16-#19)

- **Opens from**: always drawn under the paper (not in flat/reader mode): `PaperFrame.tsx:1935-1975`; scroll bar `PaperFrame.tsx:1930-1934` (`!flat && !narrow`).
- **Features**:
  - Zoom pill: "−" (Zoom out, ÷1.25, minimum fit), "NN %" (Fit width, resets to 100 %), "+" (Zoom in, ×1.25, max 400 %). Tooltips ids `paper.zoomOut/zoomFit/zoomIn` with hover tips off on touch (`touchInfo={false}`).
  - Pinch with two fingers (stylus touches ignored, `PaperFrame.tsx:402-405`), Ctrl/Cmd + wheel (388-396); below fit the paper gives way to about 73 % and springs back in 220 ms (`sheets.ts:11-24`, `PaperFrame.tsx:375-386`). Range `MIN_ZOOM 0.5` to `MAX_ZOOM 4` (`sheets.ts:5-9`).
  - Page scroll bar: ▲ "Previous page" / ▼ "Next page" (one sheet each), thumb sized to the page and dragged 1:1, aria "Scroll the page" slider (`PageScrollBar.tsx:163,210`), ArrowUp/ArrowDown keys (218-219).
  - Sheets: A4 or Letter, border, page background lined / grid / dots (8 mm lined, 5 mm grid and dots), "Continues on next page ↓" at the blank end of a sheet (`paginate.ts:122`).
  - Paper size and paper style are NOT controlled here; they are in the page ⋯ menu (`PageView.tsx:269-290`) and the Pen card (LayersCard "Paper behind the ink", `LayersCard.tsx:227-234`).
- **Gating**: scroll bar and sheets off on phones (<600 px); zoom pill off in flat mode (full-screen reader); ink at a zoom other than 100 % is listed as "Not yet" in the paper doc.
- **Empty / error / toast**: none.
- **Keyboard / Pencil**: Pencil never pinches; Safari's `gesturestart` is cancelled (`PaperFrame.tsx:442`).
- **Odd**: the View-tab "Zoom" tool opens the reading Aa sheet, not this zoom (`toolRegistry.ts:573-579`).

### B5. Empty-paper affordances and Text-tool Pencil (A1 #20-#25)

- **Features**:
  - Hint "Type, or tap anywhere on the paper to add a text box" (key `textTool.hint`; first 3 pages per device, `hintPages.ts`; `PaperFrame.tsx:1851-1864`).
  - "+ box" tag near the pointer with mouse/pen hover (`PaperFrame.tsx:1883-1895`).
  - Phone: dashed "Add a box here" at the end (`PaperFrame.tsx:1754-1763`); a Text-tool tap in the gap under a box adds one after it.
  - Hold still 1.5 s on blank paper (finger/mouse only, not Pencil) shows "Start typing here" (`blankHold.ts:60-85`, key `textTool.startTyping`).
  - Text tool + Pencil: arrow, line, circle, rectangle (not triangle) become clean ink shapes; a circle drawn round a digit makes a circled number (`textPencil.ts:4-60`, `PaperFrame.tsx:1264-1400`). The Pen tool never converts.
  - Pencil on blank paper (any ink tool) makes a box with an empty line under the stroke (`penOnPaper.ts:1-6`).
  - Safe edges: a tap within 8 mm of a sheet edge, a tap over 10 px / 600 ms, a second tap within 700 ms and 60 px, or a tap within 500 ms of a drag makes no box (`PaperFrame.tsx:2131-2147`; docs "Safe edge").
- **Gating**: Text tool only (hint, tag, hold, tap, shapes). Mouse/pen for the tag. Pencil only for shapes and ink-box.
- **Keyboard / Pencil**: see Part C.

### B6. Page ⋯ menu paper style/size, sheet thumbnails (A1 #26-#28)

- **Opens from**: page ⋯ menu > Paper style / Paper size (`pages/PageView.tsx:269-290`); new-page start screen (`templates/NewPageStart.tsx:92-104`); left panel > Pages (`app/panel/LeftPanel.tsx:522-534`).
- **Features**: Paper style submenu: blank, lined, grid, dots with a tick on the current one (labels from `pages/paperStyles.ts`); Paper size submenu: "A4", "Letter"; sheet thumbnails "Sheet N", tap scrolls to that sheet (`sheetNav.ts:42-50`); Settings > Default paper style for new pages (`settings/builtinSettings.ts:717-723`).
- **Gating**: paper pages only; thumbnails need 2+ sheets; run through `runTask` (one write).
- **Empty/error**: none.
- **Note**: no page add / delete / reorder UI exists in `src/paper`, `src/ink`, `src/layers` or the toolbar.

### B7. Drawing selection handles, hidden-layer chip, layer toasts, eraser ring, a11y door (A2 #32, #36, #37, #43, #44, #49)

- **Opens from**: Lasso selection (handles); layer state (chip and toasts); Eraser tool (ring); first Tab stop when the page has strokes (door).
- **Main file:line**: `InkSurface.tsx:700-790` (handles), `1020-1047` (chip), `controller.ts:1236-1262` (toasts), `controller.ts:394-395,994,1040` (ring), `InkSurface.tsx:462-535` (door).
- **Features**:
  - Handles: dashed accent box, four corner scale handles (aria "Resize"), edge-middle handles when a side is over 110 px (`EDGE_HANDLE_MIN`, 1007), rotate knob on a stem above or below (aria "Turn", snaps to 90° per toolbar-map), faint dashed link lines to the text line each stroke is attached to (`data-ink-links`, 718-745). 44 px targets.
  - Two-finger twist turns and moves the selection (`controller.ts:2865`).
  - Chip "Layer 2 hidden · Show" for each hidden layer that has ink; one tap shows it (`InkSurface.tsx:1034-1045`).
  - Toast "Unlock a layer to draw." (`ink.locked`), or "{name} is locked / hidden" with action "Unlock" / "Show".
  - Eraser ring shows the size on hover (mouse/pen) and during the rub.
  - Keyboard door: sr-only button, visible on focus, text "Select drawn strokes. Arrow keys choose a stroke, Delete removes it, Escape lets go." (`ink.region.step`); status "Stroke N selected".
- **Gating**: handles only under Lasso with a selection; chip only when a hidden layer has strokes; door only when strokes exist; palm guard and "Finger can draw" policy (Automatic / Always / Never) decide who can drive any of these (`controller.ts:1940-2040`).
- **Empty/error**: none beyond toasts.
- **Keyboard / Pencil**: arrows, Delete, Backspace, Escape (see Part C); a stylus touchstart on the overlay is cancelled so Scribble cannot act (`ink.md` "Where the code is").

### B8. Magnifier, Ruler, Tape, shape drawing (A2 #38-#41)

- **Opens from**: Insert pill (Tape, Magnifier, Ruler, Shape) and ribbon Draw > Tools; Pen "Draw as" (`DetailCard.tsx:491-501`).
- **Main file:line**: `MagnifierView.tsx:49-392`, `RulerView.tsx:27-240`, `controller.ts:1293-1350,1431-1450`, `shapes.ts`.
- **Features**:
  - Magnifier pad (bottom, 30 % of the screen, resizable 20-50 % by the top bar, aria "Drag to make the writing pad taller or shorter"): zoom buttons 2×, 2.5× (default), 3×, 4× (`MagnifierView.tsx:322-335`); "Space"; "Next line"; ✕ "Close the magnifier"; pad (aria "Writing pad: what you write here lands small in the dashed box on the page"); hint "Write big here. It lands small in the box on the page, which moves on by itself." until the first stroke (`ink.magnifier.hinted` pref); with the Eraser the hint changes to "Eraser: rub here to erase on the page". Target box on the page with a drag label "Writing lands here · drag to move" (aria "Move the target box").
  - Auto-advance: pen lift in the pad's right fifth moves the box by 60 % of its width; past the right margin it wraps to the next line (`ink.md`).
  - Ruler: 80 px bar; middle shows the angle in degrees; two end handles aria "Turn or stretch the ruler" (↻); close ✕ aria "Put the ruler away"; one finger/Pencil moves, second finger twists; clicks to 15° within 2°; a pen starting within 24 px of a long edge slides along it. Not saved.
  - Tape: solid box (a stroke of kind tape); tap peels it off (dashed outline), tap again sticks it back; Eraser removes it in one touch; peel state is kept only while the page is open.
  - Shapes: drag corner to corner draws a live clean shape (Line, Arrow, Box, Oval, Triangle); Snap shapes and Fill closed shapes switches; Draw-and-hold 0.6 s (`HOLD_MS = 600`, `controller.ts:293`; slop `HOLD_SLOP 3`) turns freehand into a shape.
- **Gating**: Pen/Highlighter for ruler slide and shapes; Magnifier supports finger, Pencil and mouse; Tape peel is Pen or Tape tool, not a drag.
- **Empty/error**: first-stroke hint is the only guidance.
- **Keyboard / Pencil**: none for the overlays.

### B9. Toolbar-scope items not in v8 (A3 #2, #50, #54, #56, #62-#64, #67-#69, #72, #75, #77)

#### B9a. Hold-and-slide on a tool button

- **Opens from**: press and hold a main-bar tool button 350 ms (`TOOL_HOLD_MS`, `mainTools.ts:342`); hook `main/holdSlide.ts:20-80`, used at `MainBar.tsx:82`.
- **Features**: pill opens; the option under the finger gets `data-hot`; lifting clicks it; a short press is a normal tap (`holdSlide.ts:4-9`). Made for the Pencil, works with finger and mouse.
- **Gating**: main bar only; not documented in v8.

#### B9b. Ribbon, Ribbon options, tabs, pins

- **Opens from**: ⋯ menu > "All tools", Settings > Tool bar layout, or the setting "Ribbon" at startup (`toolbar-map` §2.7).
- **Main file:line**: `Ribbon.tsx:90-354`, `RibbonOptions.tsx:56-116`, tabs `tabs.ts:2-9`, groups `toolRegistry.ts:728-744`.
- **Features**:
  - Chrome: Undo/Redo + Layout button (left, `Ribbon.tsx:193-234`), tab list "Tool tabs" File, Home, Insert, Draw, View, Review (237-267) with an extra coloured tab for Picture / Video / Table / Drawing / Text selections (268-291), pin "Tools stay open" / "Tools close after use" (297-310), search "Search all features" (313-324), ⋯ "Ribbon options" (RibbonOptions), button "Short bar" or "Hide ribbon" (326-341).
  - Ribbon options menu: "How it shows" (Ribbon replaces short bar / Ribbon + short bar), "Size" (Full (icons and names) / Slim (one row of icons)), "Position" (Same as short bar / Top / Bottom / Floating (drag ⠿)), "Reset ribbon" (`RibbonOptions.tsx:81-112`, labels `ribbon.mode.*`, `ribbon.size.*`, `ribbon.place.*`).
  - Tools by tab (verified in `toolRegistry.ts`): File (Save & restore, Share, Print, Anki import, Lock page, Lock notebook, Lock settings, Settings); Home (Copy & paste group: copyPaste, copyPlain, copyMarkdown, pastePlain, copyPictures; Bold, Italic, Underline, Strike, Code, Font, Text size, More formatting, Colours, Highlight, Heading 1-3, Bullet, Numbered, Checklist, Quote, Align x4, Indent, Outdent, New line, Make blank, Layout, Undo, Redo, Revise marks); Insert (Picture, Write pad, Last tool, Handwriting, Audio, File, Camera, Table, Database, Callout, Divider, Link, Page link, Insert template, Save template, Mind map, To Home, Make card, Summary); Draw (5 pens, Highlighter, Eraser, Lasso, Shapes, Tape, Magnifier, Ruler, Ink layers, Ink colour, Thickness, Opacity, Pressure, Smoothing, Tapered ends, Paper x4, "Only the Pencil draws"); View (Reading mode, Full-screen reader, Line focus, Read aloud, Contents, Self-test, Find, Lock, Exit reading, Audio list, Databases, Theme, Zoom, Tool bar on or off, Keep tools open); Review (Study now, Due today, All cards, Card stats); Go-to (Today, Search, Calendar, Favourite, Sidebar, Control panel, Choose device type).
- **Gating**: ribbon tabs can be hidden in the old Edit bar panel (`editor/EditBarPanel.tsx`); phone wraps buttons under tabs; Slim size hides names; "Ribbon + short bar" hides the short bar's "All tools" button.
- **Empty/error**: coming tools toast their reason (`tool.coming.*`, `toolRegistry.ts:~740-755`); "needs a page" toast `tool.needsPage` when no editor.
- **Keyboard**: Left/Right/Home/End on the tab list (`Ribbon.tsx:105-120`), Enter/Space opens, Esc closes; shortcut labels in Part C.

#### B9c. Theme ▾ and Copy & paste ▾

- **Opens from**: small ▾ beside Theme (ribbon View > Look; short bar Read set) and the Copy & paste button at the end of Home (`ThemePicker.tsx`, `CopyPasteMenu.tsx`, mounted by `ToolButton.tsx:24,31`).
- **Features**: Theme list: Day, Night, Sepia each with a swatch, tick on current (the Theme button itself cycles). Copy & paste menu: Copy as plain text, Copy as Markdown, Copy page with pictures, Paste as plain text.

#### B9d. "Which device is this?" dialog

- **Opens from**: first start, Settings > Device section (`settings/DeviceSection.tsx:55`), ribbon Go-to "Choose device type" (`toolRegistry.ts:677-682`), lazy in `App.tsx:198,1998`.
- **Features**: radio group Computer / laptop, iPad / tablet, Phone (`picker.device.*`); checkbox "I use an Apple Pencil" only for tablet; buttons "Not now" and "Use this"; preselected by detection (iPad posing as Mac handled).

- **Keyboard**: Esc, Tab trap, arrow keys move the radio (`DevicePicker.tsx:59-84`).

#### B9e. Bar overflow (BarScroll / ScrollFade) and ToolsBoundary

- **Features**: when buttons do not fit, the bar slides sideways with edge fades, a "More tools this way (N out of sight). Tap to slide the bar." chip and a drag handle "Slide the bar sideways (drag)" and "Slide the bar back" (`BarScroll.tsx:132,145,169`). `ToolsBoundary` is an error boundary for "selection bar", "options pill" and "ink surface" (`DockedBar.tsx:496-510`, `PageEditor.tsx:997`).

- **Features**: video strip (Open link, Copy link, Duplicate, Delete) and table strip (Copy as Markdown, Duplicate, Delete) (`stripItems.ts:102-125`); caption field Enter commits (`ContextStrip.tsx:37`). Floating ("free") bar reset (`freeBar.ts`), pill placement edges and nudging (`pillPlace.ts`, `OptionsPill.tsx:179-191`).

### B10. Layer gallery (A4 #78-#79)

(112); each pack card: "What it adds" list, "Add" (shows "Adding…") / "Remove" (131-181); "Added from files" section (165); "Add a layer file…" with hint "A file a friend shared with you." (189-190); remove confirm "You changed some things in "{name}": {list}. They will stay. Remove everything else?" with "Remove the rest" (202-206); toast `Added "{name}".`; close "Close the Layer gallery" (106).

- **Empty/error**: `layers.err.already` ("is already added. Remove it first to add it again."), `err.tooBig`.
- **v8**: none. It is not a toolbar surface. Decide whether it stays in Settings/More.

### B11. Ink layers card details and Merge dialog (A2 #35 partly)

- **Opens from**: Pen/Highlighter/Eraser pill layer chip, Pen/Highlighter/Lasso card "Layers >", ribbon "Ink layers" (`DetailCard.tsx:676-688`, `PillContent.tsx:332-347`).
- **Main file:line**: `LayersCard.tsx:31-245`.

Undo brings it back." with Undo, 188); "Page (the text)" row (199); "Add Layer 2" (211); "Merge {from} into {into}" opens dialog "Merge layers?" with warning and Cancel / Merge (55-66); "Merge extras into Layer 2" when a page has more than two layers ("This page has {count} layers from an earlier version.", 89-100); "Paper behind the ink" tiles Blank, Lined, Grid, Dots (229); "View as drawn" disabled with note (237-240).

### B12. Reader bar and reading frame (A5 #80-#82, #94-#99)

- **Opens from**: ribbon View > Reading mode / Full-screen reader, main bar Read, page ⋯ menu, Settings "The Read button opens" (In place / Full-screen reader), or a page/notebook flagged "Always open in Reading" (`PageView.tsx:262-269`).
- **Main file:line**: `ReaderControls.tsx:79-249`; frame `ReadingFrame.tsx:60-503`; state `readingState.ts`; tools `readingTools.ts:1-69`.
- **Features**:
  - Bar buttons (aria labels): Undo/Redo (`HistoryButtons`), "Exit reading", "Contents", "Bookmarks", "Reading look (Aa)", "Find", "Listen", "Edit page" (in place only), title (full-screen only), "Keep the bar visible" / "Let the bar hide while scrolling", "More reading options" (menu: Lock to reading, Line focus, Self-test (hide blanks), Full screen / Back to the page).
  - Bar states: full bar, or a mini ✕ top-left (`ReaderControls.tsx:150-170`); modes mini / always / top (Settings > Reading > Reader bar, `barMode.ts`); bar slides up and stays mounted but `inert` when hidden.
  - Progress: in place, a chip bottom-left "{page} · {percent}% · about N min left" or "finished" (`ReadingFrame.tsx:421-430`); full screen, a bottom bar with a seek slider (aria "Move through the page") and the same text (`ReadingFrame.tsx:483-503`).
  - Dim: black layer 0-60 % (`ReadingFrame.tsx:403-410`).
  - Line focus: mode off / focus (dims all but about two lines) / ruler (marks one line); follows the mouse, or the last tap for finger/Pencil; Alt+↑ / Alt+↓ moves one line (`ReadingRuler.tsx`).
  - Full-screen reader gestures: tap middle quarter toggles the bar; left/right quarters turn pages; swipes turn pages when paged; keys below; tap a hidden blank to reveal; while read-aloud runs, a tap on text reads from there (`ReadingFrame.tsx:222-285`).
  - Contents: opens the left-panel Outline tab (and leaves full-screen first) (`ReaderControls.tsx:124-130`).
  - Bookmark ribbons in the margin, also while editing (`bookmarkRibbons.ts`, `ReadingFrame.tsx:122-125`).
  - Reading time is counted for the streak and the screen is kept awake (`ReadingFrame.tsx:172-200`, `wakeLock.ts`).
- **Gating**: bar replaces the main bar and ribbon while reading; "Edit page" only in place and blocked while locked (toast "Reading is locked. Turn off "Lock to reading" in the Read tools first."); paged flow only in full screen (in place always scrolls; choosing Pages in place opens the full screen with a note, `reading.pagesNote`); Lock to reading blocks Esc exit (same toast).
- **Empty/error**: "No headings on this page." (outline), reading-locked toast.
- **Keyboard / Pencil**: Esc (when no panel is open), ArrowRight / PageDown, ArrowLeft / PageUp (paged only), Alt+↑/↓ (see Part C). The reader takes focus on open.

### B13. Aa sheet "Look and reading" (A5 #83-#88)

- **Opens from**: reader bar "Reading look (Aa)"; ribbon Font, Text size, Zoom (View/Home) (they call `openReadingPanel('settings')`, `toolRegistry.ts:239-252,573-579`).
- **Main file:line**: `AaSheet.tsx:60-371`, `FontTiles.tsx:40-148`, `savedThemes.ts`.
- **Features** (tablist "Look and reading", tabs Font, Layout, Themes, More):
  - Font: font tiles with sample sentence (Same as notes, Literata, Source Serif 4, Atkinson Hyperlegible Next, Lexend, OpenDyslexic, Caveat, Kalam, Patrick Hand, Shantell Sans; old Serif/Sans have no tile) and "Try your own words" field; "Import font" (.ttf, .otf, .woff2, up to 10 MB, note "...You are responsible for its licence."), "Remove this font", error "This is not a font file we can use (.ttf, .otf, .woff2)." (`FontTiles.tsx:74-138`); text size stepper "Smaller text" / "Bigger text" over 80, 90, 100, 115, 130, 150, 175, 200, 250 % (`readingPrefs.ts:134`).
  - Layout: Line spacing (130, 155, 180, 210), Margin (none, small, medium, large), Line width (narrow, medium, wide, full), Text alignment, Scrolling or pages (+ pages note).
  - Themes: Reading theme (Same as the app, Day, Night, Sepia, Paper, Dark contrast), "Saved looks" (four ready-made: Standard, Compact, Large, Low vision, plus up to 8 saved, each with remove "Remove the saved look {name}"), "Name for this look" field (max 40) and "Save current as…".
  - More: Line focus, Blanks and hidden words, Dim the page (0, 10…60 %, note that a web page cannot change brightness), Reader bar, Keep the screen awake while reading, switches "Always open this page / notebook in Reading", "Lock to reading (no editing)", streak "{count} days reading streak", "Daily reading goal" (No goal, 5, 10, 20, 30, 60 min) with progress "Today: x of y min" / "Goal reached today" / "Goal reached on N days this week".
- **Gating**: device settings (not synced per page); pages flow only full screen; "Always open this page" writes `readFirst` on the record.
- **Empty/error**: font import errors above.
- **v8**: V8.audit "Reading look sheet" names it only. The old reading card at `DetailCard.tsx:917-1018` (Font, Layout, Themes, More) is a third copy (Part D).

### B14. Read-aloud panel, Now playing, Find, Bookmarks, selection bar (A5 #89-#93)

- **Opens from**: reader bar "Listen" / "Find" / "Bookmarks"; ribbon View tools; selecting words while reading (selection bar); starting speech anywhere (Now playing pill, mounted in `App.tsx`).
- **Main file:line**: `ReadingPanels.tsx:182-332`, `NowPlayingPill.tsx:22-67`, `ReaderFind.tsx:150-289`, `BookmarksPanel.tsx:40-109`, `ReadingSelection.tsx:20-97`, controller `aloudController.ts`.
- **Features**:
  - Read-aloud panel: "Read this page" / "Pause" / "Resume", "Stop", "Back" / "Forward" (sentence or about 15 s), "Next page", speed chips 50, 75, 100, 125, 150, 175, 200 %, "Voice" select (This device's usual voice; ranked Premium > Enhanced > on-device) with the iPad voices guide text, "Sleep timer" (Off, End of page, 10, 20, 30, 60 min) with "Stops in {time}", hint "While it reads, tap a word to read from there.", "At the end of a page it goes on with the next page of the notebook." Unsupported browser message "This browser cannot read aloud. Try Safari or Chrome."
  - Toasts: "The sleep timer stopped reading.", "Reached the end of the notebook." (`ReadingFrame.tsx:167`).
  - Now playing pill: title, ⏯, ⏹, tap opens the page (any screen).
  - Find: scope tabs "This page / This notebook / Everywhere" (aria "Where to look"), field "Find" (Enter = next, Shift+Enter = previous, `ReaderFind.tsx:216-219`), "{n} of {total}", "Previous match" / "Next match", "Results" list for wider scopes, "No matches.", note "Carried on to the next page." when the last match moves on.
  - Bookmarks: "Add a bookmark here", list (snippet, position, date), remove "Remove bookmark {name}", toast "Bookmark removed" with Undo, "Bookmark added", empty "No bookmarks yet." (max 50 per page, `readingMarks.ts:25`).
  - Selection bar "Selected text": Highlight (yellow mark saved with the page, toast with Undo), Look up (opens Wiktionary in a new tab on tap only), Read from here.
- **Gating**: Web Speech API support; panels belong to Reading mode (closing reading closes them); only one panel at a time; scrolling the page does not close them.
- **Keyboard / Pencil**: see Part C.

### B15. Surfaces judged covered but with sub-gaps worth carrying into the v8 spec

- **Selection menu (A2 #31)**: v8 PMAP lists the items; gating not stated: menu sits above the selection when there is more than 120 px above, else below (`InkSurface.tsx:700,885`); "Play the recording from here" only if the strokes were drawn during a recording (`InkSurface.tsx:454-466,929-936`); "Paste" only if copied (`controller.canPaste()`); "Add to Summary" needs a name (toast "Name this drawing first (⋯ › Name this drawing), then add it to the Summary."); cut toast "Cut. Paste it from the ⋯ menu of a selection."; PNG copy toast "Picture copied" / "The picture could not be copied. Try Save as picture.".
- **Mixed menu (A2 #33)**: handles move/scale/turn the ink only; note `ink.mixed.moveNote`.
- **Layers card (A2 #35)**: see B11.
- **Hold menu**: PMAP names it; blank-paper variant in B5.

**Not present in code:** no handler for Apple Pencil squeeze, Pencil double-tap (hardware), or Pencil hover preview other than the pointer-events listed above. `grep` for squeeze / barrel / pencil double-tap in `src` finds only settings text and the Pencil lab counter (`app/lab/labStats.ts:83` counts pen hover moves). The web platform exposes none of them. v8 Pencil rows ("Pencil picks the Pen", "Tool bar: finger only", "Pencil writes text (Scribble)", "Pencil can scroll", "Pencil hold time" in `v8a.js` devRows) are Settings toggles and demos only.

## PART D: surprising, duplicated, half-built

1. **mapping.md covers Text only.** It is titled "v6 Text options"; it has no rows for ink, paper, zoom, layers, reading or the ribbon. Anyone reading it as "the v8 feature map" will believe far more is covered than is. The real v8 map lives inside the mock (`PMAP`, `toolbar-mock-v8.html:3463-3499`), not in `mapping.md`.
2. **"Turn into" does not exist.** `mapping.md` lists "Box menu: Turn into" as "Box ⋯ menu (unchanged)". The Box ⋯ menu has only Box style, Copy, Cut, Duplicate box, Colour, Layout, Delete box (`usePaperBoxes.tsx:306-362`). Searching `src` for "Turn into" / `turnInto` returns nothing. (There is an `ink.sel.rotate` label "Turn", which is a handle, not a menu item.) Also "Box menu: Format…" is NEW in v8, but no Format card exists in code (v8 plans it).
3. **Layout Assist is gone but still documented and mapped.** `mapping.md` ("Layout Assist"), `docs/features/paper-page.md` ("Layout Assist ... `LayoutAssist.tsx` (lazy)"), and the Box bar "▦" button all describe the old zone-picking flow. In code there is no `LayoutAssist.tsx`; the replacement is `LayoutDialog.tsx` (Layout mode v2, "owner, 11 Nov"). Roughly 30 translation keys are orphaned (`layout.assist.back/choose/empty/emptyBox/fail/leave/newBox/none/notebook/notebookConfirm/notebookDone/notebookTitle/phone/pick/recent/sheet/title/tray/unchanged/wholePage/words/zone`, `layout.mode.all/coach/pickFirst/undo`, `layout.notEnough` - `src/i18n/en/late/ink.ts:88-146`). "Apply to whole notebook" has no UI now (only the Pages list inside the new dialog, which applies per page together on Done). `BoxBar` takes an `onLayout` prop and never uses it (`BoxBar.tsx:38`, passed at `usePaperBoxes.tsx:~395`).
4. **Dead "layout mode" code path.** `PaperFrame.tsx` still has `layoutMode`, `layoutBar`, `data-layout-cover`, `LAYOUT_ZOOM` (`PaperFrame.tsx:128,170,195,1610-1670,2149`; `BoxFrame.tsx:331-337`), but `usePaperBoxes` hard-codes `layoutMode: false`, `layoutBar: null` (`usePaperBoxes.tsx:372-374`). Unreachable.
5. **Lock in place is unreachable.** Docs say Box ⋯ has "Lock in place"; keys `paper.box.lock` / `paper.box.unlock` exist (`late/ink.ts:157,164`) but nothing uses them. `box.locked` is honoured by drag, resize, delete and shows 🔒 (`BoxFrame.tsx:262-266`), so only pre-existing or synced data can be locked. v8's NEW "Lock size" has no matching code.
6. **Layout button duplicated three ways, absent from v8.** Fixed button beside Undo/Redo (`LayoutButton.tsx`), registry tool `layout` (Home > More format), Text strip button, Box menu item, Ctrl+Alt+L. The v8 first row has no Layout button.
7. **"Zoom" means two things.** View > Look "Zoom" (`toolRegistry.ts:573-579`) opens the reading Aa sheet; the paper zoom lives only in the pill under the page. The Magnifier tool also uses the `zoom` icon (`toolRegistry.ts:~478`). v8 More says "zoom" without saying which.
8. **Reading look exists three times.** Aa sheet (`AaSheet.tsx`), old Read Aa card (`main/DetailCard.tsx:917-1018` per tool-inventory), and Settings > Reading (`settings/readingSettings.ts` rows). The v8 mock keeps one "Reading look sheet".
9. **Contents is in the left panel, docs say a reading panel.** `docs/features/reading-mode.md` describes `ContentsPane.tsx` and a docked contents pane; the file does not exist in `src/app/reading` (44 files, none). The reader's Contents button calls `requestOutline` and, in full screen, first drops to in-place (`ReaderControls.tsx:124-130`). Keys `reading.contents.dock/fold/unfold` may be unused.
10. **Hold-and-slide gesture is invisible to v8** (A3 #54) and is a Pencil-first feature. `toolbar.md` documents it; the v8 mock has no trace.
11. **Handles mismatch.** v8 notes say a selected box has "corner dots (width)". Code has 8 handles including top and bottom (height) (`BoxFrame.tsx:405-466`). Docs also say "the red landing line, snap guides and side width handles are gone" (box grid); later code added smart guides (`guides.ts`) and the dividers.
12. **Stale doc statements already known** (tool-inventory): `toolbar.md:142` says Insert items "coming"; Mini toolbar "Save to notes" is a coming-later toast while SelectionMenu's works; "Toggle list" and "View as drawn" are disabled. `docs/features/paper-page.md` still says "a default paper style for new pages (Settings)" is "Not yet", but the setting exists (`settings/builtinSettings.ts:717-723`).
13. **Tool bar names in this area:** "Layout" (page grid), "Layouts" (v8 saved toolbar layouts), "Layer" (ink layer), "Layer gallery" (add-on packs, `src/layers`) are four unrelated things sharing words. `views-and-layers.md` covers the pack gallery under "Layers".
14. **Page add / delete / reorder was asked about but is not in scope code.** No such UI under `src/paper`, `src/ink`, `src/layers`, or the toolbar. It lives with notebooks/sidebar.
15. **Pencil gestures:** the web build has no squeeze or hardware double-tap handling; the v8 mock only toggles Settings.
16. **Mixed-case labels with no i18n:** hard-coded characters (✕, ✓, ≡, ⋯, 🔒, "−", "+", "%") and `aria-label={name}` for colour dots using raw ids (`LayoutDialog.tsx:564`, `InkSurface.tsx:989`) are not translated.
17. **Beginner / Professional:** nothing in paper, ink overlays, reading or layers varies by experience level; only the toolbar cards do. v8's rule "Beginner and Professional show the same rows" is already true for these.

---

Part A numbered rows 1-99; excluding row 29 (n/a), row 30 (dead), row 76 (duplicate marker) and the placeholder in 77 that is merged into groups, there are **96 live surfaces**.

Coverage tally (distinct live surfaces):

- **yes**: 29
- **partly**: 27
- **no**: 40

Tallied from the Part A tables by script (first word of the v8 column). Some rows bundle a family (for example row 52 DetailCard), so treat as approximate.

# APPENDIX I (09-mocked-areas-gaps.md)

## Gap check: mocked areas vs real code (read-only audit, 12 Nov 2026)

Method: every `t('...')` label, `role=`, `aria-label`, `MenuPopover`, `*Dialog`, `toast(` in the scoped files was read and compared with the matching inventory. Paths are relative to the repo root. Label text is the English value from `src/i18n/en/**` (resolved by key). Inventory references: Home = `home-inventory.md` (H#n = row n), Notebooks = `notebooks-inventory.md` (H01.., N01.., S01.., T01.., P01.., L01.., R01.., G01..), Search = `search-inventory.md` (E1..E34), Review = `review-inventory.md` (S/K/M/D/P/T/I ids), Reader = `reader-inventory.md` (R01..R92), Library = `library-inventory.md` (L/S/B/O ids), Settings = `settings-inventory.md` (setting ids). **AI has no inventory file**: the reference is `docs/features/ai.md` ("ai.md"), `docs/design/ai-tool-framework.md` and `pro-features.md` (which states "no AI mode mock exists yet").

Last column: **yes** = named in the inventory (quote given); **partly** = area/tab/menu is named but contents or some states are not; **no** = nothing in the inventory.

Reached from these areas but outside the requested scan paths, so listed but NOT deep-dived: `src/app/**` (QuietCorner, SpaceStrip, panel/LeftPanel, summary/*), `src/databases/**` (CalendarScreen, DatabasesScreen), `src/sync/*`, `src/connector/*`, `src/prompts/*`, `src/capture/*`, `src/history/*`, `src/templates/*`, `src/links/*`, `src/tags/*`, `src/revise/*`, `src/reader/pdfTools/*`, `src/trash/*`, `src/app/lock/*`.

Important finding up front: **there is no experience level (Beginner / Standard / Professional) anywhere in the code.** `grep -ri "experience|professional|beginner" src` hits only `src/i18n/en/late/ai.ts`, `src/prompts/seedPrompts.ts`, `src/templates/starterTemplates.ts` (sample text). `help.experience`, `help.firstLaunch`, `help.resetMock`, the "Your experience" and "Help & tour" categories, `data-pro` switches, the AI space and the Library tabs (Books/Knowledge/Bank) are mock-only. The only real gating axes are: device mode (Computer / Touch / Pencil, `src/app/deviceMode.ts:10`), per-setting switches, hidden spaces, AI on/off + per-notebook Allow AI, phone width (`phone:` classes), pointer type (`any-pointer-coarse:`), locks, and signed-in state.

## PART B. Full sections for every surface marked "partly" or "no"

Conventions: "Opens from" is the user path. Gating names the condition in code. All paths relative to repo root.

### B1 Home gaps

#### B1.1 Routines "no reminders" note

- Opens from: Home › Routines (bottom of tab). Main: src/home/Routines.tsx:436 (`home.routine.noReminders`: "There are no reminders or notifications: the web cannot send them without a server.", src/i18n/en/home.ts:155).
- Features: static text only; no button.
- Gating: shown whenever the Routines tab is open.
- States: none. (Documented in the code comment Routines.tsx:278-279.)

#### B1.2 Timer live region

- Opens from: always mounted (App.tsx:2025). Main: src/home/TimerChip.tsx:52-60.
- Features: sr-only `aria-live="polite"` text "Timer started" / "Timer stopped" (`home.timer.started` / `home.timer.stopped`).
- Gating: screen-reader only; renders when no timer is running.

#### B1.3 Home sidebar placeholder

- Opens from: sidebar while space = Home (src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:696-698).
- Main: src/app/spaces/SpacePlaceholder.tsx:20-53. Features: button "Open today's journal ({date})" (`space.home.today`, :42). For Library: button "Import" (`space.library.import`, :53, title `space.library.importSoon`) — only shown when no library panel is supplied (Sidebar.tsx:701).
- Gating: `compact` prop in the sidebar; phone drawer rules of the sidebar apply.

### B2 Notebooks gaps

#### B2.1 Notebooks home "⋯" / right-click menu (partly)

- Opens from: Notebooks space › "⋯" button on a card (NotebooksHome.tsx:411-430) or right-click / context menu on a card or row (:448-450).
- Features (NotebooksHome.tsx:161-200): "Pin to the top" / "Unpin" (`nbh.star` / `nbh.unstar`); "Show a colour cover" / "Show the first page as the cover" (`nbh.cover.colour` / `nbh.cover.page`); "Change colour" (`nbh.colour.next`, cycles to next of 8).
- Not in this menu (inventory H14-H18 imply they are): Rename, Delete, Review this notebook, Add page, Move up/down. They live in the sidebar row menu (src/notebooks/NotebookRowMenu.tsx:35-84).
- Gating: mouse right-click vs touch "⋯" (button always rendered); `any-pointer-coarse` makes controls 44 px.
- States: virtualised list (rows computed from scroll, NotebooksHome.tsx:104-135); empty messages for Starred / Recent.

#### B2.2 "+" add tile

- NotebooksHome.tsx:531-545, `aria-label` "New notebook", only in shelf layout on the All tab (`addTile`, :111).

#### B2.3 First-run empty state

- Opens from: Notebooks space with zero notebooks and no open page (App.tsx:1880-1888).
- Features: icon 📓, text "Create a notebook to begin, or open today's journal.", button "Create notebook".
- Gating: `notebooks.length === 0 && currentPage === null`, any space except home/library/review.

#### B2.4 Notebook "Tab options" menu (partly)

- Opens from: "⋯" next to the tab strip (NotebookScreen.tsx:245-250).
- Features (:122-188): Rename "{tab}" (`notebook.renameTab`); Hide "{tab}" (`notebook.hideTab`, only when more than one tab); Export notebook; Pause reviews / Resume (`pause.pause`/`pause.resume`, src/app/pauseMenu.ts:21); Lock this notebook / Remove lock (useLockItem); Edit-lock item (editLockItem); Share as layer file (`layers.share`, toast `layers.shared`); Page address ▸ (`path.menu` "Page address" with path styles and `path.menu.default`, src/pages/pageAddressMenu.ts:15-25); Show "{hidden tab}" (one per hidden tab).
- Missing from inventory: Pause reviews, Page address ▸.
- Gating: edit-lock disables items; Hide needs > 1 tab.

#### B2.5 Table view / Summary tab / Revise tab / extra-tab bodies

- Table view: src/notebooks/NotesViews.tsx:270-300, header cells `notebook.col.title`, `notebook.col.sub`, `notebook.col.changed`.
- Notebook Summary tab: src/app/summary/SummaryView.tsx:63-300 — filter "Show the Summary of" (AppSummary only), page heading link ("Open page"), line list (`summary.lines`), each line: text, goto (`summary.goto`), remove (`summary.remove`), "gone" marker (`summary.gone`), own-line field (placeholder `summary.linePlaceholder`, button "Add" `summary.lineAdd`). Notebook scope mounted NotebookScreen.tsx:268.
- Revise tab: src/revise/ReviseTab.tsx:44-190 — lesson list (`revise.lessons`), "Open lesson" (`revise.openLesson`), "Practise this lesson" (`revise.practice`), marks (named in Settings review.reviseLabels), "My summary" textarea (`revise.summary`, placeholder `revise.summaryPlaceholder`), empty texts `revise.noLessons`, `revise.noMarks`.
- Gating: tab visible unless hidden (Tab options); Summary tab auto-added to older notebooks (NotebookScreen.tsx:100-104).

#### B2.6 Sidebar extras

- "All summaries" (Sidebar.tsx:344-354, `summary.sidebar`) opens the Study hub Summary segment (App.tsx:1652-1668).
- Notebook drag reorder (Sidebar.tsx:235, 279-296): hold-drag a notebook row, a drop line shows before/after.
- Template confirm dialog (Sidebar.tsx:222-230, `sidebar.confirm.template`, confirm label `ui.delete`).
- SidebarResizer (App.tsx:1523; `src/app/SidebarResizer.tsx`): drag handle for width (`--sidebar-width`, min(…,50vw)); hidden class `wide:hidden`.
- Gating: Study group only in Notebooks and Review spaces; Quick access, Notebooks, Tags, Templates only in Notebooks space; Saved searches in Notebooks and Review; on phone the sidebar is a fixed drawer (`phone:` classes, Sidebar.tsx:364-369) with a close-menu button (:372-384).

#### B2.7 Quiet corner extras

- src/app/spaces/QuietCorner.tsx:96-190. Items: Settings (`settings.title`), Save & restore (`sidebar.data`), Trash section (TrashSection), Device mode row (DeviceModeMenuRow), Layer gallery (`layers.open`, :155), Refresh app (`refresh.app`, :168 → softRefresh), Help (`corner.help`, :174), Theme group (`sidebar.theme.label`). Closes on Esc / outside tap / choosing Settings, Save & restore or Help (QuietCorner.tsx:21-24, 56-64).

#### B2.8 Page breadcrumb (PagePath)

- Opens from: top bar slot of any page (PageView.tsx:462-472). Main: src/pages/PagePath.tsx:178-320.
- Features: breadcrumb nav (`page.breadcrumb`); sibling-pages menu "Pages at this level" (`path.siblings`, empty text `path.noPages`) PagePath.tsx:200-247; "Hidden parts of the path" menu (`path.hidden`) :253-282; "Full path" menu (`path.full`, `path.showFull`) :288-320.
- Gating: style = notebook/page/Settings `general.pathStyle` ("Page address", page > notebook > Settings); row of a database shows the database name (`rowDatabase`).

#### B2.9 Page "More options" (⋯) menu

- Opens from: ⋯ in the page top bar (`page.more` "More options", PageView.tsx:471-487).
- Features in order (PageView.tsx:294-420): Review this page (Alt+R hint) · Prompts and skills (`prompts.menu`) [both only in a notebook and not a template]; History; Full-screen reader (`tool.readerFull`); Paper style ▸ and Paper size ▸ (A4/Letter) [not for templates]; Lock this page / Remove lock; Edit-lock (hidden when the notebook or account already edit-locks); Add to / Remove from favourites (toast `fav.added`/`fav.removed`); Save as template (notebook, not template); Pause reviews / Resume (notebook, not template); Import (opens Save & restore); Export; Search all features… (Ctrl+Shift+K); Copy & paste ▸ (Copy page with pictures, Copy page as plain text, Copy page as Markdown, Paste as plain text); Page address ▸; Device mode ▸; Hide tool bar / Show tool bar (`bar.hide`/`bar.show`); Delete page (red, with Undo toast, `tree.deletePage`); Save & restore (`page.restoreBackup`).
- Gating: `notebook && !isTemplate` for six items; `isTemplate` hides paper items; edit-lock source (page, notebook, account) hides/changes lock items; Hide/Show tool bar depends on `barPlacement`.
- States: toasts `fav.*`, `page.savedTemplate`, `history.restored`, `editLock.unlocked`.

#### B2.10 Banners and gate bars

- Template banner (PageView.tsx:500-513): "Template." + `page.template.text` + button "New page from this template".
- Today banner (PageView.tsx:514-523): "Today's journal" + `page.today.text`.
- Read-only bar (PageView.tsx:527-535): "Read-only until you sign in" (status role, `editLockBy === 'account'`).
- Edit-lock bar (PageView.tsx:537-554): "Locked — tap to unlock" / "Notebook locked — tap to unlock"; tap unlocks and toasts "Unlocked. You can edit again."
- Gating: hidden in the reader view (`reader`).

#### B2.11 Page tab row and tab bodies

- Page tab row (src/app/summary/PageTabs.tsx:24-90): group "Page views" (`summary.seg.label`) with buttons Notes · Summary · Review plus the notebook's extras Mind map, Slides, Audio & video, Database; "⋯" button (`tabs.menu` "Tabs") opens menu (`tabs.menuLabel`) with a check item for each extra tab (toggle on/off for the notebook, `setNotebookPageTab`).
- Page Review tab (PageTabs.tsx:98-127): "{due} due, {new} new" counts (`summary.review.counts`), empty `summary.review.none`, button "Practise" (`summary.review.practice`). Opens the Review session for the page and sub-pages.

## PART C. Specific questions

1. **Calendar screen / year-month-week view outside databases?** Yes, a screen exists: `src/databases/CalendarScreen.tsx:51` (Day / Week / Month, MODES at :34, default month, Today/Previous/Next), opened from the Notebooks sidebar (Quick access › Calendar, `src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:446-466`, handler `onOpenCalendar`) and the `hub.kind === 'calendar'` branch at `src/App.tsx:1689`. It lives in the databases folder and shows dated database rows and journal days (`calendarEntries`). **No year view** (modes are 'day' | 'week' | 'month'). There is no calendar on Home (Plan tab is a one-day hourly timeline, `src/home/PlanTab.tsx:165`, with prev/next day buttons :99-113). The database Calendar _view_ is separate (`src/databases/views/calendarData.ts`).
2. **Journal / daily-page screen?** No dedicated screen. The journal is a special notebook (`notebook.kind === 'journal'`, `src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx:663`, `src/pages/PageTree.tsx` journal prop) of daily pages created by `src/journal/journalService.ts`. Entry points: sidebar "Open today's journal" (`Sidebar.tsx:391-406`), Home button (`src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:133-141`), Home sidebar placeholder (`SpacePlaceholder.tsx:42`), Calendar day card "Open / Start this day's journal" (`CalendarDayCard.tsx:80`), Evening review appends to it (`EveningReview.tsx:62`). A journal page shows a "Today's journal" banner (`PageView.tsx:514-523`) and a Day record block.
3. **Notifications / reminders UI?** No push or local notifications and no reminder settings: `grep -ri "Notification\|requestPermission"` finds nothing in code; Home states it explicitly (`src/home/Routines.tsx:436`, text `home.routine.noReminders`). What exists is in-app banners: Backup reminder bar ("Save backup now" / "Remind me later", `src/share/ReminderBar.tsx:20-29`, logic `src/share/backupReminder.ts`, mounted `src/App.tsx:2012`), update banner/pill (`src/app/UpdateBar.tsx`, `UpdatePill.tsx`, `MandatoryUpdate.tsx`), the attention banner for sync/Drive/clock problems (`src/app/attention/AttentionBanner.tsx:28-103`), the locked-alert and account gate bars (`src/sync/LockedAlert.tsx`, `AccountGateBar.tsx`), the "Still working?" timer prompt (`TimerChip.tsx:121-170`), the review "waiting" message, and toasts. `src/settings/reviewTime.ts` is only a time estimate.
4. **Activity log?** Three partial ones, no general log: (a) Claude Activity tab in Settings › Claude (`src/connector/inbox/ClaudeActivity.tsx:100-180`, undoable rows); (b) AI "Recent calls" list + CSV in Usage and cost (`src/ai/UsageSection.tsx:150-216`) and the send table in Permissions (`src/ai/PermissionsSection.tsx:116`); (c) page history (`src/history/HistoryDialog.tsx`) and the append-only review log (not a screen). Design only: AI Activity tab (ai-tool-framework §14).
5. **Statistics / reports screen?** Home › Reports tab (`src/home/ReportsTab.tsx:101-175`: Today/Week, hours per project, plan vs actual). Also: database dashboards/charts (`src/databases/dashboard/DashboardView.tsx`, `ChartDialog.tsx`), AI Usage and cost (`UsageSection.tsx`), reading streak in Reading mode (`src/app/reading/ReadingFrame.tsx`, `AaSheet.tsx` grep "streak"), Review counts New/Learning/Due on deck tiles (`ReviewHome.tsx:268`). There is **no review statistics / forecast / heatmap screen** (review inventory T04 "Forecast" is mock-only; "Answers per day and study streak" T03 are data in `databases/trackerHistory.ts`, shown only through a Study tracker database/dashboard).
6. **"What's new" / changelog screen?** Does not exist in code (`grep -ri "whatsNew\|what's new\|changelog\|release notes" src` returns nothing). Only the update offer (`src/app/UpdatePill.tsx`, `UpdateBar.tsx`) and Settings › About "Check for updates now" (`AboutSection.tsx:65`).
7. **Feedback / report-a-bug form?** Does not exist (`grep -rni "feedback\|report a bug\|contact" src --include=*.tsx` returns no UI). Nearest: Settings › Advanced "Slow-moments report" and "Tool state" copy buttons (`src/settings/labSettings.ts:28-119`, copy to clipboard) and the error boundary's "Go home / Save everything" screen (`src/app/ErrorBoundary.tsx`).
8. **Language picker?** Yes, a setting, not a screen: `general.language` segmented control English / हिन्दी / Español (`src/settings/builtinSettings.ts:138-155`, names in `src/i18n/languages.ts:10-14`), device-scoped, in Settings › General. Spanish/Hindi load lazily (`languages.ts:24-28`).
9. **Phone-only screens / bottom tab bar?** There is a bottom tab bar: the space strip becomes a fixed bottom bar with icon + label on phone (`src/app/spaces/SpaceStrip.tsx:8, 27-57`, `phone:fixed phone:bottom-0 phone:h-14`). The sidebar becomes a slide-in drawer on phone (`Sidebar.tsx:364-369`, close button :372-384, `phone:bottom-14`). The Study hub shows a phone-only close "×" (`AllCardsScreen.tsx:46-52`). Calendar uses `phone:px-3`. There are no separate phone-only _screens_; everything is responsive via Tailwind `phone:` / `any-pointer-coarse:` variants.
10. **Widgets / PWA install prompt?** No home-screen widgets (`grep -ri widget src` only matches editor embed/clock helpers: `src/editor/embed/embedClock.ts`, `tableResize.ts`, `writePad/ghost.ts`, dashboard chart data). No in-app install prompt: no `beforeinstallprompt` handler (grep empty); the PWA is a web manifest (`public/manifest.webmanifest`, `index.html:7`) + service worker (`vite-plugin-pwa`, `src/main.tsx`) and the only install UI is text in Settings › This device ("Install first, then start", `DeviceSection.tsx:77-80`).
11. **Print dialog?** No app-built print dialog; two paths call the browser's: Export dialog "Print" (`src/share/export/ExportDialog.tsx:163, :201`; `exportService.ts:285 win.print()` through a hidden frame) and the recovery code "Print" button (`src/sync/RecoveryCodeView.tsx:57-76`). Print CSS: `src/index.css:659` hides ink canvases.

---

## PART D. Surprising, duplicated or half-built

1. **Experience levels exist only in mocks.** `settings-inventory.md` tags rows E/C/A and adds `help.experience`, `help.firstLaunch`, `help.resetMock`, categories "Your experience" and "Help & tour"; none of it is in code (see top). Same for `pro-features.md` (`data-pro` ids). All 137 rows in the Settings inventory are visible to everyone in code, subject only to per-setting `shownWhen` rules (`SettingsScreen.tsx:236-257`).
2. **Settings category mismatch.** Code has 13 categories (Account, General, Review, Journal, Reading, Lock, Sync, AI, Permissions, Claude, Tool bar, Advanced, Connections). The inventory regroups into 13 different ones (AI & connections merges four; "Pencil & touch" splits from Tool bar; Journal is an empty category in both: `journal.none`, "nothing to set yet").
3. **Notebooks inventory attributes sidebar-menu actions to the Notebooks home.** H14-H18 (Rename, Delete, Review, Add page, Move up/down) are in `NotebookRowMenu.tsx` (sidebar) only; the home menu has Pin / cover / colour (B2.1). A mock that puts them on the home card would add UI, not just restyle it.
4. **Duplicate names.** Two files called `ReviewHome.tsx`: `src/notebooks/ReviewHome.tsx` (Review space home, `rvh.*`) and `src/recall/ReviewHome.tsx` (decks block, `decks.*`). Two "DatabaseTab"s: `src/pages/tabs/DatabaseTab.tsx` (page tab) and the one used in `NotebookScreen.tsx:276` (notebook tab). Two "AloudPanel"s: `src/reader/aloud/AloudPanel.tsx` (left panel) and `src/app/reading/ReadingPanels.tsx:152` (in-place reading). Review "Practise" button appears in four places (page Review tab, Revise tab, Cards tab "Practice", Review done screen).
5. **Saved-search management already exists.** `search-inventory.md` A5 lists "rename, duplicate, delete with Undo" as added by the mock; code already has Rename (NameDialog), Delete (ConfirmDialog, no Undo) and "Review N cards" (`SavedSearchesSection.tsx:97-150`). Only Duplicate and Undo are new.
6. **Library tabs are mock-only.** Books / Knowledge / Bank, shelves, collections, themes, reading journal, etc. (library-inventory last paragraph) have no code; the real Library is a filter-chip grid with five kinds. `search.coming.book` and `search.coming.highlights` placeholders confirm Books/Highlights search is parked.
7. **Half-built / documented but missing.** Reader R85 Liquid mode (docs only; reader-inventory says "code not found"); Find in reader works for PDF only (R08 note). Quiet-corner "Trash" and "Device mode" are in the corner (QuietCorner.tsx:143-145) although the home inventory #30 omits them.
8. **Static "no reminders" statement vs Home mock.** Home mock adds reminders-like glance widgets and Customise (#34-#39 tagged A); code explicitly states the web cannot do notifications (Routines.tsx:278-279).
9. **Large unlisted page menu.** The page ⋯ menu (B2.9, ~22 items incl. History, Full-screen reader, Paper style/size, Import, Export, Search all features, Copy & paste ▸, Device mode ▸, Hide tool bar) is not in any inventory in this set; same for the page tab row's Review/Summary segments and the Page history dialog. These are the biggest hole in the Notebooks inventory.
10. **AI is much smaller in code than in the mock.** Real: Settings AI/Permissions/Connections/Claude pages, Ask this book, page actions, Convert ink, Write pad AI read. Not real: AI space, Build, chat, Studio, Jobs, Workflows, Activity (AI), Tool catalog, effort, Auto model chip, top-bar AI button, per-job cost guard, plan executor (`docs/design/ai-tool-framework.md` is a design, 474 lines, rollout slices 1-7 unbuilt).
11. **Two versions of "AI off" copy.** `AiOffCard` is shown in Settings AI, Ask this book, Prompts, Skills, Convert and Write pad; but page-action buttons simply hide when AI is off (ai.md Audit item 2), so Library header shows no hint of AI.
12. **Touch-only close button on the Study hub** (`AllCardsScreen.tsx:46`, `hidden … phone:flex`) — on wide screens the hub has no Close; users leave via the sidebar.
13. **Home "Search Tasks" works but Home has no search box**; Tasks results open Home (`TaskResults.tsx`).
